Can fly.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"Snipe the sniper down with a stellar hit.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+100%","amount":"-","speed":"-","theme name":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Alamaric Sniper","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_SNIPER","flight":"Yes","armor":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","alias":"Sniper","theme":"","movespeed":"+80%","controller":"TP_Sniper_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Arrow of Goth"}
The Alamaric Sniper is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapon is  Arrow of Goth.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Snipe the sniper down with a stellar hit."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must defeat 100  Amalaric Sniper enemies with the  Stellar Blade, the evolution of the  Discus. The top of the Cathedral (the top part of the far east building), as well as the bridge next to the entrance to the Clock Tower, are the best places to do this.
Once unlocked, the Alamaric Sniper can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Alamaric Sniper can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "gothangel".
## Passive bonuses
The Alamaric Sniper flies over terrain, thus ignoring walls and obstacles. It also starts with +80%  Move Speed, +100%  Luck and gains +1% Luck every level, which makes weapons with a luck-based effect (like his Arrow of Goth) perform well.
The Alamaric Sniper can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## External links
- Amalaric Sniper on the Castlevania Wiki

Gains a bonus to  Might and  Cooldown depending on how many enemies are in sight.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_ALBUS_2DB Optical Shot Ebony Diabologue
Albus is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Optical Shot. 
Upon unlocking the  Ebony Diabologue, Albus is able to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Albus can be unlocked by fully evolving  Confodere into  Melio Confodere after obtaining the  Black Disk. Albus is then made available for purchase for 2,000, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Albus can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bigbrother".
## Passive bonuses
Albus starts with +20  Max Health and +40%  Move Speed.
Albus gains a bonus to Might and Cooldown based on the proportion of on-screen enemies out of 300. At 300 enemies, Albus has a maximum of -40% Cooldown and +40% Might.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Items that increase  Curse and  Charm, such as  Torrona's Box and  Karoma's Mana, can be picked to increase the number of enemies on the screen and maximize the bonuses.
-  Wicked Season (XIII) may also be chosen early to increase Curse.
## External links
- Albus in the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_ALUCARD_2 Alucart Sworb Candybox
Alucard is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Alucart Sworb.
Unlocking and using the  Morning Star,  Coat of Arms,  Spectral Sword,  Belnades' Spellbook and  Ebony Diabologue will unlock the  Candybox as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Alucard can be unlocked by defeating the  Doppelganger, located in the Art Gallery on the Ode to Castlevania map, with  Trevor Belmont. He can then be purchased for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Alucard can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "adrianfahrenheit".
## Passive bonuses
Alucard starts with +100  Max Health, +2  Armor and +50%  Move Speed. He is immune to health drain.
He can also trigger various hidden weapons when meeting specific conditions:
- Standing still will trigger  Soul Steal via a Charge, with a charge time of 30 seconds.
- Upon levelling up, he will trigger  Dominus Anger.
- When overhealing by +8, he will trigger  Summon Spirit.
- Upon reaching a critically low health, being 20% of his max health, Alucard will trigger  Sword Brothers. This can only be triggered again after full healing. Fatal damage that defeats Alucard will not trigger this effect.
Alucard can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

## Trivia
- Soul Steal, Dark Inferno, Summon Spirit and Sword Brothers are all spells that Alucard can use in Symphony of the Night.
- Alucard has a special attack animation when using the  Fire Wand, lifting his cape like he does when using the Hellfire and Dark Inferno spells in Symphony of the Night.
- The spell "adrianfahrenheit" is a reference to his real name, Adrian Fahrenheit Tepes.
- Alucard's pose in the selection menu changes when altering his starting weapon. He is shown mid-walk when the  Candybox is selected.
- Alucard is one of the few characters with animated idle sprites.

DEFAULTSKIN_EME_SELECT Spirit Rings Intuition
Ameya Aisling is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Spirit Rings. She can also pick  Intuition as a starting weapon (which needs to be unlocked).
## Unlocking
Ameya Aisling can be unlocked by fully evolving the  Twin Dragon (into  Gekkabijin, then maxing it to level 6).
Evolving  Spirit Rings unlocks  Intuition as her optional starting weapon.
Alternatively, Ameya Aisling can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "yumehaizumi".
## Passive bonuses
Ameya Aisling starts with +20%  Move Speed, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip, and +5  Banish.
Depending on Ameya's level, more cats will spawn at a higher frequency:
- From Lvl. 1 to Lvl. 20, 1 cat spawns every 5 seconds.
- From Lvl. 21 to Lvl. 40, 2 cats spawn every 5 seconds.
- From Lvl. 41 to Lvl. 60, 2 cats spawn every 4 seconds.
- From Lvl. 61 to Lvl. 80, 3 cats spawn every 4 seconds.
- After Lvl. 81, 3 cats spawn every 3 seconds.
Ameya Aisling gains stat bonuses when collecting her summoned cats. The stat bonuses from the cats are:
| Stats | Bonus
| Might | +2%
| Area | +2%
| Speed | +2%
| Duration | +2%
| Cooldown | -1%
| Luck | +2%
| Growth | +2%
| Magnet | +0.2
When approached, the cats will run away from Ameya. 
- Occasionally, a rainbow cat will spawn that is much faster than the other cat variants. Collecting this cat will attract all other cats. Otherwise, the variant of cat does not matter.
Ameya also triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
## Tips
- The cats will spawn out of bounds, making levels with narrow passages like Inlaid Library less effective at stat gains, however, due to how cats are dragged towards you while out of bounds it is possible to farm them very easily with a large enough magnet radius and large amounts of surrounding out of bounds space.
## Trivia
- Her unlocking spell is a reference to her human identity in SaGa Emerald Beyond, an 11-year-old schoolgirl named Yumeha Izumi.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_ANNETTE_PENDULUMSKIN_TP_ANNETTE_ELEVATORSKIN_TP_ANNETTE_HEADS Endo Gears Peri Pendulum Myo Lift Epi Head
Annette is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is one of  Endo Gears,  Peri Pendulum,  Myo Lift, or  Epi Head.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Build one tower inside another"
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Annette can be unlocked by creating the  Clock Tower while playing on Gallo Tower.
Once unlocked, Annette can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Annette can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "aljibarian".
## Passive bonuses
Annette starts with +1  Recovery and -10%  Cooldown. When entering a stage as Annette, all four clockwork weapons are spawned nearby as Stage Items.  Endo Gears spawns to the north,  Myo Lift to the west,  Peri Pendulum to the east, and  Epi Head to the south. 
- The choice of starting weapon does not affect which weapons spawn, so the player is effectively given a free level up for their choice of starting weapon.
Picking up  Dominus Hatred and upgrading it to level 6 will transform Annette into  Carmilla along with recieving her stats and passive bonuses.
## Trivia
- Annette's unlock condition, theme music, and starting weapons are all a reference to her being imprisoned in the Clock Tower in Castlevania: Rondo of Blood.
- Annette's sprites were initially designed after her appearance in Castlevania: The Dracula X Chronicles.
## External links
- Annette on the Castlevania Wiki

Gains 10% more Damage every 10 levels (max +50%).
DEFAULTLEGACY
Antonio Belpaese is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors. He is the character that every player begins with. His starting weapon is the  Whip.
## Passive bonuses
Antonio starts with +20  Max Health and +1  Armor. He gains +10%  Might every 10 levels until level 50. The maximum Might gained this way is +50%.
The following table shows how much Might he gains by level:
| Levels | Might
| 1-9 | 0%
| 10-19 | +10%
| 20-29 | +20%
| 30-39 | +30%
| 40-49 | +40%
| 50+ | +50%

## Other

 [Antonio with his Whip as depicted in the main menu cover art (center).]
 Antonio with his Whip as depicted in the main menu cover art (center).

 [Antonio as seen in the pre-release trailer of Ode to Castlevania.]

## Trivia
- Antonio's family name Belpaese is derived from the phrase Bel Paese, which is Italian for "beautiful country", and also refers to a semi-soft Italian cheese with the same name.
- Antonio's family name, overall design, and use of a whip as his primary weapon are all nods to the Belmont family, the clan of vampire slayers known for slaying Dracula several times in the Castlevania series.
- His Adventure mode shop quote, "What a wonderful night to hunt beasts!" is an ironic reference to the famous line "What a horrible night to have a curse." from Castlevania II: Simon's Quest.
- The color scheme of his hair and clothing have a striking resemblance to Simon Belmont in Haunted Castle.
- Antonio is the one of two free characters in the game, the other being  Queen Sigma.
- However, Antonio is the only one that is obtainable at the start of the game. Unlocking Queen Sigma requires for the player to complete the Collection 1.0.
- Including DLCs, Antonio is one of six free characters, as Ode to Castlevania introduces four more.
## In other media
- Antonio Belpaese makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. He is a reskin of Haiku in that game.

Arca LadonnaArca Ladonna (Legacy)Weapon cooldown is reduced by 5% every 10 levels (max -15%).
Weapon cooldown is reduced by 5% every 10 levels (max -15%).
DEFAULTLEGACY
Arca Ladonna is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.0. His starting weapon is  Fire Wand.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by getting the Fire Wand to level 4, then purchasing this character with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Arca can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "noneladonna".
## Passive bonuses
Arca starts with +10%  Might. He also gains -5%  Cooldown every 10 levels until level 30. The maximum Cooldown reduction gained this way is -15%.
The following table shows how much Cooldown reduction he gains by level:
| Levels | Cooldown
| 1-9 | 0%
| 10-19 | -5%
| 20-29 | -10%
| 30+ | -15%
## Tips
- The explosion effect for  Fire Wand or  Hellfire from  Heart of Fire (XIX) is very useful in the early game. It is advisable to select it as the first Arcana.
## Sprites
 -

 Default Arca sprite.

 [Legacy Arca sprite.]

## Animated sprites

 [Movement animation of default Arca.]
 Movement animation of default Arca.

 [Movement animation of legacy Arca.]
 Movement animation of legacy Arca.
## Trivia
- Arca is Italian for "ark", and Ladonna is Italian for "the woman", a reference to Arc the Lad, a JRPG starring the titular Arc.
- Arca's complete name is "Arca Ladonna," which when combined with  Porta's name closely resembles the Italian expression "Porca Madonna," a blasphemy which heavily insults the Virgin Mary relating her to a sow.
- "Arca Ladonna" in Italian sounds like "'orca Madonna", which is a regional variant on the blasphemous idiom "Porca Madonna"
- Arca also seems to heavily reference Alucard from the Castlevania series, his name being not far off, his outfit and hairstyle appearing similar, and his starting weapon being similar to Alucard's Hellfire spell from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
## In other media
- Arca Ladonna makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. He is a reskin of Splunk in that game.

Ariana can be unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Short Gun. She needs to be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.

Summons remnants of defeated enemies every time the clock strikes a minute.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Ashella.","secret":"no","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Ashtart","movespeed":"-","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","coffin":"no","dlc":"bm","recovery":"-","might":"-20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+8","curse":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"ASHTART","flight":"","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","alias":"","theme":"Borrowed Feathers","skin id":"DEFAULT","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_Ashtart","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"Incineration"}
Ashtart is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Incineration.
## Unlocking
Ashtart can be unlocked by evolving the  Ashella. She then must be purchased.
Alternatively, Ashtart can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "gonehollow".
## Passive bonuses
Ashtart starts with -20%  Might, -10%  Cooldown, +50%  Greed and +8  Banish.
Ashtart will gain +1  Revival upon falling to CriticalHP.
As the start of every minute (X:00), Ashtart will summon skeletons depending on how many enemies were defeated in that minute. Defeating an enemy has a 25% chance, multiplied by  Curse, to add 1 skeleton to the next batch summoned. When summoned, Ashtart's skeletons are invulnerable for 0.3 seconds and seek out enemies, exploding upon collision and insta-killing nearby enemies. If an enemy hit has insta-kill immunity, they will instead be dealt damage directly. A maximum of 200 skeletons can be on-screen at a time. Skeletons will return to Ashtart if no enemies are able to be targeted.
Ashtart is immune to Health Drain effects.
## Tips
- Increasing both  Curse and  Charm can drastically increase how many skeletons Ashtart summons each minute, as more enemies will spawn while being more likely to increase the number of skeletons spawned.
- Ashtart works well with the  Velvet Dodecahedron, as it allows her to gain up to +6  Revival simply from levelling it up. In addition, the lowered health is less of an issue for her than usual thanks to all the Revivals she can generate and her skeletons distracting enemies.
- If building around Revival, Ashtart can make great use of  Phieraggi and  Meal Ticket [DLC].
-  Sarabande of Healing (VI) ensures that Ashtart's HP-critical ability recharges immediately upon revival, making her effectively immortal against anything that can't deal more than 20% of her max HP. If she also has the  Awake (IV) arcana, she can infinitely scale her stats for as long as enemies are able to kill her.

Can turn defeated enemies into followers.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Practice non-violence amongst the flowers with the charmed young Morris.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Astarte","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","hidden weapon":"Star Flail","recovery":"+1","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","defang":"+15%","greed":"+50%","id":"TP_ASTARTE","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Astarte_Character","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"No"}
Astarte is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Practice non-violence amongst the flowers with the charmed young Morris."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After unlocking  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run as  Jonathan Morris,  Charlotte & Jonathan, or  Jonathan & Charlotte, using one of their skins to start with a weapon selector ( Morning Star,  Coat of Arms,  Spectral Sword, or  Belnades' Spellbook) on Il Molise. The player must skip the selection of a weapon. This will lead to the player not having a starting weapon. Then, the player must avoid defeating any enemies until the timer reads at least 5:00, then quit the run. 
Alternatively, Astarte can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "nomancandefy".
## Passive bonuses
Astarte starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Recovery, +20%  Move Speed, +50%  Greed, and +15%  Defang.
She is adept with  Star Flail [DLC].
She starts with a hidden Star Flail. It is upgraded every 7 levels starting at level 7, up to level 49 when it is maxed at level 8.
She can turn enemies she kills into followers if they can become one. The maximum number of followers is calculated by level/10 rounded to the nearest integer, with a minimum of 1 and a maximum of 10. The following table shows the maximum number of followers by level:
| Levels | Maximum followers
| 1-14 | 1
| 15-25 | 2
| 26-34 | 3
| 35-45 | 4
| 46-54 | 5
| 55-65 | 6
| 66-74 | 7
| 75-85 | 8
| 86-94 | 9
| 95+ | 10
Astarte is also able to destroy light sources by touching them.
## Trivia
- For the maximum number of followers, because of the chosen rounding method, numbers ending in .5 are rounded to the even number. This caused the increases to take 9 or 11 levels alternatively, instead of every 10 levels.
## External links
- Astarte on the Castlevania Wiki

Atlantis Shrine Wizard is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapons are the  Hydro Storm and a hidden  Acquazzone.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Combine holy liquids in a sunken city"
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, The Shrine Wizard can be unlocked by creating the  Hydro Pump Climax, the union of  La Borra and  Hydro Storm, while playing on Moongolow.
Once unlocked, Atlantis Shrine Wizard can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Atlantis Shrine Wizard can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "dampmage".
## Passive bonuses
Atlantis Shrine Wizard starts with with +40%  Move Speed, +40%  Duration, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip and +5  Banish.
Atlantis Shrine Wizard triggers all equipped  Coat of Arms weapons when overhealing by +32. When overhealing by +32 the hidden  Acquazzone will begin to rise from bottom of the screen, with small increases in height after each instance of +32 overhealing. This effect is capped at 50% the height of the screen.
## Tips
- An easy way to achieve max height with his passive is to use  Lifesign Scan and  Pummarola with the Arcana  Sarabande of Healing (VI).

Good friends might then show the way.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+50%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+10","name":"Avatar Infernas","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"+1","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"+50%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"AVATAR","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Patch 1.0","alias":"Avatar","theme":"It Stares Back","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerAvatar","release":"20 October 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Flames of Misspell"}
Avatar Infernas is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.0. His starting weapon is  Flames of Misspell.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "It is tangible only in the inverted Inlaid Library. Good friends might then show the way."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After clearing Eudaimonia Machine by defeating  The Directer and obtaining the  Greatest Jubilee, the player must start a run in Inlaid Library with inverse mode enabled.
- The player must then travel far to the right of the stage, past the  Gold Ring, until they encounter  The Trickster in front of a piano. If the player moves out of view of The Trickster, it will simply teleport to the other side of the screen.
- After killing The Trickster, interact with the piano by moving in front of its keys; doing so will pause the game and open an interactive pop-up menu with clickable piano keys. If the player has  Peachone and  Ebony Wings, the birds will fly towards the keys that must be pressed and continuously cycle through each note. Keep in mind they are not necessary to have if the player already knows which keys to press.
- It is also important to remember that the  Vandalier and the hidden Peachone and Ebony Wings that  Cosmo Pavone starts with cannot be used. To avoid evolving the bird weapons into the Vandalier, it is recommended to  Banish one of them to prevent it from reaching level 8, or the Vandalier can be Sealed before starting the run.
- The keys the player must press are: D, A, A#, G, and C# (2nd white key, 6th white key, 5th black key, 5th white key, and the 1st black key).
- After playing the final note, the player will then be teleported to a dark area that infinitely spawns  Undead Heads and  Undead Eyes. This area contains nine coffins in a square grid around the player, which can be seen on the map.
- One of these coffins contains  Avatar Infernas (enemy), while the rest are empty, and is randomized in each run. After opening the coffin containing Avatar Infernas, the darkness will fade, all of the remaining enemies disappear, and the now hostile Avatar Infernas will spawn and slowly chase the player. Defeating the hostile Avatar Infernas unlocks him as a playable character and causes the  White Hand to appear and end the run.
Avatar Infernas does not normally need to be purchased and is immediately available upon completion of the secret.
Alternatively, Avatar Infernas can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "kalvasflam". This will require the player to purchase Avatar Infernas for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Avatar starts with +60 Max Health, +50%  Might, +50%  Luck, +100%  Magnet, +1  Revival, and +10  Reroll. He gains +2%  Move Speed, +0.5% Might, -0.25%  Cooldown, and +0.5%  Curse every level. There is no cap on these bonuses.
He starts with an extra Arcana  Heart of Fire (XIX).
## Tips
- Since Avatar gains +2% MoveSpeed with each level, moving is more difficult at higher levels because of the absurdly high speed. This is mentioned in his description, by implying that he loses control with every level.
- This drawback can be avoided with  Edge of the Earth (VIII), setting your Move Speed to +100%.
- Avatar Infernas is one of the few characters in the game whose cooldown scales infinitely (alongside  Smith). This makes Avatar a fantastic user of powerful weapons with long cooldowns like  Pentagram/ Gorgeous Moon.
- Picking  Clock Lancet will make Avatar Infernas virtually untouchable at a certain point, as the speed of the Lancet firing will keep enemies permanently frozen. This also makes Avatar a decent user of  Out of Bounds (XII).
- Picking  Laurel will make Avatar Infernas completely invincible starting at -87.5% Cooldown, as charges will regenerate faster than damage depletes them.
- Avatar's scaling movement speed makes him a fantastic user of  Shadow Pinion,  Valkyrie Turner, and  Darkness Illusion [DLC]. In addition, Valkyrie Turner and Darkness Illusion benefit from its innate Heart of Fire.
## Trivia
- His silhouette is similar to The Directer.
- The name "Avatar" is most likely a reference to the role-playing game series Ultima.
- The spell that can be used to unlock Avatar are the words of power spoken when casting the spell "Flamestrike" in Ultima Online.
- He is the only secret character with a theme.
- The game developer Luca G named himself Virgil Infernas in the official Discord server.
- The solution to the piano puzzle to unlock Avatar Infernas is the same as the solution to the piano puzzle in the 1999 survival horror game Silent Hill.
- If the player has high enough speed or low enough framerate, it is possible for this character to clip through walls. This is because when the player moves the game does not check between the player's location and the target destination, instead simply moving the player, checking if the player is in a wall, and if so pushing them back. This phenomenon is most observable in Avatar Infernas due to his infinite potential speed.
- His base cost is "666", which is a reference to the number of the beast.
- Avatar Infernas's likeness was most likely inspired by Virgil from the 1997 role-playing game SaGa Frontier.

The Axe Armor is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Axe.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Prove to them that there is something that spins better than axes."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must defeat 100  Axe Armor enemies in the Ode to Castlevania Stage with the  Stellar Blade, the evolution of the  Discus in the Cathedral area of the map. The Cathedral (the top part of the far east building) contains a room that only spawns Axe Armors. This spacious prayer room is located in the bottom-left area of the Cathedral with a pseudo-high / standing cross in the middle with a caped, praying statue at the base. There is also a  Mirror of Truth pickup. Doing this unlocks the Axe Armor as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Axe Armor can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Axe Armor can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "missaxalotl".
## Passive bonuses
The Axe Armor starts with +10  Max Health, +10%  Might, +10%  Move Speed, and +10%  Curse. They gain another 5% Might, 5% Move Speed and 5% Curse every 10 levels, for a max increase of +20% or +30% in total. 
As the description implies, they gain an additional hidden Axe every 10 levels, up to a max of 6.
The Axe Armor can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Baal'Thamut is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Damnation.
## Unlocking
Baal'Thamut can be unlocked by holding the  Velvet Dodecahedron and obtaining  Damnation with  Baal'Thasar. They must then be purchased for 100,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Baal'Thamut can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "eatingtheground".
## Passive bonuses
Baal'Thamut starts with +0.30  Recovery, +100%  Might, +100%  Duration, +50%  Greed, and +10  Banish.
- Upon starting a run, Baal'Thamut's  Max Health will be set to -1.
Baal'Thamut retains all of  Baal'Thasar's passive ability, such as being adept with  Hell damage weapons.
Baal'Thamut possesses flight and is able to ignore walls.
Baal'Thamut will replace the background of the stage with a coiled snake backdrop, with their body also surrounding the borders of the gameplay area.
Baal'Thamut cannot receive damage but their run will automatically end once the normal stage time limit has been reached.
 Damnation's central tear is made permanently active. 
Every 100 enemies Baal'Thamut defeats during a run will increase their Might by +1%.
## Tips
- Because the kill threshold for increasing Baal'thamut's Might is so low, it can easily shatter the cap of 900% past which Might stops having any effect on normal weapons, especially with high  Charm and  Curse and  Wicked Season (XIII). To fully utilize Baal'thamut's staggering Might, consider picking up some weapons that are multiplied by Might without a cap, even if they are not Hell nor Blood weapons. As of currently, the only such weapons are  SpellStrike [DLC] and  FireFall, as well as the respective Unions that incorporate them,  SpellStrom [DLC] and  FireStall.
## Trivia
- The name "Baal'Thamut" appears to be an amalgamation-word of "Baal" and "Bahamut".
- "Baal"-word alone in this context could tell a progression story of Baal'Thasar rising from a humble (Hebrew / Phoenician) "lord / master" into an omnipotent (Baalim) "great god" alongside being (Christian) fallen angel / demon / saint.
- While generally the mythological "Bahamut" is depicted as a gargantuan fish (or a whale), in some depictions the roles of Bahamut and Leviathan have been switched around (and vice versa), making Bahamut having more of a serpentine appearance.
- The spell "eatingtheground" is likely a reference to the song "Serpent Eating The Ground" (technically a mistranslation of "Serpent Eating the Horizon"), the final boss theme of the 2012 role-playing game Bravely Default. The character's description in the menu references both variations on the song name.
- Baal'Thamut, alongside  Megalo Sargon [DLC] and  Chaos [DLC], will immediately be defeated upon entering Eudaimonia Machine by  The Directer.
=====

Baal'Thasar is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Blacken Firmament.
## Unlocking
Baal'Thasar can be unlocked by defeating Baal'Thasar in Red Moon Manor.
Alternatively, Baal'Thasar can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "whythelongrobe".
## Passive bonuses
Baal'Thasar starts with +66  Max Health, +0.30  Recovery, +30%  Might, +30%  Duration, +50%  Greed, and +10  Banish.
Baal'Thasar is adept with  Hell damage weapons.
When defeating an enemy with  Blood damage, the Experience Gem dropped by the enemy will be converted into a Blood Gem. Blood Gems grant the same experience as the gem would have, but heal Baal'Thasar, contribute to the Might and  Cooldown bonuses granted by his Last Breath ability, and increase the duration of his Last Breath.
As a Last Breath skill, Baal'Thasar will summon a demon skull above his head, granting him invulnerability and a minimum of +10% Might and -10% Cooldown for 8 seconds. The amount of Might and Cooldown granted, as well as the duration of this ability, depend on the number of Blood Gems collected:
- First, the Might bonus is increased by +1%
- Second, the Cooldown bonus is increased by -0.5%
- Third, the duration of Baal'Thasar's Last Breath will increase by 0.2 seconds.
These bonuses continue cyclically. There is no hard limit on the Might and Cooldown bonuses and to the duration of this ability.
Every +1% increase to  Curse will increase Baal'Thasar's Might by +1%.
When Baal'Thasar evolves Blacken Firmament into  Damnation, and posseses the  Velvet Dodecahedron, he will morph into  Baal'Thamut.
## Tips
-  Wicked Season (XIII) is a good way of scaling Baal'Thazar's Might throughout the game in addition to its other benefits, though it may make the early game difficult as Blacken Firmament is poorly suited for dealing with dense and fast hordes.
- Blood Astronomia (XXI) expands the list of weapons that can do blood damage, as it gains its own weapon effect for Garlic, Pentagram, Song of Mana, and Sonic Dash, as well as their evolutions, as well as Laurel and Clock Lancet. All of these effects will independently do blood damage while none of the linked weapons do on their own.
- Despite its components being labelled as both blood and hell damage, the only special effect Baal'Thasar gets from  Power of Sire [DLC] is being adept with the attack originally from  Dominus Hatred [DLC].
- To maximize the use of Baal'Thasar's passive, it may be preferable to avoid some Unions that may otherwise have been desirable. Notably,  Hellfire and  Bloody Tear count as a Hell and Blood weapon, respectively, whereas  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC] and  Fuwalafuwaloo do not.
- Baal'Thasar is perhaps the best user of  Dust to Dust, which counts as a Hell weapon. Especially when combined with other sources of Cooldown reduction, including  Empty Tome and Baal'Thasar's own Last Breath, this greatly reduces the normally long cooldown of the weapon and can wipe the screen clean of enemies with regularity.
- With Baal'Thasar's high starting Health and Greed, the combination of  Mazo Familiar and  Muramasa [DLC] may be desirable, as both are Blood weapons that benefit from these respective stats and the life loss from the latter triggers the former.
- If planning to fight The Reaper, banish Velvet Dodecahedron as Baal'Thamut automatically dies once the stage timer limit is reached.

## Trivia

Weapons fire at a fixed interval when moving.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Summon Night.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+10","name":"Babi-Onna","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"lm","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"ONNA","flight":"No","update":"DLC & Patch 1.2.0","armor":"-","theme":"Crystal Allure","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerOnna","release":"15 December 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Mirage Robe"}
Babi-Onna is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Mirage Robe.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by evolving the  Summon Night weapon. She needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Babi-Onna can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "vogue".
## Passive bonuses
Babi-Onna starts with +10  Reroll. She ignores the  Cooldown stat, but poses while moving, using one weapon each time she poses. The faster she moves, the lower the Cooldown will be for every weapon the player has.
When overhealing by +16, Babi-Onna will gain +30%  Move Speed for 10 seconds. There is no limit to this bonus.
## Tips
- Due to Babi-Onna's unique firing patterns, most weapons do not synergize well with her. In order to be effective, she requires powerful weapons with long cooldowns and big effects.  Santa Water/ La Borra,  Four Seasons/ Godai Shuffle,  Summon Night/ Echo Night,  Shadow Servant/ Ophion,  Science Rocks/ Rocket Science are strong picks.
- Babi-Onna can fire the  Pentagram/ Gorgeous Moon,  Troll Bomb, and  SpellStrom's singularity faster than any character in the game. If using Pentagram, it is best to wait for enemies to move close to your Babi-Onna before moving, so you can quickly pick up experience gems before they are erased. You will want to pick  Clover before Pentagram for  Luck and evolve Pentagram into Gorgeous Moon as soon as possible.
- The unique nature of her cooldown makes  Wings a must-pick item, since higher speed allows her to fire her weapons faster. Conversely, the normally prized  Empty Tome is completely worthless to Babi-Onna, since she completely ignores Cooldown.
- Due to Babi-Onna's only using a single weapon at a time, she can greatly benefit from single weapon runs or picking only a few strong weapons rather than collecting all six weapons.
-  Paranormal Scan is an extremely strong weapon with Babi-Onna, as she can use it very quickly to get high amounts of stat boosts. It is best to pick  Lifesign Scan and evolve it as early as possible to maximize this benefit.
-  Sword Brothers can be used to become permanently invincible very early into the game.  Soul Steal can also be effective, although it takes more time to kill bosses than  Troll Bomb.
- On the other end of the spectrum, Babi-Onna and  Alucard Shield have possibly one of the worst synergies in the game. This is due to the fact that  Alucard Swords and the rest of the absorbed weapons do so by becoming Hidden weapons, which cannot by activated by Babi-Onna's movement. While the shield itself does high damage and will still be triggered, the six (or more) evolved weapons that were absorbed will effectively be lost for the rest of the run.

## Trivia
- Babi-Onna resembles the Italian word "Babbiona", an insulting word commonly used for old people.
- In addition to this, Babi-Onna may be a corruption of "onibaba" (鬼婆, demon hag), a type of oni (鬼, demon in Japanese folklore). Onna (女) also means "woman" in Japanese.
- Babi-Onna's spell is a reference to American pop star Madonna's 1990 hit song "Vogue", itself inspired in turn by "voguing", a style of dancing in which the dancers pose themselves as if for a photograph in a fashion magazine. This reference is reinforced by Babi-Onna's walking animation cycle, which has cycling through seven poses as she walks.
- One of her poses is similar to one of the signature poses of Josuke Higashikata from the anime and manga series JoJo's Bizarre Adventure.

Barlowe is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. He can pick either  Dominus Anger or  Dominus Hatred as his starting weapon. 
Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook and  Ebony Diabologue, Barlowe is able to start with either weapon the player has unlocked as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
Defeating 8,000 enemies with Barlowe unlocks  Dominus Agony.
## Unlocking
Barlowe can be unlocked by evolving  Optical Shot. Once unlocked, Barlowe can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Barlowe can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "pitiablemadman".
## Passive bonuses
Barlowe starts with +200  Max Health, +1  Armor, +20%  Duration, +20%  Area, and +10  Banishes.
Barlowe gains  Might and  Curse based on how much he overheals based on log(OverhealingTotal). However, Barlowe does not accumulate overhealing at a 1:1 ratio to normal healing, instead multiplying the amount he overhealed in that instance by 0.001 before adding it to the total.
Up until level 11, Barlowe will receive Ebony Diabologue as a guaranteed 4th option when he levels up. Ebony Diabologue can still appear if it has been  Sealed.
Barlowe can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- His unlocking spell is a reference to his enemy description from Order of Ecclesia: "A pitiable madman, his body is now Dracula's vessel."
- Despite Globus being one of his attacks in Order of Ecclesia, his starting weapons are the Dominus Glyphs that he created.
## External links
- Barlowe in the Castlevania Wiki

Bat Robbert is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.9.0. His starting weapon is the  Pako Battiliar.
## Unlocking
Upon evolving the  Pako Battiliar, Bat Robbert is unlocked as a playable character. 
Once unlocked, Bat Robbert can be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Bat Robbert can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bimbumbam".
## Passive bonuses
Robbert starts with +0.5  Recovery, +1  Armor, and +20%  Move Speed. 
Robbert has an HP-Critical skill that activates as soon as his HP drops below 20% of his  Max Health, which gives a bonus of +32 Max Health (caps at +665) for the current run and recovers 30% of his HP once triggered. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all of his HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a Game Over will not trigger the skill.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
XXI - Wandering the Jet Black: Reach 665 Max Health with Bat Robbert.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Wandering the Jet Black (XXI).
## Tips
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) is an optimal choice for an Arcana on Bat Robbert, as its effect of increasing the flat damage of all weapons by +0.1 per missing HP complements his skill that increases his Max Health whenever his health reaches a low point. Furthermore, his starting weapon, Pako Battiliar deals retaliatory damage, and its evolution, the  Mazo Familiar further increases its damage based on the character's Max Health, which works well with Divine Bloodline. In addition, the  Hollow Heart, which is required to evolve the weapon, has obvious synergy with the aforementioned Arcana.
## Trivia
- His name is a reference to Bat-Roberto, a parody of Batman created for the 1990's Italian children's television series Bim Bum Bam, which is also referenced by the spell used to unlock Robbert.
- Bat Robbert's name and starting weapon may also be a reference to the English actor Robert Pattinson, who has played both Batman in the 2022 film The Batman and vampire Edward Cullen in the Twilight Saga film series.
- The sprite used by Bat Robbert upon his addition to the game on beta version 1.9.100 is nearly identical to the new sprite for the enemy Nesufritto, save for some minor differences. A dedicated sprite for Robbert was later added with the release of version 1.9.0.

Receives a follower after reviving.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"It's as simple as ABC: obtain the ring of rings.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Bats Bats Bats","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"180","revival":"+2","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"BATSBATSBATS","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"Bats","movespeed":"+80%","controller":"CharacterControllerBatsBatsBats","release":"15 August 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Mazo Familiar"}
Bats Bats Bats is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.11. Its starting weapon is  Mazo Familiar.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "It's as simple as ABC: obtain the ring of rings."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Bat Country after collecting both the  Apoplexy and  Chaos Malachite from the stage in previous runs. Afterward, the player must travel to the ring of nine  Gold Rings located to the northeast of their spawning location, which can be tracked by arrows on the screen. Upon collecting all nine Gold Rings, Bats Bats Bats is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Bats Bats Bats can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Bats Bats Bats can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "whydithavetobebats".
## Passive bonuses
Bats Bats Bats starts with +80  Max Health, +80%  Move Speed, and +2  Revival.
Bats Bats Bats's starting weapon,  Mazo Familiar, which begins at 10 base damage (instead of the normal 40), gains +1 base damage every level. There is no cap to this bonus.
Revival increases  Amount by 1. If the Revival is consumed, this bonus is lost. Because Bats starts with +2 Revivals, it will always have at least +2 Amount right out of the gate.
When the character dies and revives, another Bats Bats Bats appears on the screen. This follower moves about randomly but will not leave the screen. Followers have  Pako Battiliar which functions as normal, spawning bats when they are hit. They can also collect  Experience Gems which are given to the player. Every time the player levels up, all followers gain random stat bonuses. If a follower dies it retreats into a coffin to regenerate health, once it is full, the coffin opens and the follower comes out again.
## Tips
- Because Bats Bats Bats gets scaling Amount based on Revival and gains an extra follower for each time it revives, it is almost mandatory to grab  Awake (IV) and  Tirajisú to acquire the most revivals possible.  Paranormal Scan [DLC] can also give extra revivals over time. With the Revival and Amount upgrades purchased from the shop and Awake as a starting Arcana, Bats can start with an awe-inspiring +7 Amount right out of the gate.
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) can be built around to maximize Mazo Familiar damage.  Hollow Heart is also a decent pickup for the same reason.
- The character's massive speed makes it very easy to explore most stages, although  Mask of the Red Death remains faster.
- The followers are considered like teammates in Co-op mode, they can benefit from the healing of  Lifesign Scan [DLC]/ Paranormal Scan [DLC] and their deaths can trigger the "Dead Body Reported" screen nuke of  Report! [DLC]/ Emergency Meeting [DLC] by Bats Bats Bats.
-  Phieraggi scales well with Bats Bats Bats, as it gains amount from both the revivals and the extra amount bonuses.
-  Flash Arrow [DLC] pairs well with Bats Bats Bats, due to being able to get massive damage boosts from the high amount it can have.
- If possible, Bats Bats Bats should try to pick up an Operation Guns weapon to be able to access  Weapon Power-Up [DLC], thus gaining more revival and amount.
- Bats Bats Bats is a great choice for Lake Foscari and Abyss Foscari, as it can use the  Academy Badge [DLC] to gain even more revivals and amount.

Bianca Ramba is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.4.2. Her starting weapon is the  Carréllo.
After obtaining the  Chaos Lazulia, Ramba can evolve the  Carréllo into  Carrozza! upon reaching level 80.
## Unlocking
Bianca Ramba is unlocked by killing a total of 3,000  Milk Elementals.
Once unlocked, Ramba can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased. 
Alternatively, Ramba can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "carramba".
Ramba's Halloween skin is unlocked by entering the Spell "spoopyseason". This will make the player enter The Bone Zone with either Bianca Ramba,  Yatta Cavallo,  Mortaccio, or  O'Sole Meeo in their Halloween costume. It is not required to reach 30:00 in the Bone Zone to have the skin unlocked, so the player can exit the stage at any time they like in order to do so.
## Passive bonuses
Ramba gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 60. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
The following table shows how much Amount she gains by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60+ | +3

## Trivia
- "Bianca" is an Italian name, but read together with her family name it evokes the Spanish expression "caramba", used to express surprise.
- The spell to unlock her "carramba" is caramba with an extra "r", referencing her weapon being a "car"t.
- Ramba,  Mortaccio,  Cavallo, and  O'Sole all have identical passives and stats and only differ in starting weapons.
- Ramba, Mortaccio, Cavallo, O'Sole, and  Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the base game at level 60 with 2 levels of Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +6 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe gains +10 Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul,  Horse, and  Simondo Belmont can get one more persistent Amount by reaching level 80.
- Prior to v1.3.0, Ramba's Halloween skin would be persistent after unlocking and revert once exiting the game.
- According to Luca Galante, the character "is the ghost of an old lady that used to steal supermarket carts, and is now throwing them around".
- The "To End an Ice Age" adventure informs us that Bianca Ramba and  Mortaccio were in a relationship before their death, with the descriptions of the Ice Cream and A Fiery Apparition stages making reference to this.
- Bianca's sprite looks similar to one of the variants of the  Dead Molisano, except that the Dead Molisano does not have a halo.
=====

Has a 4X chance of drafting FOIL Survarots.","cost":"50000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Unearthly Bolt","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Big Troubler","movespeed":"+20%","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+30%","id":"EX_BIGTROUBLER","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Legacy of the Bloodmoon is coming this summer!","theme":"","skin id":"DEFAULT","alias":"Troubler","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_EX_BigTroubler","release":"6 June 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Unearthly Bolt"}
Big Troubler is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.15. His starting weapon is  Unearthly Bolt.
## Unlocking
Big Troubler can be unlocked by evolving the  Unearthly Bolt.
Once unlocked, Big Troubler can be purchased for 50,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Big Troubler can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "wifeseeker".
## Passive bonuses
Big Troubler starts with +20%  Move Speed and +30%  Greed.
Whenever Big Troubler picks up a gold pick-up from the ground, his starting weapon is triggered, regardless of its current cooldown. This effect can trigger multiple time depending on the amount of gold received from the pick-up, with 1 gold equaling 1 activation and each activation having a delay of 1 frame.
When Big Troubler collects gold during a run, he uses it as a shield/secondary health bar. Any damage taken will instead be subtracted from the player's current gold count for that run. Once the player's gold count is reduced to 0, Big Troubler will begin receiving damage to his health bar again. Big Troubler has reduced Invulnerability Time when he takes damage while carrying gold. 
Big Troubler has a 4 times higher chance of drafting FOIL  Survarots, and a 2 times chance of drafting a Survarot with an Edition in general.
Big Troubler destroys light sources automatically when coming into contact with them.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
0 - Stake to Your Heart: Defeat a Bat Dragon with Big Troubler while using the Game Killer Arcana.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Stake to Your Heart (0).
## Tips
- Due to the nature of Big Troubler's passive,  The Reaper can quickly negate any gold gained during a run. If the player wishes to retain their gold, they should play with Endless Mode turned on or quit as soon as the The Reaper appears. Doing so will still count towards unlocks and stage completion.
- The FOIL Survarot skills that grant Revivals based on enemies killed or gold collected are key pieces for Big Troubler, as they allow him to massively increase the damage of  Spirit Disturbance, possibly into the dozens or hundreds of thousands of damage per hit.
- Big Troubler's starting weapon is rather weak. Because of this, you should try to get as much value out of the first part of his passive as you can to ensure your survival early on.
-  Stake to Your Heart (0) can be a good Arcana for Troubler, as it massively helps his terrible early game by providing far more gold coins than usual to trigger his weapon, although the other effect of the Darkana is wasted on him. If building around Stake to Your Heart,  Vicious Hunger is a must-pick as it helps trigger his starting weapon. If going with this method, consider using  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) as a way to increase Troubler's gold gain (for the purpose of the FOIL Survarot that grants Revivals based on gold collected) without having to level up.
- Disco of Gold (XV) can be a good pick as it supports his already existent playstyle of farming light sources for the purpose of proccing his starting weapon, and it lets him keep his gold count / secondary health bar high. (note: the gold gained from killing enemies during Gold Fever does not trigger the first part of his passive.)
- If not building Stake to Your Heart, one can instead use  Wicked Season (XIII) to boost Curse and Greed to increase the potency of the abovementioned FOIL Survarots.
## Trivia
- Big Troubler is a reference to David Lo Pan, the main antagonist of the 1986 film Big Trouble in Little China.
- The spell for unlocking Big Troubler is also a reference to the film, as David Lo Pan seeks to marry a woman with green eyes and sacrifice her to the god Ching Dai in order to gain immortality.
=====
# Big Troubler (Merchant)
For the playable character, see Big Troubler.
Big Troubler is a merchant found in Vampire Survivors. He can be found at the top of The Lycaeum.
Initially, Big Troubler will offer the  Green Eyes Vizard and the  Survarocchi for 60,000 and 120,000, respectively.
After purchasing the Green Eyes Vizard, and defeating any 2 unique  Bat Dragons, Big Troubler will sell the  Enemahs for 180,000.
After purchasing any of the above relics, they cannot be purchased again.

Gold Fever lasts longer.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Master all 16 accessories in Moongolow.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Big Trouser","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+20%","id":"PANTALONE","flight":"No","update":"Patch 0.10.0","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"Trouser","movespeed":"+30%","controller":"CharacterControllerPantalone","release":"4 August 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Candybox"}
Big Trouser is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.10.0. His starting weapon is the  Candybox.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Run away from Moongolow with all 16 accessories mastered."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Moongolow, collect all 16 standard stage items, and level them all up to their maximum level. After the run is left by quitting it manually or by being teleported to and completing Holy Forbidden, Big Trouser is unlocked as a playable character.
Because  Tirajisú is lost once both of the revives have been used, ending the run through death will result in not having a Tirajisú at the end of the run if only 1 revival was left, and Big Trouser will not be unlocked.
 Gains Boros is recommended for the exponential leveling. In addition, it is recommended to set the weapon limit to 1 in order to avoid diluting the item pool with weapon pickups, since Gains Boros's Heaven Sword is ample enough to handle the enemies. Characters that cannot die except by reaching the time limit are also suitable choices, such as  Megalo Menya Moonspell and  Ghost Lino.
Once unlocked, Big Trouser can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Big Trouser can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "earrivatolarrotino".
## Passive bonuses
Trouser starts with +30%  Move Speed and +20%  Greed. He gains +1% Greed every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
Trouser extends the duration of  Gold Fevers by multiplying its duration cap and duration extension from gold pickups by 1.5.
## Tips
- With Candybox, Trouser can start with any base weapon (or no weapon at all).
- With his scaling  Greed and longer  Gilded Clover, he is one of the best suited for gold runs.
- Trouser is an optimal character to gold farm with for players without access to  Sammy [DLC] from the Tides of the Foscari DLC, or for players who have low-end hardware that may lag when using Sammy. If Sammy is unlocked, it should be used instead, as it can greatly outperform Trouser in gold farming.
- For gold farm runs, it is recommended to have at least 5,000 Golden Eggs on Trouser. Queen Sigma can be used to effectively farm enough gold to buy these first 5,000 Golden Eggs on Trouser.
- Between 5,000 and 150,000  Golden Eggs,  Greatest Jubilee should be used along with  Sarabande of Healing (VI) (or  Blood Astronomia (XXI)) and Disco of Gold (XV) with the Inverse, Hyper, and Endless stage modifiers. Limit Break should be disabled, and Gold Bags should be selected upon level up. Picking up a weapon like  Crimson Shroud can help kill enemies, which may be needed.
- 150,000-1,000,000,000 Golden Eggs on Trouser is much the same as above, although an additional weapon besides Greatest Jubilee shouldn't be needed.
- Above 1 billion Golden Eggs, Greatest Jubilee can be swapped with  Garlic/ Laurel/ Pentagram, and Disco of Gold can be swapped with  Wicked Season (XIII). At this point, Gold Fevers will not be worth pursuing, since the level-up gold and the Greed from  Wicked Season (XIII) outperform it.
- Recommended settings are Hyper Mode on, Arcanas on, Limit Break off, Inverse on, and Endless on. It is extremely important that Limit Break is off and Big Gold Bags are selected as level-up choices. Limit Break should not be used for Trouser gold farm runs.
- Once past 1,000,000 Golden Eggs, most weapons stop killing enemies due to  Curse enemy scaling. Damage from Sarabande of Healing or Blood Astronomia is required after this point.
- Outside the context of gold farming runs, Trouser's scaling Greed makes him one of the best users of  Muramasa.
## Trivia
- Big Trouser's design and code name are a reference to the character Pantalone found in Commedia dell'arte (or Italian comedy). With his exceptional greed and status at the top of the social order, Pantalone is "money" in the commedia world. This also explain his growing Greed each level.
- The spell "earrivatolarrotino" is a reference to the figure of the "arrotino", a travelling salesman who sharpens tools and often fixes other items as well. It's a popular figure in many Italian cities due to its notorious slogan, which starts with the words "E' arrivato l'arrotino" ("The grinder has arrived"); similar figures and slogans can be found abroad too (e.g. in Madrid).

Can find special pickups.","cost":"100","duration":"-","unlock":"Find and open the coffin in Neo Galuga","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"+20%","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Bill Rizer","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"150","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"+4","banish":"+4","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_BILLRIZER","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","alias":"Bill","theme":"Bloody Falls - VS version","movespeed":"-","controller":"FB_Bill","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Long Gun"}
Bill Rizer is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Long Gun.
## Unlocking
Bill Rizer can be unlocked for purchase by finding and opening his coffin in Neo Galuga. He needs to be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Bill Rizer can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "runandrizer".
## Passive bonuses
Bill starts with +50  Max Health, +20%  Speed, +4  Skip, and +4  Banish. 
Bill gains 2.5% Projectile Speed (up to 50%) and 1%  Might (with no cap) at specific kill thresholds. Bill will not gain Speed if Speed has reached its cap of +400%. Kill thresholds start at 100 total enemies killed, and increase by 1.3x whenever they are reached, meaning Bill will get stat bonuses at 100, 130, 169, 220, etc. total kills. 
- Since the kill threshold scales exponentially, it will drastically slow down. In a typical run, the player will not get much more than +30% to +33% Might. By +35%, the kill threshold is just under 1 million.
Bill can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.

# Bisconte Draculó
Bisconte Draculó in the main menu.
Bisconte Draculó is a character mentioned in the description of the itch.io demo of Vampire Survivors, implied to be the main antagonist of the game. It is generally assumed that the vampire-like character shown in the main menu as well as in various marketing materials is indeed Bisconte Draculó, though this is unconfirmed. Whether or not Draculó is still canon to the game's current lore is also unconfirmed.
Outside of the main menu cover art and external box art for the game, Bisconte Draculó does not exist in the game, neither as a playable character nor an NPC.
## Cover art
 - 
 [Bisconte Draculó as seen in the mobile app icon of the game.]
 Bisconte Draculó as seen in the mobile app icon of the game.
 - 
 [Bisconte Draculó in a vertical cover art of Vampire Survivors.]
 Bisconte Draculó in a vertical cover art of Vampire Survivors.
 - 
 [Bisconte Draculó in a horizontal cover art of Vampire Survivors.]
 Bisconte Draculó in a horizontal cover art of Vampire Survivors.
 - 
 [Bisconte Draculó as depicted in the main menu cover art (left).]
 Bisconte Draculó as depicted in the main menu cover art (left).
 - 
 [Bisconte Draculó as depicted in a horizontal cover art of Vampire Crawlers.]
 Bisconte Draculó as depicted in a horizontal cover art of Vampire Crawlers.
## Steam profile backgrounds
These profile backgrounds, which feature Bisconte Draculó, are from the Steam Points Shop of Vampire Survivors.
 - 
 - 
## Other
 - 
 [A puppet version of Bisconte Draculó, used to portray Luca Galante in the Noclip documentary "The Making of Vampire Survivors."]
 A puppet version of Bisconte Draculó, used to portray Luca Galante in the Noclip documentary "The Making of Vampire Survivors."
## Trivia
- He is based on Count Dracula from the Castlevania series.
- "Bisconte" is a play on Italian words "biscotto" (biscuit/cookie) and "visconte" (viscount).
- The prologue text from the description of the itch.io demo saying Bisconte Draculó's "many evil magics[sic] created a bad world filled with famine and suffering" is a reference to the infamous English opening text crawl of Castlevania III: Dracula's Curse.
- There is a recurring gag in Vampire Survivors where the survivors are trying to find the vampire. One way in doing so is by opening coffins. The character in the coffin would say "Thank you (character name), but the vampire is in another coffin." This is an allusion to the game Super Mario Bros., in which the Toads that appear in every castle level prior to the final eighth will state "Thank you Mario, but your princess is in another castle." after being saved by Mario.
- In the video "The Making of Vampire Survivors - Documentary" by Noclip, Luca Galante, the original developer of the game, responded to various questions during the interview. A custom puppet version of Bisconte Draculó was used to portray Luca.
- This custom puppet was made by My Puppet Pal.
- Bisconte Draculó has appeared in the outro of many trailers.
- In the mobile release trailer of Vampire Survivors in particular, Bisconte Draculó winked.
- The trivia section of the Bisconte Draculó page on the Vampire Survivors Wiki has a cipher that was used as part of the Gallo Tower ARG.
- The cipher is WDCNRIWVFWBLKXOIXRPNBAXWWLMUGQUUWXYCGOAPNCCWOS.
- A staff member from poncle made a couple of intentionally low-quality and comedic drawings of Bisconte Draculó kissing characters from other indie game IPs. Those drawing are based on this drawing by @PossiblyDog609 on X, and they were done using Microsoft Paint.
- The first instance of this would be Bisconte Draculó kissing  Jimbo from Balatro. This was later turned into the cover for the Friends Survivors album.
- The second instance of this would be Bisconte Draculó kissing Ironclad from Slay the Spire. The drawing was made to celebrate the release of Slay the Spire 2.
## In other media
- Bisconte Draculó is one of the three characters to appear in the Friends of Jimbo update of the poker roguelike game Balatro. This update allows customization of face cards, replacing them with characters from other games and IPs, including Vampire Survivors.
- Bisconte Draculó is depicted in the form of the King of Clubs.
- The two other characters who appear in Balatro are  Queen Sigma and  Poe Ratcho, who are depicted as the Queen of Clubs and the Jack of Clubs, respectively.
=====

Blackmore is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Summon Spirit.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Let no Shadow fall on the founder of Ecclesia."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After unlocking the  Scorpion Gate in the Ode to Castlevania stage, Blackmore will appear as a boss enemy from his summoning circle, located in the hidden section of the Library (inside the top part of the middle building). The room where his summoning circle is located in also contains an  Arma Dio. Then, defeat him while playing as  Barlowe.
Once unlocked, Blackmore can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Blackmore can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bizarreshadeadventure".
## Passive bonuses
Blackmore starts with +40%  Move Speed, +40%  Duration, and +100%  Greed. 
He can choose a per-level bonus after defeating a set amount of enemies up to 5 times: 1%  Might,  Area,  Speed,  Duration,  Recovery (0.01),  Growth,  Luck,  Greed,  Curse, or  Move Speed. Bonus selection is available at 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000, and 17000 kills. 
Blackmore starts with an extra  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) Arcana. Blackmore is also able to gain up to five Arcana.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Blackmore's 5 per-level bonuses make him, along with  Scientist, the highest scaling character for nine of the ten stats he can select (the exception being Recovery, where they are outclassed by  Cosmo). This allows him to synergize with a variety of weapons and arcana depending on the focus of their stat bonuses:
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Might, Blackmore can make particularly good use of SpellStrike.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Area, Blackmore can make particularly good use of SpellStream,  Santa Water,  Lightning Ring, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Speed, Blackmore can make particularly good use of SpellString, King Bible, Runetracer, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Duration, Blackmore can make particularly good use of Runetracer, Bone,  La Robba, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Luck, Blackmore may be able to make good use of Gatti Amari, Muramasa,  108 Bocce, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Curse, Blackmore can make particularly good use of  108 Bocce and Twilight Requiem (II).
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Move Speed, Blackmore can make particularly good use of Shadow Pinion, Vento Sacro, Celestial Dusting, and so on.
- Regardless of build, selecting  Growth for some of Blackmore's bonuses will result in enough additional levels that you will gain more of other stats than if you were to exclusively select another bonus. The value proposition of more Growth bonuses depends on how long you continue your run and how dense with enemies it is.
## Trivia
- The spell "bizarreshadeadventure" is a reference to him being inspired by JoJo's Bizarre Adventure.
## External links
- Blackmore on the Castlevania Wiki
=====

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_CORNELL_B Trinum Custodem Ebony Diabologue
Blue Crescent Moon Cornell is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Trinum Custodem, but he also has the option of starting with the  Ebony Diabologue if it has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Slay the castle's denizens under the Blue Crescent Moon to make him a beast."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania while playing as  Cornell, and then equip  Dextro Custos,  Sinestro Custos, and  Centralis Custos and evolve them into  Trinum Custodem to transform him, and then kill 100,000 enemies in a single run in Ode to Castlevania. Doing this unlocks Blue Crescent Moon Cornell as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Blue Crescent Moon Cornell can be purchased for 9,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Blue Crescent Moon Cornell can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "azurecroissant".
## Passive bonuses
Blue Crescent Moon Cornell starts with +100  Max Health, +2  Armor, +40%  Move Speed, +3  Amount, and +10  Reroll.
He also gains +1%  Might every level.
Blue Crescent Moon Cornell is the permanent transformation  Cornell receives upon obtaining  Trinum Custodem.
Blue Crescent Moon Cornell can also find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Bonnie Blair is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Punch.
Fully evolving  Punch into  Gilded Hand unlocks  Kick as an optional starting weapon, as well as her 'solo' skins for both weapons.
## Unlocking
Bonnie Blair can be unlocked by fully evolving the  Splashers. Once unlocked, she must then be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Bonnie Blair can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "goinherownway".
## Passive bonuses
Bonnie starts with +40  Max Health and +20%  Move Speed.
Bonnie gains +0.1%  Might whenever she fires a glimmered tech.
She also teams up with  Formina Franklyn as her secondary character. Formina will follow Bonnie, but will not take damage. If the player chooses a 'solo' skin, Formina will not be summoned.
Bonnie will trigger Showstopper when her health falls below 20%:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect to The World's Heartbeat - VS version
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"","cost":"666","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.6.1","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Boon Marrabbio","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"+10%","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Follow the trail after pillaging Pummarola and Skull O'Maniac from the Mad Forest.","greed":"-80%","movespeed":"+30%","theme":"","alias":"Marrabbio","speed":"-110%","release":"23 May 2022","id":"NEO","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Thousand Edge"}
Boon Marrabbio is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.6.1. His starting weapon is the  Thousand Edge.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Follow the "trail" after pillaging  Pummarola and  Skull O'Maniac from the Mad Forest."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the Yellow Sign, the player must start a run in Mad Forest and remove the  Skull O'Maniac and  Pummarola stage items. This can be accomplished by picking them up, even if they are discarded after, or by obtaining them by leveling up and getting their maximum level. When these conditions are met,  Pies begin to appear and form a line towards a specific part of the map (to the left on the grid of the  Hollow Heart and above  Metaglio Left), where a  shadowy enemy will appear; it will slowly move towards the player even if they do not travel there. Defeating it unlocks Boon Marrabbio as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Boon Marrabbio can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Boon Marrabbio can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "fettinepanate".
## Passive bonuses
Marrabbio starts with +20  Max Health, +30%  Move Speed, +20%  Might, -110%  Speed, -80%  Greed, and +10%  Curse.
## Tips
- Without any other bonuses, he will shoot out projectiles backwards. With just one rank of Projectile Speed making it -100%, the projectiles will be stationary.
- Having -100% or lower Projectile Speed means certain weapon cannot be fired, like  Discus [DLC].
- Boon Marrabbio can place projectiles on stages as traps and wait for enemies to touch them, which works well with projectile weapons that ignore  Duration. On the other hand, projectile weapons that scale with Duration become hard to use.
- Weapons' Pool Limit becomes much more important for Boon Marrabbio.
- Weapons that ignores Speed work as usual for Boon Marrabbio.
## Trivia
- The Pies that lead to the shadowy witch will appear every 2 seconds, and up to 100 can be spawned.
- This may reference the fairy tale "Hansel and Gretel", where the siblings used bread crumbs to try to find their way home, but found the witch's hut made of sweets and pastries.
- The name Boon is a reference to the character Noob Saibot from the Mortal Kombat series, while the name Noob is a reference to Ed Boon, one of the creators of the series.
- The requirement to remove Skull O'Maniac and Pummarola from the field may be a reference to things that get pulled out of characters during Fatalities in the Mortal Kombat games.
- Marrabbio is a palette swap of  Gennaro Belpaese in the same way that Noob was a palette swap of another Mortal Kombat character, Kano, in Mortal Kombat 3, who is known for using throwing knives.
- The design of the character is a reference to Shadow Dio from JoJo's Bizarre Adventure: Heritage for the Future.
- Both Dio and Marrabio appear as blonde long-haired shirtless men with their faces obscured by shadow.
- Shadow Dio is famous for throwing knives and stopping time. He can use both of these abilities simultaneously, setting up tons of knives then resuming the flow of time, causing the target to be hit by a wall of blades. Marrabio, with the right weapon speed values can do the same.
- Shadow Dio is a dark recolor of Dio Brando, similar to how Marrabbio is a recolor of Gennaro Belpaese.
- The name Marrabbio references the Italian localization of the anime Ai Shite Naito, where it is the last name of the main character.
- This is further driven home by the cheat code to unlock Marrabbio ("fettinepanate", literally "fried cutlets"), which is an Italian meme tied to the series.
- Boon Marrabbio is also a reference to Neo, the main protagonist of The Matrix film series, with his ID in code being NEO and his projectiles moving at 10% speed.
- His base cost is "666", which is a reference to the number of the beast.

Can find special Pickups.","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Find and open the coffin in Hectic Highway.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Brad Fang","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"200","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_BRADFANG","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","alias":"Brad","theme":"The Hard Corps - VS version","movespeed":"-","controller":"FB_Bradfang","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Firearm"}
Brad Fang is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Firearm.
## Unlocking
Brad Fang can be unlocked for purchase by opening his coffin in the Hectic Highway, which is unlocked upon evolving the  C-U-Laser. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Brad Fang can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "alwaysarmednevershaved".
## Passive bonuses
Brad starts with +100  Max Health and +20%  Might. 
He gains up to -50%  Cooldown, +10%  Move Speed, and +10%  Speed based on missing percentage of Max Health:
- Cooldown will decrease by -0.5% per 1% missing health.
- Move Speed and Speed will increase by +1% per missing 10% of max health. Note that this bonus only happens in intervals of 10% missing health - 15% missing health and 19% missing health will both provide the same bonus as 10% missing health.
Brad can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Picking up  Hollow Heart is an easy way to receive some of the low health bonuses without having to actually take any damage.
- Thanks to his high starting health and being incentivized to stay at low health percentages, Brad can be a great user of  Divine Bloodline (IX). Not only does it grant a damage bonus, which synergises well with the other bonuses Brad receives at low health, but the extra health means that it's easier to stay at low percentages.
- Weapons that cause health drain such as the  Muramasa [DLC], the Dominus weapons, and  Power of Sire [DLC] are great for Brad.
- Since so much of Brad's game plan involves self damage,  Wandering the Jet Black (XXI) can be a decent pick for him.

[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Drain the crimson "paint" from his most lively works."
