Calogero Bloodmoon is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Ashella.
## Unlocking
Calogero Bloodmoon can be unlocked by evolving the  Scarlet Needle.
Alternatively, Calogero Bloodmoon can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "depravedbeast".
Once unlocked, Calogero Bloodmoon needs to be purchased for 3,000.
## Passive bonuses
Calogero Bloodmoon starts with +50  Max Health, +2  Armor, -20%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, -30%  Speed, -30%  Area, and +50%  Greed.
On Last Breath, Calogero will multiply his Max Health by 1.5x and become invulnerable for 10 seconds.
Upon being damaged, Calogero will gain temporary boosts to Might, Speed,  Area,  Cooldown,  Amount, and weapon activations. The amount of stats that can be gained this way increases as Calogero's current HP decreases.
## Tips
- Calogero enjoys having the  Slash (XVI) Arcana, as his starting weapon thrives on dishing out hideously powerful critical hits when evolved into  Bloodlust.
- As  Bloodlust, the evolution of his starting weapon, has special interaction with  Amount, Calogero greatly benefits from the  Beginning (X) Arcana.
- Calogero can also be built as a  Divine Bloodline (IX) user, as he has special interaction with retaliation which increases in potency based on his missing health.
- Strongly consider using  Muramasa [DLC], which also benefits from Slash arcana, scales with Calogero's bonus Greed, retaliates, and can damage Calogero to trigger his stat boosts regularly.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Using Skip when levelling up has a chance to trigger a Survarot draft.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve Infernolatro.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+7","name":"Canio","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"ac","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"LEM_CANIO","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"Ante Chamber - Balatro Main Theme","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"LEM_CharacterController_004","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"Fibonacci Spritz"}
Canio is a playable character from the Ante Chamber DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Fibonacci Spritz.
## Unlocking
Canio can be unlocked by evolving  Infernolatro into  NaneInferno, with a max level  Outer Saboteur.
Alternatively, Canio can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "butdoctoriamcanio".
## Passive bonuses
Canio starts with -10%  Cooldown, +20%  Luck, and +7  Reroll.
Canio can not find  Survarot from Light Sources and does not start the run with a draft. Instead, Canio has a chance to trigger a Survarot draft by  Skipping a Level Up.
- The chance for Canio to get a Survarot with each Skip starts at 100%, decreasing by 5% for every Survarot obtained until a minimum of 10% is reached.
## Tips
-  Christine Davain's Survarot will provide Canio with a Skip every single Level Up, giving him a free chance to get another Survarot. Multiple copies of this card can lead to the possibility of infinite Survarot being obtained.
- The player can save a stage item for if they decide to obtain more Survarot after being previously unable to Skip, due to their build being fully levelled.
-  Preserve can help Canio get more chances at obtaining extra Survarot.
## Trivia
- The spell "butdoctoriamcanio" is likely a reference to the original opera where Canio appeared, Pagliacci and the 1986 DC comic Watchmen. In which, the character Rorschach says the following when discussing another character, The Comedian: "Heard joke once: Man goes to doctor. Says he's depressed. Says life seems harsh and cruel. Says he feels all alone in a threatening world where what lies ahead is vague and uncertain. Doctor says, 'Treatment is simple. Great clown Pagliacci is in town tonight. Go and see him. That should pick you up.' Man bursts into tears. Says, 'But doctor…I am Pagliacci.' Good joke. Everybody laugh. Roll on snare drum. Curtains."
- Canio's passive ability to gain Survarot cards when skipping level-ups is a reference to the effect of the Canio joker in Balatro, where the player depriving themselves of resources by destroying Face Cards grants multiplicative bonuses to Mult.
- Prior to v1.15, either using this character, entering the Ante Chamber stage, using  Secretino Dagsson or any of the other Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.

Flies.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"May the power of the first Belmont break her mask.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"+1","theme name":"","growth":"-","reroll":"+10","name":"Carmilla","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"200","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","hidden weapon":"Dominus Hatred","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"+10","banish":"+10","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_CARMILLA","flight":"Yes","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","alias":"","theme":"","speed":"-","movespeed":"+80%","controller":"TP_Carmilla_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Carmilla is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. She starts with  Dominus Hatred as a hidden weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "May the power of the first Belmont break her mask."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Black Disk in Ode to Castlevania, Carmilla will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, where her summoning circle is located directly east of the  Capra Gate. Upon evolving the  Hex into the  Nightmare and defeating her with it, Carmilla is unlocked as a playable character.
The  Curse zones summoned by  Nightmare must land the final hit against Carmilla. Luckily, these zones will instantly defeat Carmilla, playing a "pop" noise when it happens. 
Once unlocked, Carmilla can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Carmilla can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "weepingmask".
## Passive bonuses
Carmilla starts with the following bonuses:
- +100  Max Health
- +1  Armor
- +80%  Move Speed
- +20%  Might
- +100%  Greed
- +10  Reroll
- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
Carmilla has a charging ability whose duration is decreased by  Curse, and when activated, triggers  Dominus Hatred,  Dominus Anger, and  Dominus Agony if they are equipped. Carmilla is also immune to health drain and can also fly over walls and obstacles.
Carmilla can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## External links
- Carmilla on the Castlevania Wiki

Carrie Fernandez is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Rock Riot. She is also eligible to pick  Belnades' Spellbook as optional starting weapon via  skin selection provided that the Belnades' Spellbook has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
Carrie can be unlocked by uniting  Dextro Custos,  Sinestro Custos, and  Centralis Custos into  Trinum Custodem. She is then made available for purchase for 1,665, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Carrie Fernandez can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "polygonalbelnades".
## Passive bonuses
Carrie starts with +40%  Move Speed, +1  Amount, +50%  Magnet, and +10  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips. She also starts with an extra  Waltz of Pearls (XI).
Carrie can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

The Cave Troll is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Troll Bomb.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Just go low and defeat 3,000,000 of those jokers."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must defeat 6,000  Cave Trolls in the Ode to Castlevania stage and have obtained the  Pile of Secrets. Note that the kills can be done prior to obtaining this relic, but both conditions must be met for the Cave Troll to be unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Cave Troll can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Cave Troll can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "chupacabra".
## Passive bonuses
Cave Troll starts with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
Notably, the Cave Troll cannot find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Because  Troll Bomb, its starting weapon, has a 2 minute cooldown, you basically need to either start with an Arcana that can do damage or buy a weapon from one of the merchants in order for this character to function, otherwise you would need to wait two minutes before even making your first attack. Unlike  Christine, whose starting  Pentagram has a similar issue, the Cave Troll does not start with an extra level to select a more useful weapon.
## Trivia
- The Spell used to unlock it, "chupacabra", alludes to this enemy's name in the Japanese version of the Castlevania games, "Cave Troll" being the English localization.

Celia Fortner is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapons are  Luminatio and  Umbra.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Perfectly balance the darkest and brightest of Glyphs."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Upon evolving  Luminatio and  Umbra into  Universitas, Celia Fortner is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Celia Fortner can be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Celia Fortner can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "withlight".
## Passive bonuses
While playing as Celia, a  Karma Coin appears above her every second level.
She can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- The spell used to unlock Celia directly references the name of the cult she founded, With Light. Moreover, the method required to unlock her serves as a nod to the cult’s ultimate goal — achieving balance between light and darkness.

| {"magnet":"+200%","description":"Starts with Arcana I, II, III, IV, V, VI, VII, VIII, IX, X, XI, XII, XIII, XIV, XV, XVI, XVII, XVIII, XIX, XX, and XXI.","cost":"1048576","duration":"-","unlock":"Exhaust the rest of this miserable pile of secrets.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-99%","reroll":"+65536","name":"Chaos","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"2097152","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"+65536","banish":"+65536","greed":"-","id":"TP_CHAOS","flight":"Yes","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"-","theme":"Dracula's Castle (Bonus Track) - Vampire Survivors","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"TP_Chaos_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Infinite Corridor"}
Chaos is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapon is the  Infinite Corridor.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Exhaust the rest of this miserable pile of secrets."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Upon unlocking almost all the other secrets from the Ode to Castlevania DLC, Chaos is unlocked as a playable character.
Chaos does not require the Secrets added in v1.14's Ode to Castlevania 2.0 Update, only those originally added as part of this DLC. 
The following characters do not have to be unlocked to unlock Chaos:
-  Hugh Baldwin
-  Morris Baldwin
-  Annette
-  Tera
-  Genya Arikado
-  Stone Skull
-  Ruler Sword
-  Persephone
-  Astarte
-  Drolta Tzuentes
-  Witch Actrise
-  Atlantis Shrine Wizard
-  Fake Trio
-  Slogra and Gaibon
-  Zephyr
-  Jiangshi
Once unlocked, Chaos can be purchased for 1,048,576, which scales with other characters purchased, but will never exceed 9,999,999.
Alternatively, Chaos can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "iiiiiiivvviviiviiiixxxixiixiiixivxvxvixviixviiixixxxxxi". This spell is the roman numerals from 1 to 21 in order without spaces, matching the 21 arcana that Chaos starts with (I, II, III, IV, etc.).
## Passive bonuses
Chaos starts with +2097052  Max Health, -99%  Growth, +200%  Magnet, and +65536  Reroll,  Skip, and  Banish, making it virtually invincible. It also starts with every Arcana except for Game Killer (0) and the Darkanas, and no Arcana chests normally spawn for it.
Chaos ignores collision, allowing it to pass through walls, obstacles, and gaps in the terrain.
Chaos can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
When playing as Chaos, a unique background is displayed on top of the stage's terrain, fully obscuring it and moving along with the player's motion. The obscuring effect does not, however, extend to certain stage elements, such as light sources, barriers, and walls, as well as the artificial walls that conceal hidden areas or passages.
## Tips
- While Chaos has -99% Growth, naturally starting with  Wicked Season (XIII) means that its Growth will scale throughout the game, though  Crown is still a good pickup to speed up the process.
- In addition to having every Arcana in the game, Chaos still gets one starting Arcana pick.  Crystal Cries (XII) is likely the best pick here, as it can be used to push Chaos's already massive health further, augmenting the power of its synergistic weapons, as well as scale its initially poor Growth much faster.  Moonlight Bolero (VI) can also be used to acquire more Darkana, but this is best done on Inverse Mode, where it can be acquired together with Crystal Cries by buying an Arcana from the Merchant. Crystal Cries should have higher priority on a regular run.
- While Chaos starts with every Arcana in the game and as such can make use of virtually any build, its most notable stat is its absurd Max Health. With  Hollow Heart,  Metaglio Left and Crystal Cries, Chaos can increase this already absurd amount even further. This makes  Mazo Familiar perhaps the absolute best weapon Chaos has access to. Unlike the vampire characters, Chaos does suffer health drain from weapons like  Muramasa [DLC] and  Power of Sire [DLC], making them good picks for continuously triggering Mazo Familiar.  Vanitas Whip [DLC] and its evolution  Aurablaster Tip [DLC] also synergize with its massive health.
- Chaos's enormous Max Health also means that if it's increased from its normal amount through items like Hollow Heart and Metaglio Left, a massive gap of several million health will be created that Chaos is unlikely to ever be able to heal off. This is excessively powerful with  Divine Bloodline (IX) that it innately has, adding hundreds of thousands of bonus flat damage to all weapons, making these items must-picks even without selecting Mazo Familiar or Aurablaster Tip.
- Chaos's enormous Magnet bonus makes it a decent idea to pick up some weapons that benefit from  Blood Astronomia (XXI), such as  Garlic.
- Chaos is one of the three characters (along with a level 200  Toastie,  Dracula [DLC], and  Megalo Dracula [DLC]) that can withstand attacks from  The Reaper, as the 65,535 damage dealt by The Reaper is nowhere near enough to easily empty out Chaos's massive health pool of over 2 million. This, combined with Chaos's native Infinite Corridor, means that runs using Chaos are almost guaranteed to trigger the  White Hand ending.
- Because one of the Arcana Chaos starts with is  Mad Groove (VIII), it will immediately teleport all items on the map to it and repeat this every 2 minutes. Some care should be taken to avoid accidentally picking up less crucial items, like the Rings and the Metaglios, until Chaos has already acquired its most essential tools by level so that they don't clog up valuable inventory space.

## Screenshots
 -

## Trivia
- Its base cost of 1,048,576 reflects the number of bytes in a mebibyte (1 MiB, 220). The additional +65,536 for Reroll, Skip, and Banish is another significant computing figure, representing 216, the total number of values that can be represented with 16 bits. Its max health of +2,097,152 also connects to computing, as 2,097,152 bytes is precisely 2 MiB, 221.
- Chaos, as an immensely powerful entity and the ultimate secret character, directly references its role in the Castlevania series as the source of Dracula's power and the overarching antagonist.
- When playing as Chaos, a unique background obscures all terrain except for walls. This background is the same one featured in the battle between Chaos and Soma in Aria of Sorrow, serving as a direct callback to their encounter.
- Despite its massive appearance, Chaos' hitbox only surrounds the core in the middle of its body; therefore, it will only receive damage if enemies touch the core itself.
- Likewise, if Chaos picks up a weapon that summons clones of the user, such as  Mirage Robe or  Legionnaire, the clones will reflect the appearance of the core alone rather than Chaos's full body.
- Its unlock spell contains every Roman numeral from one to twenty one, referencing how it starts with every arcana from one to twenty one.
- It is the only character that has its character selection image completely filled by its body.
- Chaos, alongside  Megalo Sargon [DLC] and  Baal'Thamut [DLC], will immediately be defeated upon entering Eudaimonia Machine by  The Directer.

# Characters
Characters
This article has a calculator for advanced character search here.
Calculators determine dynamic values based on user input and wiki data.
Character selection menu, which is accessed after the player presses the "START" button in the main menu.
Characters are what the player can control to move around in a stage. Each character starts with its own unique weapon(s) and passive bonus.
There are currently 225 official playable characters. The base game has 56 characters, 22 can be obtained by discovering secrets. The DLCs have a combined roster of 169 characters, 56 of them being secret ones. All non-secret characters need to be purchased using gold, increased by 10% additively per characters purchased, with the exception of Antonio, being the first basic character a new player uses; Queen Sigma, who requires an achievement but costs 0 gold to purchase; and Dracula, who is immediately unlocked upon obtaining the Black Disk and does not require a purchase. Some of these characters require an achievement to unlock for purchase. Certain characters have alternate unlocking method unlisted. 
Certain characters are duo, which means a secondary character will follow the main character. Both characters get their leveling up by turns, and shares the same upgrade pool (which means if both of them takes the same weapon or passive item, both of them will not be able to evolve it). Passive items can be shared between both of them for evolution. Stage items will only be added to the main character's inventory. Secondary character will not take damage normally. 
## Interface
Character Selection menu
The Character Selection menu can be accessed upon clicking the "Start" button from the main menu. This menu displays a grid of playable characters and also includes a few panels and several buttons. The left panel lists character stats, the bottom panel includes more information and options for the selected character, and the right panel displays what stages were completed by a specific character.
In a new save file where only Mad Forest is unlocked, pressing the "Confirm" button will immediately lead to that stage. However, once Inlaid Library is unlocked, it will lead to the Stage Selection menu, instead.
The 1.15 update introduced a few major changes in the Character Selection menu, which include character setup, filtering/sorting the character grid, viewing full screen stats, and favoriting characters.
The following sections discuss more details on each part of the Character Selection menu.
## Character grid
The character grid is where the player can select a specific character. Each tile for an owned character will display their name, their sprite, and their starting weapon if any. Selecting a specific character tile will modify the bottom character information panel and display more information about them.
## Character unlocks
Here,  Antonio is owned,  Arca is locked, and  Clerici is unlocked but not bought.
In a new save file, the only character that is available from the start is  Antonio Belpaese. The unlock conditions for each character can be found in the Unlocks menu. If a new player unlocks at least one new character, the character selection menu will start to show silhouettes of other locked characters, instead of just showing the character select icons of  Antonio Belpaese,  Imelda Belpaese,  Pasqualina Belpaese, and  Gennaro Belpaese.
- Characters that are unlocked and bought will have a gray selection icon shown with their character and starting weapon, with their name marked in white.
- Characters that are unlocked but not bought will also have a gray selection icon and their starting weapon shown, but the characters themselves will have a black silhouette and their name is marked in gray.
- Characters that are locked have their selection icon entirely colored blue-gray, with the character silhouette, character name, and starting weapon being translucent.
## Filtering/sorting
Filter button
Players can press the blue "FILTER" button near the top right corner of the screen. They can filter the character grid by version groups (1.0 Collection/Extra) or any available DLC, though the player can only keep at least one group of characters visible to them. They can also sort the character grid either through the default character order, alphabetically, or through the number of complete stages per character, and the sorting can either be in ascending or descending order. In any case, favorited characters will show up first, regardless of how the grid is sorted.
## Character information panel
Character information panel of  Lama Ladonna
The character information panel, displayed just below the character grid, displays more information about a selected character. The displayed information includes the character's name, sprite, starting weapon, and description. Players can also use this panel to favorite characters or change skins if they have the  Mindbender.
## Character stats
Character stats panel with the max weapons counter and the "Character Setup" button
Fullscreen stats dialog
The panel on the left side of the screen displays a selected character's stats, as well as a max weapon counter and a button for setting up a character. Each character has a different set of stats. 
Stats whose values are displayed in white are either default character stats or stats affected by  Golden Eggs, while values displayed in yellow are values added from the PowerUps shop.
Players can alternatively view the stats through a full screen dialog, simply by pressing the "Fullscreen Stats" button found to the left of the gold coin counter.
The following is a list of character stats in order:
-  Max Health
-  Recovery
-  Armor
-  Move Speed
-  Might
-  Speed
-  Duration
-  Area
-  Cooldown
-  Amount
-  Revival
-  Magnet
-  Luck
-  Growth
-  Greed
-  Curse
-  Reroll
-  Skip
-  Banish
## Character setup
Character setup dialog
The player can select a specific character then press the "Character Setup" button at the bottom of the character stats panel to configure a few options for that character. The list of options include:
| Option | Details | Default option
| Favorite | Displays favorited characters at the top of the character grid, regardless of how it is sorted, but not if it is filtered. Can also be done from the character information panel. Unlocked through Mindbender. | -
| Max weapons | Sets the maximum number of obtainable weapons in a run, which ranges between 1 to 6. Unlocked through Mindbender. Applies to all characters. | 6
| Power creep | Allows the player to either activate Survarots, with an additional option to toggle Enemahs, Golden Eggs, or neither. These settings apply to all characters. | [Seal_I] None
| Skin | Changes which skin to display for a specific character if they have multiple skins. Can also be done from the character information panel. Unlocked through Mindbender. | Default skin of a character.
Pressing the red "CLEAR" button will reset the above options to their default setting. Whether a character is favorited or not would remain unaffected.
## Stage completion panel
Stage completion panel
This panel displays what stages are completed by a selected character. A stage with a checkmark displayed before the stage name indicates that the player completed that stage with a specific character.
Players can sort the character grid by the number of stages completed through a specific character, either in ascending or descending order.
## Co-op & Party Mode
The Character Selection menu in Party mode
With the button leading to this mode being shown on the top right corner of the screen, the player will be prompted with the size of the party, whether it should consist of 2, 3, or 4 characters. In this mode, the Character Selection menu is pretty much the same, except that the Character Setup button is not shown and that the bottom of the screen will indicate character selections for CPUs, which makes the character grid's height lower. The player will be prompted on choosing their desired characters for their CPUs and their behavior.

There are 34 standard characters available in the base game.

## Ode to Castlevania
Main article: Ode to Castlevania
This DLC introduces 96 more characters, making it nearly double the character roster of the rest of the game (including the characters from the base game and all the other DLCs before OtC).
20 of those characters are visible in the character selection screen, 20 more are hidden, and the 56 other characters are secret characters.

## Non-player characters
The following is a list of non-player characters that appear in Vampire Survivors (not counting all of the merchants that are also player characters, any enemy, or any of the unused characters):
-  Eggman: a merchant in Moongolow who only sells  Golden Eggs.
-  Healer: a merchant in Moongolow who can remove  Golden Eggs.
-  Xanthia: a merchant who appears in Planar Bazaar where they sell the  Victory Sword and the  Phas3r. They also sell the  Emerald Disk and the  Joker Disk in Moongolow.
-  Giocare: a merchant in Westwoods who asks for the player to spin the wheel in exchange for some  Gold Coins. Winning the "Spin the Wheel!" minigame would allow for the player to gain access to more parts of this stage.
-  Glaucus: the merchant that appears from the  Stake to Your Heart (0) darkana. They sell up to 12 randomly selected weapons and passives and allow for the player to sell their own weapons and passives in their possession, in exchange for gold.
## Unused characters
Main article: /Unused
Unused characters are those which have been teased in some form, but have not yet been released. Some of them may never be released and were only visible in a development version of the game.

- Build Planner

| Bisconte Draculó | A character mentioned in the description of the itch.io demo of Vampire Survivors, implied to be the main antagonist of the game, as well as the character only shown in the game's box art and main menu, but never as an in-game character. DEFAULTSKIN_TP_CHARLOTTE_2 Raging Fire Belnades' Spellbook
Charlotte & Jonathan are secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors.  Charlotte Aulin uses the  Raging Fire as her starting weapon, but she also has the option of starting with  Belnades' Spellbook, provided it has been unlocked.  Jonathan Morris uses the  Javelin as his starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Spell what they shout, backwards"
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Charlotte & Jonathan can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ettolrahcnahtanoj".
Inputting the spell in iOS unlocks Jonathan & Charlotte instead of Charlotte & Jonathan.
They can also be unlocked by unlocking  Stella and  Loretta Lecarde.
## Passive bonuses
As a duo, Charlotte takes the lead followed by a computer controlled Jonathan. Both characters can Level up and select their own Weapons and Passive Items, similar to when playing in multiplayer.
Jonathan retains the passive bonuses from his individual character Jonathan Morris, while Charlotte has different passive stats than her individual character Charlotte Aulin.
Charlotte starts with +20%  Move Speed, +40%  Duration, +40%  Area.
She has a Charge ability that charges when she is standing still. When fully charged it triggers all weapons she has equipped from the Belnades' Spellbook once. The charge time is 15 seconds by default, which is affected by  Growth.
Jonathan Morris starts with +30  Max Health and +20%  Might.
He has a Charge ability that charges when he is standing still. When fully charged It triggers all weapons he has equipped from the  Coat of Arms once. The charge time is 15 seconds by default, which is affected by  Speed.
Charlotte & Jonathan can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- The player can only see Jonathan's stats via the Level up screen when selecting an item for Jonathan.
## Trivia
- Charlotte & Jonathan's description on the Character Selection screen is a reference to their appearance as duo protagonists in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin. In Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin the player can freely switch between Jonathan and Charlotte during gameplay, when doing so the pair calls out each others names. This is seen frequently in speed runs of the game as switching constantly is faster than walking normally.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_CHARLOTTE_2 Raging Fire Belnades' Spellbook
Charlotte Aulin is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Raging Fire or the  Belnades' Spellbook via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Charlotte can be unlocked by obtaining 7  Mirrors of Truth, which can appear after obtaining the  Capra Gate. She can then be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Charlotte can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notachild".
## Passive bonuses
Charlotte starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Duration, +20%  Area, +10  Reroll, +10  Skip, and +10  Banish.
She has a charge ability that charges while she is standing still. When fully charged, all weapons featured in the Belnades' Spellbook will be triggered. The default charge time is 15 seconds, but can decreased by  Growth down to a minimum of 3 seconds at +400% Growth.
Charlotte can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Triggering all the weapons of the Belnades' Spellbook with her charge ability means that she is encouraged to use weapons from this subset, which all are affected by the Arcana  Gemini (I).
- Because her charge ability is affected by Growth,  Crown is a must-pick item. It is also recommended to use  Wicked Season (XIII) and/or  Crystal Cries (XII) as ways to continuously scale Growth throughout the game. However, due to her weak starting weapon, Charlotte struggles to deal with high-Curse hordes right off the bat if using Wicked Season. Picking the Belnades' Spellbook as a starting weapon instead of Raging Fire and choosing  Hex can help with high amounts of Curse in the early game,  Refectio can also help by providing a bubble of relative safety.
-  Sanctuary is almost a must-pick for Charlotte due to her charge ability letting her trigger its very powerful effect far more often than most characters. Because Sanctuary provides a full heal, it can be beneficial, somewhat counterintuitively, to build Charlotte as a tank, with items like  Armor,  Hollow Heart and  Parm Aegis to help her survive while Sanctuary handles the hordes and keeps her healed up.
## Trivia
- The spell "notachild" is a callback to Portrait of Ruin where Charlotte gets angered when somebody calls her a child,
- Her charge ability is a reference to the charge mechanic that Charlotte can do to power her spells in Portrait of Ruin, even using the same pose.
- Raging Fire is the first spell found by Charlotte in that game.
- Charlotte has a melee attack animation that triggers when using some weapons like whips. That animation, using the book, is the same as her attack animation in Portrait of Ruin.
## External links
- Charlotte Aulin at the Castlevania Wiki

Reduces boss health and blocks some special attacks.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve Fibonacci Spritz.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+7","name":"Chicot","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"ac","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"LEM_CHICOT","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"Ante Chamber - Balatro Main Theme","alias":"","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"LEM_CharacterController_003","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Gros Michel"}
Chicot is a playable character from the Ante Chamber DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Gros Michel.
## Unlocking
Chicot can be unlocked by evolving the  Fibonacci Spritz into the  Royal Flush, with a max level  Outer Saboteur.
Alternatively, Chicot can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "knockknock".
## Passive bonuses
Chicot starts with +20%  Move Speed and  Luck, as well as +7  Reroll.
Chicot starts each run with a draft of  Survarot, and is able to gain more from Light Sources.
- The chance to get a Survarot from a Light Source decreases as Chicot gets more Survarots.
Chicot halves the Health of all Bosses and disables certain special attacks, namely those from the Boss Blinds on Ante Chamber.
## Trivia
- Chicot's passive ability to weaken Bosses is a reference to the Chicot joker's effect in Balatro, which negates the effects of Boss Blinds.
- Despite the video "chicot seduto su una sedia - sopravvissuti ai vampiri - Vampire Survivors" showcasing Chicot in the title and the thumbnail, the video features  Jimbo instead. Though, among the featured characters, Jimbo's name is listed but not Chicot.
- Prior to v1.15, either using this character, entering the Ante Chamber stage, using  Secretino Dagsson or any of the other Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.

Christine Davain (Legacy)Christine DavainStarts with 1 extra level.
Starts with 1 extra level.
LEGACYDEFAULT
Christine Davain is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.3.1. Her starting weapon is  Pentagram.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by leveling Pentagram to level 7. Afterwards, she must be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Christine can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "crystalmakeup".
## Passive bonuses
Christine starts with 1 extra level, -50  Max Health, +30%  Move Speed, -35%  Might, and -25%  Cooldown.
## Tips
- Christine starts with a high Cooldown reduction bonus, any strong but slow rate of fire weapons are greatly benefited by this initial boost even at the cost of lower initial Might.
- Starting with Pentagram, especially at early game, is the cost of having a stronger start compared to  Arca or  Porta. While the weapon is extremely powerful, there is always a risk that all Pickups and Treasure Chests on your screen will be gone because of Pentagram's purging effect. Until then, always look out for the  Crown and level up Pentagram to evolve it into  Gorgeous Moon as soon as possible to save any future headaches.
- With the initial level up at the start of the run made in order for you to get a weapon that will supplement Pentagram, be vigilant to not choose weapons without any damage output like  Clock Lancet or  Laurel. However, if the merchant is available, you can pick up one of his weapons instead to deal damage.
- Pentagram ignores Amount, so the  Beginning (X) arcana is mostly useless on Christine until it evolves into  Gorgeous Moon, where Amount increases the extra gems created.
- Picking  Blood Astronomia (XXI) allows Pentagram to deal direct damage.
## Sprites

 [Default Christine sprite.]

## Animated sprites

 [Movement animation of legacy Christine.]
 Movement animation of legacy Christine.
 - 
 [Movement animation of default Christine.]
 Movement animation of default Christine.
## Trivia
- Christine Davain is a reference to Cristina D'Avena, an Italian singer that is mostly known for voicing the Italian adaptations of theme songs of popular cartoons and anime broadcasted on Italian public television.
- This likely explains her appearance akin to a Sailor Moon character, and the Gorgeous Moon akin to Sailor Moon's pendant, since Cristina sang the Italian version of the opening theme for the anime series.
- Christine,  Gallo, and  Divano are the only characters that start with an extra level.
- They all start with an extra level so that the player can pick up an extra weapon, either because their starting weapon does not deal damage, or in Christine's case, her weapon has a massive cooldown.

Greed can occasionally affect Might and Armor. Can find special pickups.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_CHRISTOPHER_MORNINGSTAR Jet Black Whip Morning Star
Christopher Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Jet Black Whip. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star as optional starting weapon.
## Unlocking
To unlock Christopher, evolve the  Alucart Sworb.
Alternatively, Christopher can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "firsttodoittwice".
## Passive bonuses
Christopher starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, +1  Revival, and +5  Banish.
Whenever Christopher damages an enemy, there is a 50% chance for  Greed to be added to  Might, and to multiply  Armor. Otherwise, nothing happens. Since this is checked every hit, it can rapidly change. Each bonus is independent of the other, and either, both, or neither bonus may be active at a given moment.
Christopher can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- There is a random chance for Greed to affect Christopher's Might and/or Armor. They are randomized separately, so one could be affected, or both, or neither. It is re-randomized multiple times every second, and isn't affected by  Luck. Each 1% Greed translates to 1% Might, and each 100% Greed translates to +1 Armor.
## Trivia
- His unlocking spell is a reference to him being the first person to defeat Dracula twice, in Castlevania: The Adventure and Castlevania II: Belmont's Revenge.
## External links
- Christopher Belmont at the Castlevania Wiki

| {"magnet":"+7%","description":"Chances his luck every 30 seconds or when picking up a Rerollo.","cost":"777","duration":"-","unlock":"Survive 20 minutes in Westwoods.","secret":"No","image":"Select-Chula-Reh.png","luck":"+7%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+7","name":"Chula-Reh","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"EX_CHULAREH","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"+7%","controller":"CharacterController_EX_Chulareh","release":"October 28 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Magi-Stone"}
Chula-Reh is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.14. His starting weapon is the  Magi-Stone.
## Unlocking
Upon surviving to the 20:00 mark in Westwoods, Chula-Reh is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Chula-Reh can be purchased for 777, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Upon obtaining a  Rerollo, or once every 30 seconds, Chula-Reh rolls a die above his head. Getting a 2, 3, 4, or 5 just gives him a  Luck boost equal to the value rolled for the remainder of the run.
- Hurry Mode does not impact this timer.
Rolling a 1 darkens the screen, zooms the camera towards Chula-Reh, and causes the text "Unlucky!" to appear above him for about 3 seconds. He then gains +77%  Curse and -777%  Luck for 30 seconds before triggering  Shooting Stars to land on him. 
Rolling a 6 darkens the screen, zooms the camera towards Chula-Reh, and causes the text "Lucky!" to appear above him for about 3 seconds. He then gains a bonus of +777%  Luck and +77%  Move Speed for 30 seconds and spawns light sources around him, similar to  Greatest Jubilee.
- The chance to roll a 6 is based on 2 values. The first value is equal to the higher value between 1 and TotalLuck+2. This value is then entered into a base-3 logarithm. The result is then multiplied by 1/6 for the final chance. Essentially, this chance will be 1/3 when the player reaches +600% Luck, 1/2 at +2400%, and guaranteed at +72600% Luck.
- Since the result of 1 in a logarithm is always 0, Chula-Reh will normally not be able to roll a 6 while he is "Unlucky!", unless his Luck is relatively high outside of this state.
Rerollos are also made 5 times more common.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
XII - Call of a Mad Moon: Reach a Luck bonus of +999% with Chula-Reh.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII).
## Tips
- As his Luck may potentially increase whenever he picks up a  Rerollo, trying to get more is very important. To that end,  Hail from the Future (X) is a potent pick as it can potentially drop Rerollos every time he levels up.
- For the same reason,  Greatest Jubilee is a very good pick, especially as his scaling luck means that it'll activate more often.
- While not as consistent as other characters with scaling Luck, Chula-Reh can still run a very potent  Slash (XVI) and  Sapphire Mist (I) build.
-  108 Bocce synergizes incredibly well with Chula-Reh, since it gains more damage both if he rolls normally or if he hits a 1. This will make surviving the Curse boost much easier.
## Sprites

## Screenshots
 - 
 [Poe Ratcho talking to Chula-Reh in the video "boschi occidentali - sopravvissuti ai vampiri."]
  Poe Ratcho talking to Chula-Reh in the video "boschi occidentali - sopravvissuti ai vampiri."
## Trivia
- The name "Chula-Reh" could be a pun based on the word "jewelry", as his starting weapon,  Magi-Stone, attacks by dropping jewels on enemies.
- Also, in Italian "Chula-Reh" sounds quite similar to "giullare" (lit. "jester"), but even more like "ciulare", which is a rather coarse synonym of "having intercourse" (meta-trivia: "giullare" itself historically derives from "ciulare", as taught by Italian Nobel Prize winner Dario Fo). "Giullare" comes from Provençal "joglar" meaning "facetious, jolly", derived from Latin "jocularis" that means "funny, facetious".
- The manner in which Chula-Reh moves likely serves as a reference to the "Gang Dance" from the seventh episode of the anime television series JoJo's Bizarre Adventure: Golden Wind.
- In Westwoods, there is a merchant called  Giocare who looks similar to Chula-Reh, except that the purple and yellow accents of their clothes are replaced with blue and white accents respectively.

Can find special pickups.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeating Big Fuzz in Neo Galuga","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Colonel Bahamut","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"200","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"+50%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_COLONEL","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"+2","theme":"Last Springsteen","alias":"Bahamut","movespeed":"+80%","controller":"FB_Colonel","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Metal Claw"}
Colonel Bahamut is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Metal Claw.
## Unlocking
Colonel Bahamut can be unlocked for purchase by defeating  Big Fuzz in Neo Galuga. He needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Colonel Bahamut can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "badbetrayerbahamut".
## Passive bonuses
Bahamut starts with +100  Max Health, +2  Armor, +80%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, and +25%  Magnet.
Bahamut gains +1% Might per +1%  Greed or +1%  Curse. There is no cap on this bonus.
Bahamut can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
-  Wicked Season (XIII) is Bahamut's most key Arcana, as it provides him scaling Might throughout the game in addition to the other benefits it offers.
-  SpellStrike and consequently  SpellStrom are very powerful on Bahamut due to his high starting Might that only gets stronger over the course of the game.
-  Torrona's Box is highly recommended due to its impressive other benefits in addition to increasing Curse by 100%.
- Having  Gemini (I) and  Nightmare will allow Bahamut to double the effects of the plague balls, easily allowing for Bahamut to reach maximum Might.
- While Bahamut's is generally able to keep up with the high amount of enemies spawned by increased Curse due to his Might bonuses,  Sonic Whip [DLC] can be a useful tool as its chance to insta-kill also scales on Curse.

Concetta Caciotta (Legacy)Concetta CaciottaGains +1% Area every level.
Gains +1% Area every level.
LEGACYDEFAULT
Concetta Caciotta is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.5.2. Her starting weapon is  Shadow Pinion.
## Unlocking
Concetta is unlocked for free by finding her  Coffin in the Gallo Tower. The coffin is located in a hidden room, and is entered via a mirror on the left side of the tower near the coffin.
Alternatively, Concetta can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ifeellovevenus". This will require the player to purchase her for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Concetta starts with +20%  Move Speed and +10%  Curse. She gains +1%  Area every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
## Tips
- Concetta has little bonus at the start of the game and her passive ability is outclassed by  Porta Ladonna until level 31. The uncapped nature of this bonus means weapons like  Garlic,  Santa Water, and  Song of Mana can be extremely effective at high levels.
- Shadow Pinion can be a difficult weapon to use, at the start of a run you can move in a small circle without stopping to create a good defensive wall. It will sometimes be better to keep moving and not release the pinions if you are getting overwhelmed by enemies.
- Using a combination of  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) and  Wicked Season (XIII) will help maximize Concetta's bonus.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Legacy Concetta sprite.]
 Legacy Concetta sprite.
 - 
 [Default Concetta sprite.]
 Default Concetta sprite.
## Animated sprites
 - 
 [Movement animation of legacy Concetta.]
 Movement animation of legacy Concetta.
 - 
 [Movement animation of default Concetta.]
 Movement animation of default Concetta.
## Trivia
- "Concetta" is an Italian female name. It means conceived, a reference to the Immaculate Conception.
- "Caciotta" is a type of cheese produced from the milk of cows, sheep, goats, or water buffalo.
- Concetta shares several traits with other coffin characters. See Coffin § Traits for more details.
- However, her theme "Peji Eighteen" is not referencing the weapon. This is a reference to the PEGI rating system, the European system for video game content ratings.
- She and  Zi'Assunta Belpaese are the only coffin characters that start with +10% Curse.
- Concetta could be a reference to Eddie / Zato-ONE from the Guilty Gear franchise, with whom she shares many similarities.
- Both have pointy black wings, a black bodysuit, and long hair.
- Both can fight by summoning black drills made from shadows from the ground.
- Guilty Gear is a fighting game series known for having many allusions to rock and heavy metal music, and Concetta wields an electric guitar.
- Both suffer from a curse.
- Concetta is one of the three characters whose "Legacy" skin is counted as the default skin. The other characters where that is also the case is  Pugnala Provola and  Christine Davain.

Congregation is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Screams from the Void.
## Unlocking
Congregation can be unlocked by dying seven times with  Nameless Fool. This does not need to be in the same run, and can be done across multiple runs.
Alternatively, Congregation can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "pizzathehutt".
## Passive bonuses
Congregation starts with +194  Max Health, +25%  Magnet, +70%  Greed, and +7  Banish.
Congregation gains one passive weapon slot for every +1  Revival they possess beyond the base value (0).
- For example, the Revival PowerUp will provide +1 passive weapon slot, while  Tirajisú will provide up to +2. However, Congregation starts with no extra passive weapon slots at base.
Upon reaching CriticalHP or as a Last Breath, Congregation will become invulnerable for 1 or 2 seconds, respectively, and activate Screams from the Void or its evolution,  Forbidden Siren, nine times, with the number of activations being multiplied by 1 + the number of Revivals possesed by Congregation. So if the player has +1 Revival, then Congregation will activate its main weapon 18 times.
Upon using a Revival, Congregation will obtain the  Velvet Dodecahedron [DLC] if they do not possess it already. This can happen once per run. Congregation's passive is unable to provide levels to the Velvet Dodecahedron.
## Tips
-  Awake (IV) and  Tirajisú can provide Congregation with plenty of extra passive weapon slots.
- With high health and a powerful retaliation weapon with a massive area, Congregation is well suited for  Divine Bloodline (IX) runs. This makes it somewhat hard to die and obtain the Velvet Dodecahedron, but its usefulness in Divine Bloodline builds is dubious anyway.
- If running Divine Bloodline, exceptionally consider not picking up  Empty Tome and other Cooldown reduction sources. This is because Screams from the Void and Forbidden Siren will only count as retaliatory damage if they actually retaliate. If Congregation is firing its weapons too often without being damaged, that may make it difficult for enemies to reach the Survivor and hamper max HP scaling.
- Forbidden Siren will cause Congregation's character ability to activate more often by pulling enemies into the player; make sure to have a source of healing to allow you to 'reload' the ability, and avoid actually dying, as this will reduce your revive count and thus reduce its damage output.
## Trivia
- The spell "pizzathehutt" is a reference to a character in the movie Spaceballs, himself a reference to Jabba the Hutt from Star Wars and the restaurant chain Pizza Hut.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_CORNELL_2SKIN_TP_CORNELL_2DB Dextro Custos Sinestro Custos Ebony Diabologue
Cornell is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. He has the option of starting with  Dextro Custos or  Sinestro Custos.
Upon unlocking the  Ebony Diabologue, Cornell is able to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
Defeating 7,000 enemies with Cornell unlocks  Centralis Custos.
## Unlocking
Cornell can be unlocked by evolving the  Silver Revolver. Once unlocked, Cornell can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Cornell can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "emeryboard".
## Passive bonuses
Cornell starts with 100  Max Health, +1  Amount and +10  Rerolls. He can also transform into  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell by obtaining  Trinum Custodem. In this form, he gains +100 Max Health, +2  Armor, +40%  Move Speed, and +2 Amount.
Cornell can pick the Custos weapons he currently does not possess as a 4th leveling up option, as long as they are not in the slot: no matter whether they are unlocked or not, and whether there are weapon slots left or not. These weapons can also appear if they have been  Sealed, but will not appear again once they have been evolved into Trinum Custodem.
Cornell can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## External links
- Cornell in the Castlevania Wiki

Has hidden weapons.","cost":"666","duration":"-","unlock":"With a pure heart and two good friends, visit the fiery balcony in Cappella Magna.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Cosmo Pavone","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"20","revival":"+1","curse":"-","dlc":"","hidden weapon":"Peachone; Ebony Wings","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"PAVONE","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Patch 0.10.0","theme":"","alias":"Cosmo","movespeed":"+30%","controller":"CharacterControllerPavone","release":"08 August 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Cosmo Pavone is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.10.0. It does not have a starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "With a pure heart and two good friends, visit the fiery balcony in Cappella Magna."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After the player has collected the Yellow Sign and unlocked  Zi'Assunta Belpaese by opening her coffin, the player must start a run in Cappella Magna while playing as a character that has not picked up any  Golden Eggs, as part of the "pure heart" requirement. Toggling off eggs in the character select menu does not count as having 0 eggs.
The player must then obtain  Peachone and  Ebony Wings, which must not be evolved into the  Vandalier. After obtaining the two bird weapons, the player must travel north beyond the  Crown and  Tirajisú, where a  Nduja Fritta Tanto can be found near a balcony. Collecting the Nduja Fritta Tanto when all of the conditions above have been met spawns Cosmo Pavone on the balcony, and the gate blocking it is removed. Approaching and interacting with Cosmo Pavone creates a flash similar to that of a  Rosary and then unlocks it as a playable character.
Alternately, if  The Ender has not yet been defeated, activating the battle against The Ender while standing near the balcony causes all the walls and gates to disappear, and the player can walk over and collect Cosmo Pavone immediately after the battle.
Once unlocked, Cosmo Pavone can be be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Cosmo Pavone can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lhovistoio". Note that the first letter is a lowercase "L".
## Passive bonuses
Cosmo starts with -80  Max Health, +1  Recovery, +20%  Luck, +30%  Move Speed, and +1  Revival. It also gains +1 Recovery and +1% Luck every level, and +1 Revival every 100 levels (+0.01 Revivals every level). There is no cap on these bonuses.
It starts with hidden  Peachone and  Ebony Wings without taking up any weapon slots; the actual weapons themselves may still be obtained from level-ups, and doing so results in the character having two of each weapon. The hidden weapons are upgraded every five levels, starting at level 10, up to level 40 when both reach level 8. The birds cannot be united into  Vandalier, and as usual with hidden weapons, they cannot gain limit break stats.
Every time Cosmo is revived, it triggers a  Rosary effect. This will not be converted to a Weird Souls Purifier if the player has  Seraphic Cry.
## Tips
- Cosmo is the only character in the game that gains +1 Recovery per level and will likely remain so, as having such a steep Recovery rate can push the damage of certain weapons into the millions per second. Other characters scale Recovery at a much slower rate of +0.01 or +0.02 per level at most. The highest possible Recovery scaling beside Cosmo is 0.05 per level, which remains 20 times slower than Cosmo's scaling.
- Since  Queen Sigma cannot obtain Golden Eggs, she can always be used to unlock Cosmo, even if the player has eggs on all of their other characters.
- Using a character that ignores walls (such as  Gyoruntin) can allow you to unlock Cosmo without collecting Peachone or Ebony Wings.
- Cosmo does not have a starting weapon, giving it more customization when designing a build.
- Cosmo can pair well with  Sarabande of Healing (VI), as its Recovery keeps on scaling every level, making it pulse with increasing power.
-  Wandering the Jet Black (XXI) is also an extremely strong pairing for similar reasons, since Cosmo can obtain incredible amounts of healing, leading to a large increase in damage.
-  Hidden Anathema (III) also has a strong pairing with Cosmo. Not only will Cosmo find more  Floor Chicken, because of their  Luck scaling, but they will get more stats from each chicken because of  Recovery.
- Though the player can obtain two  Peachone and  Ebony Wings with this character,  Gemini (I) will only grant one of each counterpart weapon.
- With Gemini and both sets of the weapons, the player can have 6 total birds following them at once.
- Cosmo also pairs extremely well with  Divine Bloodline (IX) and  Crystal Cries (XII), most notably increasing the player's  Max Health, which counteracts Cosmo's low base HP. Crystal Cries also gives extra  Growth, synergizing well with Cosmo's scaling.
- Due to its extremely high regeneration, it pairs well with weapons that damage the wielder, such as  Muramasa [DLC],  Gatti Amari and the Dominus [DLC] weapons, essentially negating the risk of dying to these weapons. In addition, Muramasa also synergizes well with Divine Bloodline and its critical hit chance scales with Cosmo's ever-growing Luck.
- Cosmo's powerful regeneration can allow him to greatly boost  Soul Eater's damage.
-  Sharp Tongue [DLC] /  Impostongue [DLC], as well as  Star Flail [DLC] /  Moon Rod [DLC] and  Cardinal Rain [DLC], works extremely well with Cosmo because their damage is multiplied by Recovery. Reaching even double digit levels will have them dealing far higher damage than any other weapon.
-  Phieraggi,  Meal Ticket [DLC], and  Grand Cross all gain extra damage from Cosmo's  Revival scaling, and extra Amount in the case of Phieraggi.
## Trivia
- Cosmo may be based on the phoenix, as it starts with a Revival and has the ability to keep reviving.
- The name comes from the anime series Yattodetaman, in which the main characters travel through time looking for a mythical entity called the Firebird, which has been localized in Italian as "Cosmo Pavone".
- Cosmo actually gains 0.01 Revivals per level, however this is not visible in the display.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Count on him to change halfway through dinner.","cost":"16650","duration":"+20%","unlock":"Let the souls of the damned get their revenge on the other Count.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+10","name":"Count Olrox","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"150","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"+10","banish":"+10","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_OLROX","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+1","theme":"Dance Of Pales","alias":"Olrox","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Olrox_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Dark Rift"}
Count Olrox is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Dark Rift.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Let the souls of the damned get their revenge on the other Count."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Black Disk in Ode to Castlevania, Count Olrox will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, where his summoning circle is located in the left room of the Dinner Hall, in the far east building of the castle. Upon defeating him with  Dark Rift, Count Olrox is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Count Olrox can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Count Olrox can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "dontdisturbdinner".
## Passive bonuses
Count Olrox starts with the following bonuses:
- +50  Max Health
- +1  Armor
- +20%  Move Speed
- +20%  Might
- +20%  Duration
- +100%  Greed
- +10  Reroll
- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
Count Olrox is immune to health drain. 
After obtaining the  Ebony and Crimson Stones, at certain kill thresholds, Count Olrox transforms into a more powerful form, called  Megalo Olrox, starting at 1,000 kills. He gains +200% Might and -50% Cooldown, and becomes invulnerable. He also gains a permanent  Nduja Fritta Tanto for the duration of the effect.
Count Olrox can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Items that increase  Curse or  Charm, like  Torrona's Box and  Karoma's Mana, are important for Olrox in order to consistently kill as many enemies as possible.  Wicked Season (XIII)} should likely be your first Arcana. Built properly, Olrox should be able to spend most of the run in Megalo form.
-  Paranormal Scan is recommended. One of Olrox's weaknesses is that, even with high Curse, kill thresholds may be difficult to achieve later into the run, stranding him in his normal form against jacked-up enemies. Due to Olrox's massive Cooldown reduction in Megalo form, he can snowball Paranormal Scan very quickly and this helps build up his stats for the late-game to help him compete with powerful enemies.
## Trivia
- The spell "dontdisturbdinner" and his description both reference his appearance in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night where he is sitting at a table awaiting Alucard, who can also sit on the table without triggering the fight.
## External links
- Olrox on the Castlevania Wiki

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Gains stat bonuses every time anyone's weapon is brought to max level.","cost":"100","duration":"-","unlock":"Find and open the coffin in Polus Replica.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Crewmate Dino","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"em","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"C1_CREWMATE","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Adventures + Vampire Survivors: Emergency Meeting DLC on Steam Dec 18th","alias":"Crewmate","theme":"No Pirates Among Us","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"C1_Crewmate","release":"18 December 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Report!"}
Crewmate Dino is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Report!.
## Unlocking
Crewmate is unlocked for purchase by finding and opening their coffin in Polus Replica. They need to be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Crewmate can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "redissus".
## Passive bonuses
Crewmate starts with +20%  Move Speed. Crewmate gains minor stat bonuses every time a unique weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no limit. This passive also includes maximum level items by teammates in multiplayer.
Active weapons will randomly provide one the following stats upon reaching max level, multiplied by the number of levels that weapon has:
-  Might: +4%
-  Speed: +4%
-  Duration: +4%
-  Area: +4%
Passive weapons will provide the follow stats upon reaching max level, multiplied by the number of levels that passive has:
-  Max Health: +20
-  Recovery: +0.1
-  Armor: +0.2
-  Move Speed: +8%
-  Cooldown: -3%
-  Amount: +0.2
-  Revival: +0.2
-  Magnet: +0.5
-  Luck: +5%
-  Growth: +5%
## Tips
- Crewmate gains bonuses any time they or a teammate upgrades a weapon to maximum level, making them stronger in multiplayer.
- The Darkana  Hail from the Future (X) can provide up to 14 additional sources of stat increases due to the Candyboxes and Arma Dios it spawns.
- In order to maximize on Crewmate's stat bonuses in single player, it is recommended to choose weapons that unite together to open new spaces in the inventory, as this will allow the stat boost to trigger more times per run. Relevant weapons include  SpellString,  SpellStream, and  SpellStrike (uniting into  SpellStrom);  Endo Gears,  Peri Pendulum,  Myo Lift, and  Epi Head (uniting into  Clock Tower);  Dextro Custos,  Sinestro Custos, and  Centralis Custos (uniting into  Trinum Custodem);  Dominus Anger,  Dominus Hatred, and  Dominus Agony (uniting into  Power of Sire); Peachone and Ebony Wings (uniting into Vandalier); Whip and Vento Sacro (uniting into Fuwalafuwaloo); and Phiera Der Tuphello and Eight The Sparrow (uniting into Phieraggi).
- Weapons which evolve multiple times, such as the Bracelet or weapons from the Emerald Diorama DLC, are also recommended.
- Due to upgrading a passive item also triggering Crewmate's effect, weapons from the Emergency Meeting DLC are very effective picks, as their evolution items are subsumed into the evolved weapon, opening up a new spot in the inventory.
- By far the most key weapon for Crewmate is the  Penshin Fatcha, this weapon evolves an unlimited number of times, triggering Crewmate's ability every time one of its forms is upgraded to maximum level. Having this weapon alone mostly negates the need for any of the above weapons.

Dario Bossi is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Salamender.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Burn bright until the end while rejecting all but the inferno."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start and finish a run as any character in any stage having only used fire weapons at any point. Any non-fire-weapon will disqualify the run, even if it does no damage, such as  Laurel. Arcanas, Pickups and Passive Items are allowed and will not invalidate the unlock. 
- Weapon selectors like  Candybox are meant to be permitted, but, due to a bug, currently invalidate Dario's unlock.
Fire weapons are: 
-  Fire Wand and  Hellfire
-  Santa Water and  La Borra
-  Valkyrie Turner, but not  Shadow Pinion
-  Flames of Misspell and  Ashes of Muspell
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet, and  Tri-Bracelet
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto
-  Heart of Fire (XIX)
-  Firearm [DLC] and  Fire-L3GS [DLC]
-  Alchemy Whip [DLC] and  Vampire Killer [DLC]
-  Raging Fire [DLC],  Salamender [DLC], and  Speculo Raging Fire [DLC]
-  Dominus Anger [DLC], but not  Power of Sire [DLC]
-  Dextro Custos [DLC] and  Trinum Custodem [DLC], but not  Centralis Custos [DLC] nor  Sinestro Custos [DLC]
-  Svarog Statue [DLC] and  Darkness Illusion [DLC]
-  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC], but not  Aura Blast [DLC]
-  Dies Irae [DLC], but not  Arrow of Goth [DLC] nor  Mannajja
-  Hydro Pump Climax [DLC], but not  Hydro Storm [DLC]
-  Punch [DLC],  Pressure Point [DLC], and  Gilded Hand [DLC]
-  Saber Machine Gun [DLC],  Hecaton Machine Gun [DLC], and  Divergence [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
-  Infernolatro [DLC] and  NaneInferno [DLC]
-  FireFall [DLC],  FireBall [DLC],  FireWall [DLC], and  FireStall [DLC]
Doing this unlocks Dario Bossi as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Dario Bossi can be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Dario Bossi can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "aguniinagony".
## Passive bonuses
While playing as Dario, the base damage of every equipped Fire-type weapon increases by 0.5 per level. Fire-type weapons include:
-  Fire Wand and  Hellfire
-  Santa Water and  La Borra
-  Valkyrie Turner, but not  Shadow Pinion
-  Flames of Misspell and  Ashes of Muspell
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet, and  Tri-Bracelet
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto
-  Heart of Fire (XIX)
-  Firearm [DLC] and  Fire-L3GS [DLC]
-  Alchemy Whip [DLC] and  Vampire Killer [DLC]
-  Raging Fire [DLC],  Salamender [DLC], and  Speculo Raging Fire [DLC]
-  Dominus Anger [DLC], but not  Power of Sire [DLC]
-  Dextro Custos [DLC] and  Trinum Custodem [DLC], but not  Centralis Custos [DLC] nor  Sinestro Custos [DLC]
-  Svarog Statue [DLC] and  Darkness Illusion [DLC]
-  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC], but not  Aura Blast [DLC]
-  Dies Irae [DLC], but not  Arrow of Goth [DLC] nor  Mannajja
-  Hydro Pump Climax [DLC], but not  Hydro Storm [DLC]
-  Punch [DLC],  Pressure Point [DLC], and  Gilded Hand [DLC]
-  Saber Machine Gun [DLC],  Hecaton Machine Gun [DLC], and  Divergence [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
-  Infernolatro [DLC] and  NaneInferno [DLC]
-  FireFall [DLC],  FireBall [DLC],  FireWall [DLC], and  FireStall [DLC]
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- As with all characters that gain benefits from leveling up, Dario benefits most from starting with  Wicked Season (XIII). The  Crown and some Curse-boosting items are also advisable. One of those is  Torrona's Box, which naturally synergizes with Dario's playstyle as it evolves the Flames of Misspell.
- Because Dario's ability gives a flat base damage boost, he benefits more from weapons with low base damage that can output attacks quickly than weapons with high base power, which won't scale as well. He is one of the best users of Svarog Statue for this reason, as its main drawback is usually that its damage is extremely weak in the lategame. For the same reason Hellfire may be suboptimal as it's a high-damage, low-hit rate weapon.
- If Svarog Statue is picked, one might also want to consider using  Wings to maximize the trail of fire, which also can evolve the  Shadow Pinion into the fire-based Valkyrie Turner, and then united into the  Darkness Illusion [DLC].
-  Heart of Fire (XIX) can be a decent Arcana to pick, as it affects most of the weapons Dario's boost works on, and the explosions given from this arcana are boosted as well, increasing Dario's options for weapons.
-  Heir of Fate (XIX) Is an incredibly solid pick, as it synergises greatly with Dario's boost.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Doesn't appreciate getting blasphemously mockered.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"Make it feel at home.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Death","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"255","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_DEATH","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"-","theme":"Dance of Illusions (Harmony of Despair)","alias":"","movespeed":"+100%","controller":"TP_Death_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Death Spiral"}
Death is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Death Spiral.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Make it feel at home."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania while playing as  Richter Belmont, and then obtain the  Endo Gears,  Peri Pendulum,  Myo Lift, and  Epi Head, which are unlocked after obtaining the  Serpent Gate, then evolving them into the  Clock Tower and entering the boss fight against  Death. Doing this unlocks Death as a playable character.
Note: When entering the boss fight against Death in Dracula's room, the player(s) must exit the run via the quit menu before the boss has a chance to take the player's weapons or the character unlock will fail.
Once unlocked, Death can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Death can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "sewardzead".
## Passive bonuses
Death starts with +155  Max Health, +100%  Move Speed, and +20%  Might. Stat-wise, Death is identical to  Mask of the Red Death. 
Once the  Ebony and Crimson Stones have been obtained, he morphs into a smaller version of his boss form, called  Megalo Death, upon reaching level 80. This transformation gives Death the  Death Hand, two giant skeletal hands that move in tandem with him and create large cracks in the ground that shake the screen and deal 65 base damage to enemies. In this form, he gains +200% Might, -20%  Cooldown, and +100%  Curse. He also cannot die in this form, but instead disappears at the time limit, ending the run automatically, similar to  Megalo Menya Moonspell and  Ghost Lino.
Death can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Death's description is a reference to Mask of the Red Death's description, "A blasphemous mockery."
- The hint for unlocking Death is likely a reference to how, in the Castlevania series, he is typically fought in the Clock Tower.
- The spell for unlocking Death refers to Death's human disguises, Seward and Zead.
- Death is one of the very few characters in Vampire Survivors who has voice acting, alongside Richter Belmont and Vlad Tepes Dracula. He is voiced by SungWon Cho, who is also the voice for Richter Belmont.
## External links
- Death on the Castlevania Wiki

Diva No. 5 is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Super Missile. Her "Virginia" skin uses  Twin Dragon as starting weapon instead.
Defeating  Living Anguish in Inverse Emerald Diorama as Diva No. 5 will unlock  Mr. S as playable character.
## Unlocking
Diva No. 5 is unlocked by fully evolving  Eagle Gun. 
Alternatively, all skins of Diva No. 5 can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the following spells in any order. 
- "ttwosixtyg"
- "avalonssongstress"
- "mechwardrobe"
## Passive bonuses
Different skins (body types) of Diva No. 5 have different stats and passive effects. Every skin can trigger Showstopper at critical health, even if it isn't listed:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
| Skin | Name | Starting Weapon | Stats | Passive | Unlocks by
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 with no link] | Retro Pod | Super Missile | +20 Max Health +1 Armor | Triggers Showstopper at critical health. | Fully evolve the Eagle Gun.
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 (Battle Tank) with no link] | Battle Tank | Super Missile | +100 Max Health +2 Armor -20% Move Speed | Charge ability: fires an array of Super Missiles. | Glimmer at least 11 techs as "Virginia".
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 (Mover) with no link] | Mover | Super Missile | -50 Max Health +100% Move Speed +100% Magnet | Can Fly. | Glimmer at least 7 techs as "Retro Pod".
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 (Shanks) with no link] | Shanks | Super Missile | +20 Max Health +1 Armor +30% Speed +30% Area | Charge ability: triggers the Ballistic Missiles tech. | With Diva No.5, Defeat all bosses in Emerald Diorama in a single run (completing the stage in Inverse mode also counts).
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 (Paradox) with no link] | Paradox | Super Missile | +40 Max Health +1 Armor +30% Might +30% Duration | Can Fly. Can innately use the Cosmic Rave tech. | With Diva No.5, Defeat all bosses in Inverse Emerald Diorama in a single run.
| [A sprite icon representing Diva No. 5 (Virginia) with no link] | Virginia | Twin Dragon | +20 Max Health +1 Armor | Gains bonuses to Might, Speed, Duration, Area, Cooldown, and Recovery, based on the total of glimmered techs. Triggers Showstopper at critical health. Bonuses are +5% Might, +5% Speed (max +100%), +5% Duration (max +100%), +2.5% Area (max +50%), -1% Cooldown (max -10%), and +0.03 Recovery (max +1.00) per glimmered tech learned. | Fully evolve the Super Missile.
## Notes
- The bosses required to unlock 'Shanks' and 'Paradox' are the main 6 bosses, being:
-  Divine Wood Spirit
-  Earth Dragon
-  Malevolent Door Spirit
-  Iron Maiden
-  Specter of Iwanaga-hime
-  Living Anguish
- The charge abilities of 'Battle Tank' and 'Shanks' increase with movement (not movement speed), and do not charge while the player is standing still. However to use the charge ability the player must stand still. Movespeed does not seem to affect charge distance.
- Innate and charged techs do not interfere with the activation of techs glimmered normally. Notably, the Ballistic Missile tech 'Shanks' charges often activates at the same time as the one from its weapon, shooting all missiles simultaneously. Also, the innate tech of 'Paradox' has greatly increased area, mostly hitting distant enemies.
- 'Virginia's ability synergizes well with  Spirit Rings due to naturally increasing 3 of the 4 attributes needed to access its techniques. With full powerups, only a  Growth and  Area bonus is required to unlock the full set, due to Area only gaining half the benefit. Do note, however, that the techs from Spirit Rings itself do not count towards Virginia's stats, due to lacking a glimmer cutscene.
- The stat increases 'Virginia' receives differ from those gained using  Torrona's Box by having higher increases in Might, Speed, and Duration, while also increasing Cooldown and Recovery, which the item doesn't.
