Gains +1 Armor every 5 levels (max +5).","cost":"2000","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.6.1","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+30%","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Divano Thelma","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+10","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Obtain the Crimson Shroud","greed":"-50%","movespeed":"-","theme":"","alias":"Divano","speed":"-","release":"23 May 2022","id":"FEBBRA","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Laurel"}
Divano Thelma is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.6.1. Their starting weapon is the  Laurel.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by obtaining the  Crimson Shroud. They must then be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Divano can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "quandolodicelui".
## Passive bonuses
Divano starts with 1 extra level, +1  Recovery, +30%  Luck, -50%  Greed, and +10  Banish. They gain +1  Armor every 5 levels up to level 25. The maximum amount of Armor gained this way is +5.
The following table shows how much  Armor they gain by level:
| Levels | Armor
| 1-4 | 0
| 5-9 | +1
| 10-14 | +2
| 15-19 | +3
| 20-24 | +4
| 25+ | +5
## Tips
- Due to Divano's armor level bonus, they are a great users of the  Divine Bloodline (IX) arcana. Using weapons that specialize in retaliation damage, with the most important being  Legionnaire, along with the likes of  NO FUTURE and  Victory Sword, can allow Divano to achieve large amounts of maximum health and an enormous damage output.  Armor is a key pickup for this build.
## Trivia
- Divano and  Iguana Gallo Valletto are the only two non-secret characters to be left out of the dev log. Despite this, they are not secret characters because they have unlock requirements explained in-game as well as related achievements.
- "Divano" means "Sofa" in Italian. But in this case, the reference is about the "Divino Otelma", an Italian television personality.
- Divano,  Iguana Gallo Valletto, and  Christine Davain are the only characters that start with an extra level.
- They all start with an extra level so that the player can pick up an extra weapon, either because their starting weapon does not deal damage, or in Christine's case, her weapon, the  Pentagram, has a massive cooldown.

Dmitrii Blinov is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Ebony Diabologue.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Undo the fused demon with she whom seeks perfect balance."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Black Disk in Ode to Castlevania,  Menace will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, located to the right side of the Sewer (underneath the first section of the front building) inside Dracula's Castle. Upon defeating him while playing as  Celia Fortner, Dmitrii Blinov is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Dmitrii Blinov can be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Dmitrii Blinov can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "menacinghaircut".
## Passive bonuses
Dmitrii gets a hidden copy of the latest weapon he received. Of note is that if the player gives him  Game Killer (0), he will get a hidden copy of that initially rather than his starting weapon.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- His name was misspelled in-game as "Dmitri" instead of "Dmitrii". This was fixed in a later patch.

Dolores is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Bullova.
## Unlocking
Dolores can be unlocked by defeating the  Specter of Iwanaga-hime with  Siugnas in Yomi (southeast world) in Inverse Emerald Diorama.
Alternatively, Dolores can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "heavymetalvocals".

Dolores starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Might, +1  Revival, and +20%  Luck.
Dolores triggers Showstopper at critical health and on revival. She also gains a -0.8% bonus to  Cooldown every time Showstopper is triggered (Max -20%).
Triggering Showstopper has the following mechanics:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health, or on revival (Dolores only).

DEFAULTLEGACY
Dommario is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.10. Dommario's starting weapon is the  King Bible.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by getting over 5,000  Gold Coins in a single run. He is then available to purchase for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Dommario can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bioparco".
## Passive bonuses
Dommario starts with -40%  Move Speed, +40%  Projectile Speed, and +40%  Duration.
## Tips
- Weapons that benefit greatly from both Speed and Duration synergize well with Dommario, especially early on. Alongside his starting King Bible, consider weapons such as  Runetracer/ NO FUTURE,  Bone,  Mace/ Stamazza and  Hydro Storm which all work well with these benefits.
- With enough leveling, the  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) and  Lost & Found Painting (XVII) Arcana's can greatly enhance Dommario's already high base passive bonuses. These in particular can make King Bible devastatingly powerful on its own without the need to evolve into  Unholy Vespers.
- Dommario's low Movement speed is difficult to offset. As a result, any weapons or Arcanas that benefit from Movement Speed such as  Vento Sacro or  Tragic Princess (III) are not suitable for him.
- If Dommario's low Movement speed is proving to be problematic, consider taking  Wings and/or  Sonic Dash, as both can quite easily bring his Movement speed to similar levels as other characters.  Edge of the Earth (VIII) can be used to instantly set Dommario's Speed to +100%.

## Other
 - 
 [Dommario as seen in the pre-release trailer of Operation Guns.]
 Dommario as seen in the pre-release trailer of Operation Guns.
## Trivia
- Dommario's name is the countryside pronunciation of "Don Mario", meaning "Mario the priest".
- His name is a reference to the main character of the TV drama Don Matteo, a crime-solving priest played by actor Terence Hill (real name Mario Girotti).
- His unlocking spell "bioparco" is an Italian word for "zoo" which is used as a sweeter substitute of the Italian swear phrase/blasphemy "Dio porco", which relates God (Dio) to a pig (porco), in the same way as "gosh darn it" functions in English.
## In other media
- Dommario makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. He is a reskin of Pinion in that game.

Drolta Tzuentes is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Anura.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Witness the 7th rebirth of Dracula's niece on holy ground."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Drolta Tzuentes is unlocked by having  Elizabeth Bartley die 7 times in the same run in Cappella Magna. This can be achieved by using the Revival PowerUp, the  Awake (IV) Arcana and picking up  Tirajisú on the stage and levelling it up.
Once unlocked, Drolta can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Drolta Tzuentes can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "inadvertentresurrector".
## Passive bonuses
Drolta Tzuentes starts with +70%  Duration, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip and +5  Banish.

# Eggman
The Eggman appears directly north in Moongolow near one of the secret items if the player has at least 5,000 Golden Eggs total, at least 1,000,000 gold, and has acquired the Yellow Sign. He acts (and appears) similar to the Merchant, but only sells Golden Eggs. 
When the player touches the Eggman, a shop will be opened with 2 options: 1 Golden Egg for 10,000 gold, or "MAX EGGS", which purchases as many Golden Eggs as the player can afford.
To remove Golden Eggs, see  Healer.

DEFAULTSKIN_ELEANOR_AREASKIN_ELEANOR_MIGHT SpellString SpellStream SpellStrike
Eleanor Uziron is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  SpellString.
## Unlocking
Eleanor is unlocked for purchase by finding and opening her coffin in Lake Foscari. She needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Eleanor can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "spellsomething".
## Passive bonuses
Eleanor starts with -10  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed, +25%  Growth, and +2  Reroll.
Upon reaching level 10, 20, and 30,  SpellStream,  SpellStrike, and  Academy Badge will spawn underneath her respectively.
## Alternative starting weapons
Casting the spell "starpupil" in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane unlocks two alternative "skins" that changes Eleanor's starting weapon to SpellStream or SpellStrike. Her passive bonus at level 10 and 20 are also adjusted accordingly; starting with SpellStream spawns SpellStrike then SpellString, while starting with SpellStrike spawns SpellString then SpellStream.
## Tips
- Received weapons from Eleanor's passive effect spawn on the ground, and therefore can be picked up after the max weapon limit, allowing Eleanor to get more than six weapons. This is very useful for uniting them later into  SpellStrom without having to rely on levelling up to get all three weapons and taking up weapon slots in the meantime. As a result, gathering all of a player's desired weapons early instead of levelling them up can be beneficial, as this minimizes the amount of time the player has to wait to fill out their weapon slots.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_ELIZABETH_2SBSKIN_TP_ELIZABETH_3DB Hex Belnades' Spellbook Ebony Diabologue
Elizabeth Bartley is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Hex. 
Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook and  Ebony Diabologue, Elizabeth is able to start with either weapon as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Elizabeth can be unlocking by evolving the  Umbra. She is then made available for purchase for 6,665, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Elizabeth can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thelizardqueen".
## Passive bonuses
She starts with +100  Max Health, +2  Armor +50%  Move Speed and  Might, +100%  Greed +10  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips, and +50  Charm
She also is immune to Health Drain.
Elizabeth can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
After acquiring the  Ebony and Crimson Stones, at certain kill thresholds, starting at 1,000 kills, she morphs into a more powerful form,  Megalo Elizabeth Bartley, for 30 seconds, similar to  Menya Moonspell and  Impostor Rina. In this form, she gains +200% Might and -50%  Cooldown, and becomes invulnerable. Kill thresholds are as follows:
- 1,000 kills
- 3,000 kills
- 5,000 kills
- 7,000 kills
- 9,000 kills
- 11,000 kills
- 13,000 kills
- 15,000 kills
After this, Elizabeth will continue to morph every 15,000 kills. A new morph will only begin once the current one has ended.
## Tips
- Items that increase  Curse or  Charm, like  Torrona's Box and  Karoma's Mana, are important for Elizabeth in order to consistently kill as many enemies as possible.  Wicked Season (XIII) is a good first Arcana. Built properly, Elizabeth can spend most of the run in her Megalo form.
## Trivia
- Her transforming into a Giant Medusa Head is a nod to Castlevania: Bloodines where she transforms into a new form resembling a gorgon.
## External links
- Elizabeth Bartley in the Castlevania Wiki

Engineer Gino is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Lucky Swipe.
## Unlocking
Engineer is unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Sharp Tongue. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Engineer can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "isaworangevent".
## Passive bonuses
Engineer starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20  Reroll, +20  Skip, and +20  Banish. Engineer can choose an extra passive item at levels 2, 12 and 22.
## Tips
- Engineer can guarantee getting the  Crown at level 2, which is very helpful for snowballing near the start of a run.
- If Engineer is already full on passives upon reaching a level where they get an extra passive, the extra passive will be added to their inventory anyway, allowing them to go over the normal limit of 6 if they prioritize filling those slots early.
- Due to its scaling Luck bonuses decreasing the effectiveness of Lucky Swipe, it is not recommended for Engineer to choose Wicked Season as an arcana unless the Lucky Swipe has been evolved.

Eric Lecarde is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Alucard Spear. Alternatively, he can also choose to start with the  Coat of Arms or  Spectral Sword via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Eric Lecarde can be unlocked by evolving the  Javelin. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Eric can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lecardigan".
## Passive bonuses
Eric Lecarde starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Might, +20%  Speed, +20%  Area, and -5%  Cooldown.
When Eric Lecarde's health reaches below 20%, he triggers  Burning Alcarde, becoming invulnerable for 20 seconds, gaining -20% Cooldown, and a flame aura that deals damage to surrounding enemies. This skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- His unlocking method of evolving the Javelin is a callback to Portrait of Ruin where Wind gives the Alucard Spear to Jonathan Morris after mastering the Javelin skill.
## External links
- Eric Lecarde at the Castlevania Wiki

| {"magnet":"-","description":"At least they're lucky.","cost":"777","duration":"-10%","update":"35th Anniversary Patch","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+100%","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Exdash Exiviiq","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"77","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-10%","area":"-10%","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"1) Cast the spell x-x1viiq, or find a lot of Little Clovers.","greed":"-","movespeed":"-10%","theme":"","alias":"Exdash","speed":"-50%","release":"21 January 2022","id":"EXDASH","cooldown":"+10%","weapon":"Ebony Wings"}
Exdash Exiviiq is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.9. Its starting weapon is  Ebony Wings.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "1) Cast the spell x-x1viiq."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Upon quickly typing "x-x1viiq", either while in the main menu or as a spell in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane, Exdash Exiviiq is unlocked as a playable character.
If the player is using a French azerty keyboard, they will need to set your keyboard from FR to EN in Windows, and then type x)x&viia. They can then can set the keyboard back to the previous settings. If the player is using a macro (like AutoHotkey), they will need to put the keyboard in English (qwerty) but the macro needs to output x-x1viiq, and not x)x&viia.
Alternatively, there is a TotalCollected/65535 chance to unlock Exdash Exiviiq each time the player collects a  Little Clover, where TotalCollected is how many Little Clovers are collected in the save file.
Once unlocked, Exdash Exiviiq can be purchased for 777, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Exdash starts with -23  Max Health, -10%  Move Speed, -10%  Might, -10%  Area, -50%  Speed, -10%  Duration, +10%  Cooldown, and +100%  Luck. It gains +10% luck every 10 levels up to level 50. The maximum amount of luck gained that way is +50%, or 150% with the starting bonus included.
The following table shows how much Luck it gains by level:
| Level | Luck
| 1-9 | +100%
| 10-19 | +110%
| 20-29 | +120%
| 30-39 | +130%
| 40-49 | +140%
| 50+ | +150%
## Trivia
- Exdash's family name Exiviiq and the code x-x1viiq are references to the cheat code X-X!V''Q used in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, which also gives the main hero Alucard a very high Luck stat in exchange for other stats being lower.
- Exdash's starting max health before powerups is 77, and 7 is considered a lucky number, thereby further emphasizing Exdash as a character revolving around luck.

Fake Trio is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Bone,  Bracelet, or  Pocket Knife.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Find the final form of the Librarian's offerings."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After unlocking the  Pile of Secrets, the player discover every evolution or union of every Weapon sold by the  Master Librarian. 
The player just has to discover each of the below weapons. They do not need to get them all at the same time.
| Librarian Weapon | Requirement | Union
| Valmanway [DLC] | Thousand Edge | Million Cut [DLC]
| Icebrand [DLC] | Unholy Vespers | Ninth Circle [DLC]
| Arrow of Goth [DLC] | Mannajja | Dies Irae [DLC]
| Aura Blast [DLC] | Hellfire | Kardía Phlegeton [DLC]
| Kaiser Knuckle [DLC] | Tri-Bracelet | Lapiste Tepisto [DLC]
| Pocket Knife [DLC] | Max Torrona's Box | Claimh Solais [DLC]
| Svarog Statue [DLC] | Valkyrie Turner | Darkness Illusion [DLC]
| Troll Bomb [DLC] | NO FUTURE | Carnage Heart [DLC]
| Hydro Storm [DLC] | La Borra | Hydro Pump Climax [DLC]
| Grand Cross [DLC] | Heaven Sword | Arch Angle [DLC]
| Summon Spirit [DLC] | Holy Wand | Spirit of Light [DLC]
| Soul Steal [DLC] | Vicious Hunger | Power of Lire [DLC]
| Dark Rift [DLC] | Death Spiral | Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC]
| Sword Brothers [DLC] | Thunder Loop | Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
| Summon Spirit Tornado [DLC] | Gorgeous Moon | Venus Crescent [DLC]
| Anura [DLC] | Soul Eater | Dark Frogamorphosis [DLC]
Alternatively, Fake Trio can be unlocked in Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "theysmelldifferent".
## Passive bonuses
The chosen main character will be joined by the other two as follower characters. These followers can be damaged and die as normal.
Each character of the Fake Trio has a different passive and starting stats, mimicking certain characters from Ode to Castlevania. 
Fake Trevor, the  Antonio Belpaese lookalike, has the same starting stats and passive as  Trevor Belmont [DLC].
- Fake Trevor starts with +20%  Move Speed and an extra  Beginning (X). Fake Trevor also gets +1  Amount every 10 Levels, with a max of +3.
Fake Sypha, the  Imelda Belpaese lookalike, has the same starting stats and passive as  Sypha Belnades [DLC].
- Fake Sypha starts with +40%  Move Speed and  Duration, +10  Reroll,  Skip, and  Banish, and an extra  Jail of Crystal (XIV).
Fake Grant, the  Gennaro Belpaese lookalike, has the same starting stats and passive as  Grant Danasty [DLC].
- Fake Grant starts with +60%  Move Speed and +40%  Luck. Fake Grant can also trigger any equipped knives ( Knife,  Curved Knife [DLC],  Pocket Knife [DLC], and their evolutions) in retaliation or by overhealing by +8 Health in a single instance.

## Trivia
- The spell "theysmelldifferent" may be an allusion to dialogue from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, where Alucard identifies Richter Belmont by the scent of his blood. Alternately, because the fake trio are zombies, the smell could be the scent of rot.
- The Fake Trio is a reference to the false versions of Trevor, Sypha, and Grant who appear as bosses in the Reverse Colosseum in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night. These impostors were created to confuse  Alucard, as Trevor, Sypha, and Grant were his allies in Castlevania III: Dracula’s Curse.
- Each of the characters in the Fake Trio appears as a base game character dressed as the Castlevania character that they were based on.
- Fake Grant resembles  Gennaro Belpaese.
- Fake Sypha resembles  Imelda Belpaese.
- Fake Trevor resembles  Antonio Belpaese.

The Familiar contains a group of secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors., similar to  Innocent Devil.
Each of them has a different starting weapon. They can be summoned by  Familiar Forge as secondary characters.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Entrust their care to the gentlest soul, twice."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
As  Julia Laforeze, obtain two  Familiar Forges in a single run. It can be done on Ode to Castlevania by picking a Familiar Forge from  Candybox, then picking the one as a stage item up. It will not show up in the Candybox if you pick up the stage item first.
Alternatively, the Familiar can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "friendlevania".
## Passive bonuses
All Familiars start with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels (up to +3).
All Familiars can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## List of Familiars

| Faerie | Refectio
| Sacred Cardinal | Raging Fire
| Sacred Dragon | Fulgur
| Sacred Tiger | Ice Fang
| Sacred Turtle | Rock Riot

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Water included.","cost":"1665","duration":"-","unlock":"You don't need his services if you can walk on water.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Ferryman","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","hidden weapon":"Acquazzone","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_FERRYMAN","flight":"Yes","armor":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","theme":"The Lost Portrait (Symphony of the Night)","alias":"Ferryman","movespeed":"-","controller":"TP_Ferryman_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
The Ferryman is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Although he does not have a starting weapon, he starts with a hidden  Acquazzone.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "You don't need his services if you can walk on water."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania and evolve the  Sonic Dash into  Rapidus Fio. Then, they must cross the moat in front of the castle using any method of Wall Clipping, including Flight. Note that Rapidus Fio must be active and the player must be moving. Only specific sections of the moat will unlock this secret, such as the section that connects this moat to the larger body of water to the north, or the section directly south of the bridge leading into the castle.
Once unlocked, the Ferryman can be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Ferryman can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "hehhehheh".
## Passive bonuses
The Ferryman starts with +100  Max Health and with a hidden  Acquazzone. It behaves similarly to  The Drowner's attack, and covers the bottom half of the screen below the Ferryman. It flashes white when it strikes and damages all enemies within its area of effect, and will pulse several times with damage based on  Amount. Acquazzone has a base damage of 10 and base  Cooldown of 5 seconds.
The Ferryman possesses Flight.
The Ferryman can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
-  Wings may be useful in the early game to help position the Ferryman over the enemies to let them get wiped out by his Acquazzone.
- Using a combination of Acquazzone and  Dark Rift allows the Ferryman to achieve almost full screen coverage long before any evolution chests are available.
## Trivia
- The Ferryman's spell, "hehhehheh", is a reference to one of his quotes from Castlevania II: Simon's Quest: "SURE, I'LL TAKE YOU TO A GOOD PLACE. HEH!! HEH!! HEH!!"

Triggers Showstopper at critical health.","cost":"25000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeating his demon form with Bonnie Blair or Formina Franklyn in Grelon in Inverse Emerald Diorama.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Final Emperor","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"ed","recovery":"-","might":"+50%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"EME_EXGREATSWORD","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","armor":"-","theme":"A Fierce Engagement - VS version","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"EME_CharacterControllerFinalEmperor","release":"10 April 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Flamberge"}
Final Emperor is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Flamberge.
## Unlocking
Final Emperor can be unlocked by defeating his demon form ( Malevolent Door Spirit (enemy)) with  Bonnie Blair or  Formina Franklyn (either one but not in the solo configuration) in Grelon (North-West world) in Inverse Emerald Diorama.
Alternatively, Final Emperor can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bestoutcome".
## Passive bonuses
Final Emperor starts with +50%  Might and +50%  Greed.
Final Emperor triggers glimmered techs more often than normal. 
He also triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

Or more. hopefully their tiny ears will listen at some point.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","theme name":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Fleaman","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"1","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_FLEAMAN","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","alias":"","theme":"","movespeed":"+100%","controller":"TP_Fleaman_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
The Fleaman is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. He starts with no weapon, but starts with  Game Killer (0).
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Try 3000. Or 6000. Or more. hopefully their tiny ears will listen at some point."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must defeat a total between 3,000 and 9,000 of Fleamen in the Ode to Castlevania stage.  Flea Armors and  Flea Riders count towards this total.
If 3,000 Fleamen have been defeated, a random number is rolled at the end of a run between 0 and 9,000. Fleaman is unlocked if the amount of Fleamen defeated is greater than the random number.
Because the random number cannot exceed 9,000, defeating over 9,000 Fleamen when a run ends guarantees unlocking the Fleaman as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Fleaman can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Fleaman can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "finalboss".
## Passive bonuses
The Fleaman starts with no weapon, only 1  Max Health but +100%  Move Speed. He also starts with an extra Game Killer arcana, which prevents him from leveling up and obtaining weapons or passive items normally. At the start of the game a single  Experience Gem will spawn near Fleaman.
Unlike other Ode to Castlevania characters who can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources, the Fleaman can only find  Wall Chicken.
## Tips
- Playing as Fleaman (either for the challenge or with the intent of unlocking Slogra and Gaibon) is less about making use of his synergies and more about dealing with his weaknesses. With only one health and the inability to level up or obtain any items other than what is on the map, Fleaman is the hardest character to play in the game. However, there are a few strategies that can be used to work around his weaknesses.
-  Stake to Your Heart (0) is pretty much a cheat code for Fleaman, allowing him to make his nonexistent health completely irrelevant and to gain new weapons while simultaneously still enjoying the benefits of Game Killer.
-  Victorian Horror (XVIII) is one of the ways of increasing Fleaman's health.
-  Crystal Cries (XII) is another way of increasing Fleaman's health, as well as scaling his  Growth for the purpose of maximizing Game Killer damage. However, because he dies in one hit, the player must find a way other than Crystal Cries' own effect to freeze enemies. To that end,  Icebrand may be purchased from the Master Librarian. If going for this strategy, consider playing on Inverse in order to additionally acquire  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) from the merchant to maximize the power of Icebrand in the early game.
-  Heir of Fate (XIX) is another possible way of trying to work around Fleaman's health problem. To deal fire damage,  Svarog Statue can be purchased from the Master Librarian.
-  Sword Brothers with Silent Old Sanctuary and an  Empty Tome is one of the highest upside builds available to Fleaman, as it can make him permanently invincible if one pulls it off, rendering his nonexistent health irrelevant. However, this cannot easily be accomplished on any stage that does not have an Empty Tome or a  Arma Dio, and requires some time to set up even if the stage does contain one. This strategy also works best on Inverse. Silent Old Sanctuary is mandatory, and as a second Arcana the player must select either  Mad Groove (VIII) or  Moonlight Bolero (VI).
- The former pulls in all stage items, including Arma Dio, but requires some time to set up as Fleaman can only level up his items using chests, which will require waiting for bosses to spawn or seeking out stage bosses, which may be too dangerous for Fleaman to safely handle.
- The latter provides more chests to level up Fleaman's items, but it means he may have to make the dangerous trek to pick up an Arma Dio himself. This method may be preferable, as there is a chance of obtaining an Arcana chest to also acquire Mad Groove. If this strategy is chosen, picking up black item chests early may be ill-advised, as if Fleaman collects passive items other than Empty Tome, it becomes much more difficult to prioritize leveling up only the Empty Tome and Sword Brothers.

Formina Franklynis a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Saber Machine Gun.
## Unlocking
Formina Franklyn can be unlocked by fully evolving  Kick (into max level  Triangle Kick).
Alternatively, Formina Franklyn can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "futurepresident".
## Skins
Fully evolving  Saber Machine Gun unlocks an alternate skin that uses  Eagle Gun as starting weapon, as well as 'solo' skin for both weapons.
## Passive bonuses
Formina Franklyn starts with +1  Armor and +20%  Move Speed.
Formina Franklyn gains +0.1%  Duration whenever she fires a glimmered tech. This bonus caps at +400% Duration,
Formina Franklyn teams up with  Bonnie as secondary character. Secondary character follows the main character and won't take damage.
She also triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must defeat 100  Frozen Shade enemies in Ode to Castlevania with the  Jewel Gun or  Gemma Torpor. The room where  Joachim Armster is located on the right side of the Crystal Cave is a perfect place to do this, as only Frozen Shades will spawn there. Doing this unlocks the Frozenshade as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Frozenshade can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Frozenshade can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "coldshoulder".
## Passive bonuses
The Frozenshade starts with +2  Armor and +20%  Move Speed.
Overhealing by +32 will trigger a  Sorbetto.
The Frozenshade can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Gains +2% Growth every level.","cost":"666","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.8.0","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Gains Boros","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Find the only one place where flowers bloom in The Bone Zone.","greed":"-","movespeed":"-","theme":"","alias":"Gains","speed":"-","release":"7 June 2022","id":"BOROS","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Heaven Sword"}
Gains Boros is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.8.0. Its starting weapon is the  Heaven Sword.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Find the only one place where flowers bloom in The Bone Zone."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in The Bone Zone and travel 10 units north of their starting location, beyond the  Silver Ring, where they will eventually encounter a ring of green ground with flowers which heals the player by 8 every 0.5 seconds. Staying inside of the ring for 10 seconds causes it to disappear and unlocks Gains Boros as a playable character. However, the player will still be healed.
Once unlocked, Gains Boros can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Gains Boros can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "highfive".
## Passive bonuses
Gains gains +2%  Growth every level. There is no cap on this bonus. 
## Tips
- Gains is one of the most useful characters to use if a player wants to see just how powerful any given weapon can become when leveled to max with Limit Break, as its Growth growth means it will get levels faster and faster as time goes on.
- Having infinitely scaling Growth makes the Wind Whip/Spirit Tornado Tip and Vol Luminatio both powerful weapons for Gains.
- Wicked Season (XIII) can help push Growth even further, and it also increases Curse, which means more enemies to drop experience gems. Because Gains starts with an evolved weapon, it can usually easily survive the early game even after picking Wicked Season as its first Arcana.
- The damage of Game Killer (0) is multiplied by Growth. Picking this arcana in the late game, likely after Gains has acquired a thousand or more levels, will result in insane damage output from the experience gems, especially if paired with Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) to increase the area of each explosion.
- Using the map as a reference (where 1 unit = 1 square of the map), the flower circle to unlock the character is exactly 10 map units north of the merchant directly in the intersection of the map units.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Default Gains sprite.]
 Default Gains sprite.
 - 
 [Unlocks menu icon.]
 Unlocks menu icon.
 - 
 [Old Gains sprite.]
 Old Gains sprite.
## Animated sprites
 - 
 [Movement animation of default Gains.]
 Movement animation of default Gains.
 - 
 [Movement animation of old Gains.]
 Movement animation of old Gains.
## Trivia
- Gains Boros may be a reference to Aerith Gainsborough, a character from Final Fantasy VII, which also has a couple other references in the patch the character was released in.
- To unlock Gains, the player must stand in a place with flowers where everywhere else is in ruin. Similar to the church where Aerith takes care of her flower as a flower girl in the Slums, which is also referenced in the code name for the secret checklist, "SlumsFlowers".
- The update name "The Winged One" may be a reference to "One Winged Angel", the theme of the main antagonist Sephiroth.
- The relic Great Gospel that unlocks Limit Break mechanic is the name of the final limit break ability for Aerith.
- In the update 0.10.0, a list of spells are added. The spell to unlock Gains is "highfive" which may be a reference to the multiple awkward high fives in Final Fantasy VII Remake.
- The spell to unlock Great Gospel is "icanhearthecriesofcaptainplanet" is a reference to the theme "You Can Hear the Cry of the Planet" which plays in the Forgotten City. The code has the "the" replaced with "captain", referencing Captain Planet.
- Given the general tone of the game, it's also possible that the name was chosen due to its resemblance of the vernacular Italian (specifically from the Veneto region) exclamation "ghe sboro", which literally translates to "I cum on that", but is mostly used without vulgar intent to express a vast array of emotions, or just as a filler word.
- Gains Boros is likely also a reference to Ouroboros - a serpent or a dragon eating its own tail, symbolizing eternal cyclic renewal. This apparent in its trait that indefinitely increases its experience gain as it gains levels.
- Prior to version 0.10, Gains Boros' and Leda's sprites were mirrored and their horizontal movement was reversed to account for that. Vento Sacro and its evolution Fuwalafuwaloo would slash to the back instead of the front.
- Gains, similarly to enemy Dragon Shrimp, bears a resemblance to the White Dragon enemies found in many Castlevania titles and Bundles from the Contra series.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Bigger is better.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"A secret so big it won't pass through the Castle's doors.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Galamoth","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"256","revival":"+1","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"+100%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_GALAMOTH","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+2","theme":"Festival Of Servants","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"TP_Galamoth_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Tenebris Tonitrus"}
Galamoth is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Tenebris Tonitrus.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "A secret so big it won't pass through the Castle's doors."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Beast Gate and  Pile of Secrets in Ode to Castlevania, Galamoth will appear in the stage as a boss enemy from his summoning circle located in the center of the Labyrinth. Upon evolving  Dominus Anger,  Dominus Hatred, and  Dominus Agony into the  Power of Sire, and allowing the Power of Sire to defeat Galamoth, Galamoth will be unlocked as a playable character.
It is recommended for the player to use  Megalo Dracula for this unlock, as this character can start with all three "Dominus" weapons, making obtaining the Power of Sire incredibly easy while boasting great stat bonuses.
Once unlocked, Galamoth can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Galamoth can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "sizematters".
## Passive bonuses
Galamoth starts with the following bonuses:
- +156  Max Health
- +2  Armor
- +100%  Might
- +100%  Greed
- +1  Revival
He starts gaining hidden  Lightning Ring weapons as he levels up, similar to  Scorej-Oni.
Galamoth gains an additional hidden  Lightning Ring every 8 levels, up to a max of 6 at level 40, without taking up any weapon slots; the actual weapon can still be obtained from level-up choices, allowing the player to have a total of 7 Lightning Rings. After that, each of the hidden weapons is upgraded after every 12 levels, 7 times, up to level 124, where all rings reach their maximum level at level 8; however, they do not gain Limit Break bonuses. The hidden Lightning Rings also fire whenever the character takes any form of damage.
Galamoth starts out massive, but upon reviving shrinks to a more reasonable size on screen.
Galamoth can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Despite the size of Galamoth's sprite, his actual hitbox is just the same as any normal character's. This also means that, despite his unlock description, he can, somehow, fit through normal doors.
- Galamoth has the exact same passive ability as  Scorej-Oni and therefore can run a similar  Muramasa [DLC] +  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) limited weapons build as that character to spam his passive Lightning Rings at an insane rate. While Galamoth starts with a weapon and therefore gets a slightly worse cooldown reduction than Scorej-Oni (who can run a self-damage weapon and nothing else), his much beefier stats more than make up for it, in addition, his massive Greed paired with his impressive Might lets him achieve a DPS with the Muramasa almost approaching that of all the Lightning Rings combined.

- His description, unlocking spell, and secret hint are references to his size as he is the biggest character in the game. His playable version is also far bigger than his appearance as a boss. This is a reference to his appearance in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, as he is also a massive enemy whose size causes him to appear larger than the screen.
- Him starting with Tenebris Tonitrus is a reference to his wide array of electrical attacks in Symphony of the Night.
- Galmoth is one of the two characters whose height exceeds the top border of their character selection button, with the other being  Peppino.

Gav'Et-Oni ★ is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Mille Bolle Blu.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by killing a total of 6,000 Kappas then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Gav'Et-Oni ★ can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "chevolano".
Finishing a level with Gav'Et-Oni ★ will unlock the next level of Gav'Et-Oni , all the way up to Gav'Et-Oni ★★★★★. The player must complete different levels for each new variant to unlock, as replaying the same map over will not unlock the next stage of the character.
## Passive bonuses
Gav'Et-Oni ★ starts with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
The following table shows how much Amount they gain by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4
As the player completes stages with Gav'Et-Oni ★, they will accumulate Prestige ranks, granting Gav'Et-Oni ★ extra skins that have extra, accumulating stats and bonuses:
- Completing one stage will grant Gav'Et-Oni ★ +1  Amount.
- Completing two stages will grant Gav'Et-Oni ★ +40  Max Health and +20%  Duration.
- Completing three stages will grant Gav'Et-Oni ★ another +40 Max Health and +20% Duration.
- Completing four stages will allow Gav'Et-Oni ★ to start each run with a level 5  Spellbinder.
Note that repeating the same stage will not grant Prestige ranks; the player must compelete four different stages.

## Trivia
- Gav'Et-Oni ★ resembles the Italian word "Gavettoni", which means "Water balloons".
- The spell "chevolano" translated from Italian into: "That fly". It can combine with his weapon name into "Mille bolle blu che volano" ("A thousand blue bubbles that fly) to complete the reference of the song "Le mille bolle blu" by Italian singer Mina.
- Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul and  Horse are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the game at level 80 with 2 levels of  Duplicator, 6 levels of  Academy Badge, and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +10 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, O'Sole, and Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent Amount in the base game at level 60 at a total of +9 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe gains +10 Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.

Gazebo is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.13. Its starting weapon is the  Gaze of Gaea.
## Unlocking
Gazebo can be unlocked by surviving to 20:00 on The Coop.
Once unlocked, Gazebo can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Gazebo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "allegedcannibal".
## Passive bonuses
Gazebo starts with +50%  Move Speed.
Overhealing by +30 provides a stat bonus. After the first time a stat bonus is granted, the bonus will be lessened. Gazebo's sprite will also slightly increase in size for each instance this skill activates.
The stats provided and their caps are: 
| Stat | Initial Bonus | Subsequent Bonus | Max Increase
| Max Health | +40 | +6 | +220
| Recovery | +0.5 | +0.05 | +2
| Armor (stat) | +1 | +0.04 | +2
| Move Speed | +20% | +1% | +50%
| Might | +20% | +1% | +50%
| Speed | +20% | +1% | +50%
| Duration | +20% | +1% | +50%
| Area | +20% | +1% | +50%
| Cooldown | -10% | -0.5% | -25%
| Amount | +1 | +0.04 | +2
| Revival | +1 | +0.08 | +3
| Magnet | +1 | +0.5 | +16
In total, it takes 357 instances of overhealing by +30 Health to max out Gazebo's bonuses.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
III - Hidden Anathema: Eat as much as possible with Gazebo.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Hidden Anathema (III).
## Tips
- Extra sources of  Luck can benefit Gazebo as the player will be more likely to find  Floor Chicken and  Wall Chicken [DLC].
-  Paranormal Scan is extremely important, as in addition to being one of the few ways for Gazebo to consistently overheal by over 30 health, it also provides its own (uncapped) stat bonuses, allowing Gazebo to scale much faster. It also can go over the limit (357), and can keep giving Gazebo stat boosts.
-  Greatest Jubilee spawns light sources, which have a chance of containing Floor Chicken.
- Increasing received healing via  Recovery and Arcanas such as  Sarabande of Healing (VI) will make it easier for the player to overheal enough to make use of Gazebo's passive.
- Note that instances of Overhealing that are multiples of 30, (60, 90, 120, etc.) will NOT grant the player extra stats. Only one stat bonus is given per instance of healing.
- Also, if the player is using  Disco of Gold (XV), sources of  Greed should be taken to increase the healing received from Gold received.

## Other
 -

## Trivia
- Gazebo's original unlock, from v1.13 to v1.14, was evolving the  Gaze of Gaea. After 1.14, Gazebo is now instead unlocked by surviving to 20:00 in The Coop, much like the characters of most stages added after v1.0.
- The name Gazebo could be a portmanteau of its starting weapon, the Gaze of Gaea, and the Chocobo from the Final Fantasy series.
- It may also be a reference to Final Fantasy VII's Chocobo Sound Effect #2 (Woheeho!), which sounds similar to the word "Gazebo" in its cadence/flow.
- It may also be inspired by the famous anecdote of "Eric And The Dread Gazebo" wherein a Dungeons And Dragons player who does not know the word "gazebo" gets puzzled at the dungeon master's mention of one and starts trying to interact with it in various ways. When his attempts to harm the gazebo prove futile, he tries to run away, prompting the dungeon master to declare "It's too late. You have awakened the Gazebo, and it catches you and eats you."
- Using "Gazebo" as a name may be inspired from the Italian musician of the same stage name.
- poncle joked about Gazebo being a banana.

Gekkojin is one of the playable characters in Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  LunarMight [DLC]. They also start with  Pearl magatama [DLC].
## Unlocking
Gekkojin can be unlocked by uniting LunarMight,  LunarSight [DLC], and  LunarBight [DLC] into  LunarFlight [DLC].
Alternatively, Gekkojin can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "? (edit)".
Reaching level 80 as Gekkojin unlocks  Spiritosa as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Gekkojin starts with +50%  Might, +100%  Luck, and +10  Banish. Gekkojin cannot be damaged, but their run ends as soon as the stage Time Limit is reached, even if Endless Mode is turned on.
After taking damage, Gekkojin will heal for all of the damage received within the next one second after one second has elapsed. This damage will always count as overhealing.
Gekkojin has hidden Water Veil, which shoots curving bubbles from bottom of the screen. Bubbles and sprite of a weapon are similar to  Mille Bolle Blu

# Genevieve Gruyère
Not to be confused with Je-Ne-Viv.

Once per life, triggers World Eater after receiving fatal damage.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"With Eleanor, break the Seal of the Banished.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+5","name":"Genevieve Gruyère","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"tf","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"GENEVIEVE","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"-","theme":"The World Eater (track)","alias":"Genevieve","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"CharacterControllerGenevieve","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Shadow Servant"}
Genevieve Gruyère is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Shadow Servant.
## Unlocking
Genevieve can be unlocked by breaking the Seal of the Banished in Abyss Foscari with  SpellStrom while playing as  Eleanor Uziron. She needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Genevieve can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "basicwitch".
## Passive bonuses
Genevieve starts with +20%  Move Speed, +50%  Greed, +50%  Magnet, and +5  Reroll.
Upon levelling up, Genevieve triggers a  Vacuum. Once per life, she triggers World Eater after receiving fatal damage, becoming invincible for 3 seconds, then killing every enemy on screen like the  Rosary, turning them into  Little Hearts to heal her.
## Tips
- Having a built-in vacuum effect allows Genevieve to be more mobile around the map than most other characters.
- Genevieve has an effect that saves her from death once per life, but does not get any built-in revives like  Luminaire. Stacking  Awake (IV) Arcana,  Tiragisú and PowerUp bonus can allow her to eat the world up to seven times this way, essentially giving her the equivalent of 14 lives.
## Sprites
 -

## Other
 - 
 [Genevieve Gruyère as seen in the launch trailer of Tides of the Foscari (with  Luminaire Foscari to the left.)]
 Genevieve Gruyère as seen in the launch trailer of Tides of the Foscari (with  Luminaire Foscari to the left.)
## Trivia
- The spell "basicwitch" might be a reference to the euphemized version of the insult "basic bitch".
- Her name is most likely a reference to the French translation of the sentence "I don't live". This is also the case for  Je-Ne-Viv.
- Gruyère is a type of Swiss cheese, which is usually presented with "holes" inside.
- Both parts of her name together seem to be a reference to how the character is "hollow" or "lifeless" as it can be seen later on as a part of  Je-Ne-Viv's body.

DEFAULTLEGACY
Gennaro Belpaese is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Knife. 
## Unlocking
He is unlocked by default. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Gennaro starts with +20  Max Health and +1  Amount.
## Tips
- Gennaro's innate Amount bonus makes him one of the stronger character early in the game, but this initial boost falls behind in the mid and late game.

## Trivia
- Gennaro's family name Belpaese is derived from the phrase Bel Paese, which is Italian for "beautiful country", and also refers to a semi-soft Italian cheese with the same name.
- Gennaro's name is the Italian version of Latin Januarius, referring to Janus, the two-faced god.

Genya Arikado is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Pocket Knife.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Let Hakuba rebuke the lying Reaper to reveal her allies' truth."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Genya Arikado can be unlocked by defeating  The Trickster as  Mina Hakuba. Outside of special events, the Trickster can be encountered in:
- Boss Rash at the 11:00 mark.
- Carlo Cart at the 2:00 and 14:00 marks.
- To the far right in Inlaid Library with  Inverse Mode enabled, after defeating  The Directer in Eudaimonia M.
Once unlocked, Genya can be purchased for 50,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Genya Arikado can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "odakira".
## Passive bonuses
Barring starting weapons, Genya Arikado is functionally identical to  Alucard, thus he has the same starting stats (+100  Max Health, +2  Armor and +50%  Move Speed), hidden weapons, and immunity to Health Drain.

- Genya Arikado on the Castlevania Wiki

Ignores walls.","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Lifesign Scan","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+100%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Ghost Lino","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"256","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"em","recovery":"-","might":"-100%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"C1_GHOST","flight":"Yes","update":"Adventures + Vampire Survivors: Emergency Meeting DLC on Steam Dec 18th","armor":"-","theme":"No Ghosts Among Us","alias":"Ghost","movespeed":"-","controller":"C1_Ghost","release":"18 December 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Lifesign Scan"}
Ghost Lino is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Lifesign Scan.
## Unlocking
Ghost is unlocked for purchase by evolving the Lifesign Scan. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Ghost can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "foundyellowinmedical".
## Passive bonuses
Ghost starts with +156  Max Health, +100%  Luck, and -100%  Might. Ghost cannot be damaged or deal damage directly, but their run ends as soon as the stage Time Limit is reached, even if Endless Mode is turned on.
Although the Might modifier can be raised on the character select screen through Powerups or Golden Eggs, it will return to -100% in-game and cannot be increased by any means outside of  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX).
## Tips
- Because Ghost has -100% Might, most weapons are completely useless on them, and will deal no damage to enemies. In order to defeat foes, Ghost must rely on sources of damage that are not based on Might.
- Taking the Sarabande of Healing (VI), Divine Bloodline (IX), or Blood Astronomia (XXI) Arcana will allow Ghost to deal damage and level up right off the bat:
- Sarabande of Healing doubles healing and pulses flat damage in a relatively large area equal to healing. Since this is only 1 damage per second with  Recovery Powerups, and can only be increased to 3 with  Pummarola, this Arcana is reliant on  Lifesign Scan for damage, which has likewise low dps until extensively upgraded.
- The immediate cast of  Lifesign Scan after an upgrade can be chained alongside  Pentagram upgrades to help climb up Ghost's otherwise very slow early levels. Ghost's high  Luck can help prevent Pentagram from erasing experience gems and stop it from erasing them completely after reaching weapon level 5, assuming maximum Luck Powerups.
- Sarabande of Healing works with Bloody Tear and Fuwalafuwaloo, which heal on critical hits, but it will not work with weapons that steal hearts on kill such as  Soul Eater, Celestial Dusting,  Silver Wind/Festive Winds and Night Sword/Muramasa, since Ghost will never be able to kill enemies with those weapons.
- Disco of Gold (XV) can massively boost Ghost's damage through Sarabande of Healing. All sources of gold (ground pickups, chests and during Gold Fever) will heal for their respective amount and cause damage via Sarabande of Healing.
- Darkana Crystal Cries (XII) increases health regeneration on killing frozen enemies. Wheter or not the increased regeneration out paces the grow of health of the enemies is another question.
- It is highly recommended to turn on Random Events, as the Pet, Candybox, Rosary, Light Sources and Chicken Bonanza events are all massively helpful for getting through early levels, and none of the events are particularly harmful, with only the Summon II Molise event wasting your time.
- Blood Astronomia gives Ghost's magnet itself (5 x  Amount) flat damage on a 2 second cooldown. Since it is contact-based instead of pulsed, Ghost can use it to mow through weak enemies, allowing for a much faster start than Sarabande. However, besides Duplicator and Clock Lancet, this damage cannot be improved.
- Blood Astronomia makes taking Mini Ghost earlier a better proposition. Although not in the list of affected weapons, Mini Ghost will generate damaging zones identical to those created by Clock Lancet. Unfortunately, while it improves how often Clock Lancet zones are created, Clock Lancet and mini Ghost's zones share a cooldown, and will not damage enemies standing in both twice.
- It is not recommended to use  Garlic, Song of Mana, or  Laurel on Ghost, as Blood Astronomia takes player stats into account for these weapons, causing them to deal zero damage.
- Divine Bloodline causes enemies to take (10 x  Armor) flat damage every time they attempt to hit Ghost, and gives flat damage to its listed weapons equal to (5 x Armor). It also gives Ghost 0.5  Max Health every time they defeat an enemy with retaliatory damage (which does not increase current health), and grants all weapons (Missing Health/10) flat damage.
- Turning  Recovery Powerups off and avoiding  Duplicator will reduce the amount of healing Ghost receives, letting them maintain more of the damage bonus. (Although Duplicator might be optimal in the long run depending on chosen weapons)
- Game Killer (0) is the only other Arcana that can directly do damage despite Ghost's limitations. However, it is useless at level 1 if the maps do not offer experience gems or rosaries to start the chain, and it is not recommended as your only method of defeating enemies.
- Instant-kill weapons such as  Pentagram/ Gorgeous Moon, Ophion, Emergency Meeting, Just Vent, and Sanctuary offer other ways for Ghost to defeat enemies.
- Note that Vicious Hunger and Unjust Ejection are not instant-kill weapons, and some enemies that are vulnerable to other instant-kills will not be killed or damaged by Just Vent.
-  Infinite Corridor in conjunction with any of the aforementioned damaging Arcana allow Ghost to eventually defeat enemies immune to instant kills, including bosses and Atlanteans, which may otherwise endure Ghost's damage until the time limit runs out.
- Due to Ghost's extremely limited options outside of Divine Bloodline, it's worth noting that Celestial Voulge, Festive Winds, and Luminaire can offer utility by changing Orologions into Starry Heavens, lowering defense on hit, and increasing the value of experience gems and gold, respectively. Of these, only Luminaire is situationally recommended, and only in a six-weapon run, as these weapons offer no damage, all require evolving an otherwise useless weapon, and are not very effective compared to instant-kill weapons or  Infinite Corridor.
## Trivia
- Ghost's starting  Max Health before Powerups is 256, which is likely a reference to the highest number that can be stored in a byte: 255 with zero included. Because of this, 256 is sometimes used as a placeholder for numbers above the maximum value, and when used for health, can be a placeholder for invincibility.
## External links
- Ghost at the Among Us Wiki

# Giocare
Giocare is a non-playable character in Vampire Survivors. They are introduced in the 1.14 update alongside Westwoods, the only stage that they appear in.
When the player interacts with Giocare, Giocare will ask the player if they want to pay for 1, 5, or 10 spins in the Spin the Wheel! minigame. Each spin may give a certain item, event, or the chance to unlock a part of Westwoods.
## Offers
| Item | Description | Cost | Notes
| Spin the Wheel! | Allows for the player to spin the wheel once. | 99 | 
| Spin the Wheel 5 times! | Allows for the player to spin the wheel 5 times in a row. | 499 | Costs 4 more than if the player has bought for a single spin.
| Spin the Wheel 10 times! | Allows for the player to spin the wheel 10 times in a row. | 999 | Costs 9 more than if the player has bought for a single spin.
## Trivia
- The word "giocare" is an Italian verb that means "to play."
- Giocare looks identical to  Chula-Reh, except with Chula-Reh's purple and yellow accents being replaced with blue and white accents respectively.
- Despite this,  Secretino Dagsson has an internal name called EX_GIOCARE.
=====

Giovanna GranaGiovanna Grana (Legacy)Gains +1% Projectile Speed every level.
Gains +1% Projectile Speed every level.
DEFAULTLEGACY
Giovanna Grana is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.5.0. Her starting weapon is  Gatti Amari.
## Unlocking
Giovanna is unlocked for free by finding and opening her  Coffin in Inlaid Library. 
Alternatively, Giovanna can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thetwoassassins". This will require the player to purchase her for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Giovanna starts with +20%  Move Speed. She gains +1%  Speed every level; there is no cap on this bonus.
## Tips
- Giovanna has little bonus at the start of the game and her passive ability is outclassed by  Dommario until level 41. The uncapped nature of this bonus means weapons like  Runetracer,  La Borra and  Bone can be extremely effective at high levels.
-  Gatti Amari requires some build-up to become effective. Increasing  Luck allows more light sources to be accessible, increases the chance that a cat using the scratch attack, start a scuffle with another cat, and not hurt Giovanna. More light sources also means  Floor Chicken are dropped more often, which increases Luck multiplicatively and Base damage of the weapon.
- As such, Gatti Amari may not be a viable starting weapon. Focusing on a second weapon is advisable for early-game survival.
- It is not recommended to evolve  Gatti Amari, as  Vicious Hunger prevents enemies it kills from dropping XP gems, which massively slows down the character's leveling rate and prevents Giovanna from making full use of her passive ability.
-  Pale Diamond Incursion (V) is especially useful for Giovana due to both scaling with  Speed and preventing self damage from  Gatti Amari. Unfortunately, it also prevents Gatti Amari from creating cat fight clouds.

## Trivia
- Giovanna Grana's name is likely derived from Giovanni Rana, a famous Italian pasta maker and the founder of the Italian food products brand specialized in pasta named after himself.
- Grana is a type of cheese.
- Giovanna shares several traits with other coffin characters. See Coffin § Traits for more details.
- Her default theme starts off slower at 0.825x speed. Once  Gatti Amari levels up, it speeds up by 0.25 until it reaches max level to the original beat per minute.
- The secondary appearance of Giovanna bears a stark resemblance to Undead Sassy Witch.
- Giovanna is canonically a transgender woman. The Undead Sassy Witch's Bestiary entry states that Giovanna resented being "assigned Mage at birth," a reference to how transgender women are typically assigned male at birth (AMAB).
- Giovanna Grana has an official pin set. The pin set also includes her starting weapon, the Gatti Amari.
- She is one of the four characters to have an official pin or pin set, alongside  Queen Sigma,  O'Sole Meeo, and  Krochi Freetto.
## In other media
- Giovanna Grana makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. She is a reskin of Slate in that game.

Immune to health drain.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"Bring bad luck to the count's belligerent bosses.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Graham Jones","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_GRAHAM","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+1","theme":"Into The Dark Night (Dawn of Sorrow)","alias":"Graham","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Graham_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Vol Umbra"}
Graham Jones is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Vol Umbra.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Bring bad luck to the count's belligerent bosses."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must defeat 22 unique Bosses on the Ode to Castlevania Stage in the same run. This includes bosses spawned directly on the stage and those from certain minutes. Note that these kills can be done in the same run as, or after, unlocking the  Pile of Secrets, but runs prior to this point will not count.
Once unlocked, Graham Jones can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Graham Jones can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "umbraleclipse".
## Passive bonuses
Graham Jones starts with 100  Max Health, +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, +100%  Magnet, and +100%  Greed, and he also starts with an extra  Wicked Season (XIII) Arcana. He is immune to health drain.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Graham is a fantastic user of  Victorian Horror (XVIII) as he has naturally very high Greed and Magnet and naturally scales the former via Wicked Season, on top of that, he is incentivized to pick up the  Attractorb in order to increase the power of his starting weapon, further increasing his Magnet area. This can serve as a pseudo-evolved weapon in the early game to help him survive Wicked Season-boosted hordes.
## External links
- Graham Jones on the Castlevania Wiki

Grant Danasty is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Curved Knife. He is also eligible to pick  Coat of Arms as optional starting weapon.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by evolving the  Dragon Water Whip, then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Grant can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "actuallyquitenice".
Defeating  Slogra and  Gaibon in Ode to Castlevania as Grant unlocks  Sypha Belnades as a playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Grant starts with +60%  Move Speed, and +40%  Luck.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
Taking damage or overhealing by +8 will trigger  Knife,  Curved Knife, and  Pocket Knife, as well as their evolutions  Thousand Edge,  Bwaka Knife,  Claimh Solais, and  Million Cut.

Guardian Pina is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Clear Debris.
## Unlocking
Guardian is unlocked for purchase by defeating 1 enemy with  Ghost Lino. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Guardian can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "foundblueinstorage".
## Passive bonuses
Guardian starts with +0.1  Recovery, +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +1  Revival. Guardian provides +1 Armor and +0.3 Recovery to nearby characters. They can walk through walls.
Guardian has a 50% chance, unaffected by  Luck, to gain +1 Revival when anyone spends a revival, including themselves.
## Tips
- In general, Guardian is a much stronger character in multiplayer due to their chance to gain a Revival when anyone revives and their Recovery and Armor benefits to nearby characters.
- Since Revivals are essentially 50% more efficient on Guardian,  Awake (IV) is a strong choice for them, especially in multiplayer.
-  Phieraggi and  Meal Ticket are both powerful options for Guardian because of their 50% chance to effectively not consume a Revival when they normally would, and their potential for extra Revives in multiplayer.

Ignores Walls.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Forsake victory in favor of sightseeing in Carlo Cart.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Gyoruntin","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"130","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"GYORUNTIN","flight":"Yes","armor":"+3","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","alias":"","theme":"","movespeed":"+80%","controller":"CharacterControllerGyoruntin","release":"30 April 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"NO FUTURE"}
Gyoruntin is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.10. Its starting weapon is  NO FUTURE.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Forsake victory in favour of sightseeing in Carlo Cart."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Carlo Cart and travel opposite to the stage's movement. Normally, the player should move to the left, but if "Visually Invert Stages" and Inverse mode are enabled, the player needs to move to the right instead. Even if this has no visible effect, a  coffin will eventually spawn guarded by six  Tri-Anchors. Defeating the six Tri-Anchors and interacting with the coffin will unlock Gyoruntin as a playable character.
After unlocking, Gyoruntin can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Gyoruntin can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "blunderingfool".
## Passive bonuses
Gyoruntin starts with +30  Max Health, +3  Armor, +80%  Move Speed. It gains +1%  Curse every level. There is no cap on this bonus. 
Gyoruntin's starting weapon NO FUTURE, begins at 5 base damage (instead of the normal 30) and gains +1 base damage every level. There is no cap to this bonus.
Gyoruntin flies over the terrain and thus ignores any walls, obstacles, and terrain gaps.
## Tips
- Gyoruntin is one of the best users of the  Divine Bloodline (IX) arcana in the game. Thanks to its high starting armor, as well as its powerful starting weapon that naturally retaliates, Gyoruntin can quickly start snowballing into an unstoppable juggernaut. Its scaling  Curse is more of a blessing on this build, as it feeds more and more enemies into Gyoruntin's retaliatory attacks, scaling its  Max Health.
- Limited weapon runs using  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) and Limit Breaking  NO FUTURE are highly recommended, due to the level scaling base damage of NO FUTURE.
-  Crown is highly recommended to maximize Gyoruntin's leveling and thus its Curse stat and the power of NO FUTURE.
-  108 Bocce [DLC] is recommended as Gyoruntin's passive Curse gain will allow the damage to increase by up to 11 times.
- Thanks to having high speed and ignoring walls, Gyoruntin can shine on exploration-heavy maps like Lake Foscari or Abyss Foscari.
## Trivia
- Gyoruntin's name is a portmanteau of "Gyorunton" and "tin", a type of metal. This refers to Gyoruntin being a mechanized version of Gyorunton.
- Much like how Gyorunton may be a reference to King Ghidorah from the Godzilla franchise, Gyoruntin is likely a reference to Mecha-King Ghidorah.
- Similar to Gyorunton, Gyoruntin having a head with a happier personality than the other two is a reference to an internet meme of King Ghidorah, in which one of its heads (San, nicknamed Kevin) is more childish and aloof in demeanor.
- The spell used to unlock Gyoruntin, "blunderingfool" is a reference to Steam user "BlunderingFool" who repeatedly asked for the inclusion of "mecha Gyorunton" no less than 13 times since 2022.
=====
