| {"magnet":"-","description":"Gains +1% Curse every level. Can find evolutions in any Treasure Chest.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Survive the Boss Rash with just one weapon.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Gyorunton","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"300","revival":"+2","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"+30%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"DRAGOGION","flight":"No","update":"Patch 0.9.0","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerGyorunton","release":"21 July 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Bracelet"}
Gyorunton is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.9.0. Its starting weapon is the  Bracelet.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Survive the Boss Rash with just one weapon."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Boss Rash and survive to the 15:00 mark while only using one weapon. Passive items are not counted, so the player can use as many as they so choose. Dying or exiting the run after the 15:00 time is reached unlocks Gyorunton as a playable character.
The easiest way to complete this is to unlock the  Mindbender, which allows the player to limit how many weapons they can pick up. Alternatively,  Game Killer (0) can be used to avoid leveling up entirely, so the player can avoid taking more weapons. Playing as an invincible character like  Ghost Lino and  Megalo Menya Moonspell also makes this process trivial.
Once unlocked, Gyorunton can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Gyorunton can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "secondevolution".
## Passive bonuses
Gyorunton starts with +200  Max Health, +30%  Might, and +2  Revival. It gains +1%  Curse every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
Gyorunton can find evolutions in any  Treasure Chest, regardless of any other conditions.
## Tips
- The easiest way of unlocking Gyorunton is to select  Porta Ladonna and  Game Killer (0) as a starting Arcana, leaving her with a  Lightning Ring with a -90%  Cooldown for the entire game. The Lightning Ring can still be evolved into  Thunder Loop if  Mad Groove (VIII) is picked as a second Arcana, as Boss Rash contains a  Duplicator.
- With its unique ability to evolve weapons with any treasure chest, you can evolve weapons much earlier in runs than normal. Combined with its uncapped Curse bonus, which indirectly leads to more levels, Gyorunton can theoretically progress faster than any other character. This makes it useful for shorter stages and Hurry mode.
-  108 Bocce [DLC] is highly recommended as Gyorunton's passive Curse gain will allow the damage to increase by up to 11 times.
- Because Gyorunton has an uncapped Curse bonus but no other scaling stats, it can be difficult to keep up with the ever-increasing power and number of enemies. There are a few ways to try to get around this:
- Use Arcana that scale with level, like  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII), to keep Gyorunton's weapons relevant against bigger and tougher hordes.
- Run weapons and Arcana that scale with Curse, like 108 Bocce,  Nightmare [DLC],  Crissaegrim Tip [DLC],  Ophion [DLC] (its chance to instantly kill is based on Curse, despite the game not telling you this) and  Twilight Requiem (II) (which notably applies to  Tri-Bracelet, evolving from Gyorunton's starting weapon).
-  Wicked Season (XIII), Nightmare, Ophion, Boogaloo of Illusions and ideally  Gemini (I) results in a character that will mow down most non-boss enemies long before they can approach thanks to the boosted Curse making a one-hit kill from Ophion almost a foregone conclusion, however, it comes at the expense of being able to see your surroundings at all due to Ophion + Boogaloo covering the screen in darkness.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] lets Gyorunton scale in power throughout the game.
- Run a  Divine Bloodline (IX) build, as these offer have some of the highest damage outputs and scaling in the game. Gyorunton's somewhat high starting Health has decent synergy with Divine Bloodline, although it lacks armor or a relevant starting weapon.
- Once Gyorunton's build is complete, Game Killer (0) can be used to lock its level, which stops the Curse scaling.
## Trivia
- Gyorunton is the third character to be unlocked by defeating them. The others are  Mask of the Red Death,  Leda, and  Avatar Infernas.
- Gyorunton is a reference to Ashton Anchors and the two-headed dragon merged to his back, called Gyoro and Ururun. All three characters are from the 1999 role-playing game Star Ocean: The Second Story.  Tri-Bracelet, Bracelet's final evolution, is a reference to tri-Ace, the video game developer of the Star Ocean series. Ashton's final ability is also named after the video game developer.
- Gyorunton's design may be inspired by King Ghidorah from the Godzilla franchise, being an armless three-headed dragon. More specifically, by an internet meme in which one of Ghidorah's heads (San, nicknamed Kevin) is more childish and aloof than the other two.
- Gyorunton having two extra Revivals is a reference to the Lernaean Hydra of Greek mythology. The Hydra could only be killed by cutting off all of their heads, and as such, Gyorunton has 3 total lives.
- Gyorunton might also inspired by Gyarados from the Pokémon franchise, both names beginning with “Gy” and sharing the same yellow, blue, and white color schemes. Another Pokémon it may have taken inspiration from is Hydreigon, as both are armless, three-headed dragons with six total wings.

Hammer is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  The RPG.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Survive while wearing a fully armed coat."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must evolve a total of six Ode to Castlevania weapons from the  Coat of Arms in a single run. This means getting any six evolutions from the following:
-  Bwaka Knife
-  Yagyu Shuriken
-  Long Inus
-  Wrecking Ball
-  Stellar Blade
-  Jewel Gun
-  The RPG
-  Meal Ticket
-  Darkness Illusion
-  Carnage Heart
-  Hydro Pump Climax
-  Arch Angle
Dying or exiting the stage after reaching its time limit mark unlocks Hammer as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Hammer can be purchased for 9,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Hammer can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "whatwouldyouspenditon".
## Passive bonuses
Hammer starts with the following bonus:
- +1  Amount
He also gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to 3 times for a maximum of +4 Amount at level 60. Also, Amount is doubled while he is invulnerable.
Hammer can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- A 2-slot limited build with  Sword Brothers [DLC] and  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) alongside  Empty Tome can be used in order to quickly make use of Hammer's passive ability.
- In non-limited weapon builds,  Jetstream [DLC] can be combined with  Wicked Season (XIII) to achieve permanent or near-permanent invulnerability.

# Healer
The Healer is a dark void that will appear due south of the starting location in Moongolow when playing a character with more than 5,000 Golden Eggs after acquiring the Yellow Sign.
It offers the  Flames of Moonspell, which removes up to 100 Golden Egg bonuses at random for free, and can be purchased any amount of times.
Additionally, if a character has more than 25,000 Golden Eggs, the Healer will offer a second item called the  Lantern of Foscari, which removes all movement speed bonuses from Golden Eggs for free.
The counterpart to the Healer is the Eggman. For more information, see his page.
## Trivia
- The Healer's items are a reference to the DLCs Legacy of the Moonspell and Tides of the Foscari.
- The Healer looks like a black silhouette of  The Directer.

Hector is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Mace. He is also eligible to pick  Coat of Arms or  Spectral Sword as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Hector can be unlocked by obtaining 7  Karma Coins on the Ode to Castlevania stage, which are available after obtaining the  Stallion Gate. He is then available to purchase for 500, which scales on other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Hector can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "deckbrush".
## Passive bonuses
Hector starts with +25%  Magnet and +20%  Growth, as well as 20 additional  Rerolls,  Skips and  Banishes. He gains a hidden Familiar every 10 levels until level 40. The maximum number of Familiars gained this way is 4. 
Additionally, he is offered a  Familiar Forge every 7 levels as a 4th level up option up to a max of 16 Familiar Forges, regardless of weapon slot caps. Familiar Forges will stop being offered if the player has no valid weapon or passive weapon level up choices.
Hector can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- His unlocking spell, "deckbrush" is a reference to the weakest spear weapon in Curse of Darkness that serves as a joke weapon.
## External links
- Hector at the Castlevania Wiki

Henry can be unlocked by evolving the  Tyrfing. He is then made available for purchase for 2,000, scaling with other characters purchased.

Horse is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapon is  Hats.
## Unlocking
Horse is unlocked for purchase by defeating a total of 6,000 suspicious looking enemies, which are Pinthot & Coldellini, Space Apparition, and Suspicio. It needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Horse can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "haaayimpasta".
## Passive bonuses
Horse start with +20%  Move Speed. It gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
The following table shows how much Amount they gain by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4

Might receive Coat of Arms as 4th level up option until Level 15.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Cut butter with six swords forged to slay vampires.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Hugh Baldwin","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_HUGH","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"+1","theme":"New Messiah - Vampire Survivors","alias":"Hugh","movespeed":"+40%","controller":"TP_Hugh_Character","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Valmanway"}
Hugh Baldwin is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Valmanway.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Cut butter with six swords forged to slay vampires."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Hugh Baldwin can be unlocked by obtaining 6 "sword" weapons in the Dairy Plant, all six must be present in the weapon slot when that run ends. Only sword weapons from the base game and the Ode to Castlevania DLC qualify. "Sword" weapons include:
-  Victory Sword and its gift,  Sole Solution
-  Heaven Sword (the evolution of  Cross)
-  Tyrfing [DLC] and its evolution,  Rune Sword [DLC]
-  Alucart Sworb [DLC] and its evolutions,  Alucard Swords [DLC] and  Alucard Shield [DLC]
-  Valmanway [DLC] and its union,  Million Cut [DLC]
-  Icebrand [DLC]
-  Sword Brothers [DLC] and its union,  Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
-  Confodere [DLC] and its evolutions,  Vol Confodere [DLC] and  Melio Confodere [DLC]
-  Claimh Solais [DLC] (the evolution of  Pocket Knife [DLC])
As most of the base weapons already count for this secret, adding up to more than six, and half of them can be purchased from the  Master Librarian, the player should avoid picking ones that need to be evolved. While evolving and uniting is discouraged, be careful with uniting Icebrand into  Ninth Circle [DLC], as it does not count as a "sword" unlike other unions of weapons that can be purchased from the Master Librarian.
Once unlocked, Hugh can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Hugh Baldwin can be unlocked in Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "enviousblade".
## Passive bonuses
Hugh Baldwin starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +40%  Move Speed.
He is also adept with  Valmanway [DLC],  Million Cut [DLC],  Tyrfing [DLC], and  Rune Sword [DLC].
Hugh may also get  Coat of Arms [DLC] as a 4th Level Up option multiple times until Lvl 15 (including when he reaches Lvl 15).
## Trivia
- The spell required to unlock him is a nod to his appearance in Castlevania: Circle of the Moon, where he is jealous of  Nathan Graves because Nathan, rather than him, was chosen by his father  Morris Baldwin to inherit the Hunter Whip.
## External links
- Hugh Baldwin on the Castlevania Wiki

Gains 10% Growth every 5 levels (max +50%).","cost":"2000","duration":"+15%","update":"Patch 0.6.1","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+5","name":"Iguana Gallo Valletto","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"+5","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Obtain the Infinite Corridor","greed":"-50%","movespeed":"-","theme":"","alias":"Gallo","speed":"-","release":"23 May 2022","id":"PINO","cooldown":"-15%","weapon":"Clock Lancet"}
Iguana Gallo Valletto is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.6.1. Their starting weapon is the  Clock Lancet.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by obtaining the  Infinite Corridor. They must then be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, they can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "waitreaction".
## Passive bonuses
Gallo starts with with 1 extra level, +15%  Duration, -15%  Cooldown, -50%  Greed, +5  Reroll, and +5  Skip. They gain +10%  Growth every 5 levels up to level 25. The maximum Growth gained this way is +50%.
The following table shows how much  Growth they gain by level:
| Levels | Growth
| 1-4 | 0%
| 5-9 | +10%
| 10-14 | +20%
| 15-19 | +30%
| 20-24 | +40%
| 25+ | +50%
## Tips
- Picking  Out of Bounds (XII) or  Blood Astronomia (XXI) as the starting Arcana allows Clock Lancet to deal damage, negating the necessity to pick a damaging weapon on Gallo's second level.
## Trivia
- Gallo may have created Gallo Tower, the teleporting mirrors, and the Relics there. They are also seen holding the  Empty Tome.
- Gallo and  Divano Thelma are the only two non-secret characters to be left out of the dev log. Despite this, they are not secret characters because they have unlock requirements explained in-game as well as related achievements.
- Gallo,  Christine Davain, and  Divano Thelma are the only characters that start with an extra level.
- They all start with an extra level so that the player can pick up an extra weapon, either because their starting weapon does not deal damage, or in Christine's case, her weapon, the  Pentagram, has a massive cooldown.
- Gallo's design is most likely inspired by Lezard Valeth from the Valkyrie Profile franchise, with his visuals being more specifically inspired by Lezard's outfit from Valkyrie Profile 2: Slimeria.

Imakoo is a secret playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. They do not have a starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "End anguish with an otherworldly melody as the greatest mechanical songstress."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Imakoo can be unlocked by defeating  Living Anguish, the boss in Providence as  Diva No. 5's 'Virginia' alternate skin while the player has  Song of Mana or its evolution,  Mannajja.
Either of these weapons simply need to be present in the player's inventory, but any weapon can get the final blow.
Alternatively, Imakoo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "awoooooooooo".
## Passive bonuses
Imakoo start with +20  Max Health, +10%  Move Speed, +30%  Might, and -40%  Duration.
Imakoo innately knows the "Hell's Fury" and "Blood Rage" techs. 
They also gain between +5% and +50% bonus Might whenever Showstopper is triggered at critical health.
Triggering Showstopper also:
- Grants greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

Imelda Belpaese is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Magic Wand. 
## Unlocking
She is unlocked by default. She needs to be purchased for 10, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Unblinded Imelda Belpaese
Completing the Adventure World of Light and Dark for the first time unlocks an alternative skin named Unblinded Imelda Belpaese.
Alternatively, Unblinded Imelda can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "unblindimelda".
## Passive bonuses
Imelda gains +10%  Growth every 5 levels until level 15. The maximum Growth gained this way is +30%.
The following table shows how much Growth she gains by level:
| Levels | Growth
| 1-4 | 0%
| 5-9 | +10%
| 10-14 | +20%
| 15+ | +30%
## Unblinded Imelda Belpaese
Compared to Imelda, Unblinded Imelda starts with +40%  Speed, +40%  Move Speed, and -10%  Cooldown. She also starts with a level 5  Empty Tome.
## Tips
- As there is no further benefit to gaining levels beyond Level 15, Imelda can very early on make effective use of the  Game Killer (0) Arcana. This is double-effective, as Imelda's passive boost also increases the damage of the Arcana.
- Imelda can make use of  Wind Whip and  Vol Luminatio early on, since they grow stronger with extra Growth.

Impostor Rina is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Sharp Tongue.
## Unlocking
Impostor is unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Report!. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Impostor can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "youaretheimpostor".
## Passive bonuses
Impostor starts with +20  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed, and +20%  Might. 
Imposter enters a boosted state upon defeating a specific amount of enemies. When this bonus is triggered, all enemies are erased from the screen with the effects of the  Rosary; They gain 6 level 1 Sharp Tongue which is only active during the boosted duration. While inside this state, they cannot be damaged, gains +200% Might, -20%  Cooldown, and 80%  Move Speed. This remains active for 30 real seconds (60 in-game seconds if hurry mode is active). 
Impostor's boosted state is triggered upon reaching the following numbers of total enemies defeated:
- 500
- 1,000
- 2,000
- 3,000
- 5,000
- 7,000
- 10,000
- 15,000
After this, the bonus will continue to trigger every multiple of 10,000 enemies defeated (20,000, 30,000, 40,000, 50,000, etc).
The game only checks to see if the player has reached the next number needed when the bonus is not active. For example, say the player was in a bonus triggered by defeating 500 enemies. If they defeated 1,000 total enemies with five seconds remaining in her current bonus, the new bonus would not trigger for five seconds. This guarantees that all bonuses last their full duration.
## Tips
- Having more  Curse and  Charm are helpful for Impostor, as it allows them to enter their stronger form more often due to more enemies being present. Taking powerful weapons that benefit from Curse, like  Ophion [DLC] and  Crissaegrim Tip [DLC], is a good idea so Impostor is able to keep up with the ramped up enemies.  Wicked Season (XIII) is an easy way to get lots of Curse, so it's a strong pick for your first Arcana.

HP-Critical: gains a temporary Armor bonus.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Break the statue of Kujata with 108 Bocce or 108 Responsive Prayers.","secret":"no","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"In' Kujata","movespeed":"-50%","maxhealth":"400","revival":"-","coffin":"no","dlc":"bm","hidden weapon":"Eskizzibur","recovery":"-","might":"+100%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"KUJATA","flight":"no","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","alias":"Kujata","theme":"","skin id":"DEFAULT","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_BMN_Kuyata","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"+45%","weapon":"N/A"}
In' Kujata is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. They start with a hidden  Eskizzibur that creates a combo finisher every time their walking animation loops.
## Unlocking
In' Kujata can be unlocked by breaking the statue of Kujata in the graveyard, normally located in the bottom left, of the Red Moon Manor with  108 Bocce [DLC] or  108 Responsive Prayers [DLC]. Their union,  Road to Heaven [DLC], will not break the statue. It must then be purchasedfor 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, In' Kujata can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "shafted".
## Passive bonuses
In' Kujata starts with +300  Max Health, -50%  Move Speed, +100%  Might, and +45%  Cooldown.
In' Kujata starts the game without a regular weapon, instead, it has a hidden modified Ezkizzibur that cannot attack normally, but can retaliate and periodically executes its ground slam combo finish while In' Kujata is walking. This slam will always occur at the same point in In' Kujata's walk cycle: when it slams the ground. 
- When In'Kujata's Move Speed changes, the speed of its walk cycle, and thus the attack speed of its ground slam, will not change until the next level up.
Every 1 point of  Armor In'Kujata possesses above or below the base of +0 will increase or decrease In'Kujata's size by 10% of its base size. 
In'Kujata's slam has a base damage of 10. Every +1 Armor In'Kujata possesses will increase the base damage of its ground slam by 1 and its area by +10%. This damage is considered retaliatory damage.
When In'Kujata reaches CriticalHP, it will gain +50 Armor for 10 seconds.
## Tips
- Sources of Move Speed such as  Wings and  Rapidus Fio [DLC], or the Darkana  Edge of the Earth (VIII), are highly recommended to increase the attack rate of In'Kujata's hidden weapon.
- The  Trinum Custodem [DLC] is an excellent pick for In' Kujata, providing up to +20 Armor while also providing  Recovery and  Cooldown.
- The  Claimh Solais [DLC] and  Armor passive weapon can each provide up to +5 additional Armor.
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) pairs well with In'Kujata thanks to their consistent source of retaliatory damage, providing consistent Max Health and flat damage.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_ID_2SKIN_TP_ID_3SKIN_TP_ID_4SKIN_TP_ID_5SKIN_TP_ID_6 Svarog Statue Trident Summon Spirit Garlic Fire Wand +  Lightning Ring +  Magic Wand Curved Knife +  Troll Bomb
Innocent Devil contains a group of secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors., similar to  Familiar. 
Each of them has a different starting weapon, and can be summoned by the  Familiar Forge as secondary characters.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Entrust their care to the gentlest soul, twice."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run while playing as  Julia Laforeze and obtain two  Familiar Forges in a single run. It can be done on Ode to Castlevania by picking a Familiar Forge from the  Candybox, and then picking the one as a stage item. It will not show up in the Candybox if the player had picked up the stage item first. Doing this unlocks Innocent Devil as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Innocent Devil can be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Innocent Devil can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "forgemasterfriends".
## Passive bonuses
All Innocent Devils start with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels (up to +3).
All Innocent Devils can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## List of Innocent Devils
| Name | Starting Weapon
| Ukoback | Svarog Statue
| Imp | Trident
| Alleged Ghost | Summon Spirit
| Bitterfly | Garlic
| Wood Rod | Fire Wand, Lightning Ring, Magic Wand
| Pumpkin | Curved Knife, Troll Bomb

Isaac is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Trident.
After unlocking the  Spectral Sword, or  Ebony Diabologue, Isaac is able to start with either weapon as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Isaac can be unlocked by evolving the  Mace after obtaining the  Black Disk. He is then made available for purchase for 2,000, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Isaac can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "guiltydevil".
## Passive bonuses
Isaac starts with +20%  Speed, +20%  Area, +25%  Magnet, and +20  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips
Isaac gains a random Familiar as a follower every 10 levels. This effect caps at 6 followers at level 60. This effect is the exact same as Hector's effect, except that Hector caps out at 4
Isaac can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Jaman Jato ★ is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  108 Responsive Prayers.
## Unlocking
Jaman Jato ★ can be unlocked by defeating a total of 6,000  Jaman Jatos.
Alternatively, Jaman Jato ★, alongside  108 Responsive Prayers, can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "percheho".
Finishing a level with Jaman Jato ★ will unlock the next level of Jaman Jato, all the way up to Jaman Jato ★★★★★. The player must complete different levels for each new variant to unlock, as replaying the same map over will not unlock the next stage of the character.
An unlimited spawn of Jaman Jato enemies is located in the northern edge of the labyrinth above the northmost  Pummarola. Specifically, the eastern end of the corridor on the northern edge, leading towards the  Little Clover.
## Passive bonuses
Jaman Jato ★ starts with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
The following table shows how much Amount they gain by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4
As the player completes stages with Jaman Jato ★, they will accumulate Prestige ranks, granting Jaman Jato ★ extra skins that have extra, accumulating stats and bonuses:
- Completing one stage will grant Jaman Jato ★ +1  Amount.
- Completing two stages will grant Jaman Jato ★ +80  Max Health and +40%  Duration.
- Completing three stages will grant Jaman Jato ★ another +40 Max Health and +20% Duration and an additional +1 amount.
- Completing four stages will allow Jaman Jato ★ to start each run with a level 2  Duplicator.
Note that repeating the same stage will not grant Prestige ranks; the player must compelete four different stages.
## Tips
- The length of a stage does not matter for obtaining Prestige ranks. Therefore, to obtain the maximum amount of Prestige ranks as quickly as possible, it is recommended to focus on short stages like Moongolow, Il Molise and Carlo Cart.
- Eudaimonia Machine cannot unlock Prestige ranks.
- Jaman Jatos spawn infinitely on the west side of the map. By taking the right entrance on the bottom, stay to the right and go up into the vertical hall that is a dead end. There are cage decorations on the floor.

| {"magnet":"+50%","description":"It will literally eat the world.","cost":"300000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeating 100000 enemies in a single run with Genevieve Gruyère.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+7","name":"Je-Ne-Viv","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"250","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"tf","recovery":"-","might":"+50%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"MEGAGENEVIEVE","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"+5","theme":"The World Eater (track)","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerMegaloGenevieve","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Shadow Servant"}
Je-Ne-Viv is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapon is the  Shadow Servant.
## Unlocking
Je-Ne-Viv can be unlocked by defeating 100,000 enemies in a single run with  Genevieve Gruyère.
After unlocking, Je-Ne-Viv can be purchased for 300,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Je-Ne-Viv can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "theworldeater".
## Passive bonuses
Je-Ne-Viv starts with +150  Max Health, +5  Armor, +50%  Might, +50%  Greed, +50%  Magnet, and +7  Reroll. It gains +1%  Might and +0.5%  Curse every level. There is no cap on these bonuses. 
It starts with  Insatiable without taking up any weapon slots.
Upon levelling up, Je-Ne-Viv triggers a  Vacuum. Once per life, it triggers World Eater after receiving fatal damage, becoming invincible for 2 seconds, then killing every enemy on screen like the  Rosary, and turning them into  Little Hearts. Unlike Genevieve, its World Eater can eat through wall tiles, letting the player walk out of bounds, the range being the ground tiles that turn black and smoky when being eaten. The range of this effect is based on the size of Insatiable, which is affected by Magnet. It also triggers World Eater every 6th level, which also physically increases the size of Je-Ne-Viv.
Upon reaching level 10, 20, and 30,  SpellString,  SpellStream, and  SpellStrike will spawn underneath it respectively.
## Tips
- Having a built-in vacuum effect allows Je-Ne-Viv to be more mobile around the map than most other characters.
- Je-Ne-Viv has an effect that saves it from death once per life but ironically does not get any built-in revives like  Luminaire Foscari. Stacking  Awake (IV),  Tirajisú, and the  Revival PowerUp bonus can allow it to eat the world up to seven times this way, essentially giving it the equivalent of 14 lives.
- The  Crown can help Je-Ne-Viv trigger its Vacuum effect more often.  Luminaire is a recommended weapon for the same reason.
- If you wait until all of Je-Ne-Viv's weapon slots have been filled before taking the SpellString, SpellStream and SpellStrike it spawns, Je-Ne-Viv can have 9 weapons (7 once they fuse into  SpellStrom) instead of the normal 6.
- Wicked Season (XIII) helps scale Je-Ne-Viv's Greed, and with it the damage of her hidden Insatiable aura, as well as its Growth for triggering World Eater more often.
- Je-Ne-Viv has a very strange relationship with  Attractorb. It's an item Je-Ne-Viv should absolutely not need, as it triggers a Vacuum every level-up, but, until other ways of increasing Magnet are implemented, it is the only way (other than eggs) of increasing the range of Je-Ne-Viv's hidden Insatiable and the area of her map-destroying effect upon triggering World Eater. This is not necessarily worth dedicating a whole item to on your average run, but it may be worth picking if Je-Ne-Viv is building Hail from the Future (X), or on maps like Ode to Castlevania, which has plentiful Arma Dio and where Je-Ne-Viv's World Eater can be extremely useful for opening up shortcuts that most other characters may not have access to.

## Other

 Je-Ne-Viv as seen in the launch trailer of Tides of the Foscari.
## Trivia
- "Je-Ne-Viv" name is most likely a reference to the badly made French translation of the sentence "I don't live". This is also the case for  Genevieve Gruyère.
- Je-Ne-Viv might also be a reference to Jenova from Final Fantasy VII, as they both share similar names and share a similar nature of consuming all life from the worlds they inhabit.
-  Eleanor Uziron can be seen in Je-Ne-Viv's sprite, suggesting that she was absorbed into it during the fight in Abyss Foscari. This also explains why it gains the ability to spawn the Spell weapons as it levels up.
- Furthermore, Je-Ne-Viv's Reroll bonus is +7, the sum of Genevieve's +5 bonus and Eleanor's +2 bonus.

Immune to health drain.","cost":"10000","duration":"-","unlock":"Eat the souls of 10,000[sic] Forest frogs.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Jiangshi","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"47","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_JIANGSHI","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"Followers of Darkness - The First","alias":"","movespeed":"+10%","controller":"TP_Jiangshi_Character","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Soul Steal"}
Jiangshi is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Soul Steal.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Eat the souls of 10,000[sic] Forest frogs."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must defeat 5,000 enemies with  Dark Frogamorphosis (despite the unlock condition displaying as 10,000), the union of  Anura [DLC] and  Soul Eater, in a single run of Mad Forest. 
- Note that only the central tongue attack that insta-kills enemies is tracked as "EnemiesEatenThisRun". The frogs summoned and the aura around the player from  Anura [DLC] and  Soul Eater, respectively, do not count.
Prioritizing this weapon is advised. Taking  Arcana such as  Gemini (I) and  Sapphire Mist (I) is recommended, and few other weapons should be taken unless absolutely necessary. Lower  Might can help avoid other weapons "stealing" the kills needed.
Characters such as  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC],  Young Maria Renard [DLC], and those from Emerald Diorama with a source of healing like  Crystal Cries (XII) can make survival a breeze. While not applicable to every character, like those aforementioned,  Endless Mode is heavily recommended, as it allows the player to ensure they get all 5000 required kills.
Alternatively, Jiangshi can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "hophophophophophophophophophophophophop".
## Passive bonuses
Jiangshi starts with -53  Max Health, +1  Recovery, +10%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed.
Jiangshi will trigger a random equipped weapon every 13 "hops" while the player is moving. This equates to 65 frames of its walking cycle.
Jiangshi is also immune to Health Drain.
## Tips
- Jiangshi's ability is very similar to that of  Babi-Onna, so most of the tips that apply to that character also apply to Jiangshi. However, there are a few important differences between the two.
- Unlike Babi-Onna, Jiangshi can also fire weapons normally. This means that, unlike with Babi-Onna, the  Empty Tome is not useless. It also starts with a much more impactful weapon to negate the cooldown of.
- As a tradeoff, Jiangshi must move a much longer distance compared to Babi-Onna in order to get its trigger. Just like with Babi-Onna,  Wings remains useful for maximizing the power of Jiangshi's passive.
- It is harder to build Jiangshi around  Paranormal Scan, as the slower trigger rate for its ability makes the opportunity cost much higher.
- Due to Jiangshi's frailty, it is recommended to run defensive items like  Armor and  Parm Aegis in order to maximize its survivability and give it a better chance of surviving hordes until it can set off a Soul Steal.
- While one weapon runs built around Soul Steal are certainly feasible, it may be a good idea to add  Troll Bomb or  Sanctuary for dealing with bosses. Without these weapons, Jiangshi may struggle to kill certain bosses that are too tough to kill with Soul Steal alone, like the  Levarsee Darkassos spawned by  Moonlight Bolero (VI).
- Jiangshi's high Greed stat allows them to make great use of  Victorian Horror (XVIII).
## Trivia
- The spell that unlocks Jiangshi being 13 hops alludes to its ability to trigger its weapons with the same number of hops.
## External links
- Jiang Shi on the Castlevania Wiki

Jimbo is a playable character from the Ante Chamber DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Infernolatro.
## Unlocking
Jimbo can be unlocked by entering the Ante Chamber stage, locating the coffin there, and opening it. It is found several tilesets to the north-east of spawn.
Alternatively, Jimbo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "forkliftcertified".
## Passive bonuses
Jimbo starts with +20  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed,  Duration, and  Luck, and +7  Reroll.
Jimbo starts each run with a draft of  Survarot, and is able to gain more from Light Sources.
- The chance to get a Survarot from a Light Source decreases as Jimbo gets more Survarots.
Also, Jimbo has a permanent Survarot Multiplier of 4, meaning Survarot cards that appear as him will gain 4 more levels than normal.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Character sprite]
 Character sprite
 - 
 [Movement animation]
 Movement animation
## Screenshots
 - 
 [Jimbo appearing in the cover art of the Ante Chamber DLC.]
 Jimbo appearing in the cover art of the Ante Chamber DLC.
 - 
 [Jimbo as seen in the Ante Chamber trailer, particularly before  Poe Ratcho walks in.]
 Jimbo as seen in the Ante Chamber trailer, particularly before  Poe Ratcho walks in.

## Trivia
- The spell "forkliftcertified" is a reference to a Reddit comment by Balatro's developer, LocalThunk, where he gave out more lore of Jimbo, particularly about him being "forklift certified."
- Jimbo's Survarot mulitplier of 4 is a reference to the standard Joker, used to represent Jimbo in Balatro proper, which gives an unconditional +4 Mult.
- Despite the video "chicot seduto su una sedia - sopravvissuti ai vampiri - Vampire Survivors" showcasing  Chicot in the title and the thumbnail, the video features Jimbo instead.
- He starts with 300 gold making him good for early game gold grinds by starting runs with him fast. If done fast enough can make good early game money.
- Prior to v1.15, either using this character, entering the Ante Chamber stage, using  Secretino Dagsson or any of the other Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.

Joachim Armster is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Rune Sword.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Rewrite the fate of the damsel in distress."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Serpent Gate in Ode to Castlevania, Joachim Armster will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, located in the right room of the Crystal Cave. Defeating him as  Sara Trantoul unlocks Joachim Armster as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Joachim Armster can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Joachim Armster can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "cantholdthemall".
## Passive bonus
Joachim starts with +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed. Immune to health drain. 
Can choose a per-level bonus: 1%  Might,  Area,  Speed,  Duration,  Recovery (0.01),  Growth,  Luck,  Greed,  Curse, or  Move Speed.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

John Morris is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Iron Ball. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star or  Coat of Arms as optional starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
John can be unlocked by evolving the  Raging Fire ,  Ice Fang,  Gale Force, and  Rock Riot across any number of runs. He can then be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, John Morris can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "beefbbq".
## Passive bonuses
John starts with +30  Max Health, and +20%  Might.
Upon reaching level 10, John will be granted  Iron Blue Will (VII).
Every five levels, a  Mirror of Truth will be spawned above him.
John can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- His main weapon being  Iron Ball is a reference to his son,  Jonathan Morris, being able to use them in Portrait of Ruin.
- John's pose in the selection menu changes when altering his starting weapon.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_MORNINGSTARSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_COATOFARMSSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_SPECTRALSWORD Javelin Morning Star Coat of Arms Spectral Sword
Jonathan & Charlotte are secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors.  Jonathan Morris [DLC] uses the Javelin as his starting weapon, but he also has the option of starting with  Morning Star [DLC],  Coat of Arms [DLC], or  Spectral Sword [DLC], provided they have been unlocked.  Charlotte Aulin [DLC] uses  Raging Fire [DLC] as her starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Spell what they shout"
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Jonathan & Charlotte can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "jonathancharlotte".
Inputting the spell in the iOS version will unlock Charlotte & Jonathan instead of Jonathan & Charlotte.
They can also be unlocked by unlocking  Stella and  Loretta Lecarde.
## Passive bonuses
As a duo, Jonathan takes the lead followed by a computer controlled Charlotte. Both characters can Level up and select their own Weapons and Passive Items, similar to when playing in multiplayer. 
Both Jonathan and Charlotte retain the passive bonuses from their individual characters; Jonathan Morris and Charlotte Aulin.
Jonathan Morris starts with +30  Max Health and +20%  Might.
He has a Charge ability that charges when he is standing still. When fully charged, it triggers all weapons he has equipped from  Coat of Arms [DLC] once. The charge time is 15 seconds by default, which is affected by  Speed.
Charlotte starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Duration, +20%  Area, +10  Reroll, +10  Skip, and +10  Banish.
She has a Charge ability that charges when she is standing still. When fully charged, it triggers all weapons she has equipped from  Belnades' Spellbook [DLC] once. The charge time is 15 seconds by default, which is affected by  Growth.
Jonathan & Charlotte can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
While Jonathan's stats, weapons, and passive items can be seen anytime by using the pause screen, the player can only see Charlotte's stats and items via the level up screen when selecting an item for Charlotte.
## Trivia
- Jonathan & Charlotte's description on the Character Selection screen is a reference to their appearance as duo protagonists in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin. In Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin the player can freely switch between Jonathan and Charlotte during gameplay, when doing so the pair calls out each others names. This is seen frequently in speed runs of the game as switching constantly is faster than walking normally.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_MORNINGSTARSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_COATOFARMSSKIN_TP_JONHATHAN_SPECTRALSWORD Javelin Morning Star Coat of Arms Spectral Sword
Jonathan Morris is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Javelin [DLC]. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star [DLC],  Coat of Arms or  Spectral Sword [DLC] as his starting weapon via  skin selection after unlocking each selector.
## Unlocking
Jonathan Morris can be unlocked by evolving the  Hand Grenade [DLC]. He can then be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Jonathan can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "morissson".
## Passive bonuses
Jonathan Morris starts with +30  Max Health and +20%  Might.
He has a Charge ability that charges by standing still. Upon reaching max charge, all equipped  Coat of Arms weapons are triggered once. This charge time is 15 seconds by default, but can be decreased with  Speed down to a minimum of 3 seconds at +400% Speed.
Jonathan can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Jonathan's charge ability is affected by Speed, so  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) is his key Arcana.  Wicked Season (XIII), which massively helps leveling up, is a good complement.
-  Troll Bomb [DLC] is part of the Coat of Arms, and therefore a must-pick for Jonathan as he can trigger it much more often than most characters. Since Troll Bomb on its own is so powerful, there is no need to carry other Coat of Arms weapons to make full use of Jonathan's ability. It is a good idea to increase the  Amount of Troll Bomb in order to make it last as long as possible and thus minimize the time between attacks. The union  Carnage Heart is not recommended, as it loses out on the massive damage of Troll Bomb.
## Trivia
- His unlocking spell is a nod to him being the son of John Morris.
## External links
- Jonathan Morris at the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_JULIUS_MORNINGSTAR Vanitas Whip Morning Star
Julius Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Vanitas Whip. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star as starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Julius can be unlocked by finding and opening the second coffin in Ode to Castlevania, located in a hidden room inside the library. 
Alternatively, Julius can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "omniavanitas".
Defeating  Gergoth, located at the downstairs of middle building below the library, just above the  Capra Gate, as Julius will unlock  Soma Cruz as a playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Julius starts with +80  Max Health, +40%  Might, +1  Revival, and +10  Banish. He also starts with  Heart of Fire (XIX).
His Might increases depending on the missing proportion of his Max Health, with the formula Might*(1+((1−CurrentHealth/MaxHealth))*MaxHealth/180). Thus, missing health will increase Julius's Might more depending on his Max Health.
Julius can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- To find Julius' coffin go to the end of the library where there is a red crack in the western wall.
- Selecting the Arcana Divine Bloodline (IX) pairs exceptionally well with Julius. The explosions triggered by Heart of Fire are considered retaliatory damage, which boosts his max health. Additionally, his passive ability to gain increased might as his health decreases synergizes perfectly with the effects of Divine Bloodline. Moreover, his starting weapon, the Vanitas Whip, scales with max health, which Divine Bloodline enhances, further amplifying its effectiveness.
## Trivia
- His unlocking spell "omniavanitas" refers to one of the techniques that Julius uses in his home series.
- The cross summoned by the Aurablaster Tip and Aura Blast weapons is the version of Grand Cross used by Julius in Aria of Sorrow and Dawn of Sorrow.
- Him and Leon being the only unlockable characters through opening coffins before unlocking Dracula is a reference to them being the first and last members of the Belmont Clan.
## External links
- Julius Belmont at the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_JUSTE_MORNINGSTAR Vibhuti Whip Morning Star
Juste Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Vibhuti Whip. He is also eligible to pick   Morning Star as a starting weapon, if it has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
Juste can be unlocked by evolving the  Wind Whip [DLC]. Once unlocked, he can be purchased for 2,200, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Juste can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "fatefulfusion".
Completing any one stage with both Juste and  Shanoa unlocks  Maria Renard as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Juste starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Might, +20%  Duration, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip, and +5  Banish.
Until level 7 he will be offered the  Belnades' Spellbook as a fourth level up choice, regardless if the player has Banished or  Sealed it, or if the player is at the maximum number of weapons.
Every six levels, Juste will level up equipped featured in the Belnades' Spellbook, except for  Summon Spirit Tornado and  Anura.
Juste can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
Juste will receive the Belnades' Spellbook as a level-up option every time until he reaches level 7, allowing him to pick any previously used spell. Because of this, the Gemini Arcana is a great choice, as it enhances every spell. Additionally, every 6 levels Juste will upgrade every spellbook held by 1 level (excluding Summon Spirit Tornado, Anura and any books already at their maximum level), making it easier to power up this type of weapon. This strategy allows him to maximize the effectiveness of his spells, with Gemini boosting their impact and upgrades enhancing their power over time.
## Trivia
- When using  La Robba, Juste will spawn the collectable furniture from Castlevania: Harmony of Dissonance instead of the regular ones, in this game you can find some objects of furniture that will decorate a special room in Dracula's Castle.
- The spell related bonuses are due to Juste being magically talented due to him being also descendant of the Belnades and the Spell Books that he can find in his home game.
- The spell "fatefulfusion" is a reference to the fusion mechanic of his home game where he gains special attacks by combining Sub-Weapons with spell books.

Receives Academy Badge at Level 30.","cost":"3000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolving the Eskizzibur.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+10%","amount":"-","speed":"+20%","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Keitha Muort","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"+10%","dlc":"tf","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"KEIRA","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"-","theme":"Hide and Steal","alias":"Keitha","movespeed":"+40%","controller":"CharacterControllerKeira","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"Flash Arrow"}
Keitha Muort is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Flash Arrow.
## Unlocking
Keitha is unlocked by evolving  Eskizzibur. She needs to be purchased for 3,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Keitha can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "oopsistoleoneagain".
## Passive bonuses
Keitha starts with +40%  Move Speed, +20%  Speed, -10%  Cooldown, +10%  Luck, and +10%  Curse. She gains +1%  Luck every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
She has a charge ability that charges up when she moves. When fully charged, stopping will cause Keitha to fire a volley of 7 Flash Arrow projectiles. This is not affected by  Amount. The charge time is 10 seconds by default, which is affected by  Luck.
Upon reaching level 30, an  Academy Badge will spawn underneath her.
## Tips
-  108 Bocce is highly recommended as Keitha's passive Luck gain will allow the damage to increase by up to 11 times.
- Getting either Vicious Hunger or Disco of Gold (XV) is useful to maximize the power of Millionaire.
- Slash (XVI) scales with Luck and affects both Flash Arrow and Millionaire. This makes Keitha a fantastic pick for Slash builds. Also consider Iron Blue Will (VII), which affects several Slash weapons, including Flash Arrow and Millionaire.
- Santa Javelin, in particular, also scales its damage with luck, independently from Slash. This makes the two an excellent combination with Keitha.
- Keitha can also run an efficient freezing build with Jail of Crystal (XIV) and Out of Bounds (XII) due to her infinitely scaling Luck affecting the chance of freezing enemies.
- Photonstorm is a very strong weapon on her, as it scales with both Amount (which Keitha gets from the Academy Badge) and Luck.

Triggers Keremet Morbus when overhealing by +8.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"It takes exactly two to empty the cauldron.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","theme name":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Keremet","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"2.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_KEREMET","flight":"No","armor":"+20","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","alias":"","theme":"","movespeed":"-100%","controller":"TP_Keremet_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Keremet Morbus"}
Keremet is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Keremet Morbus.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "It takes exactly two to empty the cauldron."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Black Disk in Ode to Castlevania,  Keremet will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, where his summoning circle is located at the top floor of the Laboratory (the top part of the front building). Upon defeating him while playing as  Jonathan & Charlotte or  Charlotte & Jonathan, Keremet is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Keremet can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Keremet can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "cauldronking".
## Passive bonuses
Keremet starts with the following bonuses:
- +2.0  Recovery
- +20  Armor
- -100%  Move Speed
Keremet gains +5% Move Speed, +5% Might, and +5% Curse every 10 levels up until level 40. In addition, they gain +1 Armor every 20 levels starting at level 30 until they hit the +50 Amor stat cap. The armor cap is reached at level 610 without Armor PowerUp, level 550 with Armor PowerUp, or sooner with other sources of Armor.
| Level | Move Speed | Might | Curse | Armor
| 1-9 | -100% | 0% | 0% | +20
| 10-19 | -95% | +5% | +5% | +20
| 20-29 | -90% | +10% | +10% | +20
| 30-39 | -85% | +15% | +15% | +21
| 40-49 | -80% | +20% | +20% | +22
| 50+ | +1 Armor every 20 levels until +50 Armor
Overhealing by +8 or more retriggers Keremet Morbus.
He also starts with an extra  Lost & Found Painting (XVII) Arcana.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Like  Peppino, Keremet is a character that is normally entirely stationary, although any Speed powerups, including the store-bought ones, will allow him to slowly crawl along the ground.  Wings and  Sonic Dash /  Rapidus Fio come highly recommended to allow Keremet to move at a decent speed, especially since, unlike Peppino, Keremet has no Magnet bonus to help pick up experience gems. The  Attractorb is important for the same reason. Alternatively,  Edge of the Earth (VIII) can be used to lock Keremet's movement at +100%.
- Keremet has incredibly high Armor, the highest starting amount in the game. This makes him an extremely powerful user of weapons that deal retaliation damage based on Armor, like  Legionnaire [DLC] or  Wrecking Ball.  Divine Bloodline (IX) can be powerful for the same reason, though it may be difficult for Keremet to pursue enemies in the early game to collide with them due to his slow movement.
- Thanks to his extra Arcana, Keremet benefits greatly from weapons that scale well based on  Duration, including powerful options like  Godai Shuffle [DLC] and  Seraphic Cry.
## External links
- Keremet on the Castlevania Wiki

Kina is a secret playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. They do not have a starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Defy the call to adventure in Emerald Diorama."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Kina can be unlocked by ending a run in Emerald Diorama (stage) after starting in The Junction, and never using any of the portals. This can be achieved quickly by leaving the stage, but dying will also unlock the character.
Alternatively, Kina can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bugandafeature".
## Passive bonuses
Kina start with +10  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed, and +20%  Speed.
Kina innately knows the "Swallow Slice" and "Airwave" techs. They also gain between +0.10 and +1.00 bonus  Recovery whenever Showstopper is triggered at critical health.
Triggering Showstopper also:
- Grants greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

Krochi Freetto is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.11. His starting weapon is  Cross.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by defeating a total of 100,000 enemies total across all runs. He must then be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Krochi can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "accidenti".
## Passive bonuses
Krochi starts with +30%  Move Speed and +1  Revival. He gains +1 Revival at level 33.
The following table shows how many Revives he gains by level:
| Level | Revives
| 1-32 | +1
| 33+ | +2

## Trivia
- Krochi Freetto comes from a portmanteau in Italian "crocifritto", combining "crocifisso" meaning "crucified", and "fritto" meaning "fried". It could therefore be ported over as "crucifried". This explains his weapon and red appearance.
- Krochi receiving a second Revival at level 33 may be a reference to Jesus' age at death and his subsequent resurrection, which is widely believed to be around 33–39 years old.
- Krochi Freetto has an official pin set. The pin set also includes his starting weapon, the  Cross.
- He is one of the four characters to have an official pin or pin set, alongside  Queen Sigma,  O'Sole Meeo, and  Giovanna Grana.

Kugutsu is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their optional starting weapons include the  Twin Dragon,  Khukuri,  Eagle Gun, or  Punch.
## Unlocking
Kugutsu can be unlocked by defeating Divine Wood Spirit in Inverse Emerald Diorama. The Divine Wood Spirit is located in the northern area, Miyako City, which is south when the "Visually Invert Stages" option is enabled.
Alternatively, Kugutsu can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "midosmagicdolls".
## Passive bonuses
Kugutsu starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +30%  Move Speed.
Kugutsu gains 1  Revival and 5%  Growth every time they glimmer a new tech.
They also trigger Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
## Tips
- Due to their potentially massive amount of Revivals eclipsing that of any other character (even  Luminaire and  Reborn She-Moon) given a tech-heavy build, they are, as of currently, the strongest user of  Meal Ticket [DLC] and  Phieraggi in the game.

Lama LadonnaLama Ladonna (Legacy)Gains +5% Might, Move Speed, and Curse every 10 levels (max +20%).
Gains +5% Might, Move Speed, and Curse every 10 levels (max +20%).
DEFAULTLEGACY
Lama Ladonna is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.13. Her starting weapon is  Axe.
## Unlocking
She is unlocked upon surviving 20 minutes with at least 10%  Curse active. She needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
The actual condition for the achievement is to have any amount of Curse higher than the base value by the end of the run and in the same run survive at least 20 minutes. This means unlocking Lama can be also be achieved - besides the use of the Curse PowerUp - by obtaining Curse from  Golden Eggs or passive items that grant Curse, such as  Skull O'Maniac,  Gold Ring (level 2+),  Metaglio Right (level 2+) or  Torrona's Box (level 9), either through level ups or picking them up as stage items. Due to the loose criteria, the player does not need to have the item for the full 20 minute duration, but only by the conclusion of a 20+ minute run.
Alternatively, Lama can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "superladonna".
## Passive bonuses
Lama starts with +10  Max Health, +10%  Might, +10%  Move Speed, and +10%  Curse. She gains +5% Might, +5% MoveSpeed and +5% Curse every 10 levels until level 40. The maximum stats gained this way is +20%, and +30% in total.
The following table shows how much Might, MoveSpeed and Curse she gains by level:
| Level | Might | MoveSpeed | Curse
| 1-9 | +10% | +10% | +10%
| 10-19 | +15% | +15% | +15%
| 20-29 | +20% | +20% | +20%
| 30-39 | +25% | +25% | +25%
| 40+ | +30% | +30% | +30%
## Sprites
 - 
 [Default Lama sprite.]
 Default Lama sprite.

 Movement animation of default Lama.

 Movement animation of legacy Lama.
## Other
 - 
 [Lama Ladonna as seen in the pre-release trailer of Operation Guns.]
 Lama Ladonna as seen in the pre-release trailer of Operation Guns.
## Trivia
- Lama's complete name is Lama Ladonna, which is likely derived from "La Madonna", an exclamation of surprise in Italian. It's also possible that its pronunciation, which sound like "la maladonna" (literally "the bad woman") is meant to reference the Neapolitan expression "la malafemmena", which means "the hussy", and is mentioned in a famous song and movie by the Italian comedian Totò.
- "Lama" also translates to "blade" in Italian, this might be a reference to a "Blade, the Vampire Slayer", who also is a dhampir, just like Arca, Porta and Lama herself.
- Her looks along with her weapon choice, specifically the fact Lama is heavy armor clad and her hairstyle choice is ponytail, (even more-so with Legacy version of her sprite, with red hair and even more distinctive armor) resemble that of Old Axe Armor girl artwork from the official Konami website, which very might be Lama's main design inspiration.
## In other media
- Lama Ladonna makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. She is a reskin of Echo in that game.

Can find special pickups.","cost":"100","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolving Long Gun","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"+20%","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Lance Bean","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"150","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"+4","banish":"+4","greed":"-","id":"FB_LANCE","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"-","theme":"Military Fortress - VS version","alias":"Lance","movespeed":"-","controller":"FB_Lance","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Short Gun"}
Lance Bean is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Short Gun.
## Unlocking
Lance Bean can be unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Long Gun. He needs to be purchased for 100, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Lance Bean can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "beanmachinegun".
## Passive bonuses
Lance starts with +50  Max Health, +20%  Speed, +4  Skip, and +4  Banish.
Lance gains 2.5% Speed and -1%  Cooldown each time he reaches a specific total kill threshold. He can not gain more than +50% and -25% of each stat, respectively. Kill thresholds start at 100 total kills and increase by 1.3x whenever a threshold is reached. 
- Lance's max Speed bonus will be reached at ~19005 kills, while his max Cooldown bonus will be reached at ~70565 total kills.
Lance can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.

LATODISOTTO was a placeholder character in Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Duplicator.
## Unlocking
LATODISOTTO was a placeholder/failsafe character that was accessible on the old engine by going to a previous version with a character that was not yet added into the game at that point (most commonly by leaving the public beta).
## Passive bonuses
LATODISOTTO starts with -99  Max Health, -100%  Move Speed, +100%  Magnet and +1  Amount as well as an additional amount from their starting  Duplicator.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"","cost":"666","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.4.2","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"+5","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Leda","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"+100%","area":"+10%","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Investigate the bottom of Gallo Tower.","greed":"-80%","movespeed":"-20%","theme":"","alias":"","speed":"-","release":"5 April 2022","id":"VERANDA","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"Holy Wand"}
Leda is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.4.2. Her starting weapon is the  Holy Wand.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Investigate the bottom of Gallo Tower."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Gallo Tower, and then continuously travel south.  Leda will appear in the center of a room approximately 43-44 tilesets (map tiles) below the starting area. In order to be able to reach her in time, it is recommended to play as a character with high movement speed, such as  Mask of the Red Death or  Gazebo, or play the stage in hyper mode for the added movement speed bonus.
- As the player moves south, the screen will gradually become completely dark around the edges and the letters in the background will change from runes to the words "IAMALIVEHERE". Also once the player is 5 tilesets away from the boss the music will be replaced by ominous wind-like ambient noise.
- The player will eventually encounter a hostile  Leda, who can be difficult to spot amongst everything else on screen with the player's limited vision, and she can sometimes be found stuck in a wall. Leda moves rather slowly and will not teleport to the player if she is offscreen, so it is recommended to not move away from her. Defeating Leda re-illuminates the screen and unlocks her as a playable character.
- Leda also appears at minute 31 though other possible conditions are unknown.
- If the player does not have Endless Mode, then playing in Hurry Mode is not recommended.
Once unlocked, Leda can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Leda can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "iwillneverletyouforgetaboutme".
## Passive bonuses
Leda starts with +5  Armor, -20%  Move Speed, +100%  Might, +10%  Area, -10%  Cooldown, and -80%  Greed.
## Tips
- With her high starting  Armor, Leda can be a great user of  Divine Bloodline (IX), giving affected weapons +25 flat damage and dealing 50 retaliatory damage from the start of the run.
- With her high starting  Might, Leda can make great use of  SpellStrike and  SpellStrom.
## Trivia
- Leda is the second character to be unlocked by defeating them. The others are  Mask of the Red Death,  Gyorunton, and  Avatar Infernas.
- Prior to version 0.10, Leda's and  Gains Boros' sprites were mirrored and her horizontal movement was reversed to account for that.  Vento Sacro and its evolution  Fuwalafuwaloo would slash to the back instead of the front.
- Leda may be inspired by Legion, a boss from many Castlevania games who is attached to a spherical cocoon with a similar appearance.
- Leda may also be a reference to Sorceress Adel from Final Fantasy VIII, both having red hair trapped in a large cocoon-like prison. The sine wave letters that spell "IAMALIVEHERE" are a reference to Adel's line, and Leda is Adel spelled backwards.
- "Leda" may also be a corruption of the word "Leader", as the Abyss Foscari contains a type of enemy that closely resembles Leda called the  Followa, a corruption of the word "Follower".
- The description of the Followa implies that Leda was once "a great scholar who abandoned her tower and disappeared without a trace".

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_LEON_MORNINGSTAR Alchemy Whip Morning Star
Leon Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Alchemy Whip. Leon also gains access to the  Morning Star once it is unlocked.
## Unlocking
Leon's coffin can be found north-east of the spawn area, right before the entrance to the castle in Ode to Castlevania.
Alternatively, Leon can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lamentedgenesis".
Defeating  Giant Medusa Head in Ode to Castlevania as Leon unlocks  Trevor Belmont as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Leon starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, +1  Revival and +5  Reroll. 
Leon receives a hidden  Svarog Statue at Level 5, levelling up whenever Leon's level reaches a multiple of 10.
Leon gains additional stats upon evolving the  Vampire Killer Whip, gaining +80  Max Health, +0.1  Recovery, +1  Armor, +1  Amount, +1  Revival, +10%  Move Speed, +10%  Might, +10%  Speed, +10%  Area, +10%  Luck, +10%  Growth, and -5%  Cooldown.
Leon can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- It is recommended to focus on getting  Vampire Killer as soon as possible to take advantage of Leon's enhanced stats.
## Trivia
- Him and Julius being the only unlockable characters through opening coffins before unlocking Dracula is a reference to them being the first and last members of the Belmont Clan.
## External links
- Leon Belmont in the Castlevania Wiki.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_LISA_2SB Luminatio Belnades' Spellbook
Lisa Tepes is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Luminatio.
Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook, Lisa is able to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Lisa Tepes can be unlocked by evolving the  Wine Glass. Lisa Tepes is then made available for purchas for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Lisa Tepes can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "mourned".
## Passive bonuses
Lisa starts with +20%  Move Speed and +5  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips. She also gains +1%  Growth every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
She also starts with an extra  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX).
Lisa can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Even though she starts with Silent Old Sanctuary, which encourages limited-slot builds, it is not recommended to lower her weapon slots below 2 as she cannot build  Universitas without both  Vol Luminatio and  Vol Umbra, although the latter can eventually be obtained from a  Candybox/ Super Candybox II Turbo.
- An optimized build involves limiting weapon slots to 2 and, instead of  Umbra, acquiring  Lifesign Scan to evolve into  Paranormal Scan. Even with an extra weapon slot filled, the massive cooldown reduction from Silent Old Sanctuary combined with an  Empty Tome will rapidly snowball Paranormal Scan to absurd levels, making Vol Umbra and Universitas mostly unnecessary, though they can still eventually be obtained through the Candybox.
## Trivia
- Technically, Vampire Survivors marks Lisa's first true appearance in a video game, as her presence in Symphony of the Night was merely an illusion crafted by the Succubus.
## External links
- Lisa in the Castlevania Wiki

Lita Caryx is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Glaive.
## Unlocking
Lita Caryx can be unlocked by defeating the  Earth Dragon in Witchdom Pulchra (southwest world) in Inverse Emerald Diorama with  Tsunanori Mido.
Alternatively, Lita Caryx can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "shungikuhanabusa".
## Passive bonuses
Lita Caryx starts with +100%  Move Speed, +100%  Speed, +1  Amount, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip, and +5  Banish.
Lita Caryx innately knows the "Heaven's Thunder" tech. 
She also triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

Gain a follower whenever Showstopper is triggered.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Gather the largest cadre of cats, including those that want to fight.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+40%","amount":"-","speed":"-","theme name":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr","skin id1":"DEFAULT","glimmer":"Chaos Disaster","maxhealth":"50","revival":"+8","curse":"-","dlc":"ed","recovery":"1.00","might":"-60%","area":"-40%","skip":"-","banish":"-","id":"EME_CATS","flight":"No","greed":"-","armor":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","details":"","theme":"","movespeed":"+40%","release":"10 April 2025","controller":"EME_CharacterControllerThemCats","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr are secret playable characters from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. They do not have a starting weapon. However, they innately know the Chaos Disaster tech.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Gather the largest cadre of cats, including those that want to fight."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr can be unlocked by first collecting a total of 30 cats summoned by  Ameya Aisling, across any number of runs, and then taking  Gatti Amari or its evolution,  Vicious Hunger. Upon exiting the run where the player has both of these requirements met, Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr will be available for purchase. If the player has 30 cats prior to the run where they take  Gatti Amari /  Vicious Hunger, no cats need to be collected during the run.
Alternatively, Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "maidofmagic".
## Passive bonuses
Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr start with -50  Max Health, +1  Recovery, +40%  Move Speed, -60%  Might, -40%  Area, +8  Revival, and +40%  Luck.
Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr trigger Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
They also gain a follower whenever Showstopper is triggered. Each follower has a different tech from the weapon  Spirit Rings corresponding to their color:
- Red Cats have the Sunlight tech.
- Black Cats have the Aqua Spheres tech.
- Blue Cats have the Heaven's Thunder tech.
- Yellow Cats have the Hypergravity tech.
- White Cats have the Vermillion Sands tech.
Upon obtaining one follower, subsequent levels will grant the follower copies of  Spinach,  Bracer,  Spellbinder,  Candelabrador,  Empty Tome, and  Duplicator. Further levels will upgrade these passives, and the stats granted by these stack with copies on the main character, effectively granting the player double bonus stats if they choose the same passives.
## Trivia
- Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr have 8 extra  Revival because of the common myth that cats have nine lives. They are composed of four different characters as well.
- In Episode 8 of Sopravvissuti ai vampiri,  Ameya Aisling mentions that she brought Lolo with her. Even though Lolo is supposed to be a black cat, it is depicted as a  Gatti Amari in that video.
