| {"magnet":"-","description":"Immune to health drain.","cost":"2000","duration":"-","unlock":"Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Loretta & Stella Lecarde","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_LORETTA_AND_STELLA","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+1","theme":"In Search of the Hidden Spell","alias":"Loretta & Stella","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Loretta_And_Stella_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Cocytus"}
Loretta & Stella Lecarde are secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Loretta Lecarde uses  Cocytus as her starting weapon. Stella Lecarde uses  Pneuma Tempestas as her starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Stella Lecarde and Loretta Lecarde will appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage as boss enemies, located in the top of the clock tower, between  Abbadon and  Metaglio Right. To unlock them,  Sanctuary must land the final blow with its screen-wide attack, instantly defeating Stella and Loretta Lecarde. The crosses spawned from  Refectio do not count for the unlock.
## Passive bonuses
As a duo, Loretta takes the lead followed by a computer controlled Stella. Both characters can Level up and select their own Weapons and Passive Items, similar to when playing in multiplayer.
Like  Stella & Loretta,  Stella, and  Loretta, Loretta & Stella are immune to Health Drain and start with +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed.
All variations can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- While Loretta's Stats, Weapons, and Passive Items can be seen anytime by using the pause screen, the player can only see Stella's stats and items via the Level up screen when selecting an item for Stella.
## Trivia
- The unlock method for Loretta & Stella is a reference to Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin. In Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin the player must defeat the twins Stella and Loretta Lecarde using the spell Sanctuary to access the game's good ending. Doing this in Vampire Survivors unlocks six secret characters; Jonathan & Charlotte, Charlotte & Jonathan, Stella & Loretta, Loretta & Stella, Stella Lecarde, and Loretta Lecarde.

Loretta Lecarde is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Cocytus.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Stella Lecarde and Loretta Lecarde will appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage as boss enemies, located in the top of the clock tower, between  Abbadon and  Metaglio Right. To unlock them,  Sanctuary must land the final blow with its screen-wide attack, instantly defeating Stella and Loretta Lecarde. The crosses spawned from  Refectio do not count for the unlock.
Once unlocked, Loretta Lecarde can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Loretta Lecarde and her variations can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "cooltwin".
## Passive bonuses
Like Stella and Loretta, Loretta and Stella, and Stella, Loretta Lecarde starts with 100  Max Health, +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed and is immune to Health Drain. 
All variations can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## External links
- Loretta Lecarde on the Castlevania Wiki

Can find special pickups.","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolving the Spread Shot","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+8","name":"Lucia Zero","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"+1","curse":"-","dlc":"og","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_LUCIA","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"-","theme":"Blue Gale","alias":"Lucia","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"FB_Lucia","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"C-U-Laser"}
Lucia Zero is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  C-U-Laser.
## Unlocking
Lucia Zero can be unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Spread Shot. She needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Lucia Zero can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "amitheoriginal".
## Passive bonuses
Lucia starts with +1  Recovery, +20%  Move Speed, +1  Revival, and +8  Reroll. 
When Lucia uses a Revival, she gains +20  Max Health, +0.1 Recovery, +0.2  Armor, +8% Movement Speed, +4%  Might, +4%  Speed, +4%  Duration, +4%  Area, -3%  Cooldown, +0.2  Amount, +0.2 Revival, +5%  Luck, +5%  Growth, and +1%  Greed for the rest of the run.
- This will not happen if Lucia is a follower nor if she is revived via coffin in co-op mode. The latter prevents Lucia from easily scaling her stats infinitely, as co-op mode allows players to be revived even if they have 0 Revivals.
Lucia can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Lucia needs  Tirajisú in order to maximize her revivals and evolve her starting weapon.  Phieraggi, which also evolves using Tirajisú and scales with revivals, is highly recommended.
- The  Awake (IV) arcana is essentially a must-have as it provides several additional revivals and further scales stats upon revival. Assuming Revival is picked in the shop, the player has both Tirajisú and Awake, and a  Weapon Power-Up is picked to evolve Lucia's main weapon, she can revive up to 8 times.
- Further Revivals are difficult to obtain on most maps.  Paranormal Scan can potentially represent a source of Revivals. On stages from Operation Guns, more Weapon Power-Ups can be obtained for further Revivals. In Abyss Foscari only, Lucia can get 2 extra Revivals by picking up the  Academy Badge on the ground.

Gets a temporary Might, Cooldown, and Move Speed bonus after reviving.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"With Maruto, break the Seal of the Abyss.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Luminaire Foscari","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"+7","curse":"-","dlc":"tf","recovery":"0.50","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+5","greed":"-","id":"LUMINAIRE","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"-","theme":"The Heart of the World","alias":"Luminaire","movespeed":"+40%","controller":"CharacterControllerLuminaire","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Prismatic Missile"}
Luminaire Foscari is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Prismatic Missile.
## Unlocking
Luminaire can be unlocked by breaking the Seal of the Abyss in Abyss Foscari with  Legionnaire while playing as  Maruto Cuts. 
After unlocking, Luminaire Foscari can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Luminaire Foscari can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "deusexmachina".
## Passive bonuses
Luminaire Foscari starts with +0.5  Recovery, +40%  Move Speed, +7  Revival, and +5  Banish.
Upon leveling up, Luminaire triggers a  Rosary. After reviving, she gets a temporary +200%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, and +60% (up to 300%)  Move Speed (to prevent her from moving too fast) bonus. This bonus lasts for (15+2*times revived) seconds, which means it starts at 17 seconds of bonus, then +2 seconds per Revival used. She also gets a longer invincibility frame each time she revives, based on the formula: (4+2*times revived) seconds.
## Tips
- With her extra Revivals,  Phieraggi and  Meal Ticket can be considered her best weapons.
- Awake (IV) is a very strong Arcana on her due to her massive amount of revives. If things go poorly, over the course of many revives, Luminaire can grow into a formidable force, even with her mediocre base stats.
- Wicked Season (XIII) helps scale Luminaire's  Growth and  Curse throughout the game, letting her level up more often and trigger more Rosaries. This means that other items that increase Growth, Curse and Charm ( Crown,  Skull O'Maniac,  Torrona's Box,  Karoma's Mana) can all be useful.
- Luminaire can have at least 13 revives and a few more (+7 base, +1 from shop, +2 from  Tirajisú, +3 from Awake (IV); +2 if  Academy Badge is available, and +1 per  Weapon Power-Up if it is available, which can make it beneficial to pick up some Operation Guns weapons like  C-U-Laser/ Pronto Beam).
- Despite her high movement speed, it is generally not recommended for Luminaire to move around the map a lot. This is because Luminaire wants to level up at a steady rate instead of letting XP gems gather into a single super-gem that would trigger multiple level-ups and thus Rosaries when that may not be necessary.  Attractorb is highly recommended.  Gorgeous Moon can gather XP gems to Luminaire, but triggering a Rosary immediately after Gorgeous Moon's screen-clearing effect may be overkill.
- Luminaire can very easily run a Reaper-killing build, as her Rosary effect helps offset the early disadvantages of  Laurel and  Clock Lancet before they are evolved.
-  Vicious Hunger is not recommended, as it greatly affects leveling rate and prevents Luminaire from triggering Rosaries as often.
-  Seraphic Cry does not replace the rosary effect triggered by Luminaire leveling up with a soul purifier.

## Other
 - 
 [Luminaire Foscari as seen in the launch trailer of Tides of the Foscari.]
 Luminaire Foscari as seen in the launch trailer of Tides of the Foscari.
## Trivia
- The description of the expansion mentions her having a title "the Lady of the Lake" which is a reference to the eponymous characters of Arthurian legend.
- The spell used to unlock Luminaire references the literary trope deus ex machina, a sudden resolution to a seemingly impossible to resolve conflict, often due to the intervention of an unexpected character or an improbable event occuring.
- Luminaire becomes an example of this trope during the boss battle against  Je-Ne-Viv in Abyss Foscari, with the sudden appearance of  Prismatic Missile and  Crown leading her to appear after they're evolved into  Luminaire, which quickly leads to the defeat of the seemingly undefeatable Je-Ne-Viv.

Macha Alter Ego is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Khukuri and a hidden  Rings of Calamity.
## Unlocking
Macha Alter Ego can be unlocked by defeating  Iron Maiden in Avalon (northeast world) in Inverse Emerald Diorama with  Ameya Aisling.
Alternatively, Macha Alter Ego can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notactuallythegoddess".
## Passive bonuses
Macha Alter Ego starts with +20%  Speed, +20%  Duration, +20%  Area, -10%  Cooldown, +20%  Growth, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip, +5  Banish, and +50  Charm.
Macha Alter Ego starts with a hidden  Rings of Calamity. She also increases enemy spawn quantity ( Charm) by 50 and triggers Showstopper at critical health.
During Showstopper:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will change.
Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

Malevolent Door Spirit is a secret playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Blood Chalice.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Let the last ruler re-freeze his most monstrous form."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Malevolent Door Spirit can be unlocked by defeating Malevolent Door Spirit in Grelon as the  Final Emperor while the player has  Out of Bounds (XII). Any weapon can be used to kill the boss, and the boss does not need to be frozen.
Alternatively, Malevolent Door Spirit can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "icetomeetyou".
Once unlocked, Malevolent Door Spirit can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Malevolent Door Spirit start with +77  Max Health, +1  Armor, -30%  Move Speed, +30%  Might, +30%  Area, -30%  Luck, +100%  Curse, and +10  Banishes.
Malevolent Door Spirit innately knows the "Vermillion Sands" tech.
Malevolent Door Spirit gains a Might bonuses based on 0.25*log(TotalGlimmersFired). 
- Note that this is different than Glimmering, where the latter will only happen once, while this multiplier increases as a weapon fires its tech.
- Since the attacks from  Spirit Rings [DLC] are not considered Glimmers, they will not contribute to this bonus.
Unlike the other characters in the Emerald Diorama DLC, the Malevolent Door Spirit does not have access to Showstopper.
## Tips
-  108 Bocce pairs well with Malevolent Door Spirit, as its Luck penalty and high Curse creates a large difference that will power up the weapon's damage significantly.
- Try to quickly acquire a defensive weapon such as the  King Bible or  Garlic in order to prevent getting swarmed due to high curse and low movement speed.
- Weapons from Emerald Diorama with techs (other than  Spirit Rings [DLC]) are preferred in order to maximize Might scaling. Weapons with 3 evolutionary stages are preferable over those with only 2, as they have more techs and therefore lead to faster scaling. Further, weapons whose Glimmers trigger rarely, like  Falconwind [DLC], are less effective for Might scaling.

Retaliation, HP-Critical, LastBreath, and OnRevival: triggers a wide area blood hex","cost":"1000","duration":"-","unlock":"Find and open the coffin in Red Moon Manor.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Malice Bloodmoon","movespeed":"+40%","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"bm","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"MALICE","flight":"No","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","alias":"Malice","theme":"It's Her Nature","skin id":"DEFAULT","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_Malice","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Scarlet Needle"}
Malice Bloodmoon is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Scarlet Needle.
## Unlocking
Malice Bloodmoon can be unlocked by finding and opening her  Coffin in Red Moon Manor.
Alternatively, Malice Bloodmoon can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "piumanopiuvero".
## Passive bonuses
Malice Bloodmoon starts with +20  Max Health, +40  Move Speed, +40  Greed. She gains +0.1  Recovery every 4 levels.
- This recovery gain is achieved by giving Malice +0.025 Recovery every level.
Malice Bloodmoon will trigger her Blood Hex on receiving damage, as a Last Breath, and as a CriticalHP skill.
- On triggering her Last Breath or Critical HP skills, she will become invulnerable for 1 second.
On level up, Malice will increase the base damage of her Blood Hex by 1. Malice's Recovery is also added to the base damage of her Blood Hex. If Malice is below 20% HP, then the damage of her Blood Hex is multiplied by 20. All damage dealt by her Blood Hex is considered retaliatory damage. Blood Hex can retaliate via damage at most every 5 seconds, but its other triggers allow it to trigger more often.
## Tips
- As with other characters that can scale Recovery, Malice works well with weapons that steeply scale off this stat, including  Impostongue [DLC] and  Moon Rod [DLC], even though her she cannot surpass  Cosmo Pavone. A build with  Crystal Cries (XII) can be considered for even more Recovery to take advantage of said weapons.
- Oddly for a character that feels more magical than martial, Malice is a great user of  Divine Bloodline (IX), her Blood Hex allowing her to deal heavy retaliatory damage in a massive area to potentially massively increase her HP very early on in the game. Not to mention, the max HP gain from Divine Bloodline means that Malice will be below 20% HP most of the time, which will massively juice up the damage of her Blood Hex. Finally, the rapid rate of fire of  Cardinal Rain makes it a fantastic combination with the flat damage bonus from Divine Bloodline.

Malphas is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. He starts with the  Thousand Edge (which is reskinned as black feathers with a blue outline) and  Tenebris Tonitrus as hidden weapons.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Destroy his black wings with the Morris who's[sic] tale is yet untold."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
In Ode to Castlevania,  Malphas will appear as a boss enemy in the stage at the 25:00 mark. Defeating Malphas as  Quincy Morris unlocks him as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Malphas can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Malphas can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "karasuman".
## Passive bonuses
Malphas starts with +1  Armor, +80%  Move Speed, and +20%  Might. He can also fly over terrain, preventing him from being obstructed by stage obstacles.
Malphas starts with a hidden  Tenebris Tonitrus and a weaker version of the  Thousand Edge, reskinned as black feathers. His hidden weapons get stronger with each level.
Malphas can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- The spell that unlocks Malphas is a reference to his name in the American version of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- The hidden weapons Malphas starts with are references to some of his attacks from his home series.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_MARIA_2SWSKIN_TP_MARIA_3SB Guardian's Targe Spectral Sword Belnades' Spellbook
Maria Renard is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Guardian's Targe. Upon unlocking the  Spectral Sword and  Belnades' Spellbook, Maria can start with either weapon selector as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Maria Renard can be unlocked by completing any stage with both  Shanoa and  Juste Belmont. She is then available for purchase for 1,000, scaling on other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Maria can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "holyglasses".
## Passive bonuses
Maria starts with +40%  Move Speed, +25%  Might, and +25%  Luck.
Overhealing will addivitely increase her Might by log(0.001*OverhealingAmount).
At levels 2, 12, 22, and 32 Maria gains a follower, namely the Sacred Cardinal, the Sacred Dragon, the Sacred Tiger, and the Sacred Turtle followers. They will stay at the fixed north, east, south and west positions around her.
- These familiars won't take damage.
Overhealing by +32 will trigger a  Mirror of Truth and  Rapid Fire for all of her followers.
She can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

## Trivia
- The spell required to unlock her, "holyglasses" is a nod to Symphony of the Night where she gives the Holy Glasses to Alucard to help him see Shaft's orb controlling Richter.
- The four familiars that she gains are a reference to her animal companions used as sub-weapons as Young Maria in Rondo of Blood: the cat, turtle, cardinals, and dragon.

Receives Academy Badge at Level 30.","cost":"3000","duration":"-","unlock":"Unite SpellString, SpellStream, and SpellStrike.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Maruto Cuts","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"+10%","dlc":"tf","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"VICTOR","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"+1","theme":"Slaying Dragons","alias":"Maruto","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerVictor","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Eskizzibur"}
Maruto Cuts is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Eskizzibur. Breaking the Seal of the Abyss with Maruto's upgraded starter weapon,  Legionnaire, in Abyss Foscari unlocks  Luminaire Foscari.
## Unlocking
Maruto is unlocked by uniting  SpellString,  SpellStream, and  SpellStrike. He needs to be purchased for 3,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Maruto can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "animeismypassion".
## Passive bonuses
Maruto starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, +20%  Might, and +10%  Curse.
Maruto gains +1 Armor after being damaged, resetting every 10 seconds, getting hit again extends the reset timer by 1 second. 
Upon reaching level 30, an  Academy Badge will spawn underneath him.
## Tips
- Divine Bloodline (IX) is the most key Arcana on Maruto, as it synergizes well with his playstyle of boosting his armor upon taking damage. In addition, the evolved form of his weapon, Legionnaire, deals retaliatory damage, which works with Divine Bloodline. With a build optimized for dealing retaliatory damage, Maruto can reach health values in the dozens of thousands over the course of a game. Unlike  Cosmo, he does not have high regeneration, and will therefore be stuck at low health, massively powering up his attacks as a result of the Divine Bloodline bonus.
-  Muramasa's self-damage can help trigger Maruto's armor bonus at a rapid rate and keep it at the maximum of 50, massively increasing the output of Legionnaire and other retaliatory damage sources. In addition, it repeatedly triggers  Mazo Familiar, another key item in Divine Bloodline builds. Finally, its healing can help keep Maruto alive at a relatively low health threshold. Now that Muramasa's health drain no longer scales with max Health, this makes it a must-pick item for Maruto.
- If using Muramasa, it is recommended to seal  Sole Solution. This is because both  Victory Sword, a fantastic retaliation weapon, and  Torrona's Box, which feeds more enemies into Maruto's meatgrinder, are both very desirable, but Sole Solution is actively counterproductive, because it provides windows where Maruto cannot take damage from Muramasa, causing his Armor to drop off.
- Using  Crimson Shroud to limit the incoming damage to 10 can trivilise any content due to immense scaling, survivability and retaliatory damage.
- Maruto is an excellent user of all items that increase Curse, as they give him even more targets to kill through retaliatory damage, and consequently more max health and power.

## Trivia
- Maruto Cuts is a reference to the manga Berserk. Cuts' name and appearance resembles Guts, the protagonist, and his weapon is visually identical to Guts' weapon, the Dragon Slayer.
- Maruto's theme is titled "Slaying Dragons" adding another layer to this reference.
- Maruto might also be a reference to the manga and anime Naruto, going by his first name being only one letter away from the protagonist Naruto Uzumaki's. This is supported by his evolved weapon's ability to summon magical copies of himself that attack enemies, similar to Naruto's famous Shadow Clone Technique and his cheat code being "animeismypassion" (anime is my passion).
- Maruto's name resembles the Italian phrase "m'hai rotto il cazzo", literally meaning "you broke my dick".

A blasphemous mockery.
DEFAULTLEGACY
Mask of the Red Death is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.12. Their starting weapon is  Death Spiral.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Settle the score with the Reaper."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must kill  The Reaper. A guide on how to do it is available at The Reaper § Strategies. Doing so unlocks Mask of the Red Death as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Mask of the Red Death can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Mask of the Red Death can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ablasphemousmockery".
## Passive bonuses
Mask of the Red Death starts with +155  Max Health, +100%  Move Speed, and +20%  Might.
## Tips
- They are one of the characters with the highest Movement Speed. This makes them suitable for reaching areas far away from the spawn point, such as  Coffins, Relics, and Secrets.

 - 
 [Red Death in poncle's "doot.mp7" video.]

 [Official plush image of Red Death from Youtooz.[1]]

 [A plush of Red Death found on Daniele Zandara's desk in poncle's "Embodying Chickens for Vampire Survivors "The Coop" Soundtrack" video.]
 A plush of Red Death found on Daniele Zandara's desk in poncle's "Embodying Chickens for Vampire Survivors "The Coop" Soundtrack" video.
## Trivia
- Mask of the Red Death replaced  missingN▯ as the reward for managing to kill The Reaper.
- Mask of the Red Death's starting max health before power-ups is 255, which is likely a reference to computing where 255 is the maximum value representable by an eight-digit binary number, and therefore the maximum representable by an unsigned 8-bit integer.
- Like many other Vampire Survivors characters, Mask of the Red Death is a direct reference to a character from Konami's Castlevania series, in this case Death, a recurring antagonistic character and loyal servant of Dracula, who is also depicted as a flying skeleton wearing a worn out robe and holding a scythe.
- Mask of the Red Death, and subsequently  The Reaper, closely resemble Death's appearance in Castlevania: Bloodlines.
- Mask of the Red Death's name and description as "A blasphemous mockery" are references to the 1842 short story The Masque of the Red Death by Edgar Allan Poe.
- Mask of the Red Death is the only character in the game to receive an official plush so far. It became available to (pre-)order on 27 September 2024.
- The reason why Mask of the Red Death received a plush in the first place is because, in August 2022, an employee from poncle asked, in the Vampire Survivors Discord server, which character, weapon, or other item, should receive a plush, and players could use Discord reactions to choose what should get a plush. The most voted option happened to be Mask of the Red Death.
- Death Spiral has no internal range limit, instead expiring once it leaves the player’s screen. Due to Mask of the Red Death’s high speed, the player can catch up to and stack Death Spiral projectiles endlessly by just not letting them go off-screen.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Deeply moved by money.","cost":"65536","duration":"-","unlock":"Pay a coin for every book he keeps.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+100","name":"Master Librarian","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"+1","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"+100","banish":"+100","greed":"+1000%","id":"TP_LIBRARIAN","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"-","theme":"Wood Carving Partita","alias":"","movespeed":"-100%","controller":"TP_Librarian_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Rapidus Fio"}
The Master Librarian is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Rapidus Fio [DLC].
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Pay a coin for every book he keeps."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, the player must spend over 42,000 gold at the  Master Librarian (Merchant). This amount is equal to purchasing 16 weapons at this merchant.
Doing this unlocks the Master Librarian as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Master Librarian can be purchased for 65,536, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Master Librarian can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rockingchair".
## Passive bonuses
The Master Librarian starts with -100%  Move Speed, +1  Revival, +1000%  Greed, +100  Rerolls, +100  Skips, and +100  Banishes.
He gains +80% Move Speed permanently from  Rapidus Fio [DLC] once it triggers for the first time and also gains +2% Move Speed per level.
He starts with an extra  Mad Groove (VIII) Arcana. 
The Master Librarian starts with a hidden  Power of Lire.
The Master Librarian can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
The Master Librarian has longer  Gold Fevers by multiplying its duration cap and duration extension from Gold pickups by 1.25.
## Tips
- The Master Librarian's Greed is so massive that it is almost impossible for most characters (except those with massive level scaling, like Gains Boros, and Sammy, who levels up from gold) to ever catch up with  Wicked Season (XIII). This makes him an exceptionally powerful user of  Muramasa.
- Wicked Season (XIII) is also recommended on Master Librarian himself, for the usual reasons and to scale Greed even further for his synergies.
-  Victorian Horror (XVIII) is incredibly powerful on the Master Librarian, due to dealing Greed-based damage in addition to helping further scale Greed.
- Power of Lire comes with a slight drawback, as it destroys experience gems and therefore may limit the Master Librarian's ability to level up somewhat. However, as of now, it no longer destroys chests.
-  Stake to Your Heart (0) is exceptionally potent on him, since he can very easily and quickly use it to accumulate enormous amounts of gold and become virtually invincible. It is recommended to play on Inverse and get it from the merchant as an extra card, however, because if Stake to Your Heart is picked as his first arcana, his inability to move until Rapidus Fio activates for the first time may get him killed immediately before he can get any gold.

## Trivia
- Master Librarian's unlock price, 65,536, is the maximum value of an unsigned 16-bit integer.
- The hidden  Power of Lire [DLC] is different from the union of  Soul Steal [DLC] and  Vicious Hunger. This caused an error where  Fake Trio [DLC] could not be unlocked at release as it checked for the ID of the hidden variant, which is unobtainable.

# Master Librarian (Merchant)
For the playable character, see Master Librarian.
DLC Content (Ode to Castlevania)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
The Master Librarian is a merchant that is part of the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. Much like his base-game counterpart  Merchant, the Master Librarian allows the player to purchase rare weapons using  coins. He initially only appears in Ode to Castlevania, but can be unlocked for all stages by obtaining  Wood Carving Score, obtainable after progressing through the DLC and obtaining  Pile of Secrets. After obtaining the Wood Carving Score, the Master Librarian will appear alongside the Merchant in most stages.
Much like the Merchant, the Master Librarian requires  Glass Vizard to be purchased to appear, alongside the player obtaining Wood Carving Score.
## Wares
The Master Librarian offers the following:
| Weapon | Description | Cost
| Valmanway [DLC] | Fires faster when moving. | 2,800
| Icebrand [DLC] | Fires lingering projectiles. Might freeze enemies. | 2,800
| Arrow of Goth [DLC] | Critical hits maximise amount of projectiles. | 2,800
| Aura Blast [DLC] | Generates explosive sideways. | 2,800
| Svarog Statue [DLC] | Generates damaging zones when moving. | 2,800
| Troll Bomb [DLC] | This one doesn't bounce. | 2,800
| Hydro Storm [DLC] | Generates damaging waves based on Projectile Speed and Duration. | 2,800
| Grand Cross [DLC] | Summons holy light. | 2,800
| Summon Spirit [DLC] | Fires a homing projectile. | 2,800
| Soul Steal [DLC] | Damages all enemies in sight and attempts to turn them into hearts. | 2,800
| Dark Rift [DLC] | Generates vertical damaging zones. | 2,800
| Sword Brothers [DLC] | Grants a brief moment of Invulnerability and generates a damaging wave. | 2,800
| Summon Spirit Tornado [DLC] | Summons spirits that will occasionally pick up XP gems for you. | 2,800
| Anura [DLC] | Summons frogs that damage enemies as they jump. | 2,800
| Kaiser Knuckle [DLC] | Throws a flurry of close-range punches. | 2,800
| Pocket Knife [DLC] | Throws a short-lived knife in the faced direction. | 2,800
## Notes
- All weapons bought from the Master Librarian last for the current game and cannot be bought if the max weapon limit is already reached.
- Unlike the  Merchant, the Master Librarian can be accessed infinitely, and does not disappear upon being accessed.
- The Master Librarian does not disappear after a certain time.
- Purchasing a total of 16 weapons from the Master Librarian will complete the "Pay a coin for every book he keeps" secret, unlocking the  Master Librarian as a playable character. This can be done across multiple runs, and any weapon can be purchased.
- Purchasing all of these weapons will unlock them for Level ups. This completes the "Acquire all the... books?" secret. This can be done across multiple runs.
## Trivia
- The Master Librarian has a particle effect behind their head, showcasing the various weapons he sells.
- The weapons the Master Librarian sells are mostly either hidden effects from certain evolutions, or mimic Pickups, such as  Troll Bomb [DLC] mimicking  Neutron Bomb [DLC] and  Soul Steal [DLC] mimicking  Karma Coin [DLC]. Otherwise, they are unique weapons normally only accessible by playing certain characters.
- Also, unlike the  Merchant, the weapons the Master Librarian sells mostly have a rarity of 20, while the Merchant's weapons all have a rarity of 1. The exception to this include  Soul Steal, having a rarity of 1, and  Pocket Knife and  Kaiser Knuckle, which both have a rarity of 80.
- Every weapon the Master Librarian sells has a union with an evolution from Version 1.0, except Pocket Knife, which evolves with a max level  Torrona's Box.
- Pocket Knife is also the only DLC weapon that evolves with Torrona's Box, and is the third weapon in general to do so.

Might is greatly increased while Invulnerable.","cost":"2000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Vibhuti Whip, after unlocking Vlad Tepes Dracula","secret":"No","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","image2":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Maxim Kischine","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"140","revival":"-","version2":"Coat of Arms","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","banish":"-","magnet":"-","recovery":"-","greed":"-","alias":"Maxim","weapon1":"Discus","skip":"-","might":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","id":"TP_MAXIM","controller":"TP_Maxim_Character","flight":"No","area":"-","version1":"Default","speed":"-","movespeed":"+80%","theme":"In Search of the Hidden Spell","details":"","release":"31 October 2024","skin id2":"SKIN_TP_MAXIM_2CA","cooldown":"-","weapon2":"Coat of Arms"}
Overhealing slightly increases Speed. Might is greatly increased while Invulnerable.
Overhealing slightly increases Speed. Might is greatly increased while Invulnerable.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_MAXIM_2CA Discus Coat of Arms
Maxim Kischine is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Discus.
Upon unlocking the  Coat of Arms, Maxim is able to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Maxim can be unlocked by evolving the  Vibhuti Whip after obtaining the  Black Disk. Once unlocked, Maxim can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Maxim can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the Spell "stellarfan".
## Passive bonuses
Maxim starts with +40  Max Health, and +80%  Move Speed.
Maxim gains Speed based on how much he overheals, based on log(OverhealingTotal). However, Maxim does not accumulate overhealing at a 1:1 ratio to normal healing, instead multiplying the amount he overhealed in that instance by 0.001.
Maxim's  Might increases while he is invulnerable. During this state, his Might is multiplied by the lower value between 1.5 + his Speed bonus from overhealing, and 2. 
Maxim can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Using  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) allows for further Speed scaling on top of Maxim's innate ability.
-  Melio Confodere scales off Speed and is therefore a fantastic way to weaponize Maxim's passive skill.
-  Sacred Beasts Tower Shield provides an invulnerability period when the user overheals, which means it synergizes with both things Maxim wants to be doing.
-  Paranormal Scan is an already extremely powerful item that can additionally help trigger Maxim's overheal bonus.
-  Sword Brothers provides a brief invulnerability period and can be acquired right off the bat from the Master Librarian, giving Maxim a way to survive the early hordes and use his Might increase. As the game goes on, Sword Brothers can be used to become permanently invulnerable if Cooldown is low enough thanks to Paranormal Scan. This can also be achieved without other DLC with 3 or less total weapons, a level 7 sword brothers, empty tome, and  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX).

McCoy-Oni is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  108 Bocce.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by evolving the  Mirage Robe weapon. He needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, McCoy-Oni can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ionicoionico".
## Passive bonuses
McCoy-Oni starts with +8  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +20%  Growth. His  Area temporarily increases by +150% every second.
Overhealing by 16 will grant Mccoy-Oni +1  Reroll.
## Tips
- Since the Area increase effect uses the sineArea function,  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) cannot override this effect. However, McCoy-Oni can still benefit from the Area increase every level.
## Trivia
- McCoy-Oni resembles the Italian exclamation from Rome "mecojoni" which translates to "my testicles," a way to convey surprise in a friendly way.
- This is a reference to Japanese myths revolving around the bake-danuki, a yōkai which are commonly portrayed as having oversized testicles which may be carried slung over their shoulder, just as depicted in McCoy-Oni's character art.
=====

Doesn't appreciate getting blasphemously mockered.
Doesn't appreciate getting blasphemously mockered.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_DEATH_MED_DB Death Spiral Ebony Diabologue
Megalo Death is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Death Spiral, but he also has the option of starting with the  Ebony Diabologue if it has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Unleash maximised Death on the Castle while holding the soul-stealing stones."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining both the  Ebony and Crimson Stones and the  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania while playing as  Death and kill a total of 100,000 enemies or more in a single run. Doing this unlocks Megalo Death as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Megalo Death can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Death can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "embracethereaper".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Death starts with the following bonuses:
- +300  Max Health
- +2  Armor
- +100%  Move Speed
- +250%  Might
- -20%  Cooldown
- +100%  Greed
- +10  Reroll
- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
Megalo Death is the permanent transformation  Death receives upon reaching level 80. This transformation grants Death the  Death Hand as a hidden weapon, which are two giant skeletal hands that move in tandem with him and create large cracks in the ground that shake the screen and deal 65 base damage to enemies.
He cannot die but instead disappears at the time limit and ending the run automatically, similar to  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC] and  Ghost Lino [DLC].
Megalo Death can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Megalo Death's death animation cycles through the Gate Key icons.
- Megalo Death closely resembles Death's appearance as a  boss, but only includes his head and hands.
- Much like the description of Death, Megalo Death's description references both the description of  Mask of the Red Death and the 1842 short story The Masque of the Red Death by Edgar Allan Poe.
## External links
- Death on the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_DRACULA_MED_DOMSKIN_TP_DRACULA_MED_DB Wine Glass Dominus Agony +  Dominus Anger +  Dominus Hatred Ebony Diabologue
Megalo Dracula is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Wine Glass, but he also has the option of starting with  Dominus Agony,  Dominus Anger, and  Dominus Hatred all at once if Dominus Agony has been unlocked, as well as the  Ebony Diabologue regardless of unlock status.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Hold the stones that birthed your lineage and prove you hold dominance over all your monstrous minions."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining both the  Ebony and Crimson Stones and the  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania while playing as  Vlad Tepes Dracula and kill a total of 100,000 enemies or more in a single run. Doing this unlocks Megalo Dracula as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Megalo Dracula can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Dracula can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "mathiascronqvist".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Dracula starts with the following bonuses:
- +566  Max Health
- +2  Armor
- +50%  Move Speed
- +250%  Might
- -20%  Cooldown
- +100%  Greed
- +10  Reroll
- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
Megalo Dracula is the permanent transformation  Vlad Tepes Dracula receives upon reaching level 80. Like his normal form, he gains +1% Might per level and +1  Revival every 100 levels. There is no cap on these bonuses. Every +1% of  Curse increases his Might by +1%, incoming damage is capped at 10, and he is also immune to health drain. Additionally, the transformation also grants Vlad Tepes Dracula the  Hand of Vlad as a hidden weapon, which grants him two giant claws to his left and right that swipe at nearby enemies.
Megalo Dracula can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- The Spell for unlocking Megalo Dracula is a direct reference to Dracula's former name when he was a mortal known as Mathias Cronqvist.
- Megalo Dracula is a direct reference to Dracula's appearance when he is fought as the final boss of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
## External links
- Dracula on the Castlevania Wiki

Megalo Elizabeth Bartley is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Belnades' Spellbook, but she also has the option of starting with the  Ebony Diabologue if it has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Ravage everything and everyone in Dracula's Castle while in her demonic form."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining both the  Ebony and Crimson Stones and the  Pile of Secrets, the player must start a run in Ode to Castlevania while playing as  Elizabeth Bartley and kill a total of 100,000 enemies or more in a single run. Doing this unlocks Megalo Elizabeth Bartley as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Megalo Elizabeth Bartley can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Elizabeth Bartley can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "petrifyinglyevil".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Elizabeth Bartley starts with the following bonuses:
- +300  Max Health
- +2  Armor
- +50%  Move Speed
- +250%  Might
- -20%  Cooldown
- +100%  Greed
- +10  Reroll
- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
- +50  Charm
She is also immune to health drain.
Megalo Elizabeth Bartley is the transformation  Elizabeth Bartley temporarily assumes upon reaching certain enemy kill thresholds, but playing as Megalo Elizabeth Bartley enables the transformation permanently.
Notably, Megalo Elizabeth Bartley cannot find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Starts with hidden 108 Bocce.","cost":"50000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeat 100000 enemies in a single run with Menya Moonspell.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+10%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Megalo Menya Moonspell","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"80","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"lm","hidden weapon":"108 Bocce","recovery":"-","might":"+100%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+108","greed":"-","id":"MEGAMENYA","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"DLC & Patch 1.2.0","theme":"","alias":"Menya","movespeed":"+100%","controller":"CharacterControllerMegaloMenya","release":"15 December 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-20%","weapon":"N/A"}
Megalo Menya Moonspell is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Megalo Menya Moonspell is another form of  Menya Moonspell. 
## Unlocking
Megalo Menya is unlocked by defeating 100,000 enemies in a single run with Menya. She needs to be purchased for 50,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Menya can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "elamadonna".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Menya starts with -20  Max Health, +100%  Move Speed, +100%  Might, -20%  Cooldown, +10%  Luck, and +108  Banish. She cannot be damaged at all but her run ends as soon as the Time Limit is reached.
She starts with  108 Bocce without taking up any weapon slots and the weapon versions can still be offered. It is upgraded every 10 levels starting at level 20, up to level 80 when it is maxed at level 8.
Overhealing by 16 will activate the weapon in Megalo Menya's first weapon slot.
## Trivia
- Megalo Menya's name resembles the Italian and English word "megalomania", which is an obsession with power.
- Her spell "elamadonna" is an Italian saying used to express surprise at a blatant exaggeration, and is roughly translated to "Holy Mary".

| {"magnet":"-","description":"","cost":"50000","duration":"-","unlock":"Holding the Pearl magatama and obtaining the Argent Flow with Miang Moonspell","secret":"no","image":"","luck":"+10%","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Megalo Miang Moonspell","movespeed":"+100%","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"lm","recovery":"1","might":"+100%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+108","greed":"-","id":"MEGAMIANG","flight":"no","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","theme":"","skin id":"DEFAULT","alias":"","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_MegaMiang","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-20%","weapon":"Argent Flow"}
Megalo Miang Moonspell is one of the playable characters in Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is a hidden  Argent Flow.
## Unlocking
Megalo Miang Moonspell can be unlocked by evolving  Festive Winds into  Argent Flow and obtaining  Pearl magatama at the same time as  Miang Moonspell.
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Miang Moonspell starts with +20  Max Health, +1.00  Recovery, +100%  Move Speed, +100%  Might, -20%  Cooldown, +10%  Luck, and +108  Banish.
Megalo Miang Moonspell retains all of Miang Moonspell's passive bonuses, such as her ability to multiply all sources of healing by Recovery.
Megalo Miang Moonspell's Max Health equals her current health. Thus, any healing or overhealing will increase her Max Health by the same amount. However, receiving damage will also decrease her Max Health by the same amount. 
- Falling below 20% of Miang's base Max Health can still trigger Critical-HP skills.
## Tips
- Sources of percent-based healing, such as Pearl magatama and certain Survarot skills, create a positive feedback loop of healing and increasing Max Health to the point where Megalo Miang is able to reach the true limit of 3.402823E+38 health. With sources of damage that scale with health, Megalo Miang is able to hit the damage cap without the use of  Golden Eggs.
- Note that the heal from  Sanctuary [DLC] will have no effect on Megalo Miang, as its coding prevents it from overhealing.
- Megalo Miang's passive synergizes well with  Mazo Familiar, as her increasing Max Health will increase the damage and  Amount of Mazo Familiar.
-  Aurablaster Tip [DLC] does not synergize well with Megalo Miang due to its damage capping out at 1700 health.
- Likewise,  Divine Bloodline (IX) is not recommended since it can't grant Megalo Miang any damage due to there never being any difference between her current and max health.

- +10  Skip
- +10  Banish
Megalo Olrox is the transformation  Count Olrox temporarily assumes upon reaching certain enemy kill thresholds, but playing as Megalo Olrox enables the transformation permanently. However, he does not have a permanent  Nduja Fritta Tanto, unlike the temporary transformation.
Megalo Olrox can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Megalo Olrox resembles Count Olrox's second reptilian form during his boss fight in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
## External links
- Count Olrox on the Castlevania Wiki

Echoes of the past.","cost":"100000","duration":"+50%","unlock":"Hold the Velvet Dodecahedron and obtain the FireStall with Sargon.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"+20%","reroll":"+10","name":"Megalo Sargon","movespeed":"+50%","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"+50%","dlc":"bm","recovery":"-","might":"+50%","area":"+50%","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+50%","id":"MEGASARGON","flight":"Yes","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","theme":"Lose All","skin id":"DEFAULT","alias":"Sargon","speed":"+50%","controller":"CharacterController_MegaSargon","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"FireStall"}
Megalo Sargon is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  FireStall.
## Unlocking
Megalo Sargon can be unlocked by evolving the  FireFall,  FireBall, and  FireWall into the  FireStall and picking up the  Velvet Dodecahedron as  Sargon. He must then be purchased for 100,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Sargon can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "besessenheit".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Sargon starts with +50%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, +50%  Speed, +50%  Duration, +50%  Area, -10%  Cooldown, +20%  Growth, +50%  Greed, +50%  Curse, and +10  Reroll.
- Note that, upon starting a run, Megalo Sargon's Max Health is set to -1.
Megalo Sargon gains +0.5% Move Speed, +0.5% Might, +0.5% Speed, +0.5% Duration, +0.5% Area, -0.2% Cooldown, +0.5%  Luck, +0.5% Growth, +0.5% Greed, and +0.5% Curse on every level up.
Megalo Sargon possesses flight and is able to ignore walls.
Megalo Sargon will jumble the visual stage timer at the top of the screen and replace the background of the stage with a starry backdrop instead.
Megalo Sargon cannot receive damage but his run will automatically end once the normal stage time limit has been reached.
- While Megalo Sargon jumbles the stage timer, the true time can be viewed by pausing the game.
Megalo Sargon is adept with FireFall, FireBall, FireWall, and their union,  FireStall.
If Megalo Sargon acquires  Damnation, then the central black hole attack of this weapon will be made permanently active.
Unlike Sargon, Megalo Sargon is unable to summon clones, nor do the two hands oscillating on either side of the screen retain the functionality of the clones. These hands will also align with the FireWall attacks from FireStall, but are purely visual.
## Trivia
- The spell "besessenheit" is the name of one of the battle themes of the 1999 role-playing game SaGa Frontier 2. One of the antagonists in that game is also called Sargon, and "Besessenheit" is the name of the theme used when he is fought in his Flame Lord form near the end of the game.
- Megalo Sargon, alongside  Baal'Thamut [DLC] and  Chaos [DLC], will immediately be defeated upon entering Eudaimonia Machine by  The Directer.

Megalo Syuuto Moonspell is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Megalo Syuuto Moonspell is another form of  Syuuto Moonspell. His starting weapon is  Night Sword.
## Unlocking
Megalo Syuuto is unlocked by defeating 100,000 enemies in a single run with Syuuto. He needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Megalo Syuuto can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "takeashower".
## Passive bonuses
Megalo Syuuto starts with +150  Max Health, +2  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, +50%  Greed, and +50%  Curse. He gains +1%  Might every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
Overhealing by 16 will increase the base damage of all  Blood damage weapons by +0.5. Overhealing by an amount greater than Megalo Syuuto's current Max Health will instead grant +5 base damage. Unlike morping his base form, starting as Megalo Syuuto will not allow him to gain Might when overhealing by +16.
## Trivia
- Syuuto's Night sword, design, transformation, and him being a half yokai, is likely a reference to Inuyasha from the anime and manga series Inuyasha.

Becomes stronger and invincible for a fixed interval after defeating a large amount of enemies.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Silver Wind.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+10%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Menya Moonspell","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"80","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"lm","recovery":"-","might":"+10%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+8","greed":"-","id":"MENYA","flight":"No","update":"DLC & Patch 1.2.0","armor":"-","theme":"Rumble Kachara","alias":"Menya","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerMenya","release":"15 December 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-10%","weapon":"Four Seasons"}
Menya Moonspell is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Four Seasons.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by evolving the  Silver Wind weapon. She needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Menya can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "youngatheart".
## Passive bonuses
Menya starts with -20  Max Health, +10%  Might, -10%  Cooldown, +10%  Luck, and +8  Banish.
Overhealing by 16 activates her starting weapon.
Menya enters a boosted state upon defeating a specific amount of enemies. When this bonus is triggered, all enemies are erased from the screen with the effects of the  Rosary; She gains a level 1  108 Bocce which is only active during the boosted duration. While inside this state, she cannot be damaged, gains +200% Might, -20% Cooldown, +50%  Curse, and +100% (up to 300% total)  Move Speed (to prevent her from moving too fast). This remains active for 30 real seconds (60 in-game seconds if hurry mode is active).
Menya's boosted state is triggered upon reaching the following numbers of total enemies defeated:
- 500
- 1,000
- 2,000
- 3,000
- 5,000
- 7,000
- 10,000
- 15,000
After this, the bonus will continue to trigger every multiple of 10,000 enemies defeated (20,000, 30,000, 40,000, 50,000, etc).
The game only checks to see if the player has reached the next number needed when the bonus is not active. For example, say the player was in a bonus triggered by defeating 500 enemies. If she defeated 1,000 total enemies with five seconds remaining in her current bonus, the new bonus would not trigger for five seconds. This guarantees that all bonuses last their full duration.
- Defeating 100,000 enemies in a single run with her unlocks  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC] as a playable character.

# Merchant
For the playable character, see Big Trouser.
The Merchant is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors, which allows the purchasing of rare Weapons and Golden Eggs using Gold Coins. They initially only appear in Moongolow. Then later in all other stages after the purchase of the  Glass Vizard.
## Wares
The Merchant offers the following:
| Items | Type | Description | Cost | Note
| Pick a Card | Buff | Gives an extra Arcana for this run. | 20,000 | Inverse mode only.
| Skip | Buff | Gives +1 Skip for this run. | 100 | Inverse mode only. Can be purchased repeatedly until the player has 20 Skips. Can only buy once if the character already start with 20 Skips.
| Banish | Buff | Gives +1 Banish for this run. | 500 | Inverse mode only. Can be purchased repeatedly until the player has 20 Banishes. Can only buy once if the character already start with 20 Banishes.
| Reroll | Buff | Gives +1 Reroll for this run. | 1,000 | Inverse mode only. Can be purchased repeatedly until the player has 20 Rerolls. Can only buy once if the character already start with 20 Rerolls.
| Golden Egg | Buff | Permanently improves a random stat for the current character, by a tiny amount. | 10,000 | Can be purchased repeatedly. Not available for Queen Sigma.
| Candybox | Weapon | Allows you to choose any unlocked base weapon. | 1,000 | Only available for Queen Sigma.
| Glass Vizard | Relic | Summons the merchant in all stages. | 10,000 | Sold in Moongolow only. One-time purchase.
| Bone | Weapon | Throws a bouncing projectile. | 1,000 | 
| Cherry Bomb | Weapon | Throws a bouncing projectile that explodes after some time. | 1,000 | 
| Carréllo | Weapon | Throws a bouncing projectile. Number of bounces affected by Amount. | 1,000 | 
| Celestial Dusting | Weapon | Throws a bouncing projectile. Cooldown reduces when moving. | 1,000 | 
| La Robba | Weapon | Generates bouncing projectiles. | 1,000 | 
| Mille Bolle Blu [DLC] | Weapon | Fires lingering projectiles. | 1,000 | 
| Party Popper [DLC] | Weapon | Throws bouncing projectiles. | 1,000 | 
| Hats [DLC] | Weapon | Throws cute bouncing projectiles with random properties. | 1,000 | 
## Other Merchants
The Merchant is not the only vendor in the game.
- The  Master Librarian from Ode to Castlevania, who allows purchasing of various Ode to Castlevania weapons.
- Adventure Merchants, accessible inside of Adventures, especially inside the Planar Bazaar stage. The Adventure Merchants sell a plethora of items and unlock them for the Adventure the player is currently in.
- All of the Merchants in Planar Bazaar also happen to be player characters as well, except for  Xanthia, who can also be seen selling the  Emerald Disk and the  Joker Disk in Moongolow.
- The  Eggman in Moongolow, who allows for bulk purchasing of Golden Eggs.
- The  Healer in Moongolow, who allows for the removal of Golden Eggs.
-  Giocare, who appears in Westwoods, sells spins for the Spin the Wheel! minigame.
-  Big Troubler, who appears at the top of The Lycaeum and sells the  Green Eyes Vizard,  Survarocchi, and  Enemahs.
-  Stake to Your Heart (0) has a  merchant that spawns every minute. This merchant shows 12 randomly selected weapons and passives that the player can buy from, which can be rerolled. Furthermore, the player can sell any of their weapons and passives in their possession in exchange for gold.
## Notes
- In order to access the shop, the player needs to touch the merchant.
- Golden Egg applies to the current character being played but its effects will not apply until after the current game ends.
- All rare weapons (Bone, Cherry Bomb, Carréllo, Celestial Dusting, La Robba) bought lasts for the current game and cannot be bought if the max weapon limit is already reached.
- The player is unable to purchase weapons if the player's weapon slots are full already.
- The merchant can only be accessed once per run. Once the player exits the shop, the merchant disappears from the map.
- The merchant can be accessed infinite times in Endless Mode, spawning every cycle.
- The merchant does not disappear after a certain time. They will remain like this until the player interacts with them.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Running animation in Carlo Cart, The Coop, and Hectic Highway]
 Running animation in Carlo Cart, The Coop, and Hectic Highway
## Other
 - 
 [Merchant shop UI. Here, the Merchant is offering Golden Eggs and various weapons, such as the  Bone and the  Celestial Dusting.]
 Merchant shop UI. Here, the Merchant is offering Golden Eggs and various weapons, such as the  Bone and the  Celestial Dusting.
## Trivia
- The merchant has a particle effect behind their head, similar with the head/masks of the Masked Guardians that guard the four secret passive items.
- The merchant's appearance is based on the character Pantalone from the Italian form of theatre known as commedia dell'arte.
- In Carlo Cart, the merchant runs on the top of the track at the same speed as the character's passive movement. The player can get to him by pressing right.
- The phrase, "Interested in this?" that appears at the top of the Merchant's menu is a nod to Alucard's interactions with the Master Librarian in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, where he will say to him, "I'm interested in this," upon browsing his inventory.

Over-healing slightly increases Max Health.","cost":"1000","duration":"-","unlock":"Find and open the coffin in Mt.Moonspell.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Miang Moonspell","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"lm","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"MIANG","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"DLC & Patch 1.2.0","alias":"Miang","theme":"Festive Breeze","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"CharacterControllerMiang","release":"15 December 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Silver Wind"}
Miang Moonspell is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Silver Wind.
## Unlocking
Miang is unlocked for purchase by finding and opening her  Coffin in Mt.Moonspell. She needs to be purchased for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Miang can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "shounenheroine".
## Passive bonuses
Miang starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Recovery, and +20%  Move Speed. Miang gains +0.2 Max Health each time she overheals. There is no cap on this bonus.
Miang also multiplies healing from all sources directly based on Recovery, on top of its usual multiplier which can be ignored by some form of healing like  Gold Coins with  Disco of Gold (XV).
After obtaining both  Pearl magatama [DLC] and  Argent Flow [DLC], Miang will morph into  Megalo Miang Moonspell [DLC].
## Tips
- Miang is incentivized to build sources of healing in order to consistently trigger her overheal bonuses. Her primary weapon helps, but other healing sources, like  Muramasa, are also recommended. Since her ability does not require reaching a specific overheal threshold, fast sources of minor healing are preferable, but  Paranormal Scan is never a bad pick.
-  Bloody Tear and  Fuwalafuwaloo are very good weapons for Miang for this reason, as they have a high fire rate and the healing effect occurs upon landing a critical hit rather than killing an enemy.
-  Greatest Jubilee has a chance to spawn  Floor Chicken from the Light sources it creates, making it a useful tool.
-  Hollow Heart and  Metaglio Left can further boost Miang's health. It is recommended to pick these later into a game, though, since their effect improves based on how much health Miang already has.
- The last part of Miang's toolkit consists of turning her high health into massive damage with  Mazo Familiar and  Aurablaster Tip.
- Miang can also run a freezing build with  Crystal Cries (XII). In addition to helping her scale her Max Health even further, this also improves her Recovery, which further feeds into her healing and makes her very difficult to take down.
- Miang can be a powerful user of  Sarabande of Healing (VI) thanks to having some of the best healing in the game, especially when combined with Crystal Cries.
-  Disco of Gold (XV) can be an extremely powerful arcana to use with Miang, as it makes the most common pickup, coins, into potential overhealing sources. This is even better when a Gold Fever is triggered, which can raise her Max Health by dozens, if not hundreds, of points in a very short amount of time.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, Area, and Cooldown change every minute.","cost":"666","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.7.2","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Minnah Mannarah","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"150","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"0.50","might":"-70%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Deal with the consequence of stealing cheese from the Dairy Plant.","greed":"-","movespeed":"-","theme":"","alias":"Minnah","speed":"-","release":"9 June 2022","id":"GRAZIELLA","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Bloody Tear"}
Minnah Mannarah is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.7.2. Her starting weapon is the  Bloody Tear.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Deal with the consequence of stealing cheese from the Dairy Plant."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the Yellow Sign, the player must start a run in Dairy Plant and collect the  Cheese, which is visible on the map as a  Floor Chicken. It can also be attracted using  Mad Groove (VIII). Upon collecting the Cheese, seven large  Werewolves will spawn in a circle around the player. Defeating all of the Werewolves unlocks Minnah Mannarah as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Minnah Mannarah can be bought for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Minnah Mannarah can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "foldinthecheese".
## Passive bonuses
Minnah starts with +50  Max Health, +0.5  Recovery, and -70%  Might. She gains +10% Might every three levels until 24. Her stats change every minute similar to some Arcanas. Might,  Speed,  Duration,  Area, and  Cooldown are on a cycle; when Duration, Area, and Cooldown are multiplied by 2, Might and Projectile Speed are divided by 2, and vice versa.
| Levels | Might
| 1-2 | -70%
| 3-5 | -60%
| 6-8 | -50%
| 9-11 | -40%
| 12-14 | -30%
| 15-17 | -20%
| 18-20 | -10%
| 21+ | 0%
## Tips
- The stat changes may seem complicated but it is not random. She stays at the maximum or minimum bonuses most of the time so Minnah has two modes:
- Big  Area, long  Duration and  Cooldown, slow  Speed, and weaker.
- Small Area, low Duration and Cooldown, fast Projectile Speed, and stronger.
- Minnah does not benefit from the stat oscillation from Chaos in the Dark Night (V), Lost & Found Painting (XVII) and Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII), as they require the character to not have value in the sineStat attributes to set their own value while the changing stats already occupied them. It does not override them either; however, she can still benefit from the additional stats every level.
- As Minnah started with Bloody Tear, Whip is still in the pool of options for level ups. After uniting Bloody Tear with Vento Sacro into the Fuwalafuwaloo, it is able to evolve it into another Bloody Tear. However, they must be united before Whip is maxed out.
## Trivia
- Minnah looks similar to a  Werewolf. Its secret unlock menu icon depicts a normal Werewolf.

MissingN▯ is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.11.3. Its starting weapon has a 50% chance of being either the  Axe or  Death Spiral.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "With the most adverse fate in Green Acres, run off the edge of the world."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the Yellow Sign, the player must start a run in Green Acres with hyper mode and hurry mode enabled. Move southwest, and after precisely 10 tilesets (map tiles) and west precisely 10 tilesets, the area's appearance will change drastically. After this happens, continue moving southwest, and all the stages in the game will morph together and  giant winged eyes will begin to spawn. After killing 128 of them, missingN▯ is unlocked as a playable character.
- The distance and direction the player must travel is independent of the "Visually Invert Stages" Option while  Inverse Mode is enabled, unlike other location-specific Secrets.
Once unlocked, MissingN▯ can be purchased for 66,666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, MissingN▯ can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rightninetyseven".
## Passive bonuses
MissingN▯'s base stats and starting weapon are randomized every time the character selection screen is opened. The final stat is determined by the base stat and the stats from PowerUps.
| Base Stats | Max Health | Recovery | Armor | Move Speed | Might | Area | Speed | Duration | Amount | Cooldown | Luck | Growth | Greed | Curse | Magnet | Revival
| Maximum | 97.5 | 0.5 | 2 | 0 | +80% | +260% | +80% | +170% | 2 | +80% | +80% | +80% | +80% | +95% | +80% | 2
| Minimum | -2.5 | -0.5 | 0 | -200% | -120% | -140% | -120% | -130% | 0 | -120% | -120% | -120% | -120% | -105% | -120% | 0
There are several interesting possible scenarios that may occur:
- If  Max Health is lower than 0, MissingN▯ becomes invincible.
- The  Max Health is then also unaffected by the health bonus from  Hollow Heart.
- If  Move Speed is lower than -100%, the controls become inverted.
- If Move Speed is exactly -100%, MissingN▯ will be unable to move.
- If  Growth is -100% or lower, MissingN▯ cannot gain experience.
- If  Curse is exactly -100% enemies will not spawn. Lower than -100% has enemies spawn but not move, and the  The Reaper will actually retreat.
- If  Luck is -100% or lower, no  Treasure Chests will drop.
- If  Greed is exactly -100%, no gold will be gained. If Greed is lower than -100%, gold will be lost until the player reaches 0 gold. Gold can not go below 0.

## Trivia
- MissingN▯ used to be unlocked by killing  The Reaper. Since the addition of  Mask of the Red Death, any previously unlocked MissingN▯ characters were converted to Mask of the Red Death. Because of this, MissingN▯ could not be unlocked with an unmodified save until a new unlock method was added in patch 0.11.302.
- Not only are MissingN▯'s stats calculated randomly, but its name and ability description also contain randomly-generated characters in the ASCII range 0x00 - 0xde:
- Its name is determined as MissingN▯, where '▯' represents a random character.
- Its description is determined as 'M(▯▯), where '▯' represents a random character.
- The name and item on MissingN▯'s smaller character tile is the name and item when it was previously selected.
- After selecting MissingN▯, its current name and weapon is displayed on the larger character tile.
- MissingN▯'s sprite was also used for LATODISOTTO, a placeholder character found in older versions of the game.
- The spell "rightninetyseven" (unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane, used as an alternate way to unlock MissingN▯), is likely a reference to "Lost Number" from the original Final Fantasy VII, in which a secret code was needed to unlock a safe, "(Right 36), (Left 10), (Right 59), (Right 97)", upon inputting the last missing number into the safe (Right 97), "Lost Number" would appear.
- MissingN▯ is a reference to a variety of different glitches from early titles in the Pokémon game series:
- Both its name and first two appearances are a reference to the famous glitch of the same name from the first generation of Pokémon games.
- The 128 enemy requirement and its initial item being the  Duplicator references how using MissingN▯ in battle would give the player 128 of the item in their sixth bag slot.
- The random stats may refer to MissingN▯ actually being 39 different Pokémon with the same name. Some versions have different stats, and some take their stats from different Pokémon.
- The unlock requirement of walking 'off the edge of the world' may be in reference to the unique glitch used to encounter MissingN▯ in the Spanish and Italian versions of the game, which involves walking through walls into unintended areas.
- MissingN▯'s description is an additional reference to a less renowned glitch Pokémon from Pokémon Red and Blue, which often appears identical.
- The glitchy background Green Acres changes to when unlocking missingN▯ is a reference to the Glitch City glitches in the first two generations of Pokémon, which similarly change the map into an amalgamation of various map tiles. This glitch can be used to encounter MissingN▯ itself.
- One method of accessing Glitch City is to walk out of bounds, much like the unlock requirement of walking 'off the edge of the world.' This is also the method used to access Glitch Cities in the Generation II games.
- The main method of accessing Glitch City requires for the player to run out of time in the Safari Zone, which may be referenced by the requirement of Hurry Mode.
- Prior to v1.14, having  Greed below -100% would allow the player to "underflow" their Gold eventually, setting them to the limit of 3.402823E+38.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_MORRIS_COATOFARMS Kaiser Knuckle Coat of Arms
Morris Baldwin is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Kaiser Knuckle or  Coat of Arms.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Combine Heaven and Cross with the Grave Inheritor of Vampire Killer."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, while playing as  Nathan Graves, the player must create the  Arch Angle union by combining  Grand Cross with  Heaven Sword, unlocking Morris Baldwin as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Morris can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Morris Baldwin can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bloodisthicker".
## Passive bonuses
Morris Baldwin starts with with +100  Max Health, +2  Armor and +20%  Might.
Morris is adept with every weapon in the  Coat of Arms [DLC], including their evolutions. The exceptions to this are the unions  Fuwalafuwaloo,  Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC],  Ninth Circle [DLC], and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC].
Upon taking damage, Morris Baldwin will fire  Kaiser Knuckle [DLC],  Lapiste Tepisto [DLC], and  Bracelet and its evolutions,  Bi-Bracelet and  Tri-Bracelet. This damage is not considered retaliatory damage, and these weapons can fire regardless of their cooldown.
## External links
- Morris Baldwin on the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTHALLOWS
Mortaccio is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Bone.
After obtaining the  Chaos Malachite, Mortaccio can evolve the  Bone into  Anima of Mortaccio upon reaching level 80.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by killing a total of 3,000 Skeletons then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased. Alternatively, he can be unlocked by typing up, up, down, down, left, right, left, right, escape, enter while in main menu, which also gives the player 2,800.
Mortaccio's Halloween skin is unlocked by entering the spell "spoopyseason". This will make the player enter Bone Zone with  Bianca Ramba,  Yatta Cavallo, Mortaccio, or  O'Sole Meeo in their Halloween costume. It is not required to reach 30:00 in the Bone Zone to have the skin unlocked, so the player can exit the stage at any time they like in order to do so.
## Passive bonuses
Mortaccio gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 60. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
The following table shows how much Amount he gains by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60+ | +3
After the  Chaos Malachite has been obtained, Mortaccio's weapon can be evolved into  Anima of Mortaccio upon reaching level 80, morphing him into a  Goshadokuro. He also gains +2  Armor, +1  Amount, and +100  Max Health.
