 [Mortaccio as seen in the cover art of Emerald Diorama (far right).]
 Mortaccio as seen in the cover art of Emerald Diorama (far right).
## Trivia
- The secret code for unlocking him is the famous Konami Code.
- Mortaccio,  Cavallo,  Ramba, and  O'Sole have identical passives and stats and only differ in starting weapons.
- Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, O'Sole, and  Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent  Duplicator in the base game at level 60 with 2 levels of  Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +6 projectiles to all applicable weapons. (This is not including  Gav'Et-Oni ★, a DLC character that can get even more persistent  Amount)
-  Sir Ambrojoe gains +10  Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul,  Horse, and  Simondo Belmont can get one more persistent Amount by reaching level 80.
- "Li mortacci tua" is an Italian (Rome) phrase, often used as an expression of anger or frustration, it is considered a strong and vulgar phrase. Breaking it down, "Li" is a plural form of "il" (the), "mortacci" is the plural form of "mortaccio," and "tua" means "your." "Mortaccio" is a derogatory term used in Italian slang and can be translated as "dead ancestor" or "cursed ancestor." It is an offensive way of insulting someone by referring to their deceased relatives in a negative manner.
- Morphed Mortaccio's name is "Soul of Mortaccio", which can be translated to anima di mortaccio, is used in a variant of the expression "Li mortacci tua" that is "L'anima de li mortacci tua" ("the soul of your cursed ancestors").
- Mortaccio is the first of the "Amount" characters to gain the ability to evolve his main weapon by reaching level 80 after collecting a relic.
- Prior to v1.3.0, Mortaccio's Halloween skin would be persistent after unlocking and revert once exiting the game.
- Mortaccio bears great resemblance to the skeleton summoned via Monster Vial 3 in Symphony of the Night. The similarity extends to their method of attack, as both Mortaccio and Monster Vial 3 use bones as thrown weapons.
- In the animation featured in the Steam page of the Vampire Survivors Soundtrack, there is a band of 4 characters that look like Mortaccio. However, these are not really Mortaccios or potential skins or Mortaccio, but rather separate characters, as shown in the Bitrate-Killer teaser. The characters are:
- Tooa (the singer)
- Ala'Facha (the guitarist)
- Lee Meyo (the bassist)
- De Peeppo (the drummer)
- The "To End an Ice Age" adventure informs us that Mortaccio and  Bianca Ramba were in a relationship before their death, with the descriptions of the Ice Cream and A Fiery Apparition stages making reference to this.

Mr. S is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Intuition.
## Unlocking
Mr. S can be unlocked by defeating  Living Anguish with  Diva No. 5 in Inverse Emerald Diorama.
Alternatively, Mr. S can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "tutelageregimen".
## Passive bonuses
Mr. S starts with +20%  Move Speed. He gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80 (up to +4 amount).
The following table shows how much Amount he gains by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4
Additionally, Mr. S will trigger the techs of weapons from Emerald Diorama with every attack once they are glimmered, instead of the techs being placed into rotation.
Mr. S will also trigger Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- While Mr. S will trigger a tech with every weapon activation after the tech is first glimmered, this still helps Mr. S glimmer that tech for the first time.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.

OnRevival: LastBreath ability can award an extra Darkana (max 3).","cost":"3000","duration":"-","unlock":"Get Descent Into Misery to level 9.","secret":"no","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Nameless Fool","movespeed":"+20%","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","coffin":"no","dlc":"bm","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"NAMELESSFOOL","flight":"no","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","alias":"Fool","theme":"Lying Fairy Tales","skin id":"DEFAULT","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_NamelessFool","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Descent Into Misery"}
Nameless Fool is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Descent Into Misery.
## Unlocking
Nameless Fool can be unlocked by getting  Descent Into Misery to level 9. They need to be purchased for 3,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Nameless Fool can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "namelesssecret".
Dying a total of 7 times as Nameless Fool unlocks  Congregation as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Nameless Fool starts with +20%  Move Speed.
On Last Breath, Nameless Fool gains 5 seconds of invulnerability, their  Max Health is halved, and they gain a Darkana. Nameless Fool can normally only gain Darkana if they currently have fewer Arcana than the normal Arcana limit (3, excluding ones from the merchant and  Moonlight Bolero (VI) as those add to the limit when obtained). However, using a  Revival, or acquiring or leveling up the  Velvet Dodecahedron [DLC], will allow Nameless Fool to gain a Darkana regardless of the aforementioned Arcana limit. A maximum of 3 Darkanas can be gained from Nameless Fool's passive ability.
Each Darkana has an equal weight when being given, with the exception of  Stake to Your Heart (0), which only has a 1% chance of being granted, unaffected by  Luck. 
- If Stake to Your Heart (0), or any other "Rare Darkanas," of which Stake to Your Heart (0) is currently the only one, has, or have, been obtained, then all other "Rare Darkanas" will have their chance to be granted scale based on the number of remaining Darkanas that can be drafted, up to a maximum of 10%.
## Tips
- The randomized nature of Nameless Fool's ability can make it difficult to build around, and this will become more true as new Darkanas are added. There are effectively two ways of playing the Fool: recklessly rushing to one's own death in the early game so as to max out on Darkana early, or playing as normal and treating the ability as a bonus rather than something to actively strive towards. Especially if doing the latter,  Hail from the Future (X) is invaluable, as it allows the Fool to play around the randomized nature of their ability by using the  Candyboxes and  Arma Dio spawned by Hail From The Future to pick up weapons based on the Darkana received. For instance, fire weapons for  Heir of Fate (XIX) or freezing weapons for  Crystal Cries (XII). With some luck, this can be obtained from the Fool's own ability.  Wicked Season (XIII), always a useful Arcana, lets the Fool get more Hail from the Future drops (and makes death more likely in the early game, which may be to the Fool's advantage).
-  Awake (IV) provides some extra Revival to acquire more Darkana and makes the Fool slightly stronger with every death.
- As both the Fool's starting weapon and their main ability involve the loss of max HP, it is absolutely crucial for the Fool to find some way of increasing this stat, lest they be left with such low health that any contact with an enemy spells certain doom.  Heir of Fate (XIX),  Crystal Cries (XII) and/or  Victorian Horror (XVIII), all potentially obtainable through the Fool's ability, can do the job.  Hollow Heart also helps somewhat.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Starts and is Adept with an array of holy weapons, depending on their availability in the Collection, Lastbreath: triggers a Rosary.","cost":"3000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeat Ashtart, Sargon, Congregation, and Baal in a single run.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Nameless Saint","movespeed":"-","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"bm","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"+8","greed":"-","id":"NAMELESSSAINT","flight":"No","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","theme":"Brave Fairy Tales","skin id":"DEFAULT","alias":"Saint","speed":"-","controller":"CharacterController_BMN_NamelessSaint","release":"28 August 2026","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":""}
Nameless Saint is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. They have no starting weapon, instead beginning with various weapons that have been unlocked.
## Unlocking
Nameless Saint can be unlocked by defeating  Ashtart,  Sargon,  Congregation, and  Baal'Thasar in a single run.
Alternatively, Nameless Saint can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "namelessspell".
## Passive bonuses
Nameless Saint starts with +20%  Luck and +8  Banish.
Upon starting a run, Nameless Saint will start with different weapons depending on what is available in the collection:
- Their first weapon is either  108 Bocce or  Cross. If 108 Bocce is in the collection, then they will not start with Cross.
- If  108 Responsive Prayers [DLC] has been obtained, they will start with it.
- If  Refectio [DLC] has been obtained, they will start with it. Otherwise, they will start with  Laurel.
If none of the above weapons have been collected, then Nameless Saint will not have a starting weapon.
Nameless Saint is adept with:
-  Cross and  Heaven Sword
-  Laurel
-  108 Bocce [DLC]
-  108 Responsive Prayers [DLC]
-  Road to Heaven [DLC]
Note that "holy weapons", unlike  Blood damage and  Fire damage, are not considered their own damage type and thus do not have their own tag.
As a Last Breath skill, Nameless Saint triggers a  Rosary.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_QUINCY_2MSSKIN_TP_QUINCY_2SB Sonic Dash Morning Star Belnades' Spellbook
Nathan Graves is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Sonic Dash. 
Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook and  Morning Star, Nathan is able to start with either weapon as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Nathan can be unlocked by evolving the  Sonic Whip after obtaining the  Black Disk. He is then made available for purchase for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Nathan can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "cardsharp".
## Passive bonuses
Nathan starts with +120  Max Health, +1  Armor, and -5%  Cooldown. He also starts with an extra  Awake (IV).
Upon reaching 7:00 and 14:00, Nathan will be given an extra selection of an Arcana, much like that at the beginning of the run. This allows Nathan to obtain a total of 6 (7 in Inverse Mode) Arcana throughout a run, including the extra Awake (IV) he starts with.
Nathan can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Nathan is from the game Castlevania: Circle of the Moon. The game is considered non canon due to it not being made by the main Castlevania team of the time.
- Nathan starting with the Awake (IV) Arcana is a reference to the song of the same name that is featured in his game, also is his default theme in Vampire Survivors.
- His unlocking spell "cardsharp" is a reference to the DSS (Dual Set-Up System) featured in his home game, where combining the effects of magical cards gives Nathan special abilities. His passive ability of gaining extra Arcanas is also based on this system.
- His starting weapon, Sonic Dash is based off of the Sonic Dash ability found in Castlevania: Circle of the Moon and various subsequent games.
- Due to how DSS cards were randomly dropped by specific enemies, it is possible for a player to never aquire the combo necessary to use the Sonic Dash.
## External links
- Nathan Graves on the Castlevania Wiki

Newt Plissken is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Diver Mines.
## Unlocking
Newt Plissken can be unlocked for purchase by finding 21  Grenades. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Newt Plissken can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "chiefsalamander".
## Passive bonuses
Newt starts with +40  Max Health, +50%  Move Speed, and +20%  Luck.
Newt gains +1%  Might per +1%  Speed, up to a max of +900% Might.
He can find Explosive Barrels that deal 60 Base Damage to an area around it when they are destroyed. Multiple Explosive Barrels can sometimes spawn next to each other at the same time.
Newt can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Due to the uncapped nature of Newt's ability  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) also acts as a +1% Might every level.
## External links
- Newt Plissken at the Contra Wiki

DEFAULTHALLOWS
O'Sole Meeo is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.5.1. Its starting weapon is  Celestial Dusting.
After obtaining the  Chaos Altemanna, O'Sole Meeo can evolve  Celestial Dusting into  Profusione D'Amore upon reaching level 80.
## Unlocking
O'Sole Meeo is unlocked by killing a total of 3,000  Dragon Shrimps.
Dragon Shrimp, Flame Dragon Shrimps, Serpentine Dragon Shrimp, and Serpentine Flame Dragon Shrimp all count toward the kill requirement. However, the boss versions of Dragon Shrimp, the Colossal Bone Dragon, the Colossal Flame Dragon, and Serpentine Skeleton Dragons, which appear in The Bone Zone, do not.
Once unlocked, O'Sole can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, O'Sole can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "reset".
O'Sole's Halloween skin is unlocked by entering the Spell "spoopyseason". This will make the player enter Bone Zone with  Bianca Ramba,  Yatta Cavallo,  Mortaccio, or O'Sole Meeo in their respective Halloween costume. It is not required to reach 30:00 in the Bone Zone to have the skin unlocked, so the player can exit the stage at any time they like in order to do so.
## Passive bonuses
O'Sole gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 60. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
The following table shows how much Amount it gains by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60+ | +3
After obtaining  Chaos Altemanna, O'Sole will morph into a shimmying humanoid with a sun-shaped head, with water and flowers spawning beneath them and its weapon evolving into  Profusione D'Amore upon reaching level 80. It also gains +2  Armor, +1  Amount, and +100  Max Health.
## Tips
- Once per run, typing "pet" while using O'Sole summons a ring of little hearts that heals it. (On console hold LT + RT)
- O'Sole cannot be damaged by the plants in Il Molise, mainly to compensate for the fact that its weapon can only fire backwards and cannot actually deal damage to the plants.

## Other
 - 
 [Pin set of O'Sole Meeo with the  Celestial Dusting.]
 Pin set of O'Sole Meeo with the  Celestial Dusting.
## Trivia
- The name most likely references the popular Neapolitan song "'O sole mio".
- This character is likely a reference to Amaterasu, the protagonist of the Ōkami game series. She is the Japanese goddess of the sun who appears in the form of a white wolf and uses the Celestial Brush to conjure her godly powers, as well as spawns plants when running, just like the weapon  Celestial Dusting. In addition, O'Sole Meeo's theme song, which is also used by  Giovanna, uses traditional Japanese/Asian instruments.
- "O'Sole Mio" literally translates to "My Sunshine", adding another layer of reference to the Goddess of the Sun.
- The spell to unlock O'Sole is "Reset", which is the name of the song that plays during Okami's credits.
- O'Sole transforming into a sun-entity seems to reaffirm its connection to Amaterasu.
- In the official video for "Love Profusion", which inspired the name Profusione D'Amore for the evolution of Celestial Dusting, Madonna can be seen walking surrounded by flowers, sprouting from the ground around her, waving her arms above her head as she dances and walks. This may be the inspiration for the morphed O'sole, a walking sun-figure waving its arms over its head while flowers sprout around it, on top of referencing Amaterasu's running particle effect.
- While O'Sole and Giovanna's theme songs are the same, O'Sole's theme song differs slightly from Giovanna's in that it starts off at normal speed, whereas Giovanna's theme song starts off slower.
- O'Sole,  Mortaccio,  Cavallo, and  Ramba all have identical passives and stats and only differ in starting weapons.
- O'Sole, Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, and  Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the base game at level 60 with 2 levels of Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +6 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe gains +10 Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul,  Horse, and  Simondo Belmont can get one more persistent Amount by reaching level 80.
- Prior to v1.3.0, O'Sole's Halloween skin would be persistent after unlocking and revert once exiting the game.
- O'Sole Meeo has an official pin set. The pin set also includes its starting weapon,  Celestial Dusting.
- It is one of the four characters to have an official pin or pin set, alongside  Queen Sigma,  Krochi Freetto, and  Giovanna Grana.

DEFAULTSKIN_KOOLIO_DOLPHIN_SPEEDSKIN_KOOLIO_SHARK_MIGHTSKIN_KOOLIO_SQUID_AMOUNTSKIN_KOOLIO_CRAB_AREASKIN_KOOLIO_JELLY_DURATIONSKIN_KOOLIO_SWAP_1 Penshin Fatcha Tonno Subito Tonnado Tonn'omoto Tonn'oddeeo Tonne Unsurpassed
Para Kooleo is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.15. Their starting weapon is  Penshin Fatcha.
## Unlocking
Para Kooleo can be unlocked by surviving 20 minutes in The Lycaeum.
Alternatively, Para Kooleo can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "fastisfortuna".
## Passive bonuses
Para Kooleo and all of his skins start with +30%  Move Speed and +30%  Duration.
Para Kooleo has an "HP-Critical" skill that activates as soon as his HP drops below 20% of his  Max Health, which triggers a  Vacuum. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
- If the player has evolved Penshin Fatcha at least once, this Vacuum will be replaced with a Lavatrix Machina.
Whenever Para Kooleo evolves a weapon, his hidden  Acquazzone will trigger for 7 seconds. This Acquazzone has a base damage of 60 and a base  Amount of 12. Whenever it is triggered, this Acquazzone gains 6 base damage.
When playing as one of Para Kooleo's alternate skins, his starting weapon loses the ability to evolve at the exchange of Limit Break being far more effective. Each level of Limit Break will be five times as effective and every weapon can also receive +2  Amount (Max 20) as a potential bonus.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
VIII - Edge of the Earth: Trigger a Lavatrix Machina with Para Kooleo.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Edge of the Earth (VIII).
## Tips
- Para Kooleo works best with a limited number of weapons and  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX). This allows him to dedicate all chests obtained during a run into evolving  Penshin Fatcha, getting it to absurd power levels further boosted by Silent Old Sanctuary. This also applies to his alternate forms thanks to their enhanced limit breaks. Aside from that, however, the base form's optimal playstyle differs greatly from that of the alternate forms:
- To maximize the base form's power, it is recommended to play on a stage like Ode to Castlevania, where the plentiful evolution chests available from a massive number of bosses can turn Penshin Fatcha into a juggernaut of a weapon. For even more chests, it is recommended to get  Moonlight Bolero (VI) relatively early.
- On the other hand, chests are mostly wasted on the alternate forms, since they don't evolve; instead, the priority should be maximizing experience gain with  Crown,  Wicked Season (XIII), etc.

## Trivia
- Para Kooleo is based off of the kelpie, a mythical shape-shifting aquatic spirit of Scottish folklore.
- Para Kooleo's Tonn'oddeeo sprite has a spiked shell that makes it resemble Slowbro from the Pokémon franchise.
- The name Para Kooleo's is a pun on the Italian term "paraculo", an informal way of describing someone who always looks out for their own interests (para = protect, culo = buttocks).
- Para Kooleo's unlock spell "fastisfortuna" seems to be a pun on the Latin phrase "Fortis fortuna adiuvat", meaning "fortune favors the bold". The modified phrase here can instead be read as "fast is for tuna", alluding to the character's tuna-based weapon and to this fish's notorious breakneck speed.

## Unlocking

## Passive bonuses

Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Il Molise and use Celestial Dusting to attack the enemy plants, healing them instead. Upon restoring a total of 100,000 health to the plants, Peppino is unlocked as a playable character. Playing as  O'Sole Meeo can make this trivial, as it does not take damage from the plants. Beginning (X) can help speed up the process.
Once unlocked, Peppino can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Peppino can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "pinociampino".
## Passive bonuses
Peppino starts with +20  Max Health, +2  Armor, -100%  Move Speed, -60%  Area, and +100%  Magnet. It gains +10% Area on levels 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, and 21.
| Levels | Area
| 1 | -60%
| 2 | -50%
| 3-4 | -40%
| 5-7 | -30%
| 8-12 | -20%
| 13-20 | -10%
| 21+ | 0%
## Tips
- Peppino starts with -100%  Move Speed, meaning that without any speed powerups, it is unable to move. While playing as a stationary character might seem like a fun challenge, starting with extra Move Speed from PowerUps is advised, which will at least allow it to slowly crawl to collect chests.
-  Wings can be a useful pickup to help boost Peppino's speed.
- The  Sonic Dash provides a useful way to help Peppino move around, due to providing a speed boost when active.
-  Attractorb is one of the most important pickups for Peppino as early as possible, as it allows it to collect EXP gems in a large area without needing to move.
- As such, one should focus on getting Evolutions that use the Attractorb. Of these,  J'Odore can be useful to pull stronger enemies away from Peppino, while  Vol Umbra can be incredibly strong thanks to scaling with Magnet.
- As a result of Attractorb's critical benefit, Peppino's native Magnet boost, and its starting weapon being Soul Eater,  Blood Astronomia (XXI) is a good early arcana for this character.
- For the same reasons,  Victorian Horror (XVIII) can be a good pick, though the increase in  Curse can end up doing more harm than good in the long run. It's better to pick it up later into the run.
- Because Peppino has high armor but no ability to avoid enemies,  Divine Bloodline (IX) is another viable Arcana for its retaliatory damage. However, it should be noted that Divine Bloodline (IX) does not work with Soul Eater, only its pre-evolved form, Garlic. Therefore, while this Arcana is technically viable, its actual usefulness will be somewhat limited.
-  Mad Groove (VIII) can help Peppino compensate for its nearly nonexistent speed by gathering all stage items and chests around it. However, be careful not to grab the  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left, or  Metaglio Right too early, as Peppino's awful movement speed means it absolutely must kill the Atlanteans very quickly or it will die.
- In stages where The Stalker naturally spawns, like The Bone Zone, Peppino needs to grab a  Rosary or  Victory Sword before that happens or die, as it has no way of escaping from the creeping reaper.
- If unlocked, picking up the  Edge of the Earth (VIII) darkana would modify its Move Speed to +100%, making the Peppino vastly more mobile than it would be at default Move Speed.

## Trivia
- Peppino gains Area bonus for every level in the Fibonacci sequence, which is often associated in nature with plants. The next term for this sequence is the sum of the last two term, for example, the final level it receives the bonus is at 21, which equal to 8 + 13 from the last two level threshold. However, Peppino stopped getting more stats at further terms of the sequence, which would have been at level 34 (13 + 21) and so on.
- Peppino has one single sprite and is the only character without animation when moving. However, Peppino's December skin has an idle animation, where its ornaments light up.
- Peppino is the first character to have its height exceed the top border of their character selection button.
- Peppino has the same depiction as one of the variants of the  Cute Molisano.
- The name is likely a pun[note] on Peppino Turco, the author of the lyrics to "Funiculì, Funiculà", the basis for "Vempair Survaivors", which is the default theme for Il Molise and the track used for almost all Vampire Survivors gameplay trailers.
- Peppino has eight fruits on its default skin. On its December skin, it has 17 Christmas LEDs.

Can equip up to 100 Passive Weapons.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Gros Michel.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+7","name":"Perkeo","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"ac","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"LEM_PERKEO","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"Ante Chamber - Balatro Main Theme","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"LEM_CharacterController_002","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Celestial Booster"}
Perkeo is a playable character from the Ante Chamber DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Celestial Booster.
## Unlocking
Perkeo can be unlocked by evolving the  Gros Michel into the  Cavendish, with a max level  Outer Saboteur.
Alternatively, Perkeo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "doublenegative".
## Passive bonuses
Perkeo starts with +20  Max Health, +20%  Luck, and +7  Reroll.
Perkeo is able to find  Survarots from Light Sources. 
- Notably, Perkeo does not start with a draft, unlike  Jimbo [DLC],  Chicot [DLC], and  Secretino Dagsson.
Perkeo also has 100 slots for Passive Weapons. This allows him to carry every Passive Weapon in the game without issue.
## Trivia
- His spell "doublenegative" is a reference to his joker effect in Balatro, where he creates a Negative copy of a random consumable that is currently in the player's possession. The word "double" implies the copying of a Consumable and the word "negative" implies that the copy has a Negative edition. This is also the basis for his 100 passive slots, as Negative cards in Balatro give an additional slot to whatever zone the Negative card is in, effectively gaining additional slots.
- Prior to v1.15, either using this character, entering the Ante Chamber stage, using  Secretino Dagsson or any of the other Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.

Persephone is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Summon Spirit Tornado.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Send Simon on a windy quest beneath a gorgeous moon."

To unlock Persephone, the player must obtain the  Venus Crescent [DLC] union, being that of  Gorgeous Moon and  Summon Spirit Tornado [DLC], as  Simon Belmont.
Once unlocked, Persephone can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Persephone can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "vaccuumproserpina".
## Passive bonuses
Persephone starts with +20%  Move Speed and +100%  Magnet.
Persephone is equipped with a hidden Rumba that sucks in all pickups, stage items, enemies, and light sources towards her from the direction she is facing. Its area of effect is a thin rectangle in front of her that stretches to the edge of the screen.
Persephone is also able to destroy light sources by touching them.

Poe Ratcho is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.7. His starting weapon is  Garlic.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock Poe by getting Garlic to level 7. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Poe can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "strongestcharacter".
## Retired Poe Ratcho
Completing the Adventure A Garlic Paradise for the first time unlocks an alternative skin named Retired Poe Ratcho.
Alternatively, Retired Poe can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the Spell "retirepoe".
## Passive bonuses
Poe starts with -30  Max Health and +25%  Magnet.
## Retired Poe Ratcho
Compared to Poe, Retired Poe starts with +30 Max Health and +5  Recovery. He also starts with a level 5  Pummarola.
## Tips
- Retired Poe has a massive amount of Recovery which is further boosted by  Pummarola. This makes him one of the best users of  Impostongue in the game, although he cannot compete with  Cosmo Pavone.
- Because  Garlic and  Soul Eater have the additional benefit of reducing enemy resistance to freezing, a freezing build with  Crystal Cries (XII) can be a powerful option. Crystal Cries boosts Poe's regeneration even further, synergizing with his key weapon Impostongue.

## Trivia
- Poe Ratcho is a homophone of "poraccio", dialectal form from Rome for the Italian "poveraccio", meaning "poor wretch".
- A running gag about Poe, especially in poncle's social media, is about him having a bad garlic smell.
- The description of the Adventure stage, Retirement, calls Poe a "malodorous hero."
- His dialogue as a vendor in Adventure Mode, "Can I offer you a clove in this trying time?" is a reference to Danny DeVito's character Frank Reynolds in the American sitcom It's Always Sunny in Philadelphia.
- The Retired Poe Ratcho skin may be a reference to the  Master Librarian, as they are both old men and in rocking chairs.
- Poe Ratcho (in retired form) can be seen at the bottom of the animation featured in the Steam page of the Vampire Survivors Soundtrack.
- On poncle's YouTube channel, there is a series of videos starring Poe, titled Sopravvissuti ai vampiri. The videos' titles are written in Italian, which oftentimes translate to a new update, feature, release, or DLC being available. In each of those videos, there is a random character, sitting on the right, and talking with Poe, while Poe is always sitting on the left side on the table, and between the characters, there is at least one item, usually a food item, such as the  Wall Chicken or the  Cheese.
- In general, Poe Ratcho is the character who appears in poncle's videos, trailers, and social media the most.
- In the video "chicot seduto su una sedia - sopravvissuti ai vampiri - Vampire Survivors", Poe confirms that he is from Molise, Italy.
- One of the variants of the  Dead Molisano has a similar depiction to Poe, but with the color scheme of the Dead Molisano or  Bianca Ramba.
## In other media
- Poe Ratcho is one of the three characters to appear in the Friends of Jimbo update of the poker roguelike game Balatro. This update allows customization of face cards, replacing them with characters from other games and IPs, including Vampire Survivors.
- Poe Ratcho is depicted in the form of the Jack of Clubs.
- The two other characters who appear in Balatro are Bisconte Draculó and  Queen Sigma, who are depicted as the King of Clubs and the Queen of Clubs, respectively.
- Poe also makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. He is a reskin of Rusty in that game.
- In Rusty's Retirement, his sprite is the same as the default skin in Vampire Survivors, except that he is wearing the hat used for his retired skin.
- Poe makes an appearance in the business simulation game Two Point Museum, as part of that game's update that includes weapons, items, and points of interest from Vampire Survivors.
- This makes him the only character from Vampire Survivors to appear in every external game collaboration so far.

DEFAULTLEGACY
Poppea Pecorina is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.3.0. Her starting weapon is  Song of Mana.
## Unlocking
Poppea is unlocked for free by finding her  Coffin in the Dairy Plant. 
Alternatively, Poppea can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "feldschlacht". This will require the player to purchase her for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Poppea starts with +20%  Move Speed. She gains +1%  Duration every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
## Tips
- Poppea has little bonus at the start of the game and her passive ability is outclassed by  Dommario until level 41. The uncapped nature of this bonus means weapons like  King Bible,  Santa Water, and  Song of Mana can be extremely effective at high levels.

## Trivia
- The dress of Poppea's legacy skin has the shade of a rainbow, but much darker and slightly greenish.
- "Poppea" was a real name in ancient Rome but is now a joke about breasts or being busty ("poppe" means "boobs" in some Italian dialects). This might then explain why she is found in Dairy Plant.
- Poppea may also be a reference to L'incoronazione di Poppea, the final opera written by the Italian Renaissance composer Claudio Monteverdi, which may also allude to how her starting weapon is the  Song of Mana.
- "Pecorina" is similar to the word "pecorino", a cheese made from sheep's milk. It is also the Italian name for the sexual position for doggystyle.
- Poppea shares several traits with other coffin characters. See Coffin § Traits for more details.
- Poppea was the first coffin character added to the game.
## In other media
- Poppea Pecorina makes an appearance in the idle farming simulation game Rusty's Retirement, as part of that game's update that includes Vampire Survivors content. She is a reskin of Sonnet in that game.

Porta LadonnaPorta Ladonna (Legacy)Permanent +30% Area. Starts with temporary cooldown bonus.
Permanent +30% Area. Starts with temporary cooldown bonus.
DEFAULTLEGACY
Porta Ladonna is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.0. Her starting weapon is  Lightning Ring.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by getting the Lightning Ring to level 4, then purchasing this character with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Porta can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "vivaladonna".
## Passive bonuses
Porta starts with +30%  Area. Additionally, Porta starts with -90%  Cooldown, however, the bonus depreciates by 30% each level, until the bonus is entirely gone at level 4.
| Level | Cooldown
| 1 | -90%
| 2 | -60%
| 3 | -30%
| 4+ | 0%
## Tips
- Due to  Lightning Ring's random nature and long cooldown, it is normally not a very reliable weapon in the early game, however, this is greatly mitigated by the temporary Cooldown bonus. It is often better avoid collecting XP at the start to maintain the bonus longer rather than pick up another weapon immediately. When you think it is time to proceed, pick up the accumulated Experience Gems for multiple level ups at once.
- Picking the  Game Killer (0) as a starting Arcana locks Porta at level 1 and allows her to keep her Cooldown bonus indefinitely. This can be useful on stages that already contain a  Duplicator, allowing Porta to evolve her Lightning Ring, or other powerful weapons and items, alternatively, weapons can be bought from the Merchant or the Master Librarian.
- If  Sword Brothers is obtained while Porta is locked at level 1 with Game Killer, she becomes permanently invincible for the rest of the game, as the weapon will fire faster than its invulnerability frames run out. Since this can be achieved right out of the gate in stages where the Master Librarian is present, this makes Game Killer-locked Porta one of the most powerful builds in the entire game. Banishing the final level of Sword Brothers or sealing  Vjaya Sisters is recommended, as Vjaya Sisters does not provide full invulnerability.

 [Official Ode to Castlevania pre-release trailer model sheet.]
 Official Ode to Castlevania pre-release trailer model sheet.
## Trivia
- "porta" is Italian for "gate", "door" or even "bring", and "la donna" (with the space) is Italian for "the woman".
- Porta's complete name is "Porta Ladonna", which when combined with  Arca's name closely resembles the Italian expression "Porca Madonna", a blasphemy which heavily insults the Virgin Mary relating her to a sow.
- Porta used to have  Peachone as her starting weapon.
- Porta's unlocking spell "viva la donna" is a joyful praise to women in general.

| Probotector [DLC]
| Armour[sic] increases Might. Can find special pickups.

Can find special pickups.","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Finding 14 Barriers","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Probotector","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"200","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"+20%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_PROBO","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"+1","theme":"GTR Attack! - VS version","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"FB_Probo","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Prism Lass"}
Probotector is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Prism Lass.
## Unlocking
Probotector can be unlocked for purchase by finding 14  Barriers. He needs to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Probotector can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rdooeight".
## Passive bonuses
Probotector starts with +100  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +20%  Might.
He gains +10% Might per +1 Armor.
He can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
-  Trinum Custodem is the most important weapon for Probotector, giving up to 20 Armor which translates to a massive +200% Might. That goes up to 250% Might with  Armor (item).
-  Claimh Solais [DLC] gives up to +100% Might on its own (between the Might and the Armor that it provides).
- Weapons that retaliate synergize with the plan of getting as much Armor as possible.  Divine Bloodline (IX) can also be a strong pick.
## External links
- Probotector at the Contra Wiki

LEGACYDEFAULT
Pugnala Provola is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.3.2. Her starting weapons are  Phiera Der Tuphello and  Eight The Sparrow.
## Unlocking
Pugnala is unlocked for free after finding and opening her  Coffin in the Mad Forest. 
Alternatively, Pugnala can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "flymetothemoon". This will require the player to purchase her for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Pugnala starts with +20%  Move Speed. She gains +1%  Might every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
## Tips
- Pugnala has little bonus at the start of the game and her passive ability is very slightly behind  Antonio until level 51. She is better in this regard because she gains  Might more incrementally and benefits from the higher bonus between Antonio's 10 level gap.
- The uncapped nature of her bonus means most weapons will partially catch up to enemy health scaling from leveling up.
- A Multitude of powerful Arcana are useful to Pugnala, Here are the main three:
- Since  Phieraggi's Base Damage and  Amount are determined by  Revival, the +3 Revivals from  Awake (IV) are very beneficial.
-  Gemini (I) will provide two more weapons.
-  Beginning (X) will give +3 Amount to both guns at the start and +3 Amount to  Phieraggi as well.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Legacy Pugnala sprite.]
 Legacy Pugnala sprite.
 -

## Animated sprites

## Other
 - 
 [Pugnala Provola as seen in the pre-release trailer of Emergency Meeting.]
 Pugnala Provola as seen in the pre-release trailer of Emergency Meeting.
 - 
 [Pugnala Provola as seen in the cover art of Emergency Meeting (center).]
 Pugnala Provola as seen in the cover art of Emergency Meeting (center).
## Trivia
- Pugnala's design is inspired by Bayonetta, the main character of the Bayonetta series. This is reflected in the hair for her legacy and default appearances being similar to Bayonetta's hairstyles from the first and second games in the series, and having two pairs of guns that fire in four directions, mirroring Bayonetta's guns she wields in her hands and on her heels (her alternative sprite has a gun visible in each of those locations).
- Pugnala's theme, "Red & Blue," is reminiscent of Bayonetta's jazz themed soundtracks, and also is a reference to the song "Red & Black" from the first Bayonetta game.
- The spell "flymetothemoon", used to unlock Pugnala, shares its name with the song of the same name used in the first Bayonetta game.
- "Pugnala" is Italian for "he/she stabs" or a command for someone to stab.
- "Provola", more specifically "provolone", is a type of cheese.
- There is also an Italian Roman Catholic priest during the 19th century called "Antonio Provolo", referencing  Antonio Belpaese. Both characters have a passive ability that increases  Might.
- Pugnala is the first character to have two starting weapons (not counting  Christine Davain,  Iguana Gallo Valletto, and  Divano Thelma who gain a level at the start).
- Pugnala shares several traits with other coffin characters. See Coffin § Traits for more details.
- Pugnala is one of the three characters whose "Legacy" skin is counted as the default skin. The other characters where that is also the case are  Concetta Caciotta and  Christine Davain.

| {"magnet":"+100%","description":"She owns everything.","cost":"0","duration":"+50%","unlock":"Complete the Collection.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+50%","amount":"+1","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"+108","name":"Queen Sigma","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"333","revival":"+1","curse":"+10%","dlc":"","recovery":"3.00","might":"+50%","area":"-","skip":"+108","banish":"+108","greed":"-","id":"SIGMA","flight":"No","update":"Patch 0.11.0","armor":"+3","theme":"Sole Solution (track)","alias":"Sigma","movespeed":"+50%","controller":"CharacterControllerSigma","release":"18 August 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-25%","weapon":"Victory Sword"}
Queen Sigma is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.11.0. Her starting weapon is the  Victory Sword.
Unlike all other characters, Queen Sigma is completely unaffected by  Golden Eggs, and simply launches them away from her instead of picking them up. When playing as her, Golden Eggs in the Merchant's shop are also replaced with the  Candybox.
## Unlocking
To unlock the Queen Sigma, it is required for the player to have unlocked every item that is listed in the "Version 1.0" section of the Collection menu, with the exception of a few items. This section only includes items available at the game's 1.0 release and not part of any DLCs.
The items in the 1.0 section that are not required to unlock Queen Sigma are:
-  Super Candybox II Turbo
-  Victory Sword
-  Sole Solution
-  Flames of Misspell
-  Ashes of Muspell
Once unlocked, Queen Sigma can be purchased, although the price to purchase her is 0 and does not scale.
Alternatively, Queen Sigma can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "allatonce".
Note: It may be necessary to enter one more game and immediately quit to get the unlock to register.
## Passive bonuses
Queen Sigma starts with +233  Max Health, +3  Recovery, +3  Armor, +50%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, +50%  Duration, -25%  Cooldown, +1  Amount, +1  Revival, +100%  Magnet, +50%  Luck, +10%  Curse, +108  Reroll, +108  Skip, and +108  Banish. She gains +1% Might and +1%  Growth every level. There is no cap on these bonuses.
At level 2, level 3, level 77, and level 108, the player is allowed to pick one Arcana from the full list of Arcanas, rather than picking from four randomly chosen ones. Chests that would normally contain Arcanas are replaced with regular chests when playing as her with three or more Arcanas.
## Tips
- The 1.0 Collection requirement can be identified by objects with a gold border, black background for weapons and passive items, grass background for pickups, and pink background for relics.
- Queen Sigma starts with a high number of Reroll, Skip, and Banish, and is able to buy Candybox from the Merchant. This can make achieving a specific build very easy when playing as her.

 [Spinning pin merch of Queen Sigma.]
 Spinning pin merch of Queen Sigma.
## Trivia
- Queen Sigma sparkles when hit instead of bleeding.
- When defeated, she transforms into a ball of light and launches upwards.
- This includes being defeated by White Hand, making her the only character able to survive this encounter, in a manner of speaking.
- This may be a reference to Meta Knight's defeat animation from the Kirby series.
- Her starting weapon may also be a reference to Meta Knight's Smash Bros appearance, where he strikes multiple times with his neutral attack.
- The enemy version of  Gallo in Gallo Tower likewise teleports away when defeated, albeit with a different animation.
- Queen Sigma is one of the two characters that cost 0, with the other being  Antonio Belpaese.
- According to Luca, the original developer of Vampire Survivors, Queen Sigma was supposed to be a character from a game that he was making but did not finish. Because of this, he decided to include her in Vampire Survivors instead.
- There is official merchandise of Queen Sigma, which is a spinning pin of her.
- She is one of the four characters to have an official pin or pin set, alongside  O'Sole Meeo,  Krochi Freetto, and  Giovanna Grana.
- This is the only pin where the weapon (the  Victory Sword) is not individually featured. It is part of the Queen Sigma pin instead.
- Victory Sword is likely inspired by the Yamato from Devil May Cry
## In other media
- Queen Sigma is one of the three characters to appear in the Friends of Jimbo update of the poker roguelike game Balatro. This update allows customization of face cards, replacing them with characters from other games and IPs, including Vampire Survivors.
- Queen Sigma is depicted in the form of the Queen of Clubs.
- The two other characters who appear in Balatro are Bisconte Draculó and  Poe Ratcho, who are depicted as the King of Clubs and the Jack of Clubs, respectively.

DEFAULTSKIN_TP_QUINCY_2CA Shuriken Coat of Arms
Quincy Morris is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Shuriken [DLC]. 
Upon unlocking the  Coat of Arms [DLC], Quincy will have the choice to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Quincy can be unlocked by evolving the  Platinum Whip after obtaining the  Black Disk. He is then made available for purchase for 2,000, scaling on other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Quincy can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "nonplayercharacter".
## Passive bonuses
Quincy Morris starts with +20%  Move Speed and +4  Revival.
He has a Charge Ability that triggers a  Neutron Bomb upon standing still for a total of 75 seconds. This Charge time is decreased by  Might, down to a minimum of 15 seconds at +400% Might. Upon activating his Charge, Quincy has a 45% chance to lose either 1 health or 35% of his  Max Health, whichever is higher. The chance to take damage upon activating Quincy's Charge is decreased by  Luck. 
Quincy can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Vampire Survivors marks the first appearance of Quincy in any game, as he was only mentioned until now. This is referenced in his spell "nonplayercharacter" and in the hint to unlock Malphas: "Destroy his black wings with the Morris who's[sic] tale is yet untold."
- His design is likely based on Billy Campbell's portrayal of Quincey Morris in the 1992 film Bram Stoker's Dracula.

Random is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.11.0. It can start with any base weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Look under previously owned coffins."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
On any of the normal stages that contain a coffin, opening the coffin a second time unlocks Random as a playable character.
After unlocking, Random can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Random can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "igottagettotheedgeofsoul".
## Passive bonuses
The character is presented with one of eight names, selected at random. The selected name influences the passive bonuses. Below is a table for the stat increases and oscillating stats by name where x is a decimal value between 0 and 1 and y is the multiplier from the character.
## Level Up Bonus
Every level, all stats will increase by a small amount, without cap. If the value of x is low enough, it will decrease the character's stat; for Cooldown, it will increase it. 
| Stat | Base Formula | Aren Gijus | Titto | Chalanda | SHAtsung | Marionello | Birky | Zylaz | Mi Kujino
| Max Health | 1*(x−0.025)*y | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5
| Recovery | 0.01*(x−0.5)*y | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5
| Armor | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 0.5
| Amount | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 0.5
| Revival | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 0.5
| Magnet | 0.03*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 0.5
| Speed | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 1 | 1 | 0.5 | 1
| Move Speed | 0.01*(x−0.2)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 1 | 1 | 0.5 | 1
| Might | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5
| Cooldown | −0.005*(x−0.05)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1
| Area | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5
| Duration | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5
| Luck | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 0.5 | 1 | 1
| Growth | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1
| Greed | 0.01*(x−0.1)*y | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1
| Curse | 0.01*(x−0.25)*y | 1 | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 1 | 1
## Oscillating Stats
Each level up changes the value of the maximum and minimum value of the oscillating multiplier and how long the cycle lasts. The maximum and minimum value both use the formula 2 * (0.1 + x) * y, ranging from 0.2 * y to 2.2 * y; the timer uses 60 * (0.1 + x), ranging from 6 seconds to 66 seconds. All five possible stats that can oscillate ( Might,  Speed,  Duration,  Area, and  Cooldown) share the same y value based on the character.
Note: The "maximum" value can be lower than the "minimum", it just represents the character starts with that value first.
| | Aren Gijus | Titto | Chalanda | SHAtsung | Marionello | Birky | Zylaz | Mi Kujino
| Max | 1 | 1 | 1.5 | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1
| Min | 1 | 1.5 | 1 | 0.5 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1
## Tips
- Any base weapon can appear on Random, including the individual guns Phiera Der Tuphello and Eight The Sparrow, as well as the non-damage-dealing weapons  Clock Lancet and  Laurel. Random will not be granted a bonus level at the start to account for this.
- The only way to solve this issue is to use Arcanas with offensive capability like Heart of Fire (XIX), Sarabande of Healing (VI), Divine Bloodline (IX), Blood Astronomia (XXI), Game Killer (0), or Out of Bounds (XII) (Clock Lancet only).
- Random does not benefit from the stat oscillation from Chaos in the Dark Night (V), Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII), and Lost & Found Painting (XVII) as they require the character to not have value in the sineStat attributes to set their own value while the changing stats already occupied them. It does not override them either. However, it can still benefit from the additional stats every level.
## Notes
- The random (re-)selection of Random's name and its starting base weapon will trigger upon re-entering the character selection menu (or in other words, either exiting from the character menu or the stage selection menu to the main menu and back to the character selection menu.)
## Trivia
- Some of the names are references to things that involve randomness or characters that have shapeshifting or transformation abilities.
- The name "Aren Gijus" is a play on RNGesus.
- This name, in particular, is chosen for Random's ID.
- The name "Titto" is likely referencing Ditto from the Pokémon series, who can use the move Transform to turn into an opposing Pokémon.
- The name "SHAtsung" is likely referencing Shang Tsung from the Mortal Kombat series, a shapeshifting sorcerer who can turn into other Mortal Kombat "kombatants" (combatants), and the Secure Hash Algorithms (SHA), which generate a nearly-random, nearly-unique number for a given digital input.
- The name "Birky" is likely referencing Kirby from the series of the same name, who can inhale creatures and then copy their abilities.
- The name "Zylaz" is an anagram of "Alias" with some letters replaced with homophones.
- The name "Mi Kujino" is an anagram of Mokujin, a character from Bandai Namco's fighting game series Tekken, a wooden dummy that mimics the movesets of most characters in the game, with some exceptions or minor changes depending on the specific game (such as wings when mimicking Angel or Devil, or the weapon wielded as Yoshimitsu or Kunimitsu). The name is also a play on words on the Italian "Mio Cugino", meaning "My Cousin".
- The spell cast to unlock Random (igottagettotheedgeofsoul) is taken from the lyrics in the opening-trailer of Soul Edge, another fighting-game franchise by Bandai Namco.
- In the Results screen, Random's name will simply appear as "Random" instead of any of the random names that are chosen for it.

Reinhardt Schneider is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Tyrfing.
After unlocking the  Morning Star,  Spectral Sword, or  Coat of Arms, Reinhardt is able to start with either weapon as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Reinhardt Schneider is unlocked by opening the fourth  Coffin in Ode to Castlevania map after obtaining  Black Disk. 
Alternatively, Reinhardt Schneider can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "upstartweakling".
## Passive bonuses
Reinhardt starts with +40  Max Health, and +20%  Might. He also starts with an extra  Slash (XVI).
Reinhardt has an extra +1 Critical Damage multiplier.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
-  Claimh Solais [DLC] provides even more critical damage. In addition, the Omni granted by  Torrona's Box is more effective at increasing the critical damage of  Rune Sword compared to the other relevant pickups.
- Some ways of increasing or scaling  Luck are recommended in order to maximize the chance of landing a critical hit.  Wicked Season (XIII) is the most powerful of these, as it continuously scales Luck throughout the entire game while also providing other benefits.
- Not only will Reinhardt increase the damage of  Cavendish [DLC], but his synergy with critical hits also benefits from the double crit chance this weapon provides.
- Reinhardt can output some of the highest numbers with the  Stamazza, and can start with  Mace if the Spectral Sword starting weapon is selected, which can be useful if one wants to do a limited-slots run with Stamazza and  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX).

Richter Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Platinum Whip, but he also has the option of starting with the  Morning Star provided it has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
Richter Belmont can be unlocked by evolving the  Guardian's Targe.
Once unlocked, Richter Belmont can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Richter Belmont can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "doesnthaveuno".
## Passive bonuses
Richter starts with +20%  Move Speed, -10%  Cooldown, and starts with an extra  Divine Bloodline (IX).
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
While playing as him in the Ode to Castlevania stage, a unique chamber connected by a staircase appears outside the very top of the Clock Tower. Entering this chamber despawns all enemies on the screen and begins a special cutscene with  Dracula which serves as the prelude to the final boss battle of the Ode to Castlevania DLC.

- Richter Belmont is one of the very few characters in Vampire Survivors who has voice acting, alongside Vlad Tepes Dracula and Death. He is voiced by SungWon Cho, who is also the voice for Death.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Increases enemies spawn quantity by 100.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Her royal shadow lies behind the 46th door.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-80%","reroll":"-","name":"Rose De Infernas","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","coffin":"Yes","dlc":"","recovery":"-","banish":"-","might":"-90%","area":"-","skip":"-","charm":"+100","curse":"-","greed":"-","id":"ROSE","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Adventures + Vampire Survivors: Emergency Meeting DLC on Steam Dec 18th","alias":"Rose","theme":"","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"CharacterControllerRose","release":"6 December 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Fuwalafuwaloo"}
Rose De Infernas is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.8. Her starting weapon is the  Fuwalafuwaloo.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Her royal shadow lies behind the 46th door."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Astral Stair and enter the stage's doors a total of 46 times, which can be done across any number of runs. Entering the 46th door sends the player into a dark room with a coffin. Interacting with the coffin unlocks Rose De Infernas as a playable character. If you leave the coffin room without interacting, the room reappears after another 46 doors.
Once unlocked, Rose De Infernas can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Rose De Infernas can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rescuefromtheshadows".
## Passive bonuses
Rose starts with +20%  Move Speed, -90%  Might, -80%  Growth, and +100  Charm. She gains +10%  Might every 12 levels until level 108, +10% Growth every 12 levels from level 36 to 84 and +15% Growth on levels 96 and 108.
| Levels | Might | Growth
| 1-11 | -90% | -80%
| 12-23 | -80% | -80%
| 24-35 | -70% | -80%
| 36-47 | -60% | -70%
| 48-59 | -50% | -60%
| 60-71 | -40% | -50%
| 72-83 | -30% | -40%
| 84-95 | -20% | -30%
| 96-107 | -10% | -15%
| 108+ | 0% | 0%
## Tips
- At first glance, Rose might not seem like a strong character, with her reduced initial stats, lack of endless scaling, and seemingly detrimental ability. However, she starts with the powerful Fuwalafuwaloo, which provides a lot of healing that should easily keep her alive against the hordes she attracts. Once Rose finally sheds her stunted Growth, she can skyrocket in level thanks to the massive amount of enemies, potentially allowing her to have very powerful weapons with Limit Break enabled.
- It is also a good idea to increase her Might and Growth by picking  Spinach and  Crown earlier.
- Rose has the unique ability to increase the number of incoming enemies without boosting the  Curse stat. This means that while she will always face massive hordes, the enemies will not be any stronger or tougher than normal, allowing them to be wiped out with relative ease.
- Due to the amount of enemies Rose will constantly be facing, it is recommended to favor weapons that can cover the whole screen.  Gatti Amari can be an interesting pick as Rose's Fuwalafuwaloo can outheal its self-damage. Picking the Arcana  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) early on will help massively boost Rose's area in the late game.
-  Wicked Season (XIII) is a suitable first Arcana for Rose. In addition to the general benefits it provides, it can help Rose work around her early-game Growth limitations by exploiting the double Growth window. Double Curse is rarely a problem for Rose, even in the early game, as Fuwalafuwaloo should usually be sufficient to keep her alive.
-  Sarabande of Healing (VI) is a solid pick for weaponizing the healing provided by Fuwalafuwaloo.  Bloody Tear can be run at the same time for even more health regain.
-  Slash (XVI) massively boosts the power of Fuwalafuwaloo very early on, but it is worth noting that many of other weapons it applies to may not be optimal picks for dealing with dense hordes.
-  Sole Solution and  Ashes of Muspell are two of the best weapons Rose can pick up, as they grow in power based on the number of enemies killed. Due to Rose's ability, she can rack up more kills than any other character.
- Easily the best way to cap out Rose's build is with  Game Killer (0). This is because the dense hordes she creates drop absurd amounts of experience gems for Rose to weaponize. If Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) was picked early, each individual gem explosion can cover most of the screen, translating to an absurd damage output.
## Trivia
- Rose's last name may imply she is somehow connected to  Avatar Infernas.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Gets more projectiles every 20 levels (max +4).","cost":"500","duration":"-","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Rottin'Ghoul","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"tf","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Defeat 6000 Rotting Ghoul.","greed":"-","movespeed":"+20%","theme":"","alias":"","speed":"-","release":"13 April 2023","id":"GHOUL","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Party Popper"}
Rottin'Ghoul is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Party Popper.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by killing a total of 6,000 Rotting Ghouls then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Rottin'Ghoul can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "souloftheparty".
## Passive bonuses
Rottin'Ghoul start with +20%  Move Speed. They gain +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
The following table shows how much Amount they gain by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4
## Trivia
- The spell "souloftheparty" is a reference to the idiom "The life and soul of the party", which means someone who is energetic and funny at the center of activity during social occasions. In this case, a literal soul.
- Rottin'Ghoul,  Gav'Et-Oni ★ and  Horse are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the game at level 80 with 5 levels of  Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +10 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, O'Sole, and Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent Amount in the base game at level 60 at a total of +9 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe gains +10 Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.
- The way Rottin'Ghoul's name is written resembles the word "rottinculo", an Italian insult.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Grows additional swords when accumulatively Overhealing by an increasingly larger amount (Max 12 swords).","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Spell the ruler's word","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Ruler Sword","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"200","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","hidden weapon":"Ruler Sword Body","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_SWORDRULER","flight":"Yes","armor":"+1","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","theme":"Dance of Gold - Vampire Survivors","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"TP_RulerSword_Character","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Sword Brothers"}
Ruler Sword is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapons are the  Sword Brothers and a unique hidden weapon,  Ruler Sword Body [DLC].
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Spell the ruler's word."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Upon typing "rulersword" as a spell in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane, Ruler Sword is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Ruler Sword can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Ruler Sword starts with +100  Max Health and +1  Armor.
Ruler Sword posseses flight, allowing it to ignore walls and obstacles.
Ruler Sword will add 1 sword to its hidden weapon at specific overhealing thresholds. These thresholds are 1, 40, 80, 160, 320, 480, 640, 800, 960, 1120, 1280, and 1440. Any overhealing will contribute to the next threshold, but all stored overhealing is reset when a new sword is added. Swords are added in the order of large, small, small, until the player has reached the maximum of 12 swords. 
After all 12 swords are obtained, overhealing by +32 in a single instance will cause all swords to shoot out and retract, dealing damage to enemies.
Additionally, after obtaining all 12 swords, overhealing will begin to increase the base damage of Ruler Sword Body by 10*log(0.03*CarryOverOverheal), where "CarryOverOverheal" is all overhealing accumulated after the 12th sword is obtained.

Starts with temporarily reduced Might.","cost":"50000","duration":"-","unlock":"Defeat a total of 6000 Sammies.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Sammy","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"tf","recovery":"2.00","might":"-60%","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"CTRPCAKE","flight":"No","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","armor":"-","theme":"Happy Birthday","alias":"","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerSammy","release":"13 April 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Vicious Hunger"}
Sammy is a playable character from the Tides of the Foscari DLC of Vampire Survivors. Its starting weapon is the  Vicious Hunger.
## Unlocking
Sammy is unlocked by defeating a total of 6,000  Sammy the Caterpillars. Once unlocked, it needs to be purchased for 50,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Sammy can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "happybirthday".
## Passive bonuses
Sammy starts with +2  Recovery and -60%  Might. It gains +10% Might on levels 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, and 21.
| Levels | Might
| 1 | -60%
| 2 | -50%
| 3-4 | -40%
| 5-7 | -30%
| 8-12 | -20%
| 13-20 | -10%
| 21+ | 0%
Whenever Sammy picks up Coins, it will gain experience as well. The experience gained from coins bypasses Game Killer (0) multiplying  Growth by 0, which means it can still level up with the Arcana. The conversion of gold to experience is 1 to 1, multiplied by the stage gold multiplier,  Greed, and Growth.
- The exception to this is gold obtained from Chests and Level ups, which does not grant experience.
Upon reaching level 30, it triggers a Gold Fever automatically and switches the background music to play Happy Birthday once.
Sammy has longer Gold Fevers by multiplying its duration cap and duration extension from Gold pickups by 1.25.
Sammy modifies the gold Chance of  Vicious Hunger, setting it to a base of 0.165; it would have been between 0.005 to 0.01 based on the damage of Gatti Amari from eating Floor Chicken. Every level, its Vicious Hunger's goldChance will increase by +0.005 until a max of 0.65 at level 98. This chance is multiplied by  Luck to get the final chance of transmuting defeated enemies and pickups into gold. For reference, goldChance of 1 is 100% chance for gold transmutation.
## Tips
- There are only two known places in Lake Foscari where Sammies spawn regularly enough to kill 6,000 of them (see gallery for map). While they can be found elsewhere, their spawn rate is so low that it would take hundreds of runs to kill the required amount.
- Sammy is the best character for gold farming with all of its passive bonuses, almost guaranteeing each enemy drops gold coins when killed with Vicious Hunger, which allows it to level up quickly with all of the gold earned and sustain Gold Fevers.
- For a guide on how to efficiently farm gold with Sammy, see the Sammy section of the gold farming guide.
- If trying to play Sammy "normally" instead of focusing on Limit Breaking Vicious Hunger, it may be a good idea to avoid Wicked Season at least until Sammy has evolved its key weapons. This is because Wicked Season on Sammy results in exponential growth that quickly triggers an uninterrupted chain of continuous level-ups that may last for the entire level.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Sammy's sprite]
 Sammy's sprite
 - 
 [Movement animation]
 Movement animation
## Other
 - 
 [Two of the known locations where Sammy the Caterpillar spawns. Choosing the red location is recommended.]
 Two of the known locations where Sammy the Caterpillar spawns. Choosing the red location is recommended.
## Trivia
- The levels Sammy gains Might at reference the Fibonacci sequence. The next term in this sequence is the sum of the last two terms. For example, the final level receives a bonus of 21, which is equal to 8 + 13 from the last two thresholds. Sammy would stop getting further stats after that, which would have been at level 34 (13 + 21) and so on.
- In the code, the name for the function that levels Sammy up is "AddXP_IgnoretGameKiller", so it is fully intentional that coins cause it to level up.
- Sammy appears be a reference to Colin the Caterpillar, a chocolate roll cake produced by British retailer Marks & Spencer, which is often bought for birthday parties.
- This cake has been bought and used by poncle at least during the 3rd and 4th birthdays of Vampire Survivors.
- Sammy's starting weapon being  Vicious Hunger may be a reference to The Very Hungry Caterpillar, a children's picture book about a very hungry caterpillar.
- ”Sammy” is also part of a Sardinian prank. When someone asks questions like “Who’s going?”, another person responds “Sammy”, “Who’s Sammy” “samminca” (basically means “this cock”

Likes to keep secrets.","cost":"666","duration":"-","unlock":"Survive 20 minutes in The Laborratory.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+30%","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Santa Ladonna","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"SANTA","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"+2","theme":"","alias":"Santa","movespeed":"-","controller":"CharacterControllerSanta","release":"30 April 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-70%","weapon":"Santa Javelin"}
Santa Ladonna is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.10. Her starting weapon is the  Santa Javelin.
## Unlocking
Upon surviving to the 20:00 mark in Laborratory, Santa Ladonna is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Santa Ladonna can be purchased for 666, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Santa Ladonna can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "shesawit".
## Passive bonuses
Santa starts with +2  Armor and +30%  Luck. Additionally, Santa starts with -70%  Cooldown, however, the bonus depreciates by 20% each level, until the bonus is only -10% at level 4.
| Level | Cooldown
| 1 | -70%
| 2 | -50%
| 3 | -30%
| 4+ | -10%
Upon reaching level 40 and 80,  Arma Dio and  Candybox will spawn underneath her respectively.
Santa has an "HP-Critical" skill that activates as soon as her HP drops below 20% of her  Max Health, which triggers a  Rosary. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
Santa can find evolved weapons in treasure chests that normally do not evolve weapons, and she can also find Arcanas in treasure chests that would normally evolve weapons, which does not affect her maximum amount of Arcanas.
If Santa has  Santa Water,  King Bible, and  Cross in her inventory and all three weapons are at least level 8, upon her next level up, all three weapons disappear and Santa gains a hidden  La Borra,  Unholy Vespers, and  Heaven Sword. This will not happen if any of the three weapons are evolved before this condition is met.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
X - Hail from the Future: Trigger a Weird Souls Purifier with Santa Ladonna.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Hail from the Future (X).
## Tips
- Santa Javelin starts with an extremely long Cooldown of 6.5 seconds. It is highly advised to take advantage of Santa's early Cooldown boost to survive until you can level up Santa Javelin or obtain other weapons.
-  Attractorb,  Spellbinder and  Clover are not necessary to obtain her hidden evolutions. However,  Clover is still required to evolve  Santa Javelin into  Seraphic Cry.
- Until the hidden evolutions have been obtained, either refrain from picking up the  Clover or avoid picking up any evolution chest. Otherwise, there is a risk of  Cross regularly evolving into  Heaven Sword, which will prevent Santa from obtaining her hidden weapons and freeing up 3 weapon slots.

## Trivia
- Santa Ladonna's name is likely a pun on "Madonna santa", an exclamation literally translated as "Holy Virgin Mary".
- Santa Ladonna's outfit and carrying a sack are inspired by Santa Claus. One of her hidden effect requiring religious weapons like  Santa Water (a pun on holy water),  King Bible, and  Cross to activate is a reference to the fact that the character of Santa Claus is inspired by Saint Nicholas.
- Santa Ladonna being the former owner of the Laborratory could be considered as her "Santa's Workshop", albeit devoted to crafting weapons instead of toys.
- Santa Ladonna is a woman despite being inspired by Santa Claus.
- In the Directer's Cut, instead of HP-Critical  Rosary and a hidden list of passive effects, Santa Ladonna used to gain a hidden Santa Water every 7 levels, up to a max of 6 at level 42, similar to the passive effect of  Scorej-Oni.
- In the public beta for the Laborratory update, if Santa triggered the special evolution method with Santa Water, King Bible, and Cross, there is a rare chance  La Borra,  Unholy Vespers, and  Heaven Sword can be found in level ups again, as the evolved weapons have a weight of 1.
- Excluding  Poe Ratcho, Santa Ladonna is the only character to have appeared in the video series Sopravvissuti ai vampiri multiple times so far. She appeared in the 1st and 6th episodes of the series. This also makes her the first guest character in this series.

Sara Trantoul is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Star Flail. 
Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook, Sara can choose to start with it as an alternative starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
In order to unlock Sara, the player must evolve the  Alchemy Whip after obtainining the  Black Disk. She is then made available for purchase for 1,665, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Sara can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bondedsoul".
## Passive bonuses
Sara starts with +20%  Move Speed, and +40%  Duration. She gains +0.02  Recovery every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
Once per life, when she takes fatal damage, she triggers a  Nduja Fritta Tanto for all players in the game. Notably it doesn't prevent her from dying, but if she revives, she will have the firebreathing effect still active.
When she revives, she evolves the Alchemy Whip into the  Vampire Killer [DLC]. The evolution will occur regardless of the whip's level and the  Tirajisú is not required.
Sara can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Her unlocking spell and her ability to evolve the Alchemy Whip after dying are references to Lament of Innocence where her dying, and being sacrificed, allows the Alchemy Whip to be transformed into the Vampire Killer.
- Her soul, however, isn't actually bonded to the whip.
