Sargon is a playable character from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon can be  FireFall,  FireBall, or  FireWall.
## Unlocking
Sargon can be unlocked by evolving the  Incineration. They must then be purchased.
Alternatively, Sargon can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "edelritter".
## Passive bonuses
Sargon starts with +20%  Move Speed, -10%  Cooldown, +20%  Growth, +50%  Greed, and +10  Reroll.
Upon beginning a run, Sargon will summon the other two weapons out of FireFall, FireBall, and FireWall, depending on which weapon the player chooses to start with, allowing Sargon to start a run with all 3 weapons. 
- For example, if the player starts with FireFall, then FireBall and FireWall will be summoned.
Sargon is adept with FireFall, FireBall, FireWall, and their union,  FireStall.
Upon using a Revival, or by acquiring and leveling up the  Velvet Dodecahedron, Sargon will summon a clone. Summoned clones will attract enemies, and last 10 seconds before exploding then appearing, potentially in a different position. Sargon can possess a maximum of 7 clones.
If Sargon possesses both the FireStall and Velvet Dodecahedron, they will morph into  Megalo Sargon.
## Tips
- As Sargon starts with three fire weapons that he is Adept with, both  Heir of Fate (XIX) and  Heart of Fire (XIX) are obvious Arcana picks.
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) can also be picked instead of Heir of Fate to exploit the explosions from Heart of Fire, which count as retaliation damage. This leads to an even higher damage output than Heir of Fate. Inverse Mode is recommended so Sargon can start with both Arcana and start snowballing right away.
- Even though Sargon becomes invincible upon transforming into Megalo Sargon, he keeps his max and current health, therefore, the damage from Divine Bloodline does not drop. However, be mindful that since he cannot receive damage, many self-damage synergies commonly used in Divine Bloodline runs will not work. For instance, he can no longer use a self-damaging weapon to gain the full bonus from  Trinum Custodem [DLC] nor to force  Mazo Familiar to fire. If intending to make use of these synergies, banish Velvet Dodecahedron.
- If planning to fight The Reaper, banish Velvet Dodecahedron as Megalo Sargon automatically dies once the stage timer limit is reached.

Can choose a per-level bonus every 10 levels (max 5 times).","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Lucky Swipe","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Scientist Mina","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"em","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"C1_SCIENTIST","flight":"No","update":"Adventures + Vampire Survivors: Emergency Meeting DLC on Steam Dec 18th","armor":"-","theme":"No Vampires Among Us","alias":"Scientist","movespeed":"-","controller":"C1_Scientist","release":"18 December 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Science Rocks"}
Scientist Mina is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Science Rocks.
## Unlocking
Scientist is unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Lucky Swipe. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Scientist can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "sciencepink".
## Passive bonuses
Scientist gets -10%  Luck every 10 levels until level 90, losing a maximum of 90% Luck.
Every 10 levels until level 50, the Scientist can choose to gain 1%  Might,  Area,  Speed,  Duration,  Recovery,  Growth,  Luck,  Greed,  Curse, or  Move Speed per level. There is no cap on these bonuses. Scientist can select different bonuses or stack the same bonus repeatedly, granting a potential maximum of +5% to any of these stats per level.
Despite the wording on the selection screen, selecting 1%  Recovery per level will grant 0.01 Recovery per level instead of a percentage modifier.
## Tips
- Scientist's low  Luck can increase the damage of  108 Bocce [DLC] or Lucky Swipe.
- Scientist's 5 per-level bonuses make them, along with  Blackmore [DLC], the highest-scaling character for nine of the ten stats they can select (the exception being Recovery, where they are outclassed by  Cosmo). This allows them to synergize with a variety of weapons and Arcana depending on the focus of their stat bonuses:
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Might, Scientist can make particularly good use of  SpellStrike [DLC].
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Area, Scientist can make particularly good use of  SpellStream [DLC],  Santa Water,  Lightning Ring, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Speed, Scientist can make particularly good use of  SpellString [DLC],  King Bible,  Runetracer, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Duration, Scientist can make particularly good use of Runetracer,  Bone,  La Robba, and so on.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Luck, Scientist may be able to make good use of  Gatti Amari,  Muramasa [DLC], 108 Bocce, and so on. However, Luck-based weapons will be less effective near the beginning of a run due to Scientist's Luck penalty every 10 levels, only consistently staying above net negatives at level 30 after two Luck per-level selections at levels 10 and 20. This breakpoint is further delayed if Growth bonuses are prioritized.
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Curse, Scientist can make particularly good use of 108 Bocce and  Twilight Requiem (II).
- Potentially having the highest scaling  Move Speed, Scientist can make particularly good use of  Shadow Pinion,  Vento Sacro,  Celestial Dusting, and so on.
- Regardless of build, selecting  Growth for some of Scientist's bonuses will result in enough additional levels that you will gain more of other stats than if you were to exclusively select another bonus. The value proposition of more Growth bonuses depends on how long you continue your run and how dense with enemies it is.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Gains a hidden Lightning Ring every 8 levels (max 6) that also fires when losing health.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Discover the source of the roaring thunder on the Tiny Bridge.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Scorej-Oni","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"108","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","hidden weapon":"Lightning Ring","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"+20%","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"SCOREJ","flight":"No","armor":"-","update":"Patch 1.1.0","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"-20%","controller":"CharacterControllerScorej","release":"24 November 2022","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Scorej-Oni is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.1.0. It does not have a starting weapon, but it gains up to 6 hidden  Lightning Rings after every 8 levels reached.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Discover the source of the roaring thunder on the Tiny Bridge."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Tiny Bridge and travel roughly 20 tilesets (map tiles) to the right, where they will encounter  Scorej-Oni as a hostile enemy. A sign that Scorej-Oni is nearby is the faint sound of thunder sporadically playing in the background, though this may be difficult to hear if there are many sound effects playing at once. Defeating Scorej-Oni unlocks it as a playable character.
After unlocking, Scorej-Oni can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Scorej-Oni can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notsureitsthunder".
## Passive bonuses
Scorej-Oni starts with +8  Max Health, -20%  Movement Speed, and +20%  Area.
It starts with a hidden  Lightning Ring and gains an additional one every 8 levels, up to a max of 6 at level 40, without taking up any weapon slots; the actual weapon can still be obtained from level-up choices, allowing the player to have a total of 7 Lightning Rings. After that, each of the hidden weapons is upgraded after every 12 levels, 7 times, up to level 124, where all rings reach their maximum level at level 8; however, they do not gain Limit Break bonuses. The hidden Lightning Rings also fire whenever the character takes any form of damage.
## Tips
- Scorej-Oni is incentivized into taking damage with its high Max Health, ideal for builds that focus on dealing retaliatory damage. Consequently,  Divine Bloodline (IX) can be a potent Arcana with this character due to its effects that complement the character's traits.
- It is important to note that the lightning strikes fired after receiving damage aren't considered retaliatory damage; therefore, it won't activate Divine Bloodline's effect of increasing the character's max HP whenever an enemy is killed in retaliation.
-  Twilight Requiem (II) is a must-pick Arcana, as it buffs Lightning Rings' destructive power greatly.
-  Muramasa's [DLC] health drain effect, which counts as damage to the character, complements Scorej-Oni's hidden Lightning Rings' ability to fire every time the player takes damage, enabling the hidden weapons to fire rapidly without needing direct enemy hits.
- This effect is further amplified by setting the weapon limit to 1 before starting a run, choosing the  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) Arcana, and then also getting  Empty Tome. The low cooldown resulting from the reduction bonuses allows Scorej-Oni to become very powerful due to Muramasa's health drain becoming faster and, in turn, making the hidden weapons fire even quicker. However, it is essential to secure an effective way to recover or increase health when attempting this strategy, as the rapid health drain may be great enough to outstrip the recovery rate of the character, eventually killing the player.
## Trivia
- Scorej-Oni is the fourth character to be unlocked by defeating their enemy counterpart, after Red Death, Leda, and Gyorunton.
- Scorej-Oni's name is a play on the Italian word "scoreggione", which is an informal word that means "strong flatulence" and an insult used to describe a person who "farts often and loudly."
- The spell "notsureitsthunder," used to unlock Scorej-Oni is a reference to an Italian saying/joke that compares the sound of loud flatulence to thunder.
- The spell specifically refers to the faint sound of thunder that plays in the background once the player gets nearer to Scorej-Oni's location in Tiny Bridge, implying that the "thunder" may actually be the sound of flatulence instead.
- In Japanese folklore, an oni is a kind of yōkai, demon, orc, ogre, or troll. Its weapon being a chain of den-den taiko drums is a reference to Raijin, god of lightning, thunder, and storms in Japanese mythology, fitting for its unique ability to attack with an increasing amount of Lightning Rings.

Secretino Dagsson is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.14. His starting weapon is  Kyra-Stones.
## Unlocking

Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must purchase the "Preorder Me!" item from the Giocare merchant in Westwoods. This item is available once the player has obtained the  Masquerade relic from the end of the stage and has unlocked  Chula-Reh. Once both conditions are met, Giocare will sell the item in a new run for 49,999.
Once unlocked, Secretino Dagsson can be purchased for 50,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Secretino Dagsson can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "silentlaugh".
## Passive bonuses
Upon starting the run, Secretino will be granted a draft of  Survarot, giving him a selection of various bonuses. Secretino is also always able to rarely find  Survarot cards from Light Sources.
Depending on the  Golden Eggs Secretino possesses, he will gain a Survarot multiplier in accordance to a base 10 logarithm. This multiplier increases how many level ups Survarot cards receive each time the player gains a level.
- For example, if Secretino possesses 1,000,000 Golden eggs, he will have a Survarot multiplier of 6. Every time Secretino levels up, each Survarot card will gain 6 levels. This stacks additively with the edition of these cards, which grants a multiplier of 4.
- To receive the maximum multiplier, Secretino must possess 10,000,000,000 Golden Eggs, or 1E+10, costing a total of 100,000,000,000,000.
template = Template:Secretino survarot multiplier
form = SecretinoSurvarotMultiplierCalc
result = SecretinoSurvarotMultiplierCalcResult
param = input_type|Input type|Multiplier|buttonselect|Eggs,Multiplier|Eggs=egg_count;Multiplier=multiplier|Whether to input your current Golden Egg count, or the desired multiplier amount.
param = egg_count|Golden Egg count|0|int|0-||How many Golden Eggs Secretino currently has.
param = multiplier|Multiplier|1|int|1-10||The desired multiplier amount.
| Secretino survarot multiplier
| The calculator form will appear here soon. You will need JavaScript enabled.
| Result
| The result will appear here when you submit the form.
The damage of Secretino's  Kyra-Stones always equals the player's level. 
- This leads to a decrease in damage from levels 1 to 79, but no change compared to using the weapon on other characters at level 80 and above. At level 80, the weapon matches its typical damage, and the scaling acts as normal after level 81.
Secretino has  Zi'Appunta Belpaese's ability to where he can also break Light Sources by touching them.
## Tips
- Secretino makes great use of  Greatest Jubilee, due to its ability to spawn light sources and grant Secretino more Survarots.
- The player does not have to enable  Golden eggs to make use of Secretino's Survarot multiplier. Instead, the Golden Eggs just need to be possessed by him.
## Trivia
- Secretino Dagsson was formerly the only way to obtain and use Survarots without the use of content from Ante Chamber.
- Secretino's surname refers to Magic: the Gathering character Arcum Dagsson. Furthermore, their appearance may be based on the original printing of the card Jester's Cap, which quotes Arcum.
- Secretino's internal name is the same as  Giocare, the special merchant from the Westwoods stage. While they both share a colour scheme and are both jesters, the merchant character is simply a recolour of  Chula-Reh rather than using Secretino's sprite.
- Prior to v1.15, either using this character, entering the Ante Chamber stage, or using any of the Ante Chamber characters would allow for the usage of Survarots. With the introduction of the  Survarocchi, this is no longer the case.

Gains +1%  Curse every level. Becomes invincible and deals damage to nearby enemies for 20 seconds when health reaches a critically low level.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_SHAFT_2DB Umbra Ebony Diabologue
Shaft is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Umbra. 
Upon unlocking the  Ebony Diabologue, Shaft is able to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Shaft can be unlocked by evolving the  Luminatio. He is then made available for purchase for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Shaft can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "vladsbiggestfan".
## Passive bonuses
He starts with 100  Max Health, +30%  Move Speed, +50%  Duration, +1  Revival, and +10  Banishes. He also gains +1%  Curse every level. 
When Shaft's health reaches a criticall low level, or 20% of his max health, he will become invulnerable and deal damage to nearby enemies for 20 seconds. During this time, Shaft gains +200%  Might, -20%  Cooldown, and +50%  Curse. The  Shaft Orb that is summoned when reaching critically low health deals 20 base damage and covers most of the screen around the player character. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
Shaft can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Since Shaft gets scaling from Curse, weapons that scale off this stat, namely  Nightmare,  Crissaegrim Tip,  Ophion [DLC] and  108 Bocce [DLC] are highly recommended to weaponize a normally partially detrimental stat.
- Shaft works best if he is able to activate his Shaft Orb skill in succession in order to survive the Curse-boosted hordes that he attracts indefinitely. Therefore, some ways of healing to full while Shaft is invulnerable are highly recommended.  Paranormal Scan [DLC] is a powerful source of healing that has the additional upside of helping scale Shaft's stats to let him keep up with his Curse in the lategame.  Sarabande of Healing (VI) can be helpful if running a healing-heavy build.
## External links
- Shaft in the Castlevania Wiki

Shanoa is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Confodere. 
Unlocking and using the  Morning Star,  Coat of Arms,  Spectral Sword,  Belnades' Spellbook and  Ebony Diabologue will unlock the  Candybox as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Shanoa can be unlocked by evolving the  Iron Ball and the  Alucard Spear. She is then available to purchase for 1,000, scaling on other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Shanoa can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "morningsun".
Completing any one stage with both Shanoa and  Juste Belmont unlocks  Maria Renard as a playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Shanoa starts with +20  Max Health, +50%  Move Speed, +10  Rerolls,  Skips and  Banishes. She also gains +1%  Might every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
She also has a 30 second Charge ability that charges by standing still that triggers all equipped weapons from the  Ebony Diabologue.
Shanoa can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Her special animation when using Universitas is a nod to her doing the same movement when casting Universitas in Order of Ecclesia.
- Her unlocking spell is nod to the same game when she first enters Dracula's Castle and says "I am the morning sun, come to vanquish this horrible night!" a quote similar to the one used in Simon's Quest when the night ends ''The morning sun has vanquished the horrible night''.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Leaves behind excess body parts when damaged and may shift forms.","cost":"500","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolve the Science Rocks","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Shapeshifter Nino","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"em","recovery":"1.00","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"C1_SHAPESHIFTER","flight":"No","update":"Adventures + Vampire Survivors: Emergency Meeting DLC on Steam Dec 18th","armor":"-","theme":"Hide and Survive","alias":"Shapeshifter","movespeed":"+10%","controller":"C1_Shapeshifter","release":"18 December 2023","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Just Vent"}
Shapeshifter Nino is a playable character from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is  Just Vent.
## Unlocking
Shapeshifter is unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Science Rocks. They need to be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Shapeshifter can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "greenisredyouisme".
## Passive bonuses
Shapeshifter starts with +1  Recovery and +10%  Move Speed.
When damaged, Shapeshifter Nino has a 20% chance, affected by  Luck, to change into one of 8 forms:
-  Shapeunshifter / "normal" form: Gains +1  Max Health.
-  Pinthot Form: Triggers a  Fire Nova. Gains +1  Max Health.
-  Coldellini Form: Triggers an  Ice Nova. Gains +1  Max Health.
-  Space Apparition Form: Triggers a  Fear Nova. Gains +1  Max Health.
The following forms are identical in effect and are considered "sus" forms. "Sus" forms will randomly be chosen out of the following: 
-  Meat Bean Form: Gains +8  Max Health.
-  Suspicio (trash bin) Form: Gains +8  Max Health.
-  Suspicio (brick) Form: Gains +8  Max Health.
-  Suspicio (nugget) Form: Gains +8  Max Health.
Swapping forms can happen at a maximum of once every 5 seconds, reduced by 25% of  Cooldown. Shapeshifter Nino cannot swap to the same form that they currently are. When swapping forms, Shapeshifter Nino will release a body part. These body parts will heal Shapeshifter Nino for a minimum of 8 Health and can heal more depending on Shapeshifter Nino's  Luck, scaling infinitely.
- Unlike other food-related pickups, body parts will not have their healing increased by  Recovery.
Shapeshifter Nino can gain a maximum of +300  Max Health from shapeshifting.
Shapeshifter Nino modifies their set of "nova" weapons in the following ways:
- All novas gain +10%  Area when Shapeshifter Nino levels up 10 times.
-  Fire Nova has its base damage doubled to 20, and gains +10 base damage every 10 levels.
-  Ice Nova has its base damage halved to 5, and its freeze chance set to 50%, down from 100%, but will gain +10% freeze chance every 10 levels.
Note that these bonuses are only applied every 10 levels, not every level. These scalings will stop after Shapeshifter Nino has reached level 100.
## Tips
- Shapeshifter's excess body parts can be weaponized with Sarabande of Healing (VI), though its efficacy diminishes as the run progresses.
- The body parts Shapeshifter drops act similar to Floor Chickens when interacting with Gatti Amari.
- Weapons and Arcana that can retaliate such as  NO FUTURE,  Victory Sword,  Night Sword/ Muramasa, Eskizzibur/ Legionnaire, and Heart of Fire (XIX) can be used to capitalize off of the trigger condition of Shapeshifter's ability. Retaliatory attacks can be further enhanced with Divine Bloodline (IX).
- The evolution of Shapeshifter's starting weapon,  Unjust Ejection, can capture more enemies in its area of effect by moving to the left or right in the opposite direction.  Wings can increase the number of ejected enemies, and horizontal weapons such as Whip/Vento Sacro/Fuwalafuwaloo, Glass Fandango/Celestial Voulge, and  Silver Wind/ Festive Winds can help cleave through incoming enemies while doing so.
## External links
- Shapeshifter at the Among Us Wiki

She-Moon Eeta is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.7.0. Her starting weapon is the  Glass Fandango.
## Unlocking
Upon surviving to the 20:00 mark in Whiteout, She-Moon Eeta is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, She-Moon Eeta must then be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, She-Moon Eeta can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "cygnusmagnus".
## Reborn She-Moon Eeta
Completing the Adventure To End an Ice Age for the first time unlocks an alternative skin named Reborn She-Moon Eeta.
Alternatively, Reborn She-Moon Eeta can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the Spell "rebirdshemoon".
## Passive bonuses
She-Moon starts with +2  Armor and +20%  Move Speed. 
She-Moon has an "HP-Critical" skill that activates as soon as her HP drops below 20% of her  Max Health, which freezes all enemies for 10 seconds once triggered, similar to an  Orologion pickup. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
## Reborn She-Moon Eeta
Compared to She-Moon, Reborn She-Moon starts with +100%  Luck, +7  Revivals, and level 5  Wings. She also has the  Valkyrie Turner as a hidden weapon.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
XII - Crystal Cries: Trigger a Starry Heavens with She-Moon Eeta.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Crystal Cries (XII).
## Tips
- Her starting weapon and her passive make her well-suited to run a freezing build.  Clock Lancet and  Jail of Crystal (XIV) can serve as a way to set enemies up for massive damage from Glass Fandango or  Celestial Voulge.  Out of Bounds (XII) generates additional value from freezing enemies and increases the number of Orologions spawned, synergizing with Celestial Voulge.

Simon Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Wind Whip. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star as optional starting weapon.
## Unlocking
Simon Belmont can be unlocked by evolving the  Jet Black Whip.
Alternatively, Simon can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "peterdante".
## Passive bonuses
Simon starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, +1  Revival, and +5  Reroll.
Whenever Simon damages an enemy, there is a 50% chance for  Growth to be added to  Might, and to multiply  Armor. Otherwise, nothing happens. Since this is checked every hit, it can rapidly change. Each bonus is independent of the other, and either, both, or neither bonus may be active at a given moment.
Simon can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Gets more projectiles every 20 levels (max +4).","cost":"10000","duration":"-","unlock":"Evolving the Divers Mines.","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Simondo Belmont","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"og","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"FB_SIMONDO","flight":"No","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","armor":"-","theme":"R.A.V.E.","alias":"Simondo","movespeed":"-","controller":"FB_Simondo","release":"9 May 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Simondo Belmont is a playable character from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors. He does not have a starting weapon.
## Unlocking
Simondo Belmont can be unlocked for purchase by evolving the  Diver Mines. He needs to be purchased for 10,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Simondo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "honouringmasato".
## Passive bonuses
Simondo gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 80. The maximum Amount gained this way is +4.
The following table shows how much Amount he gain by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60-79 | +3
| 80+ | +4
Simondo has an ability that spawns  Rapid Fire,  Barrier, and  Grenade pickups with base  Cooldown of 30 seconds, visually exploding them on the field. The rate of spawning the pickups is affected by the Cooldown stat. The amount of pickups it spawns is determined by  Amount, up to a maximum of 12 spawns at a time. The ability starts at 0 amount, which still spawns one pickup normally but requires +2 Amount from the player to start increasing the amount spawned. This ability also benefits from  Beginning (X) which provides +3  Amount per spawn. 
- Each pickup spawns a different distance away from the player, with Barriers spawning closest while Rapid Fires and Grenades spawning further away from the player.
Simondo has a "HP-Critical" skill that activates as soon as his HP drops below 20% of his  Max Health, which triggers the ability immediately with 12 more item spawning. The skill may only be activated again after recovering all HP. Receiving fatal damage resulting in a game-over will not trigger the skill.
Simondo can find special Operation Guns pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Due to the increase spawns of Grenades, it is possible to beat levels without using any weapons as Simondo. Although, he would still perform much better using weapons.
- Without any weapons, Simondo gets another -8% Cooldown from  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX), however he gains more benefits from Beginning (X) as a starting Arcana.
- Maximizing Cooldown will allow you to fully benefit from his bonuses.
## Trivia
- Simondo's description starting with the words "Simondo says" may be a reference to the popular children's game Simon Says, where the person ("Simon") says what the others must do, always starting with the words "Simon Says". It is more likely that this is a coincidence, however.
- Simondo's inclusion in Contra's DLC may have been foreshadowing to the fact that the next Vampire Survivors DLC release would be Ode to Castlevania, Castlevania's the game series where  Simon Belmont hails, being the character Simondo parodies. The fact that his theme from Hard Corps, Simon 1994RD, a remix of Vampire Killer, was excluded could be considered a clue.
- Simondo's based on the likeness of prolific Japanese singer Masato Shimon, with his single 'Swim, Taiyaki-kun!' serving as the basis of his projectile attack in Hard Corps. His code in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane is 'honouringmasato' in reference to that. As it happens, Shimon sang the theme song for Konami arcade game Monster Maulers, known as Kyuukyoku Sentai Dadandarn (究極戦隊ダダンダーン) in Japan.
## External links
- Simondo Belmont at the Contra Wiki
- Simondo Belmont at the Castlevania Wiki

Starts with temporary Amount bonus.","cost":"500","duration":"-","update":"Patch 0.7.3","secret":"No","image":"","luck":"+20%","amount":"+10","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Sir Ambrojoe","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"Defeat a total of 6000 Stage Killers.","greed":"+20%","movespeed":"-","theme":"","alias":"Ambrojoe","speed":"-","release":"23 June 2022","id":"AMBROGIO","cooldown":"-","weapon":"La Robba"}
Sir Ambrojoe is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.7.3. His starting weapon is  La Robba.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by killing a total of 6,000 Stage Killers, then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Ambrojoe can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "languorino".
## Passive bonuses
Ambrojoe starts with +20%  Luck, +20%  Greed, and +20%  Magnet. Additionally, Ambrojoe starts with +10  Amount, however, the bonus depreciates by 2 each level, until the bonus is entirely gone at level 6. He gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 60. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
| Levels | Amount
| 1 | +10
| 2 | +8
| 3 | +6
| 4 | +4
| 5 | +2
| 6-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60+ | +3
## Tips
- If  Game Killer (0) is selected as Ambrojoe's starting Arcana, he can keep his +10 Amount indefinitely at the cost of being unable to level up. While  La Robba alone may not be sufficient to deal with the hordes, other weapons may be obtained from the Merchant and the Master Librarian.
-  Moonlight Bolero (VI) is highly recommended as either a second Arcana or a simultaneous first Arcana (if playing on Inverse) to maximize the number of chests acquired and gain a chance of receiving passive items.
- Alternatively, a limited weapons build with  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) is also possible to maximize the power of La Robba.
- Another option is purchasing  Icebrand [DLC] from the Master Librarian and selecting  Crystal Cries (XII) as a second Arcana. Crystal Cries in conjunction with the freezing ability of Icebrand will provide continuous scaling throughout the game in spite of the Game Killer lock, and boost the damage dealt by experience gems over time as they scale with Growth.
-  Summon Spirit Tornado [DLC] can be purchased from the Master Librarian, scales well with Amount and can be a great way of weaponizing gems via Game Killer.
-  Aura Blast [DLC] can also be purchased from the Master Librarian, and Ambrojoe's massive Amount bonus will make it immediately qualify to trigger the massive explosion.
-  Stake to Your Heart (0) is arguably an even better pick than Game Killer, as it allows much greater build customization compared to having to rely on just the Merchant and the Master Librarian. Ambrojoe's moderate Greed bonus synergizes well with this Darkana.

## Trivia
- Ambrojoe's code name, "Ambrogio," is a common name for a butler or chauffeur in old Italian movies.
- Ambrojoe may refer to an old Ferrero TV advertisement that feature an elegant lady on a limousine and Ambrogio the butler which serve the famous filled chocolates Ferrero Roches.
- His weapon is La Robba, which is a Kinder Surprise Egg and made by the company Ferrero SpA. An old commercial for Ferrero Rocher, another chocolate made by the same company, has a chauffeur called Ambrogio.
- Ambrojoe is inspired by one of the stories from "Scriptures of Delphi", where an Italian adventurer Ambrogio got cursed and blessed by Greek Gods and Goddesses to slowly become more vampire-like.
- Ambrojoe shares a similar passive with  Mortaccio,  Cavallo,  Ramba, and  O'Sole, though he differs in stats, starting bonus, and starting weapons.
- Ambrojoe has the highest Amount in the game with their starting bonus, combining with the Amount PowerUp to a total of +11 Amount. However, due to the stat reaching its cap, only up to 10 projectiles are added to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe, Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, and O'Sole are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the base game at level 60 with 2 levels of  Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +6 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul,  Horse, and  Simondo Belmont can get one more persistent Amount by reaching level 80.
- It is suggested that Ambrojoe is rich and rather generous, given that in Planar Bazaar, he sells all of his items only for 1. The items that he sells are the  Bone, the  Cherry Bomb, the  Carréllo, the  Celestial Dusting, and  La Robba.

Siugnas is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Town Sword.
## Unlocking
Siugnas can be unlocked by finding and opening the second coffin in Emerald Diorama, more specifically in the Providence world.
Alternatively, Siugnas can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "dismalking".
## Skins
Fully evolving  Town Sword unlocks an alternate skin that uses  Sanguine Star as starting weapon.
## Unlockables
Defeating  Specter of Iwanaga-hime in Inverse Emerald Diorama as Siugnas unlocks  Dolores as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
- Siugnas starts with +0.2  Recovery, +20%  Move Speed, and +1  Amount.
- Defeated enemies become followers.
- Different enemies may have different behavior as followers. They will take damage, unlike familiars.
- Increasing  Max Health increases maximum followers (capped at 4).
- Below 140 HP: Maximum 1 follower
- Above 140 HP: Maximum 2 followers
- Above 200 HP: Maximum 3 followers
- Above 300 HP: Maximum 4 followers
- Triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect.
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
- Though unlisted in his description, he is immune to Health Drain.

Slogra and Gaibon is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Slogra's starting weapon is  Trident, while Gaibon's starting weapon is  Fire Wand.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Defeat Death's demonic knight duo with Dracula's smallest henchman."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Slogra and Gaibon can be unlocked by defeating their boss forms in Ode to Castlevania as  Fleaman.
- Although the unlock method can be daunting as it requires using one of the weakest characters in the game, it can be trivialized by the  Victorian Horror (XVIII) Darkana, as it grants you a damaging aura with the potential to increase your maximum health, and effectively an extra life/screen clear for mistakes. Turning off  Curse and  Charm powerups should allow easy enough navigation directly to the bosses.
Once unlocked, Slogra and Gaibon can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Slogra and Gaibon can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "platonicpartners".
## Passive bonuses
Slogra and Gaibon start with with +100  Max Health, +1  Armor and +30%  Might.
Slogra will be followed by Gaibon as a secondary character or vice versa, depending on which is chosen as the main character.
The secondary character can be damaged as normal and if he dies, the remaining character is enraged and gains a temporary -90%  Cooldown bonus for 20 seconds. The bonus is maintained even if the other character is revived.
Despite his description, Gaibon currently does not possess Flight due to a bug.
Slogra and Gaibon are adept with  Trident [DLC] and  Fire Wand, respectively, but not their evolutions,  Gungnir-Souris [DLC] and  Hellfire.
## Trivia
- Their unlocking spell is a nod to the fact that they’re usually found together in their original series.
## External links
- Slogra and Gaibon on the Castlevania Wiki

| {"magnet":"-","description":"The quirky white bear.","cost":"7777","duration":"-10%","update":"Patch 0.7.2","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"+100%","amount":"-","armor":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Smith IV","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"7","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-10%","area":"-10%","skip":"-","banish":"-","details":"","flight":"No","unlock":"3) Sometimes you just need to ask for help.","greed":"-","movespeed":"+20%","theme":"","alias":"","speed":"-50%","release":"9 June 2022","id":"SMITH","cooldown":"+10%","weapon":"Vandalier"}
Smith IV is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.7.2. Its starting weapon is the  Vandalier.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "3) Sometimes you just need to ask for help."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After the player has unlocked  Exdash Exiviiq and  Toastie, the following must be performed:
- The player must type "spam" and then press the enter key in the main menu, which starts a hidden 30-second timer. The following steps must be completed within the given time.
- On the character selection screen, the player must enter "spam" and then the enter key.
- On the stage selection screen, the player must enter "spam" and then the enter key.
- Upon starting a run in any stage, the player must enter "humbug" (after selecting an Arcana or Darkana) and press the enter key.
After the player exits the run, Smith IV will be automatically unlocked as a playable character.
This can be done on console and mobile versions by using an external keyboard.
Once unlocked, Smith IV can be purchased for 7,777, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Smith IV can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "maybeimastallion".
## Passive bonuses
Smith starts with -93  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed, -10%  Might, -10%  Area, -50%  Speed, -10%  Duration, +10%  Cooldown, and +100%  Luck. It gains +0.01  Recovery, +0.7% Might, Area, Projectile Speed, and Duration, -0.25% Cooldown, and +1% Luck every level. There is no cap on these bonuses.
## Tips
- Once their initially debuffed stats are compensated for with power ups and Passive items, Smith becomes one of the most versatile characters in the game thanks to having 7 stats which scale infinitely. One can think of Smith as a higher risk and higher reward version of  Zi'Assunta Belpaese.
- Smith's low starting Health may be seen as a disadvantage, but it combines well with the  Crystal Cries (XII) Darkana, allowing them to quickly acquire bonuses to increase their Max Health,  Growth and Recovery. This can be done multiple times in a row early on.
- Because of this, it might be considered wise to run a 'Freeze build' with Smith, using weapons such as  Clock Lancet,  Celestial Voulge,  Prism Lass/ Time Warp and  Icebrand.
- With high Luck that scales endlessly, equipping weapons affected by the passive such as  108 Bocce and  Pentagram is worth considering. It can also be combined with the  Slash (XVI) Arcana and/or  Sapphire Mist (I) Darkana to increase their effective potential.
- Smith's strategy is similar to Toastie's.
- With scaling Projectile Speed, Area and Might, Smith can make incredible use of  SpellString,  SpellStream,  SpellStrike and their union,  SpellStrom, especially since their scaling Cooldown can increase the rate at which the SpellStrom singularity can occur and their scaling Duration increases how much damage each singularity deals.
- They can make great use of the  Rune Sword, not only because they scale Area, Projectile Speed, and Duration, but also Luck, which increases the rate at which critical hits occur.
- While overshadowed by  Cosmo Pavone and  Sara Trantoul in this aspect, Smith's scaling Recovery means they can still make great use of  Sharp Tongue/  Impostongue and the  Star Flail/ Moon Rod.
## Trivia
- Smith is a reference to Smith the Horse from the Ultima series.
- In Ultima VI, there is a cheat code by talking to Iolo, then typing "spam", enter, "spam", enter, "spam", enter, "humbug", and enter. This is referenced in one of the ways to how players can unlock Smith IV.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Fights well with (and against) his father.","cost":"1665","duration":"-","unlock":"Save the son with holy magic before the transformation is complete.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Soleil Belmont","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"120","revival":"+1","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","hidden weapon":"Jet Black Whip; Vibhuti Whip; Vanitas Whip","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"TP_SOLEIL","flight":"No","armor":"+1","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","theme":"The Beginning (Castlevania track)","alias":"Soleil","movespeed":"-","controller":"TP_Soleil_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Jet Black Whip; Vibhuti Whip; Vanitas Whip"}
Soleil Belmont is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. He has three starting weapons:  Jet Black Whip,  Vibhuti Whip, and  Vanitas Whip, as well as a hidden copy of each.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Save the son with holy magic before the transformation is complete."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets in Ode to Castlevania, Soleil Belmont will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, where his summoning circle is located in the Art Gallery (the middle-left of the front building). Upon evolving  Refectio into  Sanctuary and defeating him with its passive screen-wipe effect, Soleil Belmont is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Soleil Belmont can be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Soleil Belmont can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "prodigalson".
## Passive bonuses
Soleil Belmont starts with +20  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +1  Revival.
Upon receiving damage, Soleil will fire his hidden whips in retaliation. This can occur at most every 4 seconds, with the time between attacks decreasing with  Speed until a minimum delay of 0.5 seconds is reached. The damage from this attack is not considered retaliatory damage.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Since he starts with three weapons, with extra hidden copies of each, Soleil is the best user for  Beginning (X).
## Trivia
- His unlocking method is the same as Stella and Loretta, as he is also the descendant of a previous Castlevania protagonist that was corrupted by a vampire.
- His description references him fighting his father Christopher while being controlled by Dracula.
- The Switch version and Android version of the game initially featured a recolored sprite of Christopher Belmont instead of his own sprite like the other versions, probably a placeholder from the development phase, this has been fixed since then.
- However, Soleil's secrets menu icon retains this recolored sprite.

Soma Cruz is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Hand Grenade.
Unlocking and using the  Morning Star,  Coat of Arms,  Spectral Sword,  Belnades' Spellbook, and  Ebony Diabologue will unlock the  Candybox as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Soma can be unlocked by defeating  Gergoth, located at the downstairs of middle building below the library, just above the  Capra Gate, as  Julius Belmont. He can then be purchased for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Soma can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "technicallynotavampire".
Defeating  Abbadon as Soma will unlock  Yoko Belnades as a playable character.
## Dark Lord Soma Cruz
Completing the Adventure Ode to Castlevania unlocks an alternate skin for Soma named Dark Lord Soma Cruz.
Alternatively, Dark Lord Soma Cruz can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the Spell "notwhatyouthinkiam".
## Passive bonuses
Soma starts with +20%  Move Speed, +50%  Greed, +5  Reroll, +5  Skip, and +5  Banish. He gains +1%  Might every level.
Once per life, upon receiving fatal damage, he triggers a hidden  Soul Steal.
Soma is immune to health drain.
Soma can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Dark Lord Soma Cruz
Dark Lord Soma starts with the  Candybox, all the extra stats of his regular self, as well as +566  Max Health. Additionally, his passive is doubled, gaining 2%  Might per level instead of 1%. Finally, Dark Lord Soma gains +1  Revival every 100 levels. This is distinctly similar to the passive bonuses of  Vlad Tepes Dracula.
Dark Lord Soma also has a unique passive. Upon killing an enemy, Soma has a 20% chance to spawn either a red, blue, or yellow Enemy Soul that automatically spiral towards the player. Enemy Souls act like  Crystallized Souls and  Cursed Souls, healing the player and granting them extra  Might,  Growth, or  Greed, respectively. The healing provided by each Soul is 1, and is increased by  Recovery. The chance for an Enemy Soul to drop is unaffected by  Luck, and each type of Enemy Soul has an equal chance to drop. The amount of stats the player receives is dependent on the Base XP value of the enemy that dropped it:
-  Might: 0.075% * Base XP
-  Growth: 0.075% * Base XP
-  Greed: 0.15% * Base XP
For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP of 3, will drop an Enemy Soul that gives +0.225% Growth and Might, and +0.45% Greed.
Additionally, at certain thresholds of each type of Enemy Soul, being 1000, 2000, 3000, then every 3000 Souls after (6000 total, 9000 total, etc.), Soma will received an extra reward depending on how many of that Soul has been collected:
- Red Souls grant an  Arma Dio.
- Blue Souls grant a  Candybox.
- Yellow Souls grant a  Gold Finger.
There is no limit on the number of times Soma can receive extra rewards.
## Tips
- Gergoth is the first boss available after opening up the library, in the sloped hallways.
## Dark Lord Soma Cruz
At high enemy quantities, and depending on how fast Dark Lord Soma can kill them, his passive triggers constantly. Combined with his Health Drain immunity, he easily overheals.
- Because of this, Dark Lord Soma effectively won't utilize the missing HP bonus from  Divine Bloodline (IX) unless he actively gets hit.
- With  Guardian's Targe [DLC] and the  Sacred Beasts Tower Shield [DLC], the overhealing can cause these shields to fire a lot of projectiles once they hit enemies.
 Muramasa [DLC] is one of the best weapons for Dark Lord Soma Cruz as he starts with extra Greed, will increase Greed the most out of the three, and Greed doesn't have a hard cap. 
- This allows its slashes to easily one-shot enemies and even bosses, as the damage will scale into the tens of thousands.
- Growth doesn't have a hard cap either, so weapons like  Spirit Tornado Tip [DLC] are great options.
 Victorian Horror (XVIII) is a great Darkana for Dark Lord Soma Cruz not only because it provides another source of Greed increase, but his passive increases the damage of the Darkana's  Insatiable.
- When combined with Muramasa and max PowerUps, Dark Lord Soma can reach the 30-minute mark on stages like Abyss Foscari without additional weapons, additional Arcana/Darkana,  Golden Eggs,  Survarots, or Limit Break.
In local Co-op and  Party Mode, his passive has some interactions:
- Enemy Souls function like Pickups: CPUs will not get them.
- This applies to Dark Lord Soma himself if he is a CPU.
- To conveniently pick up these Souls, it's recommended to set Dark Lord Soma to "[Aspect of Moon] Defensive" or "[Aspect of City] Follows you."
- With him set as a CPU, the player can main a character like  Megalo Miang Moonspell [DLC], gaining Max Health from picking up Souls that will overheal her.
- If Dark Lord Soma is controlled by another player, they will gain most of the Souls. As the spiraling Souls reach Dark Lord Soma, some of it is automatically given to the other player.
- The other player can intercept some of the Souls spiraling towards Dark Lord Soma, gaining the buffs for their character.

## Trivia
- Soma has the same passive ability of being immune to health drain like the other vampire characters despite him not being one, but the reincarnation of  Dracula. This is referenced in his unlocking spell "technicallynotavampire".
- The unlocking spell for the Dark Lord skin references his dialogue before he fights Julius, admitting that he does have the soul and powers of Dracula, but the personality is different.

Sonia Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Sonic Whip.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by collecting a total of 7  Heart Refresh items on the Ode to Castlevania stage, which appear after collecting the  Stallion Gate relic, then purchasing with 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Sonia can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "legendmother".
## Passive bonuses
Sonia starts with +40%  Move Speed and +10%  Luck. Every time she overheals by 8 health points or more, she triggers a  Rapid Fire, lasting for 10 seconds. This bonus can not be triggered more than every 5 seconds, but multiple instances of this bonus can be active at the same time.
Sonia can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Sonia synergizes with weapons such as  Lifesign Scan and its Evolution  Paranormal Scan, which keep the ability nearly permanently active without the player needing to do anything. Bloody Tear and Fuwalafuwaloo also have a similar synergy, though rely on getting crits and as such aren't quite as powerful on her.

- Sonia Belmont on the Castlevania Wiki

Gets more fans from defeating a large amount of enemies.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Quickly break the bounds of Space and find Love.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Space Dette","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"SPACEDUDETTE","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"+40%","controller":"CharacterControllerSpaceDette","release":"10 April 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Photonstorm"}
Space Dette is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.13. Her starting weapon is the  Photonstorm.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Quickly break the bounds of Space and find Love."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After unlocking  Space Dude, the player must start a run in Space 54 and locate a special pillar with a pulsating icon of the  Pummarola. This pillar can be found by moving less than one tile south-east (down and right) from the starting area and the player's spawning location. However, if the player has the Visually Invert Stages option enabled, and is playing in inverse mode, the pillar will instead be found by moving north-west (up and left) from the starting area. The pillar will disappear alongside the rest of the stage at 11:00.
The player can use a character that ignores walls, such as  Gyoruntin, to trivialize the process. Alternatively, achieving enough  Move Speed, lag, or visual clutter will also allow the player to pass through the walls of the stage. Making use of the game speed modifier granted by the  Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle also makes traveling to the pillar easier. It should be noted that the Pummarola icon is not indicated on the  Milky Way Map. Interacting with the Pummarola icon unlocks Space Dette as a playable character.
After unlocking, Space Dette can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Space Dette can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "loveknowsnobounds".
## Passive bonuses
Space Dette starts with +40%  Move Speed.
Space Dette's starting weapon  Photonstorm begins at 10 base damage (instead of the normal 25) and gains +10 base damage every 10 levels starting at level 20. There is no cap to this bonus.
After killing a certain number of Enemies, Space Dette will spawn a "fan" follower that will either:
- Follow enemies.
- Follow the player.
- Move opposite to the player's movement, standing still otherwise.
- Stand still.
Fans spawned include  Moon Rabbits and  Moon Ducks. Each fan starts with a  Phas3r. When Space Dette levels up, each fan will receive a copy of  Empty Tome,  Duplicator, or  Clover. Further levels will level up these Weapons and Passives. Fans can collect  Experience Gems for the player.
Fans are spawned upon reaching the following numbers of total enemies defeated:
- 100
- 250
- 500
- 1000
- Once the player has reached 1000 kills, fans will spawn for every 1000 kills (2000, 3000, etc.), until 10000 kills.
- 10000
- Once the player has reached 10000 kills, fans will spawn for every 10000 kills (20000, 30000, etc).
This will continue until the player reaches 30 followers at 180000 kills. After this point, no more followers will spawn.
## Tips
- Wicked Season (XIII) is Space Dette's most important Arcana, since it scales three stats she needs: Growth to increase the damage of her starting Photonstorm, Curse to rack up a high number of kills, and Luck to improve the chance of firing more Photonstorm projectiles.
- Like most of the characters with a starting weapon that scales with no cap, Space Dette can run a very powerful limited weapons build with Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) and Limit Break.

Space Dude is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.9.0. His starting weapon is the  Phas3r.
## Unlocking
Upon surviving to the 20:00 mark in Space 54, Space Dude is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Space Dude can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Space Dude can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thegoodguy".
## Passive bonuses
Space Dude starts with +40%  Move Speed.
Space Dude will occasionally activate all weapons twice. This effect occurs more often the more  Cooldown reduction the character has. When activated, a green and blue aura circles around Space Dude. At -0% Cooldown, this ability will activate every 10-15 seconds. At the -90% Cooldown cap, it will activate roughly every 3 seconds.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
I - Sapphire Mist: Reach a Cooldown bonus of -85% with Space Dude.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana  Sapphire Mist (I).
## Tips
- Using long Cooldown weapons or evolutions with strong or powerful effects like  Seraphic Cry ,  Paranormal Scan [DLC] ,  Emergency Meeting [DLC], and  Atmo-Torpedo [DLC] can greatly increase their effectiveness.
- Although it appears suitable, Space Dude's ability to activate weapons twice will provide little value to the  Pentagram or  Gorgeous Moon. Because the weapons' successive fires occur nearly simultaneously, the second blast would occur before any new enemies spawned after the first blast, rendering it useless. It will still doubles up the moon beams from Gorgeous Moon though.
## Trivia
- Space Dude is the mascot character of Phaser, the game engine Vampire Survivors was originally built on.
- The spell "thegoodguy" may be a reference to the character Lancer from the game Deltarune, as the character has a similar-shaped head and smile to Spaceman, and introduces himself as "The bad guy." when players first meet him.
- In Patch 1.13, a female, secret counterpart of this character was introduced:  Space Dette.

Spiritosa is one of the playable characters in Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is a hidden  Mille Bolle Blu [DLC]
## Unlocking
Spiritosa can be unlocked by reaching level 80 as  Gekkojin [DLC].
Alternatively, Spiritosa can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "? (edit)".
## Passive bonuses
Spiritosa starts with +1.00  Recovery and +50%  Move Speed.
When overhealing by 16, Spiritosa will summon a follower. The maximum number of followers summoned this way is 30. These followers follow the "Chaotic" behavior and cannot die.
Every 8 levels, Spiritosa will obtain another hidden Mille Bolle Blu, up to a max of 6. These hidden weapons are upgraded every 12 levels, up to a maximum of level 8 for each. 
Spiritosa is adept with Mille Bolle Blue,  Boo Roo Boolle [DLC], as well as  Penshin Fatcha and all of its forms, including  Miracle of Multiplication.

Spiritoso can be unlocked by evolving the  Mille Bolle Blu [DLC].

| Stella & Loretta Lecarde [DLC]
| Immune to health drain.

| {"magnet":"-","description":"Immune to health drain.","cost":"2000","duration":"-","unlock":"Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Stella & Loretta Lecarde","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_STELLA_AND_LORETTA","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+1","theme":"The Hidden Curse (Portrait of Ruin)","alias":"Stella & Loretta","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Stella_And_Loretta_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Pneuma Tempestas"}
Stella & Loretta Lecarde are secret playable characters from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Stella Lecarde uses  Pneuma Tempestas as her starting weapon. Loretta Lecarde uses  Cocytus as her starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Stella Lecarde and Loretta Lecarde will appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage as boss enemies, located in the top of the clock tower, between  Abbadon and  Metaglio Right. To unlock them,  Sanctuary must land the final blow with its screen-wide attack, instantly defeating Stella and Loretta Lecarde. The crosses spawned from  Refectio do not count for the unlock.
## Passive bonuses
As a duo, Stella takes the lead followed by a computer controlled Loretta. Both characters can Level up and select their own Weapons and Passive Items, similar to when playing in multiplayer.
Like  Loretta & Stella,  Stella, and  Loretta, Stella & Loretta are immune to Health Drain and start with +1  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed.
All variations can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- While Stella's Stats, Weapons, and Passive Items can be seen anytime by using the pause screen, the player can only see Loretta's stats and items via the Level up screen when selecting an item for Loretta.
## Trivia
- The unlock method for Stella & Loretta is a reference to Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin. In Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin the player must defeat the twins Stella and Loretta Lecarde using the spell Sanctuary to access the game's good ending. Doing this in Vampire Survivors unlocks six secret characters; Jonathan & Charlotte, Charlotte & Jonathan, Stella & Loretta, Loretta & Stella, Stella Lecarde, and Loretta Lecarde.

Stella Lecarde is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Pneuma Tempestas.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Shelter their twin souls with holy magic to restore their humanity."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Stella Lecarde and Loretta Lecarde will appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage as boss enemies, located in the top of the clock tower, between  Abbadon and  Metaglio Right. Once unlocked, Stella Lecarde can be purchased for 2,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Stella Lecarde and her variations can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "twinnado".
## Passive bonuses

Retaliates.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"Slay an inverted skeletal sphere with the Vessel of Dominus.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","armor":"+3","image2":"","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Stone Skull","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"333","revival":"-","version2":"Bull","curse":"+50%","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","banish":"-","theme":"Wandering Ghosts - Vampire Survivors","area":"-","skip":"-","greed":"+50%","speed":"-","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","skin id2":"SKIN_TP_STONESKULL_BULL","flight":"Yes","alias":"","version1":"Stone","movespeed":"-","might":"-","details":"","id":"TP_STONESKULL","release":"28 October 2025","controller":"TP_StoneSkull_Character","cooldown":"-","weapon":"No"}
Gains +1 Armor every 5 levels. Horizontally impaired. Retaliates.
Gains +1 Armor every 5 levels. Horizontally impaired. Retaliates.
DEFAULTSKIN_TP_STONESKULL_BULL
Stone Skull is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. It has no starting weapon, just innate retaliation.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Slay an inverted skeletal sphere with the Vessel of Dominus."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Stone Skull can be unlocked by defeating the  Sketamari in the The Bone Zone on Inverse Mode as  Shanoa. See Guide:Defeating the Sketamari for tips.
Once unlocked, Stone Skull can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Stone Skull can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ismycontrollerbroken".
## Passive bonuses
Stone Skull starts with with +233  Max Health, +3  Armor, +50%  Greed and +50%  Curse.
It gains an additional +1  Armor every 5 levels, starting from level 7.
It is immune to Health Drain.
Stone Skull will always move at a quarter of its normal, or vertical, speed when moving horizontally. Controls that are not locked to only 8 directions, such as a gamepad's analog stick, make it very difficult to stay on a purely horizontal axis, resulting in a meandering up-and-down motion.
Upon receiving damage, Stone Skull will deal retaliatory damage to the enemy that damaged it. This damage is equal to the player's level and is increased by +10% per point of  Armor. This damage will always be at least 1, and can not exceed the damage the enemy dealt. Afterward, any Thorns the player has will be added as extra damage.
## Tips
- Unless the player chooses vertical stages such as Gallo Tower,  Wings is a must for improving Stone Skull's horizontal movement speed.  Sonic Dash [DLC] may also be considered for periodic speed boosts.
- Amusingly enough, Stone Skull's horizontal movement speed penalty can be used to the player's benefit once excessive  Move Speed comes into play, allowing for precise movement in situations where other characters are usually sent careening off with the slightest nudge.
- As the Stone Skull has innate retaliation damage and extra armor, it is a good candidate for the  Divine Bloodline (IX) Arcana.
## External links
- Stone Skull on the Castlevania Wiki

The Succubus is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is  Nightmare.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Give the deciever[sic] a taste of her own medicine."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Beast Gate in Ode to Castlevania, the Succubus will appear as a boss enemy, where her summoning circle is located in the Gallery in a room containing  Dominus Anger. Upon evolving the  Hex into the  Nightmare and defeating her with it, the Succubus is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, the Succubus can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, the Succubus can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thatsnotlisa".
## Passive bonuses
The Succubus starts with +80%  Move Speed, -10%  Cooldown, and +100%  Greed. She also starts with an extra  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) Arcana.
She can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Her unique unlock method is a reference to her appearance and the place where she is fought as a boss in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, in a unique area called the Nightmare. The spell "thatsnotlisa" and her starting with Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) also references this moment as she initially impersonates  Lisa Tepes while creating an illusion of her dying moments to trick  Alucard.

- Succubus on the Castlevania Wiki

DEFAULTLEGACY
Suor Clerici is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.10. Her starting weapon is  Santa Water.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock Clerici by recovering 1,000 HP total over the course of all runs. She is then available to purchase for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Clerici can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "faschiuma".
## Passive bonuses
Clerici starts with +50  Max Health and +0.5  Recovery. Additionally, Clerici starts with +400%  Area, however, the bonus depreciates by 100% each level, until the bonus is entirely gone at level 5.
| Level | Area
| 1 | +400%
| 2 | +300%
| 3 | +200%
| 4 | +100%
| 5+ | 0%
## Tips
- Suor Clerici's low-level  Area bonus applies to the gem explosions of the  Game Killer (0) Arcana, allowing every single gem blast to cover most of the screen and hit dozens of enemies at once. It also locks Clerici at level 1 so her temporary bonus never goes down.

## Other
 - 
 [Suor Clerici talking to  Poe Ratcho about the PlayStation release of Vampire Survivors in the video "Sopravvissuti ai vampiri è su PlayStation".]
 Suor Clerici talking to  Poe Ratcho about the PlayStation release of Vampire Survivors in the video "Sopravvissuti ai vampiri è su PlayStation".
## Trivia
- Suor is Italian for "nun". Clerici is Latin for "priests" or "of a priest".
- Her name comes from Antonella Clerici, an Italian TV host also referenced in "La Borra".

Sypha Belnades is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Ice Fang. Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook, she can choose to start with this weapon selection as an alternative starting weapon via  skin selection.

Sypha Belnades can be unlocked by defeating  Slogra and  Gaibon with  Grant Danasty. She can then be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Sypha can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "bluecipher".

Sypha starts with +40%  Move Speed, +40%  Duration, +10  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips. She also starts with an extra  Jail of Crystal (XIV).
Sypha can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

- Slogra and Gaibon are the first optional boss west of the Giant Medusa Head.

Syuuto Moonspell is a playable character from the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Summon Night.
## Unlocking
The player can unlock this character by evolving the  Four Seasons weapon. He needs to be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Syuuto can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "itsnotaneclipse".
## Passive bonuses
Syuuto starts with +50  Max Health, +2  Armor, -20%  Move Speed, +50%  Might, -30%  Speed, -30%  Area, and +50%  Curse. 
Syuuto gains +3% Might whenever he overheals by +16. This skill is kept when morphing.
Once his weapon evolves into  Echo Night, he transforms into  Megalo Syuuto Moonspell, gaining +100 Max Health, +2 Armor, +40% Movement Speed, +30% Projectile Speed, and +30% Area, bringing him to 250 Max Health, +4 Armor, +20% Movement Speed, and negating his Projectile Speed and Area penalties. He gains +1% Might every level. There is no cap on this bonus.
He starts with a hidden  Night Sword that can only retaliate without taking up any weapon slots and the weapon version can still be offered. It is upgraded every 10 levels starting at level 20, up to level 80 when it is maxed at level 8.
Defeating 100,000 enemies in a single run with him will unlock  Megalo Syuuto Moonspell [DLC] as a playable character.

## Trivia
- Syuuto's Summon Night, Night sword, design, transformation, and him being a half yokai, is likely a reference to Inuyasha from the anime and manga series Inuyasha.

Tera is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is the  Grand Cross.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Slay 10,000 Red foes that deny Death."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Pile of Secrets, Tera can be unlocked by permanently destroying a total of 10,000  Scarleton and/or  Skulorosso. For example, the player can defeat 4,999 Scarletons and 5,001 Skulorosso. This also applies retroactively. 
Alternatively, Tera can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "skelenun".
## Passive bonuses
Tera starts with -80%  Cooldown, which depreciates to -60% at level 2, -30% at level 3, then entirely gone at level 4. She also starts with +20%  Luck.

Toastie is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.4.2. Its starting weapon is  Peachone.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "2) Uppercut a non-red reaper."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
Before Toastie can be unlocked, the player must have unlocked  Exdash Exiviiq through any method.
Upon killing  The Stalker,  The Drowner, or  The Trickster with specific weapons or pickups that can instantly kill enemies, Toastie will appear in the bottom right corner of the screen for half a second before disappearing. In this brief timeframe, the player must quickly press the Down Arrow and Enter keys at the same time while Toastie is onscreen. The S key (the down key in the WASD configuration) does not count. On console pressing down on the "D-Pad" + "confirm button" (A for Switch and Xbox, X for PlayStation) should also work. Doing this unlocks Toastie as a playable character.
 Audio cue
A sound plays whenever Toastie appears in the corner of the screen. It is a high pitched voice saying "panini".
If the half-second window is too difficult, the player can enter Inlaid Library with inverse mode enabled, then go all the way to the right, past the  Gold Ring, until they find The Trickster. The Trickster will appear at the second piano past the  Gold Ring if the "Visually Invert Stages" option is disabled. However, if this option is enabled, The Trickster will instead appear at the third piano past the  Gold Ring. The Trickster will not appear if the player has not yet defeated  The Directer. Hitting The Trickster with an "insta-kill" will not kill it, but will still cause Toastie to appear, allowing the player repeated attempts in a single run.
The following weapons and items will work for unlocking Toastie:
-  Rosary
-  Gorgeous Moon
-  Venus Crescent [DLC]
-  Infinite Corridor
-  Crimson Shroud
-  Victory Sword
-  Dairy Cart
- (Note that this is not the same as  Carréllo or  Carrozza!, and that Dairy Carts do not appear in the Inlaid Library, assuming the player chooses the easier method.)
Once unlocked, Toastie can be purchased for 777, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Toastie can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "tramezzini" after  Exdash Exiviiq, has been unlocked.
## Passive bonuses
Toastie starts with -99  Max Health, +20%  Move Speed, -10%  Might, -10%  Area, -50%  Speed, -10%  Duration, +10%  Cooldown, and +100%  Luck. Additionally, Toastie gains more Luck, Max Health and  Armor at certain levels:
| Level | Luck | Max Health | Armor
| 1-9 | +100% | -99 (1) | 0
| 10-19 | +120% | -99 (1) | 0
| 20-29 | +140% | -99 (1) | 0
| 30-39 | +160% | -99 (1) | 0
| 40-49 | +180% | -99 (1) | 0
| 50-99 | +200% | -99 (1) | 0
| 100-199 | +200% | +9899 (9999) | 0
| 200+ | +200% | +9899 (9999) | +65520
## Tips
- At 1 Max Health in the beginning, any enemies that touches Toastie results in an instant death. Toastie does have +20% additional Move Speed to be able to maneuver past enemies much easier.
- Buying Golden Eggs can increase Toastie’s survivability and help them get past their early-game weakness.
-  Awake (IV) seems like a must have to avoid dying by mistake in the very beginning, and also granting Armor to reduce damage from  The Reaper once Toastie becomes level 200. However,  Divine Bloodline (IX) is the more optimal first pick (as early-game enemies are much easier to kill with retaliatory damage), and by the time the player usually gets their second Arcana, the risk of dying with a Divine Bloodline build should be nearly nonexistent. An early  Paranormal Scan can help with acquiring the last required points of armor to fully withstand the Reaper's assault without hogging a valuable Arcana slot.
- Divine Bloodline (IX) can be used to increase Max Health by killing enemies with retaliatory damage. Tanking early enemies with retaliatory damage builds up massive Max Health, allowing for instant kills by Level 100 with uncapped flat damage boost per missing HP.
- It is recommended to play in Inverse mode to pick and buy both Awake (IV) and Divine Bloodline (IX), as well as rolling for a Retaliate weapon at the start of a run.
- The stages where the  Merchant is located farther away from starting location will be much harder.
- Having high  Luck,  Cherry Bomb is also a good option to buy from the Merchant.
- Building up  Armor is also important to survive and for the Divine Bloodline (IX) strategy.  Centralis Custos [DLC] is a good choice to increase Armor.
-  Tirajisú also helps Toastie survive early game and can boost the benefits from the Awake (IV) Arcana.
- At Level 100, Toastie becomes nearly unkillable, excluding engaging The Reaper. At Level 200, Toastie can withstand the Reaper's damage, as he only takes 15 damage before other  Armor stat increases. Additional Armor increases can lead to Toastie only taking 1 damage from the Reaper.
- Awake (IV) can give up to +6 Armor: +1 from  Revival PowerUp, +3 from Awake (IV), and +2 from Tirajisú.
- +5 Armor from the  Armor passive item.
- +3 Armor from Armor PowerUp.
- Armor increases can also be provided by  Paranormal Scan.
- It is also recommended to build up  Growth earlier to get leveling up faster.
## Trivia
The Toastie popup in-game (in the bottom right corner). 
- During unlocking attempts, Toastie shows up yelling "panini" (its code name).
- A panini is an Italian sandwich.
- Toastie's name is a reference to the Mortal Kombat series. In the original games, when performing the uppercut, a high damage move, sound designer Dan Forden will appear at the bottom right of the screen and exclaim "Toasty!"
- The strange method of unlocking the character is also a reference to Mortal Kombat II, where to fight the hidden character Smoke, players would have to play on a specific stage and wait for Dan Forden to appear and say "Toasty" before pressing Down + Start.
- Toastie is the only case of a character bypassing a player stat cap. Armor which normally has a maximum value of 50 does not apply to Toastie. This also extends to playing Toastie with large amounts of  Golden Eggs.
