Retaliates.","cost":"5000","duration":"-","unlock":"Witness the 17th Colossus wander to new horizons.","secret":"Yes","image":"Select-Torino.png","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Torino","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-35%","area":"-35%","skip":"-","banish":"+5","greed":"-","id":"TORINO","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"","alias":"","movespeed":"+50%","controller":"CharacterController_EX_Torino","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"N/A"}
Torino is a secret playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.14. He does not have a starting weapon.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Witness the 17th Colossus wander to new horizons."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
The player must start a run in Mazerella and take a specific path through the maze generally heading north, before entering a room containing the  17th Collossus. The room containing the Collossus is directly north from the starting room when using a flying character to bypass the walls. The Collossus is initially asleep and only begins to move upon being reduced to 90% of its total health.
Once the Collossus is awake, it will begin walking in a straight line directly to the bottom of the stage. It is important to keep in mind that damaging the Collossus under 75% of its total health will cause it to enrage, targeting the player and preventing the secret from completing. Instead, the Collossus must be allowed to walk beyond the southern border of the stage while it is within the screen, while the player is also standing in the area that the Collossus has destroyed. Doing so unlocks Torino as a playable character.
It is recommended for the player to use a flying character, such as  Gyoruntin, or an invincible character that can avoid dealing enough damage to cause the Collossus to target them, like  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC] to complete this secret. Once the player awakens the Collossus, they should move south to the point where the Collossus eventually walks off-screen, or when the player can not move further south. Once the Collossus walks on-screen, the player can walk right behind them, unlocking Torino. It is also advised that the player does not try to do this secret in Inverse Mode, or at least does not have the 'Visually Invert Stages' option enabled on the main menu.
Room directions when not using a flying character:
- East
- North
- North

- North
Once the Collossus starts moving, in order to avoid damaging it by following it, take the same directions in reverse back to the starting room, but do not take the final west turn into the starting room:
- South
- South
- East
- North
- North

- East
- East

- South
Passing the starting room (on the west), instead continue south:
- South
- South
- South
- South
- West
- South
The wall to the south in this room and the next room is where the Collossus needs to be followed.
Once unlocked, Torino can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Torino can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "wanderingcolossus”.
## Passive bonuses
Torino starts with a hidden weapon akin to  Eskizzibur [DLC]. This can not evolve into  Legionnaire [DLC] and only does retaliatory damage.
Additionally, Torino can transform into 2 alternate forms, JoyaTauro at level 16, and AccumulaTori at level 36.
Upon morphing into Joya Tauro, Torino heals to full HP, loses 20%  Move Speed, and gains 1.35x  Max Health, +1  Armor, +35%  Might, +20%  Duration, and +35%  Area. Torino's hidden weapon will also gain 1.65x Base Damage.
Upon morphing into AccumlaTori, Torino heals to full HP, loses another 20%  Move Speed, and gains 1.8x  Max Health, +2  Armor, +35%  Might, and +20%  Duration. Torino's hidden weapon will gain another +0.65x Base Damage, totaling to 2.3x Base Damage.
Torino can destroy light sources by touching them.
## Tips
- Torino can make incredible use of  Divine Bloodline (IX) with his built-in method of retaliation.
- Because of this, it might be a good idea to try to build up as much health as possible through retaliation before leveling up to transform, in order to gain as much value from the health multiplier as possible.
- Other notable ways of increasing Max Health with Divine Bloodine include  Mazo Familiar,  Legionnaire [DLC], and  Dark Iron Shield [DLC].
## Trivia
- Torino might be named after the city of Turin in Italy, which is called Torino in Italian. Their flag and coat of arms features a bull, which ties the two together.
- Torino's ability to transform after reaching certain level thresholds is likely a reference to the Pokémon franchise. The levels it evolves at, 16 and 36, are the same as many first partner Pokémon.
- Unlike many other characters with hidden weapons, the damage dealt by Torino's is not shown on the end screen after a run.
- The "17th Colossus" mentioned in the Secret is a reference to a rumor about an extra boss in the 2005 action game Shadow of the Colossus, which only has 16.

Trevor Belmont is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Dragon Water Whip. He is also eligible to pick  Morning Star as optional starting weapon.
## Unlocking
To unlock Trevor, defeat the  Giant Medusa Head in Ode to Castlevania with  Leon Belmont.
Alternatively, Trevor can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "wallchickenenjoyer".
Defeating Doppelganger in Ode to Castlevania as Trevor unlocks  Alucard as playable character.
## Passive bonuses
Trevor starts with +20%  Move Speed. He gains +1  Amount every 10 levels up to level 30. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
He starts with  Beginning (X).
Trevor can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- The Giant Medusa Head is the second Boss inside the castle, located at the end of front door corridor.
## Trivia
- Trevor starting with the Arcana Beginning (X) is a nod to the name of the arcana being based in the song from Castlevania III: Dracula's Curse. his home game.

- Trevor Belmont at the Castlevania Wiki

Tsunanori Mido is a playable character from the Emerald Diorama DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Fleuret. 
Fully evolving  Fleuret into  Espada Ropera unlocks  Splashers as an optional starting weapon, as well as his 'solo' skins for both weapons.
## Unlocking
Tsunanori Mido can be unlocked by opening his coffin in Emerald Diorama, located in Miyako City.
Alternatively, Tsunanori Mido can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "adoptedbydestiny".
Since Tsunanori is obtained via coffin, only using the spell will require the player to purchase him for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Tsunanori Mido starts with +20%  Move Speed, +20%  Speed, and +20%  Duration
Tsunanori Mido obtains a  Kugutsu follower every 20 levels (max 4). 
- Kugutsu followers will stay close to Tsunanori in fixed north, east, south and west positions around him.
- Every Kugutsu follower will use a different weapon.
- Kugutsu followers will take damage and can only restore health passively.
- Whenever a Kugutsu follower dies, a coffin with a charge bar will occur. A new Kugutsu follower will come out after the coffin fully charged. Coffins will stay in screen.
He also triggers Showstopper at critical health:
- Gains greatly increased stats (+100%  Might, -100%  Cooldown, +100%  Luck).
- All glimmers will be triggered by every activation.
- The music will also change during Showstopper effect to The World's Heartbeat - VS version
- Showstopper can be triggered again once restored to full health.
Tsunanori has 'Solo' alternatives of both of his starting weapons also via skin selection. These skins do not summon Kugustu followers, but keep the rest of Tsunanori's passive, and are also unlocked once the player fully evolves Fleuret

Vincent Dorin is an initially hidden playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Iron Shield. 
Upon unlocking the  Coat of Arms, Vincent can choose to start with it as an alternate starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Vincent Dorin is unlocked by evolving both the  Fulgur and  Keremet Bubbles, which can be done in different runs. He is then made available for purchase for 9,000, scaling with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Vincent Dorin can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "nofreebies".
## Passive bonuses
Vincent starts with +1  Armor, +20%  Greed, and +10  Banishes. He also starts with an extra  Disco of Gold (XV).
Vincent gains a Greed bonus while overhealing based on log(TotalOverhealing). Note that Vincent does not accumulate overhealing at a 1:1 ratio, instead multiplying overhealing by 0.0005 before adding it to the total.
While Vincent is invulnerable, his total Greed is doubled.
Vincent can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Vlad Tepes Dracula is one of the hidden playable characters in the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is the  Wine Glass, but he also has the option of starting with the  Ebony Diabologue via  skin selection provided that the Ebony Diabologue has been unlocked.
## Unlocking
Vlad Tepes Dracula is unlocked by reaching the end of the Ode to Castlevania stage while playing as  Richter Belmont, and by opening the coffin that appears after the following cutscene. 
Alternatively, Vlad Tepes Dracula can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notinthisgame".
## Passive bonuses
Dracula starts with +566  Max Health, +100%  Might, +100%  Greed, and +10  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips. He gains +1% Might every level, and +1  Revival every 100 levels.
Each extra 1% of  Curse Dracula possesses will increase his Might stat by 1%.
Dracula caps incoming damage at 10, as if he had the  Crimson Shroud, albeit without the retaliation effect. This damage cap is subject to decay in Endless Mode.
He is immune to Health Drain.
If the  Ebony and Crimson Stones have been obtained, he morphs into a new form,  Megalo Dracula, upon reaching level 80, which increases his size drastically and possesses swiping claws that can damage nearby enemies. This form gains +2  Armor, +50%  Move Speed, +150%  Might, and -20%  Cooldown.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- The Arcana  Wicked Season (XIII) and items that increase Curse, including  Torrona's Box,  Skull O'Maniac and the evolved weapon  Nightmare, come highly recommended as Dracula can easily negate the downsides of high Curse.
- A few healing items can synergize with his ability to cap out incoming damage to help keep him alive indefinitely. Importantly, his ability applies to even  The Reaper, making him capable of surviving indefinitely without the need for a specific build.
## Trivia
- The spell "notinthisgame" references the absence of vampires in the game prior to the Ode to Castlevania DLC.
- The passive ability that grants +1 revival every 100 levels is a reference to Dracula resurrecting every 100 years in his home series.
- Dracula is one of the very few characters in Vampire Survivors who has voice acting, alongside Richter Belmont and Death. He is voiced by Neil Newbon.
- Despite being a vampire, unlocking Dracula from a coffin still grants the (now voice acted) message that the vampire is in another coffin. This implies that the Vampire Survivors are not on a hunt for any vampire, but specifically Bisconte Draculó. It is also possible that Dracula and the other "vampires" are simply not vampires in this game, even if they were in their original games.
## External links
- Dracula in the Castlevania Wiki

Immune to health drain.","cost":"16650","duration":"-","unlock":"Let the alchemist personally avenge his family.","secret":"Yes","image":"","luck":"-","amount":"-","speed":"-","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Walter Bernhard","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"150","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"oc","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"+100%","id":"TP_WALTER","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","armor":"+2","theme":"Eneomaos Machine Tower","alias":"Walter","movespeed":"+20%","controller":"TP_Walter_Character","release":"31 October 2024","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Nitesco"}
Walter Bernhard is a secret playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. His starting weapon is  Nitesco.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Let the alchemist personally avenge his family."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After obtaining the  Black Disk in Ode to Castlevania, Walter Bernhard will appear in the stage as a boss enemy, where his summoning circle is located on the bridge between the front building and the middle building. Upon defeating him while playing as  Rinaldo Gandolfi, Walter Bernhard is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Walter Bernhard can be purchased for 16,650, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Walter Bernhard can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "itsallhisfault".
## Passive bonuses
Walter Bernhard starts with +50  Max Health, +2  Armor, +20%  Move Speed, and +100%  Greed. 
Walter is immune to health drain. 
When overhealing by +8 health or more, Walter will trigger any equipped weapon from the  Ebony Diabologue, but not the evolutions of these weapons.
Walter can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Tips
- Thanks to his high health, armor and movement speed, as well as a decently strong weapon, Walter easily supports  Wicked Season (XIII) starts.
- Instead of getting a bunch of Ebony Diabologue weapons, it is actually best to focus on a small number of them and fill the rest of Walter's slots with weapons that heal, like  Fuwalafuwaloo (whose area is similar to Nitesco),  Muramasa [DLC] (which also synergizes with his impressive Greed and health drain immunity), and  Paranormal Scan, which is arguably the best weapon in the game to begin with.
- Perhaps the best Ebony Diabologue weapon to focus on is  Soul Steal, which kills all normal enemies on screen and heals the user, it is normally hampered by a massively long cooldown, but Walter, like  Babi-Onna [DLC], can get around this issue from very early in the game. It is even possible to get Soul Steal to retrigger itself (by healing for 8 or more), at which point Soul Steal will continuously clear the screen of enemies with little to no further input from the player needed.  Pummarola and  Sarabande of Healing (VI) are recommended to achieve this.
-  Sword Brothers can also be used to become permanently invincible.
- With his high Greed, Walter can also be run with Wicked Season (XIII) and  Disco of Gold (XV) to trigger the required overhealing. If doing this, prioritize getting  Vicious Hunger to consistently get coins and/or a source of coin bags like  Greatest Jubilee or  Hail from the Future (X).
## Trivia
- The spell for unlocking Walter, "itsallhisfault", is a reference to his position as the main antagonist of Castlevania: Lament of Innocence, and unbeknownst to him, would serve a pivotal role in creating the generational conflict between the Belmont Clan and Dracula.
## External links
- Walter Bernhard on the Castlevania Wiki

- +20%  Move Speed

While taking damage, Wind will also trigger Aura Blast, with a cooldown of 1 second.
He can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

# Xanthia
Xanthia is a non-playable character in Vampire Survivors. They are a merchant that can be found in Planar Bazaar and Moongolow.
In Planar Bazaar, they are located in the top-middle area of the map. They offer the  Victory Sword,  Unearthly Bolt, and  Phas3r.
In Moongolow, if the player has the Emerald Diorama or the Ante Chamber expansions installed, the player will be able to locate this merchant on the left side of the stage. There, Xanthia will offer the  Emerald Disk and  Joker Disk (if the player has their respective DLC installed).
## Wares
| Items | Type | Description | Cost | Note
| Victory Sword | Weapon | Strikes with a combo attack at the nearest enemy. Retaliates. | 1,080 | Sold in Planar Bazaar only.
| Unearthly Bolt | Weapon | Critical hits generate damaging zones. Revivals affect damage of consecutive critical hits. | 160 | Sold in Planar Bazaar only.
| Phas3r | Weapon | Generates thin damaging zones. High Amount scaling. | 200 | Sold in Planar Bazaar only.
| Emerald Disk [DLC] | Relic | Allows entrance to the Emerald Diorama. | 50,000 | Sold in Moongolow only. One-time purchase.
| Joker Disk [DLC] | Relic | Allows entrance to the Ante Chamber. | 50,000 | Sold in Moongolow only. One-time purchase.
## Trivia
- They are the only merchant in Planar Bazaar that is not a playable character, while everyone else is.
- Internally, their sprite is called "MARIASOFIA." This may suggest that an alternate name for this character would be "Maria Sofia," or either "Maria" or "Sofia."
- Xanthia's name might have been inspired from the character Zanthia from Legend of Kyrandia.
[Verify]

Yatta CavalloSpoopy Yatta CavalloGets more projectiles every 20 levels (max +3).
Gets more projectiles every 20 levels (max +3).
DEFAULTHALLOWS
Yatta Cavallo is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.2.13. His starting weapon is  Cherry Bomb.
After obtaining the  Chaos Rosalia, Yatta Cavallo can evolve the  Cherry Bomb into  Yatta Daikarin upon reaching level 80.
## Unlocking
Yatta Cavallo is unlocked by killing a total of 3,000  Lionheads.
Once unlocked, Cavallo can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased. 
Alternatively, Cavallo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "yattapanda".
Cavallo's Halloween skin is unlocked by entering the Spell "spoopyseason". This will cause the player to begin a run in The Bone Zone with  Bianca Ramba, Yatta Cavallo,  Mortaccio, or  O'Sole Meeo in their Halloween costume. It is not required to reach 30:00 in the Bone Zone to have the skin unlocked, so the player can exit the stage at any time they like in order to do so.
## Passive bonuses
Cavallo gains +1  Amount every 20 levels until level 60. The maximum Amount gained this way is +3.
The following table shows how much Amount he gains by level:
| Levels | Amount
| 1-19 | 0
| 20-39 | +1
| 40-59 | +2
| 60+ | +3
After the  Chaos Rosalia has been obtained, Cavallo will morph into a constellation beast upon reaching level 80 and his weapon will be evolved into  Yatta Daikarin, which will set the chance of explosion to 100%. He will also gain +2  Armor, +1  Amount, +100  Max Health, and +25%  Luck.
## Sprites
 - 
 [Default Cavallo sprite.]
 Default Cavallo sprite.
 - 
 [Halloween Cavallo sprite.]
 Halloween Cavallo sprite.
## Animated sprites
 - 
 [Movement animation of default Cavallo.]
 Movement animation of default Cavallo.
 - 
 [Movement animation of Halloween Cavallo.]
 Movement animation of Halloween Cavallo.
## Trivia
- Cavallo is Italian for "horse", which sounds like the Italian word orso for "bear", hence this character's appearance.
- During the 80s and early 90s, the adaptation of the anime series Yatterman was very popular among kids in Italy. Yatta Cavallo refers to the naming convention of the robots appearing in the anime, which was "yatta" combined with the name of an animal, like Yatta-Pesce (fish), Yatta-Can (dog), and obviously Yatta-Panda.
- Cavallo,  Mortaccio,  Ramba, and  O'Sole have identical passives and stats and only differ in starting weapons.
- Cavallo, Mortaccio, Ramba, O'Sole, and  Ambrojoe are able to get the highest persistent  Amount in the base game at level 60 with 2 levels of Duplicator and the Amount PowerUp, adding a total of +6 projectiles to all applicable weapons.
- Ambrojoe gains +10 Amount as a starting bonus, but quickly loses said Amount as he levels up.
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★,  Rottin'Ghoul,  Horse, and  Simondo Belmont can get one more persistent Amount by reaching level 80.
- Prior to v1.3.0, Cavallo's Halloween skin would be persistent after unlocking and revert once exiting the game.
- There used to be an oversight which allowed the player to press the "R" key on a QWERTY keyboard to morph Cavallo without any requirement. This was most likely a developer shortcut that was not removed, and it has since been fixed.

Yoko Belnades is a playable character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Her starting weapon is the  Fulgur. Upon unlocking the  Belnades' Spellbook, she can choose to start with this weapon selection as an alternative starting weapon via  skin selection.
## Unlocking
Yoko Belnades can be unlocked by defeating  Abbadon, located at the top of Clock Tower, with  Soma Cruz. She can then be purchased for 1,665, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Yoko can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notasuccubus".
## Passive bonuses
Yoko starts with +40%  Move Speed, +40%  Speed, +50%  Magnet, +10  Rerolls,  Banishes, and  Skips. She also starts with an extra  Tragic Princess (III).
Yoko can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.
## Trivia
- Her unlocking spell, "notasuccubus" is a nod to Dawn of Sorrow where the Succubus enemies disguise themselves as Yoko before attacking Soma.

[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Reconnect the youngest hunter with her guardian, his friends, and their doubles."

- +100  Max Health
- +80%  Move Speed
- +80%  Luck

-  Peachone
-  Ebony Wings
-  Familiar Forge - can collect up to 30 Familiar Forges (including the starting one).
She can find special Ode to Castlevania pickups from light sources.

Zephyr is a secret playable character introduced in the 2025 update of the Ode to Castlevania DLC of Vampire Survivors. Their starting weapon is either the  Knife or  Curved Knife.
## Unlocking
[Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane] "Defeat the Chronomage with Dracula's Reincarnation after the clock strikes 30."
Full unlock method hidden. Click "Show Details" to unhide.
After unlocking the  Pile of Secrets, the player must pass 30:00 on Ode to Castlevania as  Soma Cruz. After reaching this point, the player must then defeat  Zephyr, located at the bottom of the clock tower on the right side of the same stage. Doing so will unlock Zephyr as a playable character. It is recommended to attempt this with  Endless Mode enabled to avoid having to deal with  The Reaper.
Once unlocked, Zephyr can be purchased for 5,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
Alternatively, Zephyr can be unlocked in the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "abouttimeyoushowedup".
## Passive bonuses
Zephyr starts with +50%  Move Speed, +20%  Speed and +2  Amount.
While an  Orologion is in effect, he gains an additional +100%  Move Speed and -33%  Cooldown. Orologions are also twice as common.
Zephyr is adept with  Knife,  Curved Knife [DLC],  Pocket Knife [DLC], their evolutions, and  Million Cut [DLC].
## Tips
- Since the boost to Cooldown and Move Speed only applies while the Orologion time stop is in effect,  Out of Bounds (XII) is not a key Arcana for Zephyr despite generating more Orologions as the benefit from it is too situational. Instead, it is usually preferable to run a  Slash (XVI) build backed by  Claimh Solais and possibly  Iron Blue Will (VII) depending on selected weapons.
- However, it can be useful to make room for  Greatest Jubilee in Zephyr's loadout, as it benefits from Slash and may generate more Orologions.
-  Hail from the Future (X) is also a generically powerful Arcana that can help, among other things, generate additional Orologions.
- Knife is typically preferable over Curved Knife as the latter does not benefit from Slash and the former can be evolved into the very powerful  Million Cut.
## External links
- Zephyr on the Castlevania Wiki

Can destroy light sources by touching them.","cost":"500","duration":"+20%","unlock":"Evolve the Ammo Appalate.","secret":"No","image":"Select-Zi'Appunta Belpaese.png","luck":"+20%","amount":"-","speed":"+20%","growth":"-","reroll":"-","name":"Zi'Appunta Belpaese","skin id1":"DEFAULT","maxhealth":"100","revival":"-","curse":"-","dlc":"","recovery":"-","might":"-","area":"-","skip":"-","banish":"-","greed":"-","id":"EX_ZIAPPUNTA","flight":"No","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","armor":"-","theme":"Moms are Tough","alias":"Zi'Appunta","movespeed":"+30%","controller":"CharacterController_EX_Ziappunta","release":"28 October 2025","details":"","cooldown":"-","weapon":"Ammo Appalate"}
Zi'Appunta Belpaese is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 1.14. Her starting weapon is the  Ammo Appalate.
## Unlocking
Upon evolving the  Ammo Appalate into  Gunastrophe, Zi'Appunta Belpaese is unlocked as a playable character.
Once unlocked, Zi'Appunta Belpaese can be purchased for 500, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
While playing as Zi'Appunta Belpaese, special light sources have a chance of appearing in place of regular light sources while moving around. Upon being destroyed, they have small,  Luck-based chance to drop a  Treasure Chest, with another small  Luck-based chance for it to be a  Golden Treasure Chest. Golden Treasure Chests only contain weapons that the player does not already have, and causes them to appear as stage items in a circle around the location the chest was opened instead of equipping them automatically in the player's weapon slots. Finding 10  Golden Treasure Chest in a single run will unlock  Pale Diamond Incursion (V).
Zi'Appunta Belpaese can also destroy light sources automatically upon contact with them.
## Darkana
After obtaining the  Darkasso, an initially hidden achievement is revealed to the player.
V - Pale Diamond Incursion: Discover 10 Golden Treasure Chests in a single run with Zi'Appunta.
Completing this achievement will unlock the Darkana:  Pale Diamond Incursion (V).
## Tips
- The most important stat to level up with Zi'Appunta is  Luck, as it boosts the chances of finding Golden Chests in the special light sources she spawns. As such, the Luck PowerUp should always be on when playing as her,  Clover and  Wicked Season (XIII) are must picks and the player should try to find as many  Little Clover pickups as possible.
- With her fairly well-rounded stats, Zi'Appunta is not geared towards any particular build. Rather, her ability allows her to use as many weapons as possible, more than any other character in the game. In fact, it is theoretically possible for her to have every single weapon equipped at once, though the game would be practically unplayable long before that point.
- Both  Moonlight Bolero (VI) and  Hail from the Future (X) are very useful for Zi'Appunta as they allow her to gain more passive weapons (the former from dark treasure chests, the latter from the  Arma Dio it can potentially spawn), which makes it easier to evolve the mass of weapons she'll acquire.
## Trivia
- Zi'Appunta Belpaese is a spoof of the character Lara Croft from the Tomb Raider series.
- The appearance of this character is a reference to Lara's low-poly model in early Tomb Raider games.
- The name "Zi'Appunta" is (of course) a pun on the other character "Zi'Assunta" - "Zi'" remains the shortened and dialectal form of "Zia" (lit. "Aunt"), but it can also be the shortened form or "Zinne" (chest in Roman dialect), while "Appunta" is a corruption of "A punta" (lit. "spiky"), in reference to the pointy chest.
- She can be unlocked with the code "frozenbutler". The code comes from a glitch in Tomb Raider II, which involved locking Winston the butler in the refrigerator, leaving him completely stationary.
- The Italian word "appunta" is a form of the verb "appuntare" which translates to "to note", "to attach", or "to pin".

Zi'Assunta Belpaese is a playable character from the base game version of Vampire Survivors that was introduced in Patch 0.7.2. Her starting weapon is  Vento Sacro.
## Unlocking
Zi'Assunta is unlocked for free by finding and opening her  Coffin in the Cappella Magna. 
Alternatively, Zi'Assunta can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "paradigmshift". This will require the player to purchase her for 1,000, which scales with other characters purchased.
## Passive bonuses
Zi'Assunta starts with +20%  Move Speed and +10%  Curse. She gains +0.5%  Might,  Projectile Speed,  Duration, and  Area every level. There is no cap on these bonuses.
## Tips
- Her passive ability can eventually outclass  Antonio's Might passive bonus at level 101,  Dommario's Speed and Duration passive bonuses at level 82, and  Porta's Area passive bonus at level 62.
- Like all level-scaling characters, Zi'Assunta's optimal starting Arcana is  Wicked Season (XIII) as its boosts to Curse and Growth massively improve the rate of leveling up.
- Her scaling Might, Area, Speed and Duration means she can make great use of the  Rune Sword, massively increasing its critical damage. If building around the Rune Sword,  Slash (XVI) is a great second arcana to maximize the power of critical hits, it also applies to her starting Vento Sacro and the  Fuwalafuwaloo that she will eventually want to evolve it into. Wicked Season provides the Luck needed to consistently land critical hits.

## Trivia
- "Zia" means aunt or auntie. "Assunta" is an old-fashioned stereotypical southern woman's name.
- In the Italian version of the American sitcom The Nanny, Sylvia Fine (née Rosenberg), the mother of Fran Fine, portrayed by actress Renée Taylor, is named Zia Assunta, while the grandmother is named Yetta Cacace/Concetta Caciotta.
- Zi'Assunta's family name is Belpaese, her theme song is called "Moms are Tough", and she wields what seems to be a legendary whip ( Vento Sacro). All these aspects could imply that she is the mother of the Belpaese family and a legendary hero herself.
- Zi'Assunta's family name Belpaese is derived from the phrase Bel Paese, which is Italian for "beautiful country", and also refers to a semi-soft Italian cheese of the same name.
- Zi'Assunta shares several traits with other coffin characters. See Coffin § Traits for more details.
- She and  Concetta Caciotta are the only coffin characters that start with +10% Curse.
- Zi'Assunta's theme used to be called "Vento Sacro" in the music selection. poncle made a poll for the official name for it and "Moms are tough" was chosen.
